### [1] [Full Feature Spiking Neural Network Simulation on Micro-Controllers for Neuromorphic Applications at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16474)
*L. Niedermeier,J. L. Krichmar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文成功将全功能SNN模拟器CARLsim移植到微控制器RP2350上运行，通过使用IEEE 16位浮点数降低内存需求，实现了比ARM处理器高5倍的能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SNN软件模拟依赖GPU工作站或专用处理器，能耗较高。微控制器具有极低的尺寸、重量和功耗特性，非常适合边缘计算应用，因此研究旨在将SNN模拟器部署到低功耗MCU上。

Method: 利用IEEE 16位半精度浮点数替代标准单精度浮点数，在不损失功能的前提下降低内存需求，使CARLsim模拟器能在仅8MB内存的MCU RP2350上运行全功能集。

Result: 成功运行包含1200个神经元的Synfire4基准测试，相比标准单精度精度达97.5%；在20mW功耗下实现了186神经元规模的实时运行。与Raspberry Pi Zero 2 W的最小应用级ARM处理器相比，SNN本身能效高5倍，完整SoC系统能效优一个数量级。

Conclusion: 证明了在极低功耗MCU上运行完整SNN模拟器的可行性，为边缘端的神经形态计算应用提供了高能效的解决方案，突破了传统SNN模拟对高性能处理器的依赖。

Abstract: Microcontroller units (MCU), which have an order of magnitude lower Size, Weight and Power (SWaP) than standard computers, makes them suitable for applications at the edge. Neuromorphic computing, which can realize low SWaP, relies on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs). Until now, software based simulations of SNNs required GPU-based workstations, application classified core processors such as the ARM Cortex-A53, or specialized hardware like Intel's Loihi. In the present work, we demonstrate that the SNN simulator CARLsim can run its full feature set on a MCU RP2350 with 8 MB memory. We accomplished this by utilizing IEEE 16-bit float point numbers, which reduced memory requirements without loss of function. We were able to run the Synfire4 benchmark which comprises 1200 neurons. The accuracy was 97.5% compared to the standard single precision numbers. Furthermore, we show that CARLsim runs a Synfire4 benchmark scaled-down to 186 neurons on a MCU in real-time at only 20 mW. Compared to the smallest application class ARM processor used by Raspberry in their Pi Zero 2 W, our MCU implementation is five times more energy efficient for the SNN itself, and an order of magnitude better when compared to the complete SoC (MCU/CPU + Board).

</details>


### [2] [Forge-UGC: FX optimization and register-graph engine for universal graph compiler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16498)
*Satyam Kumar,Saurabh Jha*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Forge-UGC是一个针对异构加速器上Transformer部署的四阶段编译器，在Intel AI Boost NPU上验证，相比OpenVINO和ONNX Runtime实现6.9-9.2倍编译加速、18.2-35.7%延迟降低和30.2-40.9%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（如OpenVINO和ONNX Runtime）采用不透明的编译流水线、有限的Pass级可见性和薄弱的缓冲区管理，导致编译成本高和运行时开销大。缺乏针对异构加速器的高效、透明的编译解决方案。

Method: 提出四阶段编译流程：Phase 1使用torch.export在ATen算子级别捕获图，支持RoPE、GQA、SwiGLU等现代Transformer组件；Phase 2应用六种优化Pass（死代码消除、公共子表达式消除、常量折叠、注意力融合、算子融合、布局优化）；Phase 3将优化图降低为带显式虚拟寄存器分配的类型化中间表示；Phase 4执行活跃性分析、线性扫描缓冲区分配和设备亲和性调度。引入Fusion Gain Ratio、Compilation Efficiency Index等系统评估指标。

Result: 在6个模型家族（125M-8B参数）上验证：图节点数减少14.2-21.9%；峰值缓冲区数量减少30-48%；NPU-CPU转换减少42-65%；编译速度比OpenVINO/ONNX Runtime快6.9-9.2倍；推理延迟降低18.2-35.7%；每次推理能耗降低30.2-40.9%；最大绝对logit差异低于2.1e-5，KL散度低于8.4e-9。

Conclusion: Forge-UGC通过硬件无关设计和四阶段分离架构，成功解决了现有框架的编译效率低和运行时开销高问题，在保持数值精度的同时显著提升了编译速度、推理延迟和能耗效率，为NPU编译流水线提供了系统化的评估方法。

Abstract: We present Forge-UGC (FX Optimization and Register-Graph Engine for Universal Graph Compilation), a four-phase compiler for transformer deployment on heterogeneous accelerator hardware, validated on Intel AI Boost NPU. Existing frameworks such as OpenVINO and ONNX Runtime often use opaque compilation pipelines, limited pass-level visibility, and weak buffer management, which can lead to higher compilation cost and runtime overhead. Forge-UGC addresses this with a hardware-agnostic design that separates graph capture, optimization, intermediate representation lowering, and backend scheduling. Phase 1 captures graphs with torch.export at the ATen operator level, supporting modern transformer components such as rotary position embeddings, grouped-query attention, and SwiGLU without manual decomposition. Phase 2 applies six optimization passes: dead code elimination, common subexpression elimination, constant folding, attention fusion, operator fusion, and layout optimization, reducing graph node count by 14.2 to 21.9%. Phase 3 lowers the optimized graph into a typed intermediate representation with explicit virtual register assignments. Phase 4 performs liveness analysis, linear-scan buffer allocation, reducing peak buffer count by 30 to 48%, and device-affinity scheduling, reducing NPU-CPU transitions by 42 to 65%. Across six model families ranging from 125M to 8B parameters, evaluated on WikiText-103 and GLUE, Forge-UGC delivers 6.9 to 9.2x faster compilation than OpenVINO and ONNX Runtime, 18.2 to 35.7% lower inference latency, and 30.2 to 40.9% lower energy per inference. Fidelity is preserved, with max absolute logit differences below 2.1e-5 and KL divergence below 8.4e-9. We also introduce Fusion Gain Ratio, Compilation Efficiency Index, and per-pass execution profiling for systematic evaluation of NPU compilation pipelines.

</details>


### [3] [EquivFusion: Unifying Hardware Equivalence Checking from Algorithms to Netlists via MLIR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16571)
*Jiaying Zhu,Baoqi Zhang,Mengxia Tao,Kezhi Li,Hao Yan,Qiang Xu,Min Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了EquivFusion，一个面向多模态电路设计的端到端等价性检查工具，通过统一的MLIR中间表示，实现了从深度学习框架、编程语言、硬件描述语言到网表等多源异构抽象层次的自动化等价验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代设计流程横跨深度学习框架到硬件网表等异构抽象层次，确保高层算法模型与底层硬件实现之间的功能一致性是一个关键挑战。传统验证流程依赖孤立工具或临时翻译，缺乏统一的验证框架，难以有效弥合软件规约与硬件实现之间的语义鸿沟。

Method: 提出EquivFusion工具，采用面向验证的MLIR降级流水线，将PyTorch、C/C++、Chisel、Verilog和门级网表等多种输入源统一转换为公共中间表示。通过严格的翻译流程，将设计转换为SMT-LIB、BTOR2、AIGER等标准形式化验证格式，实现跨抽象层次的自动化成对等价性检查。

Result: 成功演示了EquivFusion在弥合软件规约与硬件实现语义鸿沟方面的可行性，有效支持数据通路密集型硬件设计的"左移"形式化验证，证明了工具在处理多模态电路设计等价性检查方面的有效性。

Conclusion: EquivFusion为异构抽象层次之间的等价性验证提供了统一的端到端解决方案，能够有效促进硬件设计流程中的早期形式化验证，为跨越软件到硬件的多层次设计验证提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Ensuring functional consistency between high-level algorithmic models and low-level hardware implementations is a critical challenge, particularly as modern design flows increasingly span heterogeneous abstractions--from deep learning frameworks to hardware netlists. In this paper, we present EquivFusion, an end-to-end equivalence checking tool tailored for multi-modal circuit designs. Unlike traditional flows that rely on siloed tools or ad-hoc translation, EquivFusion leverages a verification-oriented MLIR lowering pipeline to unify diverse entry points, including PyTorch, C/C++, Chisel, Verilog, and gate-level netlists, into a common intermediate representation. This architecture enables automated, pairwise equivalence checking across diverse abstraction levels by rigorously translating designs into standard formal verification formats, i.e., SMT-LIB, BTOR2, AIGER. We demonstrate EquivFusion's feasibility to bridge the semantic gap between software specifications and hardware realizations, showcasing its effectiveness in facilitating "shift-left" formal verification for datapath-intensive hardware designs.

</details>


### [4] [E2AFS: Energy-Efficient Approximate Floating Point Square Rooter for Error Tolerant Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16964)
*Prateek Goyal,Jatin Kumar Reddy Mothe,Swara Rajesh Shelke,Sujit Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了E2AFS，一种轻量级、完全无乘法器的浮点平方根架构，在功耗、延迟和精度方面均优于现有方案，适用于边缘AI等低功耗实时系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮点平方根运算是边缘AI、信号处理和嵌入式系统中的关键操作，传统实现依赖乘法器或迭代流水线，导致硬件复杂度高、开关活动大且能耗高，亟需一种低功耗高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出E2AFS架构，采用完全无乘法器设计，通过降低逻辑深度和最小化开关活动来优化能效，并在Artix-7 FPGA上实现与验证。

Result: FPGA实验结果表明，与ESAS和CWAHA架构相比，E2AFS实现了最低动态功耗(7.63 mW)、最短关键路径延迟(4.639 ns)和最小功耗延迟积(35.39 pJ)；多精度指标评估显示其偏差低，能精确逼近精确平方根函数。

Conclusion: E2AFS在Sobel边缘检测和K-means颜色量化等应用中的验证证实了其在低功耗实时边缘和嵌入式平台上的适用性和优越性。

Abstract: Floating-point square-root computation is a power- and delay-critical operation in edge-AI, signal-processing, and embedded systems. Conventional implementations typically rely on multipliers or iterative pipelines, resulting in increased hardware complexity, switching activity, and energy consumption. This work presents E2AFS, a lightweight and fully multiplier-free floating-point square-root architecture optimized for energy-efficient computation. By reducing logic depth and minimizing switching activity, the proposed design achieves substantial improvements in hardware efficiency and performance. FPGA implementation on an Artix-7 device demonstrates that E2AFS achieves the lowest dynamic power (7.63 mW), the shortest critical-path delay (4.639 ns), and the minimum power-delay product (35.39 pJ) compared to existing ESAS and CWAHA architectures. Error evaluation using multiple accuracy metrics, together with graphical analysis, shows that E2AFS closely approximates the exact square-root function with consistently low deviation. Application-level validation in Sobel edge detection and K-means color quantization further confirms its suitability for low-power real-time edge and embedded platforms.

</details>


### [5] [Different Perspectives of Memory System Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16965)
*Pouya Esmaili-Dokht,Arash Yadegari,Victor Xirau,Julian Pavon,Adrian Cristal,Eduard Ayguade,Petar Radojkovic*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多视角验证方法论，发现CPU-内存接口是导致内存模拟器与真实硬件性能偏差的主要原因；通过修正接口问题，显著提升了Ramulator、DRAMsim3等主流模拟器的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的内存模拟器在预估高级内存系统上的应用性能时，与真实硬件相比存在显著差异。为了确保可靠的性能预测，需要探究导致这些不准确的根本原因，并建立正确的模拟器验证方法。

Method: 提出了一种从三个互补视角评估内存性能的方法论：内存模拟器视角、CPU-内存接口视角以及应用程序视角。通过对比分析这三个视角的差异，定位不准确的根源，并针对CPU-内存接口实施了修正和增强，在多个主流模拟器（Ramulator, Ramulator 2, DRAMsim3集成ZSim）上进行了验证。

Result: 分析显示这三个视角可能存在巨大分歧，应用程序层面的性能往往与模拟器内部统计数据脱钩。研究确定CPU-内存接口是导致不准确性的主要来源。通过修正接口相关问题，模拟结果能够更紧密地逼近真实系统的性能表现。

Conclusion: 修正CPU-内存接口相关问题对于实现接近真实系统性能的模拟结果至关重要。该研究为内存模拟器的验证和改进提供了系统性的方法论指导。

Abstract: Memory simulators are used to estimate application performance on advanced memory systems, yet they may exhibit significant discrepancies compared to real hardware. This paper investigates two key questions: (1) what causes these inaccuracies, and (2) how can simulators be properly validated to ensure reliable performance predictions. We propose a methodology that evaluates memory performance from three complementary perspectives: the memory simulator, the CPU-memory interface, and the application. Our analysis reveals that these perspectives can diverge substantially, with application-level performance often decoupled from internal simulator statistics. We identify the CPU-memory interface as the primary source of these inaccuracies. To address these problems, we implement a set of corrections and enhancements that improve the fidelity of integrated simulators. We evaluate these changes across multiple widely used simulators, including Ramulator, Ramulator 2, and DRAMsim3 integrated with ZSim. The results show that correcting interface-related issues is essential to achieve simulation outcomes that closely resemble actual system performance.

</details>


### [6] [From Natural Language to Silicon: The Representation Bottleneck in LLM Hardware Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17097)
*Weimin Fu,Zeng Wang,Minghao Shao,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique,Xiaolong Guo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究发现硬件中间表示（IR）的选择是决定LLM硬件设计成功率的主导因素（即“表示瓶颈”），模型选择的影响较小。不同IR的仿真通过率差异巨大（3%-88%），但模型间差异很小。LLM因简洁性偏置在资源受限FPGA上表现出更高的通过率，揭示了易用性与性能之间的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘应用需要定制硬件，但FPGA设计需要领域工程师所缺乏的专业知识。大语言模型（LLM）有望通过零知识硬件编程来弥合这一差距，即用户用自然语言描述电路，LLM将其编译为面向硅片的硬件中间表示（IR）。本研究旨在探究在此流程中影响端到端成功率的关键因素。

Method: 将LLM硬件设计流程建模为二进制滤波器的级联。评估了三个前沿LLM在六种IR（Verilog, VHDL, Chisel, Bluespec, PyMTL3, HLS C）上的表现，涵盖202个任务。评估流程包括编译、仿真、在Lattice iCE40UP5K上进行FPGA综合以及基于LLM的修复。

Result: IR选择而非模型选择是决定端到端成功的主导因素（表示瓶颈）。仿真通过率在IR间跨度为3%至88%，但在单一IR内不同模型间变化通常小于1.25倍。在资源受限的iCE40上，LLM设计的条件FPGA通过率（86.5%）高于参考方案（68.7%），这归因于简洁性偏置使其设计足够小以适应资源。分析揭示了可访问性-能力悖论：最用户友好的IR产生的LLM性能最差。

Conclusion: 研究识别出“表示瓶颈”现象和“可访问性-能力悖论”，表明当前IR选择对LLM硬件设计成功率的决定性作用超过模型选择。随着LLM能力的增长，最优IR选择策略需要相应演变。

Abstract: Edge applications increasingly demand custom hardware, yet Field-Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) design requires expertise that domain engineers lack. Large Language Models (LLMs) promise to bridge this gap through zero-knowledge hardware programming, where users describe circuits in natural language and an LLM compiles them to a hardware intermediate representation (IR) targeting silicon. Modeling this flow as a cascade of binary filters, this work demonstrates that IR choice, not model choice, is the dominant factor governing end-to-end success, a phenomenon termed the representation bottleneck. An evaluation of three frontier LLMs across six IRs spanning Verilog, VHDL, Chisel, Bluespec, PyMTL3, and HLS C on 202 tasks through a pipeline of compilation, simulation, FPGA synthesis on a Lattice iCE40UP5K, and LLM-based repair shows that simulation pass rates range from 3% to 88% across IRs but typically vary less than 1.25x across models within any single IR. On the resource-constrained iCE40, LLM designs achieve a higher conditional FPGA pass rate than reference solutions, 86.5% vs. 68.7%, not because they are better but because a simplicity bias makes them small enough to fit. The analysis reveals an accessibility-competence paradox: the most user-friendly IRs yield the worst LLM performance, suggesting that optimal IR selection will evolve as LLM capabilities grow.

</details>


### [7] [Configuration Over Selection: Hyperparameter Sensitivity Exceeds Model Differences in Open-Source LLMs for RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17102)
*Minghao Shao,Zeng Wang,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Ramesh Karri,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 在硬件设计的LLM基准测试中，解码超参数配置的影响远超模型选择本身。同一模型在不同配置下性能差距可达25.5%，是不同模型间默认配置性能差异的5倍。最优配置不具备跨基准的可迁移性，默认参数下的基准测试混淆了模型能力与配置效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开源LLM硬件设计基准测试主要关注模型选择，而将推理时的解码配置视为次要因素。本研究旨在揭示解码超参数配置对模型性能的影响程度，以及默认配置下的基准测试是否存在偏差。

Method: 1. 在VerilogEval和RTLLM基准测试上，使用综合环评估对26个开源LLM进行评估；
2. 绘制当前能力版图；
3. 对三个代表性模型进行广泛的108种配置超参数扫描；
4. 使用Spearman相关系数分析配置排名的可迁移性。

Result: 1. 同一LLM的最佳与最差配置之间绝对通过率差距高达25.5%；
2. 该差距是不同模型族在默认配置下平均性能差异的5倍；
3. 两个基准测试套件之间的配置排名Spearman相关系数接近零，表明最优配置不可迁移；
4. 默认超参数下的基准测试混淆了模型能力与配置效应。

Conclusion: 充分发挥开源LLM在RTL生成中的潜力需要架构感知和基准感知的超参数选择。研究提出了一种方法论来实现这种针对性的超参数选择，而不应依赖默认配置或假设配置在不同基准间可迁移。

Abstract: Benchmarking of open-source LLMs for hardware design focuses on which LLMs to use, while treating inference-time decoding configuration as a secondary concern. This work shows that it matters more how an LLM is configured than which model is selected. Benchmarking 26 open-source LLMs on VerilogEval and RTLLM with synthesis-in-the-loop evaluation, the study first maps the current capability landscape and then conducts an extensive 108-configuration hyperparameter sweep on three prominent models. The sweep reveals absolute pass-rate gaps of up to 25.5% between the best and worst settings for the same LLM, which is 5x larger than the average spread observed across various model families under their respective default configurations. Ranking all configurations by Spearman's $ρ$ across the two benchmark suites yields near-zero correlation, demonstrating that optimal configurations do not transfer. These results show that benchmarking conducted under default hyperparameters confounds model capabilities with configuration effects. Realizing the full potential of open-source LLMs for RTL generation requires architecture and benchmark aware hyperparameter selection, as enabled by the proposed methodology.

</details>


### [8] [Clover: A Neural-Symbolic Agentic Harness with Stochastic Tree-of-Thoughts for Verified RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17288)
*Zizhang Luo,Yansong Xu,Runlin Guo,Fan Cui,Kexing Zhou,Mile Xia,Hongyuan Hou,Yuhao Luo,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Clover，一个神经符号代理框架，用于RTL程序修复，在基准测试中修复了96.8%的错误，显著优于传统和LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTL程序修复是硬件设计和验证的关键瓶颈。传统自动程序修复（APR）方法依赖预定义模板和综合，限制了错误覆盖范围；基于大语言模型（LLM）的编码代理虽具灵活性，但在处理长RTL代码和波形时存在随机性和上下文损坏问题。

Method: 提出Clover，一个神经符号代理框架：1）将RTL修复组织为代码操作的结构化搜索；2）动态分派任务给专门的LLM代理或符号求解器；3）引入随机思维树作为测试时扩展机制，将主代理上下文管理为搜索树，平衡探索与利用；4）配备RTL专用工具箱增强代理与调试环境的交互。

Result: 在RTL修复基准测试中，Clover在固定时间限制内修复了96.8%的错误，比纯传统方法和LLM基线分别多覆盖94%和63%的错误，平均pass@1率达到87.5%。

Conclusion: Clover通过神经符号方法和结构化搜索策略，有效解决了RTL修复中的关键挑战，在可靠性和有效性方面表现出色，为硬件设计验证提供了强有力的解决方案。

Abstract: RTL program repair remains a critical bottleneck in hardware design and verification. Traditional automatic program repair (APR) methods rely on predefined templates and synthesis, limiting their bug coverage. Large language models (LLMs) and coding agents based on them offer flexibility but suffer from randomness and context corruption when handling long RTL code and waveforms. We present Clover, a neural-symbolic agentic harness that orchestrates RTL repair as a structured search over code manipulations to explore a validated solution for the bug. Recognizing that different repair operations favor distinct strategies, Clover dynamically dispatches tasks to specialized LLM agents or symbolic solvers. At its core, Clover introduces stochastic tree-of-thoughts, a test-time scaling mechanism that manages the main agent's context as a search tree, balancing exploration and exploitation for reliable outcomes. An RTL-specific toolbox further empowers agents to interact with the debugging environment. Evaluated on the RTL-repair benchmark, Clover fixes 96.8% of bugs within a fixed time limit, covering 94% and 63% more bugs than both pure traditional and LLM-based baselines, respectively, while achieving an average pass@1 rate of 87.5%, demonstrating high reliability and effectiveness.

</details>


### [9] [AccelCIM: Systematic Dataflow Exploration for SRAM Compute-in-Memory Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17692)
*Chenhao Xue,Yukun Wang,An Guo,Yuhui Shi,Jinwei Zhou,Xiping Dong,Yihan Yin,Yuanpeng Zhang,Tianyu Jia,Wei Gao,Qiang Wu,Xin Si,Jun Yang,Guangyu Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AccelCIM框架，针对大模型无法完全放入片上的问题，系统性地探索了SRAM CIM加速器的数据流设计空间，通过精确的仿真和后布局分析为加速器设计提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SRAM CIM加速器研究通常假设DNN模型能完全放入片上，忽略了模型过大时的数据搬运开销，且缺乏对高效数据流设计及其系统性评估方法的研究。

Method: 提出了AccelCIM框架，主要包含两部分：(1) 构建了一个涵盖CIM宏单元配置和宏阵列组织的系统性数据流设计空间；(2) 引入了基于周期精确架构仿真和后布局PPA分析的严格设计评估方法。

Result: 进行了广泛的设计空间探索，并将AccelCIM应用于代表性大语言模型(LLM)应用，揭示了CIM加速器设计中关键因素与性能关系。

Conclusion: AccelCIM框架填补了SRAM CIM加速器数据流设计研究的空白，通过系统性的设计空间探索和严格的评估方法，为CIM加速器的原理化设计提供了实用的见解和指导。

Abstract: SRAM-based compute-in-memory (CIM) offers high computational density and energy efficiency for deep neural network (DNN) accelerators, but its limited capacity causes on/off-chip data movement overhead for large DNN models. Existing CIM accelerator studies typically assume that DNN models fit entirely on-chip, leaving efficient dataflow design largely untapped. This paper introduces AccelCIM, a systematic dataflow exploration framework for SRAM CIM accelerator, which addresses two key limitations of prior work. (1) It formulates a systematic dataflow design space spanning CIM macro configurations and macro-array organizations. (2) It introduces rigorous design evaluation using cycle-accurate architectural simulation and post-layout PPA analysis. We conduct an extensive design space exploration and apply AccelCIM to representative LLM applications, providing practical insights for the principled design of CIM accelerators.

</details>


### [10] [Enabling AI ASICs for Zero Knowledge Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17808)
*Jianming Tong,Jingtian Dang,Simon Langowski,Tianhao Huang,Asra Ali,Jeremy Kun,Jevin Jiang,Srinivas Devadas,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了MORPH框架，首次将零知识证明（ZKP）核心计算内核重构以适配AI加速器（如TPU）执行。通过引入硬件感知的Big-T复杂度模型，在算术层面将高精度模运算转化为低精度矩阵运算，在数据流层面优化布局以避免片上数据重排。实验表明，在TPUv6e上NTT吞吐量相比GZKP提升高达10倍，MSM吞吐量相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明（ZKP）证明者的计算成本高昂，核心瓶颈在于多标量乘法（MSM）和数论变换（NTT）需要大量计算资源。现有的AI专用芯片（如TPU）具备强大的矩阵吞吐量和极佳的能效，但ZKP计算内核与AI硬件的执行模式不匹配，亟待一种框架将二者结合以提升效率。

Method: 提出了MORPH框架，核心包含两点创新：1. 引入Big-T复杂度模型，这是一种硬件感知的复杂度分析方法，揭示了被传统Big-O忽略的异构瓶颈和布局变换成本。2. 基于该分析，在算术层面开发了以矩阵乘法单元（MXU）为中心的扩展RNS惰性归约方法，将高精度模运算转化为稠密的低精度通用矩阵乘法（GEMM），消除了进位链；在数据流层面，构建了统一分片的布局静止TPU Pippinger MSM算法和优化的3/5步NTT算法，避免了TPU片上的混洗操作，从而最小化了昂贵的内存重组开销。

Result: 基于JAX实现并在TPUv6e8上评估，MORPH在NTT任务上实现了相比现有方案GZKP高达10倍的吞吐量提升，在MSM任务上实现了可比的吞吐量性能。

Conclusion: 该工作成功证明了通过算法重构使ZKP计算适配AI加速器是可行且高效的。MORPH框架有效利用了TPU的矩阵计算优势，大幅提升了ZKP核心算子的性能，为利用现有大规模AI算力基础设施加速隐私计算和区块链应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proof (ZKP) provers remain costly because multi-scalar multiplication (MSM) and number-theoretic transforms (NTTs) dominate runtime as they need significant computation. AI ASICs such as TPUs provide massive matrix throughput and SotA energy efficiency. We present MORPH, the first framework that reformulates ZKP kernels to match AI-ASIC execution. We introduce Big-T complexity, a hardware-aware complexity model that exposes heterogeneous bottlenecks and layout-transformation costs ignored by Big-O. Guided by this analysis, (1) at arithmetic level, MORPH develops an MXU-centric extended-RNS lazy reduction that converts high-precision modular arithmetic into dense low-precision GEMMs, eliminating all carry chains, and (2) at dataflow level, MORPH constructs a unified-sharding layout-stationary TPU Pippenger MSM and optimized 3/5-step NTT that avoid on-TPU shuffles to minimize costly memory reorganization. Implemented in JAX, MORPH enables TPUv6e8 to achieve up-to 10x higher throughput on NTT and comparable throughput on MSM than GZKP. Our code: https://github.com/EfficientPPML/MORPH.

</details>


### [11] [AQPIM: Breaking the PIM Capacity Wall for LLMs with In-Memory Activation Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18137)
*Kosuke Matsushima,Yasuyuki Okoshi,Masato Motomura,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对PIM架构中长上下文场景下KV缓存过大导致内存容量不足的问题，本文提出了AQPIM框架。该框架基于乘积量化（PQ）实现PIM感知的激活量化，利用PIM的高内部带宽在内存内直接对压缩数据进行计算，大幅降低显存占用和计算开销，相比现有SOTA PIM方法实现3.4倍加速，并减少90~98.5%的GPU-CPU通信延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存内计算（PIM）架构虽然能缓解数据密集型机器学习的内存瓶颈，但在长上下文场景下面临激活内存占用过大的挑战。传统PIM方法难以应对Transformer模型产生的巨大KV缓存，经常超出PIM有限的内存容量，而稀疏注意力等技术又与PIM所需的数据局部性相冲突。现有的PIM方法和量化技术难以有效利用激活值的独特特性。

Method: 本文提出AQPIM框架，采用基于乘积量化（PQ）的聚类向量量化方法。该框架针对大语言模型进行优化，直接在内存内执行量化操作，充分利用PIM的高内部带宽，实现对压缩数据的直接计算。同时，AQPIM引入多项算法优化来解决PQ在精度方面的挑战。

Result: 实验结果表明，AQPIM显著降低了GPU-CPU通信（可占解码延迟的90~98.5%），相比现有最先进的PIM方法实现了3.4倍的速度提升。该框架有效减少了内存占用和注意力计算的计算开销。

Conclusion: AQPIM证明了PIM专用激活量化的可行性，通过在PIM架构中直接处理压缩数据，有效解决了长上下文场景下的内存容量限制问题，为PIM架构与大语言模型的结合提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) architectures offer a promising solution to the memory bottlenecks in data-intensive machine learning, yet often overlook the growing challenge of activation memory footprint. Conventional PIM approaches struggle with massive KV cache sizes generated in long-context scenarios by Transformer-based models, frequently exceeding PIM's limited memory capacity, while techniques like sparse attention can conflict with PIM's need for data locality. Existing PIM approaches and quantization methods are often insufficient or poorly suited for leveraging the unique characteristics of activations. This work identifies an opportunity for PIM-specialized activation quantization to enhance bandwidth and compute efficiency.
  We explore clustering-based vector quantization approaches, which align well with activation characteristics and PIM's internal bandwidth capabilities. Building on this, we introduce AQPIM, a novel PIM-aware activation quantization framework based on Product Quantization (PQ), optimizing it for modern Large Language Models (LLMs). By performing quantization directly within memory, AQPIM leverages PIM's high internal bandwidth and enables direct computation on compressed data, significantly reducing both memory footprint and computational overhead for attention computation. AQPIM addresses PQ's accuracy challenges by introducing several algorithmic optimizations. Evaluations demonstrate that AQPIM achieves significant performance improvements, drastically reducing of GPU-CPU communication that can account for 90$\sim$98.5\% of decoding latency, together with 3.4$\times$ speedup over a SOTA PIM approach.

</details>


### [12] [VerilogCL: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Robust LLM-Based Verilog Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18162)
*Yan Tan,Tong Liu,Xiangchen Meng,Yangdi Lyu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了VerilogCL框架，通过对比学习和主动错误筛选机制，显著提升了大语言模型生成Verilog代码的编译成功率和功能正确性，7B参数模型在公开基准测试中超越了现有开源、专用和商业基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件代码生成领域表现出色，但在硬件描述语言（如Verilog）的应用中面临高质量训练数据稀缺的挑战。现有模型生成的Verilog代码常包含语法或结构错误，导致编译失败或功能设计不正确，严重限制了其在硬件设计工作流中的可靠性。

Method: 提出了VerilogCL集成框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)最小错误数据增强，生成正确RTL代码与最小扰动错误RTL代码的配对训练样本；(2)对比学习，在表示空间学习清晰的有效性边界，增强正确与错误RTL代码的区分度；(3)主动筛选模块，结合语义嵌入与token级不确定性特征，在生成过程中过滤低置信度候选。

Result: 在VerilogEval和RTLLM公开基准测试上的实验表明，7B参数模型在编译成功率和功能正确性两个关键指标上均优于所评估的开源模型、Verilog专用模型和商业基线模型。

Conclusion: VerilogCL框架通过显式学习正确与错误RTL代码的边界，有效解决了LLM在Verilog代码生成中的可靠性问题，为硬件设计自动化提供了更可靠的解决方案，证明了对比学习和主动筛选机制在代码生成任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently achieved strong performance in software code generation. However, applying them to hardware description languages (HDLs), such as Verilog, remains challenging because high-quality training data are relatively scarce. In practice, LLM-generated Verilog often contains syntactic or structural errors that either cause compilation failures or produce functionally incorrect designs, which limit its reliability in hardware design workflows.
  In this work, we propose VerilogCL, an integrated framework that enhances Verilog code generation by explicitly learning the boundary between correct and erroneous RTL through contrastive learning and proactive error screening. Our approach introduces minimal-error data augmentation, generating paired training samples of correct RTL and minimally perturbed erroneous RTL to teach the model to recognize fine-grained distinctions between correct and erroneous code. We then apply contrastive learning to learn a clearer validity boundary in the representation space, improving the separation between correct and erroneous RTL code. In addition, we introduce a proactive screening module that combines semantic embeddings with token-level uncertainty features to filter low-confidence candidates during generation. Experiments on public benchmarks, including VerilogEval and RTLLM, show that our 7B-parameter model outperforms the evaluated open-source, Verilog-specialized, and commercial baselines in both compilation success rate and functional correctness.

</details>

### [13] [Proxics: an efficient programming model for far memory accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18120)
*Zikai Liu,Niels Pressel,Jasmin Schult,Roman Meier,Pengcheng Xu,Timothy Roscoe*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文针对近数据处理（NDP）系统缺乏清晰、可移植的操作系统抽象的问题，提出了一种基于虚拟处理器和进程间通信通道的轻量级编程模型，并在真实硬件平台上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分解式内存系统（如CXL内存池）的兴起，近数据处理（NDP）技术再次受到关注。尽管已有硬件加速器设计，但缺乏清晰、可移植的操作系统抽象来对其进行编程，这限制了NDP技术的应用和发展。

Method: 提出了一种基于熟悉的操作系统抽象的NDP编程模型，包括虚拟处理器（进程）和进程间通信通道（如Unix管道）。通过利用编译技术和互连协议，以轻量级和高效的方式实现这些抽象，解决了传统进程过于重量级以及基于共享缓冲区的IPC不适合NDP系统的问题。

Result: 在真实硬件平台上运行了具有多种内存访问模式的应用程序（包括批量内存操作、内存数据库和图应用），不仅展示了相对于纯CPU实现的性能优势，还揭示了CPU与NDP加速器之间高效、低延迟通信通道的关键重要性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了基于操作系统抽象的轻量级NDP编程模型的可行性和有效性，强调了在NDP系统设计中需要重视CPU与加速器之间的高效、低延迟通信通道，为未来NDP系统的设计和优化提供了重要参考。

Abstract: The use of disaggregated or far memory systems such as CXL memory pools has renewed interest in Near-Data Processing (NDP): situating cores close to memory to reduce bandwidth requirements to and from the CPU. Hardware designs for such accelerators are appearing, but there lack clean, portable OS abstractions for programming them.
  We propose a programming model for NDP devices based on familiar OS abstractions: virtual processors (processes) and inter-process communication channels (like Unix pipes).
  While appealing from a user perspective, a naive implementation of such abstractions is inappropriate for NDP accelerators: the paucity of processing power in some hardware designs makes classical processes overly heavyweight, and IPC based on shared buffers makes no sense in a system designed to reduce memory bandwidth.
  Accordingly, we show how to implement these abstractions in a lightweight and efficient manner by exploiting compilation and interconnect protocols. We demonstrate them with a real hardware platform runing applications with a range of memory access patterns, including bulk memory operations, in-memory databases and graph applications.
  Crucially, we show not only the benefits over CPU-only implementations, but also the critical importance of efficient, low-latency communication channels between CPU and NDP accelerators, a feature largely neglected in existing proposals.

</details>

### [14] [CoLLM: A Unified Framework for Co-execution of LLMs Federated Fine-tuning and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16400)
*Shaoyuan Huang,Xiaokai Wang,Na Yan,Xiaofei Wang,Wenyu Wang,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoLLM系统，通过联邦参数高效微调(FL PEFT)与推理的协同执行框架，解决边缘智能中LLM后训练阶段资源受限、工作负载孤立的问题，实现了高达3倍的goodput提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在边缘智能中的广泛应用，资源受限环境下的后训练阶段（微调和推理）质量与效率变得至关重要。现有研究将微调和推理视为独立工作负载，忽视了它们的相互依赖性，导致冗余部署和推理质量提升延迟。

Method: 提出CoLLM协同执行框架，在共享边缘副本和模型参数上统一FL PEFT和推理。包含两个核心机制：(1)副本内模型共享机制，通过未合并推理和影子适配器策略实现实时模型参数复用；(2)双时间尺度副本间协调算法，自适应平衡微调与推理工作负载，联合优化长期模型质量增益和短期推理效率。

Result: 在多种LLM和真实轨迹上的广泛评估表明，CoLLM持续优于现有最先进LLM系统，实现高达3倍的goodput提升，证明了其在边缘智能中实现无缝LLM后训练的有效性。

Conclusion: CoLLM成功解决了边缘智能中LLM后训练阶段的资源约束与工作负载协同问题，通过统一微调与推理框架显著提升了系统效率，为边缘环境下的LLM部署提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in edge intelligence to power domain-specific applications and personalized services, the quality and efficiency of the LLM post-training phase-including fine-tuning and inference, have become critical due to constrained resources. Although recent advances in federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning (FL PEFT) and low-latency inference have improved individual task performance, fine-tuning and inference are still handled as isolated workloads, which overlooks their interdependence and results in redundant deployments and delayed improvement in inference quality. To address these limitations, we introduce a new co-execution framework and instantiate it with CoLLM, a system that unifies FL PEFT and inference on shared edge replicas and model parameters. CoLLM addresses key challenges at both replica and cluster levels through: (1) an intra-replica model sharing mechanism that enables real-time model parameter reuse via unmerged inference and shadow adapter strategies; and (2) a two-timescale inter-replica coordination algorithm that adaptively balances fine-tuning and inference workloads to jointly optimize long-term model quality gains and short-term inference efficiency. Extensive evaluation across diverse LLMs and real-world traces show that CoLLM consistently outperforms state-of-the-art LLM systems, achieving up to 3x higher goodput, demonstrating its effectiveness in enabling seamless LLM post-training for edge intelligence.

</details>


### [15] [GPUOS: A GPU Operating System Primitive for Transparent Operation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17861)
*Yiwei Yang,Xiangyu Gao,Yuan Zhou,Yuhang Gan,Yusheng Zheng,Andi Quinn*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GPUOS是一个GPU运行时JIT系统，通过持久内核架构和运行时算子注入技术，消除重复内核启动开销，在小算子主导的深度学习工作负载上相比标准PyTorch获得最高15.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习工作负载（尤其是推理、注意力机制和微批次训练）中包含大量小型张量操作，内核启动开销成为主要性能瓶颈，有时甚至超过实际计算时间。

Method: 提出GPUOS系统，采用四个关键技术：(1)具有原子任务队列的持久工作者内核，(2)基于NVRTC和可重定位设备代码的运行时算子注入机制，(3)用于安全并发算子更新的双槽别名方案，(4)通过TorchDispatch实现透明PyTorch集成以批量化微操作。系统运行单一长期GPU内核持续处理主机管理的工作队列，支持任意张量形状、步幅、数据类型和广播。

Result: 实验表明，在小型操作主导的工作负载上（包括微批次推理和注意力模式），GPUOS相比标准PyTorch实现最高15.3倍加速，同时保持与PyTorch生态系统的兼容性并提高GPU利用率。

Conclusion: GPUOS通过持久内核架构有效解决了小算子场景下的内核启动开销问题，显著提升了深度学习工作负载性能，同时保持了良好的生态系统兼容性。

Abstract: Modern deep learning workloads often consist of many small tensor operations, especially in inference, attention, and micro-batched training. In these settings, kernel launch overhead can become a major bottleneck, sometimes exceeding the actual computation time.
  We present GPUOS, a GPU runtime JIT system that reduces launch overhead using a persistent kernel architecture with runtime operator injection. GPUOS runs a single long-lived GPU kernel that continuously processes tasks from a host-managed work queue, eliminating repeated kernel launches. To support diverse operations, GPUOS uses NVIDIA NVRTC to just-in-time compile operators at runtime and inject them into the running kernel through device function pointer tables. This design enables operator updates without restarting the kernel or recompiling the system.
  GPUOS introduces four key ideas: (1) a persistent worker kernel with atomic task queues, (2) a runtime operator injection mechanism based on NVRTC and relocatable device code, (3) a dual-slot aliasing scheme for safe concurrent operator updates, and (4) transparent PyTorch integration through TorchDispatch that batches micro-operations into unified submissions. The system supports arbitrary tensor shapes, strides, data types, and broadcasting through a generic tensor abstraction.
  Experiments show that GPUOS achieves up to 15.3x speedup over standard PyTorch on workloads dominated by small operations, including micro-batched inference and attention patterns. GPUOS improves utilization while remaining compatible with the PyTorch ecosystem.

</details>


### [16] [Scene-Aware Latency Estimation for Microservices via Multi-Scale Graph Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16409)
*Zhichao Sun,Hailiang Zhao,Kingsum Chow*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MSGAF框架，通过多尺度图自适应融合和场景感知学习解决微服务延迟估计问题，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生微服务架构中，有效的主动扩缩容依赖于准确的端到端延迟估计。现有方法难以应对微服务系统的多层次特性和动态运行环境，主要采用单一尺度建模，无法捕捉固有的组织结构，且对不同类型工作负载缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出MSGAF（多尺度图自适应融合框架），包含三个核心组件：（1）系统状态编码模块，将异构监控数据转换为统一表示；（2）多尺度图自适应融合模块，利用图注意力网络通过可学习的基于聚合的粗化方法构建层次化图表示，在微观、介观和宏观层面捕捉系统行为；（3）场景感知学习模块，采用专门化专家网络和动态权重分配机制进行特定上下文的估计。此外还设计了非侵入式监控系统进行实时数据收集。

Result: 在基准微服务应用上的大量实验表明，MSGAF在各种运行场景下均显著优于现有最先进方法，为云原生性能优化提供了实质性改进。

Conclusion: MSGAF框架成功解决了微服务延迟估计中的多尺度建模和动态适应性挑战，通过层次化图表示和场景感知学习实现了准确的延迟预测，为云原生环境下的主动扩缩容提供了有效支撑。

Abstract: Cloud-Native microservice architectures have become prevalent owing to their inherent flexibility and scalability properties. To satisfy service quality guarantees, cloud providers must implement efficient proactive autoscaling algorithms. However, effective proactive scaling critically depends on accurately estimating end-to-end latency under given resource quotas, which remains highly challenging. Existing methods struggle with the multi-hierarchical nature and dynamic operational contexts of microservice systems. They primarily employ single-scale modeling that fails to capture inherent organizational structures and lacks adaptability to varying workload types. To address these limitations, we propose MSGAF, a Multi-Scale Graph Adaptive Fusion framework with Scene-Aware Learning for microservice latency estimation. Our approach constructs hierarchical graph representations through learnable aggregation-based coarsening, capturing system behaviors across microscopic, mesoscopic, and macroscopic levels. The framework comprises three components: a system state encoding module transforming heterogeneous monitoring data into unified representations, a multi-scale graph adaptive fusion module leveraging graph attention networks for hierarchical feature extraction, and a scene-aware learning module employing specialized expert networks with dynamic weight allocation for context-specific estimation. Additionally, we design and implement a comprehensive non-intrusive monitoring system for real-time data collection. Extensive experiments on benchmark microservice applications demonstrate that MSGAF significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse operational scenarios, providing substantial improvements for cloud-native performance optimization.

</details>


### [17] [Spot-and-Scoot: Peeking Into Spot Instance Availability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16457)
*Kyumin Kim,Moohyun Song,Taeyoon Kim,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Spot-and-Scoot (SnS)方法，通过在实例进入运行状态前提交并取消Spot请求，以近乎零成本收集可用性信号，实现高精度的当前可用性建模与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Spot实例虽能节省高达90%的成本，但其中断特性使得可用性理解至关重要。直接观测可用性需要持续运行实例，成本随监控的实例类型和区域数量线性增长，代价高昂。

Method: 利用云服务商配置生命周期中请求结果在实例运行前确定的特性，提交Spot请求并在被接受后立即取消，收集二元可用性信号；通过并发请求进一步量化可用容量；基于此提取三个互补特征进行可用性建模与预测。

Result: 在AWS和Azure上共68种实例类型、15个区域的336,033个请求验证了SnS的有效性；对2,635次真实中断事件的分析表明超92%的同区域同类型共中断发生在三分钟内；特征组合在当前可用性建模上F1-macro达0.90，在60分钟预测时域维持0.85；基于TPC-DS工作负载的仿真显示可减少计算损失。

Conclusion: SnS方法实现了成本高效的Spot实例可用性监测，为构建容中断系统提供了可靠的数据支撑与预测能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Spot instances offer significant cost savings of up to 90% over on-demand prices, making them an attractive resource for large-scale computing workloads. However, understanding their availability dynamics is essential for building systems that tolerate interruptions, and observing this availability directly requires keeping instances running, which incurs costs that scale with the number of monitored instance types and their per-instance price. We propose Spot-and-Scoot (SnS), a cost-efficient method that collects spot instance availability signals by leveraging the cloud provider's provisioning lifecycle. Since the outcome of a spot request is determined before the instance enters the running state, SnS submits requests and cancels them upon provisioning acceptance, collecting binary availability signals at near-zero instance cost. Submitting multiple concurrent requests per measurement point further yields a quantitative estimate of available capacity. We validate SnS through simultaneous collection of probing signals and actual running instance traces across 68 instance types and 15 regions on both AWS and Azure, totaling 336,033 spot requests. Analysis of 2,635 real-world interruption events reveals that co-interruptions within the same instance type and availability zone occur within three minutes in over 92% of cases, motivating a binary availability formulation. Based on this formulation, we derive three complementary features from SnS signals and demonstrate that their combination achieves an F1-macro score of up to 0.90 for current availability modeling and maintains 0.85 at a 60-minute prediction horizon. A trace-driven simulation using TPC-DS workloads further demonstrates the potential of SnS-based prediction to reduce lost computation compared to an unguided baseline.

</details>


### [18] [B-PASTE: Beam-Aware Pattern-Guided Speculative Execution for Resource-Constrained LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16469)
*Yanfei Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了B-PASTE方法，通过将推测执行从单个工具调用扩展到受资源约束约束的局部分支假设，解决了LLM代理串行执行导致的延迟问题，在边缘环境下实现了高达1.4倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理以交错推理-动作循环方式执行，未来工具调用必须等待当前推理步骤完成才能启动。这种串行依赖性增加了端到端延迟，并导致模型在等待工具执行期间处于空闲状态。现有工作PASTE虽然通过挖掘控制流和数据流规律推测未来工具调用来缓解这一瓶颈，但PASTE以工具为中心，仅推测单个调用而非有界的未来分支。

Method: 提出B-PASTE，一种束感知扩展方法，将推测从单个工具提升到严格资源约束下的局部分支假设。B-PASTE维护有界的未来执行子图束，根据预期关键路径缩减而非原始执行概率对其进行排序，仅在瞬时空闲资源上调度高价值分支前缀。该方法显式建模协同运行干扰、下游解锁价值和状态安全约束，使系统能够在早期完成解锁有价值未来工作时优先执行串行快速路径，同时在低竞争下利用安全并行性。

Result: 在Thor级边缘环境的初步内部测试中，实现了高达1.4倍的端到端加速，表明即使在紧张的资源预算下，分支感知的推测执行仍然有效。

Conclusion: 分支感知的推测执行在严格资源约束下仍然有效，特别适用于边缘侧部署场景，其中推测工作不能抢占延迟关键的主执行任务的稀缺资源。

Abstract: LLM agents execute in an interleaved reasoning-and-action loop, where future tool calls cannot be launched until the current reasoning step completes. This serial dependency inflates end-to-end latency and leaves the model idle while waiting for tool execution. Prior work, Pattern-Aware Speculative Tool Execution (PASTE), mitigates this bottleneck by speculating likely future tool invocations from mined control-flow and data-flow regularities. However, PASTE is tool-centric and speculates only individual invocations rather than bounded future branches.
  We propose B-PASTE, a beam-aware extension that lifts speculation from single tools to local branch hypotheses under strict resource constraints. B-PASTE maintains a bounded beam of future execution subgraphs, ranks them by expected critical-path reduction rather than raw execution probability, and schedules only high-value branch prefixes on transient slack resources. It explicitly models co-run interference, downstream unlock value, and state-safety constraints, enabling the system to prioritize serial fast-path execution when early completion unlocks valuable future work, while still exploiting safe parallelism under low contention.
  This design is especially important for edge-side deployments, where speculative work must not steal scarce resources from latency-critical authoritative execution. Preliminary internal testing on Thor-class edge environments shows up to 1.4X end-to-end speedup, suggesting that branch-aware speculative execution remains effective even under tight resource budgets.

</details>


### [19] [KAIROS: Stateful, Context-Aware Power-Efficient Agentic Inference Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16682)
*Yichao Yuan,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Nishil Talati*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KAIROS是一个面向代理式AI服务的上下文感知功耗优化系统，通过联合管理GPU频率、并发度和请求放置，在满足性能目标的前提下平均降低27%的功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理式AI成为主要工作负载，功耗已成为推理的核心瓶颈。然而现有的功耗管理技术主要针对单轮LLM服务，不适用于代理式服务场景。分析发现，代理式服务中请求携带长期演化的上下文，降低GPU频率可能导致系统进入抖动状态，使内存压力急剧恶化性能和功耗效率，这表明代理式服务的功耗优化需要重新思考。

Method: 提出KAIROS系统，将代理上下文作为一等控制信号，联合管理GPU频率、实例级并发度和多实例请求放置。系统以代理粒度跟踪请求，根据上下文增长和代理进展调整本地控制策略，并在实例间路由代理以协同改善功耗效率和内存稳定性，从而在存在内存余量时节省功耗，同时避免抖动并保持性能目标。

Result: 在多种软件和数据工程代理任务上的评估表明，KAIROS在满足性能目标的同时，平均降低27%（最高达39.8%）的功耗。

Conclusion: KAIROS证明了通过感知代理上下文并联合优化多个控制维度，可以有效地解决代理式AI服务中的功耗优化问题，实现了显著的功耗降低同时满足性能目标。

Abstract: Power has become a central bottleneck for AI inference. This problem is becoming more urgent as agentic AI emerges as a major workload class, yet prior power-management techniques focus almost entirely on single-turn LLM serving. Our analysis shows that agentic serving behaves fundamentally differently: each request carries long-lived context that evolves across tool-interleaved turns, and lowering GPU frequency can push the system into a thrashing regime where memory pressure sharply worsens both performance and power efficiency. These observations show that power optimization for agentic serving requires rethinking.
  We present KAIROS, a context-aware power optimization system for agentic AI serving. KAIROS uses agent context as a first-class control signal to jointly manage GPU frequency, per-instance concurrency, and multi-instance request placement. This enables KAIROS to save power when memory headroom exists while avoiding thrashing and preserving performance targets. At a high level, KAIROS tracks requests at agent granularity, adapts local control to context growth and agent progress, and routes agents across instances to jointly improve power efficiency and memory stability. Evaluated across diverse software and data engineering agentic tasks, KAIROS achieves an average of 27% (up to 39.8%) power reduction while meeting the performance targets.

</details>


### [20] [Scalable and Adaptive Parallel Training of Graph Transformer on Large Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16715)
*Jun-Liang Lin,Kamesh Madduri,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一个图Transformer的分布式训练框架，通过自动选择和优化并行策略，在8 GPU上实现了最高6倍的加速，并将内存消耗降低了78%，解决了大图训练中的可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图Transformer骨干模型通常局限于单GPU系统，在处理大图时面临训练时间长和显存溢出的问题；同时，在整图上并行化图Transformer训练极具挑战性，因为效率严重依赖于图结构和系统硬件特性（如带宽和内存容量）。

Method: 提出了一个分布式图Transformer训练框架，能够根据图结构和硬件配置自动选择并优化并行化策略；同时实现了分布式稀疏操作以加速稀疏图注意力计算并降低内存消耗。

Result: 与最先进的框架相比，稀疏图注意力计算加速高达3.8倍，内存消耗降低78%；在大图基准测试中，在扩展至8个GPU的系统上实现了最高6倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该框架显著提升了图Transformer的可扩展性，使其更接近于成为实用的图基础模型，为大规模图预训练提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Graph foundation models have demonstrated remarkable adaptability across diverse downstream tasks through large-scale pretraining on graphs. However, existing implementations of the backbone model, graph transformers, are typically limited to single-GPU systems, leading to long training times or out-of-memory issues on large graphs. Moreover, parallelizing graph transformer training over the full graph is challenging, as efficiency depends heavily on both the graph structure and system characteristics, such as bandwidth and memory capacity.
  In this work, we introduce a distributed training framework for graph transformers, which automatically selects and optimizes parallelization strategies based on the graph structure and hardware configuration. With our implementation of distributed sparse operations, we accelerate sparse graph attention by up to 3.8x and reduce memory consumption by 78% compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. On large graph benchmarks, our proposed framework achieves up to 6x speedup with system scaling up to 8 GPUs. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves the scalability of graph transformers, bringing them closer to serving as practical graph foundation models.

</details>


### [21] [HieraSparse: Hierarchical Semi-Structured Sparse KV Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16864)
*Haoxuan Wang,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出HieraSparse框架，通过分层KV Cache压缩和GPU稀疏张量核心加速，在保持生成质量的前提下实现显著的KV压缩比和注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型的部署面临两大挑战：自注意力机制的高计算成本和KV Cache的大量内存开销，现有方法难以有效平衡压缩效率与生成质量。

Method: 提出分层KV Cache压缩框架，利用GPU稀疏张量核心加速半结构化KV Cache注意力，支持预填充和解码阶段；通过分层设计实现灵活的质量-稀疏性权衡，将稀疏性转化为效率提升。

Result: 在相同稀疏度下，相比最先进解码方法实现1.2倍KV压缩比和4.57倍注意力加速；预填充阶段最高达1.85倍加速；使用简单幅度剪枝方法实现1.37倍预填充加速和1.77倍解码加速且无明显质量下降。

Conclusion: HieraSparse成功将稀疏性转化为实际效率提升，通过分层设计和GPU稀疏张量核心优化，在保证生成质量的同时显著降低了长上下文LLM的计算和存储开销。

Abstract: The deployment of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) poses significant challenges due to the intense computational cost of self-attention and the substantial memory overhead of the Key-Value Cache (KV Cache). In this paper, we introduce HieraSparse, a hierarchical KV Cache compression framework with acceleration kernels that leverage GPU sparse tensor cores to speed up semi-structured KV Cache attention for both the prefill and decode phases. With the hierarchical design, our method allows for a flexible quality-sparsity trade-off and successfully converts sparsity into efficiency. Compared to the state-of-the-art decode method that utilizes unstructured sparsity, HieraSparse achieves $\mathbf{1.2\times}$ KV compression ratio and $\mathbf{4.57\times}$ attention speedup at the same sparsity level. Furthermore, we extended the semi-structured KV Cache pruning to the prefill stage, which demonstrated up to $\mathbf{1.85\times}$ attention speedup at the highest sparsity. Lastly, we evaluate the generation quality of HieraSparse with a simple magnitude-based pruning method, and the results show that $\mathbf{1.37\times}$ prefill speedup and $\mathbf{1.77\times}$ decode speedup can be achieved without significant quality drop. The codebase can be found at https://github.com/psl-ntu/HieraSparse.

</details>


### [22] [From Swap Axioms to Weighted Geometric Means: A Characterization of AMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16898)
*Björn Assmann,Ulan Degenbaev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从三个基本假设（有效性不变性、帕累托效率、单位不变性）出发，推导出AMM的交易轨道必定是加权几何平均的等值面，解释了Uniswap常数乘积公式等主流设计的数学根源，并提供了Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流自动做市商（如Uniswap和Balancer）的设计均采用特定形式的闭式不变量（如xy或x^w y^{1-w}），但现有文献缺乏对这些形式为何涌现的理论解释。本文旨在建立公理化框架，从基本原理推导这些不变量形式的必然性。

Method: 建立包含三个公理的理论框架：有效性不变性（交易保持状态有效性）、帕累托效率（同一轨道上无状态弱支配另一状态）、单位不变性（计量单位变化不改变机制）。先证明两资产情形，再推广至n资产池，最后引入代币重标记对称性确定权重。

Result: 证明了满足三公理的两资产AMM交易轨道必为加权几何平均x^w y^{1-w}的等值面；n资产池轨道为∏_i x_i^{w_i}等值面，其中权重w_i>0且和为1；加入对称性后导出常数乘积形式。

Conclusion: 主流AMM设计采用的不变量形式并非任意选择，而是基本经济与数学公理的必然结果。该分类定理为AMM设计提供了理论基础，形式化验证确保了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: Many automated market makers can be understood through the geometry of their trading orbits, the sets of states reachable from one another through swaps. In prominent designs, this geometry is captured by a simple closed-form invariant such as the constant product $xy$ in Uniswap or a weighted geometric mean $x^w y^{1-w}$ in Balancer.
  This paper explains why these forms arise by deriving them from three basic assumptions: validity invariance (swaps preserve the validity of states), Pareto efficiency (no state on an orbit weakly dominates another), and unit invariance (changing measurement units does not change the mechanism). Together, these force every trading orbit of a two-asset AMM to be a level set of a weighted geometric mean $x^w y^{1-w}$. Applied pairwise, the axioms extend the classification to $n$-asset pools: orbits are level sets of $\prod_i x_i^{w_i}$ with positive weights $w_i$ summing to $1$. Imposing token-relabeling symmetry then pins down the weights, recovering the constant-product form $xy$ in the two-asset case and $\prod_i x_i$ in general.
  The main text provides an intuitive proof sketch and discusses fees and liquidity operations. Complete proofs and a machine-checked Lean 4 formalization accompany the paper.

</details>


### [23] [Predictive Sectorization and Bayesian Optimized Consensus for Admission Control in Autonomous Airspace Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17063)
*Aditya Dhodapkar,Avery Smidt,Aaron Verkleeren,Stacy Patterson,Carlos A. Varela*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化空域扇区划分与协调的三阶段流水线，结合XGBoost预测、Paxos共识协议和贝叶斯优化，实现可扩展的自主空域管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空管将空域划分为固定区域，随着交通流量增长会产生扩展瓶颈；扇区划分的选择影响工作负荷、移交频率及协调机制的容量，因此需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 三阶段流水线：1) 两阶段XGBoost分类器从23个位置无关交通特征预测最优3D网格配置；2) 无领导者Paxos共识协议让航空器自主协调扇区进入；3) 基于高斯过程代理模型的贝叶斯优化针对每个机场调优8个协议参数（50次试验）。

Result: 在FAA SWIM重放数据的65,000样本数据集上，网格配置预测准确率达91.38%；所有测试配置下扇区进入成功率保持在96%以上，且近空中碰撞率较低；贝叶斯优化显示不同交通环境需要性质不同的配置。

Conclusion: 该流水线为交通需求超过管制员能力时的可扩展自主空域管理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Conventional air traffic control divides airspace into specific regions, creating a scaling bottleneck as traffic grows. Choosing how to partition airspace is not straightforward because grid size affects workload, handoff frequency, and the capacity of whatever coordination mechanism operates within each sector. We present a three stage pipeline that automates sectorization and sector coordination while preserving human oversight. First, a two stage XGBoost classifier predicts the optimal 3D grid configuration from 23 location-agnostic traffic features, achieving 91.38% accuracy on a 65,000 sample dataset derived from Federal Aviation Administration System Wide Information Management replays. Second, a leaderless Paxos consensus protocol lets aircraft coordinate sector entries among themselves, maintaining above 96% entry success with low near mid-air collision rates across all tested configurations. Third, Bayesian Optimization with a Gaussian Process surrogate tunes eight protocol parameters per airport in 50 trials, revealing that each traffic environment requires a qualitatively different configuration. The resulting pipeline offers a practical path toward scalable, autonomous airspace management as traffic demand outpaces controller capacity.

</details>


### [24] [Sarus Suite: Cloud-native Containers for HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17064)
*Alberto Madonna,Matteo Chesi,Gwangmu Lee,Michele Brambilla,Fawzi Roberto Mohamed,Felipe A. Cruz*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Sarus Suite，一种基于未修改的Podman引擎的HPC容器架构。通过在独立系统层实现HPC特定功能，该方案在保持与上游云原生生态兼容的同时，性能对标生产级Enroot+Pyxis方案，并实现了更快的容器启动速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HPC容器解决方案多依赖专用运行时栈，这削弱了与主流云原生工作流的连续性，且需要持续维护以兼容不断演进的上游生态系统。作者希望解决HPC系统在支持快速迭代的AI/ML软件栈时，如何同时保持调度器控制、可扩展启动和生产性能这一核心矛盾。

Method: 提出Sarus Suite架构，核心是使用未修改的Podman引擎以保持与上游同步。创新点在于通过互补的系统层添加HPC特定功能，包括：声明式运行时规范、调度器原生执行、可扩展的共享镜像访问以及基于标准的主机能力注入。

Result: 在Cray EX GH200系统上的评估显示，在PyFR、SPH-EXA、Megatron-LM和Pynamic等工作负载上，Sarus Suite在性能和扩展性上与生产基准Enroot+Pyxis持平，同时实现了持续更快的单节点容器启动。此外，该架构支持直接使用OCI镜像和基于Kubernetes清单的多容器工作流。

Conclusion: HPC级容器不需要专用的运行时，只要将调度器语义、可扩展镜像访问和主机集成在明确的系统层中实现即可。这种架构设计在保持上游连续性和软件敏捷性的同时，成功维持了调度器控制、可扩展性和生产性能。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) systems must support fast-moving software stacks, especially in AI/ML, while preserving scheduler control, scalable startup, and production performance. Yet many HPC container solutions rely on specialized runtime stacks that weaken continuity with mainstream cloud-native workflows and require ongoing effort to sustain compatibility with the evolving upstream ecosystem. We argue that HPC should specialize the integration layer while keeping the container engine aligned with upstream container evolution. We present Sarus Suite, an upstream-aligned HPC container architecture built around an unchanged Podman engine. Sarus Suite adds the HPC-specific functionality needed for production use through complementary system layers for declarative runtime specification, scheduler-native execution, scalable shared-image access, and standards-based host capability injection. We evaluate Sarus Suite on a Cray EX GH200 system using communication-intensive HPC workloads, large scale AI training, metadata-heavy startup workloads, and container startup measurements. Across PyFR, SPH-EXA, Megatron-LM, and Pynamic, Sarus Suite matches the performance and scaling of the production Enroot+Pyxis baseline while delivering consistently faster per-node container startup. The architecture also enables direct use of upstream OCI images, including NGC-based images, and supports cloud-native multi-container workflows expressed through Kubernetes manifests. These results show that HPC-grade containers do not require an HPC-specific runtime, provided that scheduler semantics, scalable image access, and host integration are implemented in explicit system layers. This preserves upstream continuity and software agility while maintaining scheduler control, scalability, and production performance.

</details>


### [25] [TensorHub: Rethinking AI Model Hub with Tensor-Centric Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17104)
*Tingfeng Lan,Zirui Wang,Yunjia Zheng,Zhaoyuan Su,Juncheng Yang,Yue Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了TensorHub系统，通过细粒度的去重和压缩技术解决现代AI模型存储开销大的问题，在保持模型性能的同时实现了显著的存储节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI模型规模迅速增长且存在大量冗余，导致模型中心面临严峻的存储和分发挑战，亟需有效的存储优化方案。

Method: 提出TensorHub系统，采用张量级指纹识别和聚类技术，无需标注即可识别模型间的冗余，实现细粒度的去重和压缩。

Result: 在真实模型仓库上的实验表明，该方法在保持模型可用性和性能的同时，实现了大幅度的存储节省，且系统开销极小。

Conclusion: TensorHub能有效缓解模型中心的存储压力，为大规模AI模型的存储管理提供了一种高效、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern AI models are growing rapidly in size and redundancy, leading to significant storage and distribution challenges in model hubs. We present TensorHub, a tensor-centric system for reducing storage overhead through fine-grained deduplication and compression. TensorHub leverages tensor-level fingerprinting and clustering to identify redundancy across models without requiring annotations. Our design enables efficient storage reduction while preserving model usability and performance. Experiments on real-world model repositories demonstrate substantial storage savings with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [26] [HiveMind: OS-Inspired Scheduling for Concurrent LLM Agent Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17111)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Jerry John Kponyo,Obed Kwasi Somuah,Elliot Amponsah,Godfred Manu Addo Boakye,Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HIVEMIND是一个透明的HTTP代理，通过引入操作系统调度原语（如准入控制、令牌预算管理等），解决了多个LLM编码代理在共享限速API端点时出现的资源争用和高失败率问题，将失败率从最高100%降低至0-18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多个LLM编码代理并行共享一个具有速率限制的API端点时，会出现类似操作系统进程间无序竞争CPU、内存和I/O的资源争用现象。在一个实际案例中，11个并行代理中有3个因连接重置和HTTP 502错误而终止，失败率高达27%，尽管API的总容量足以顺序服务所有代理。这暴露了无协调并行执行在高并发场景下的脆弱性。

Method: 提出了HIVEMIND系统，这是一个透明的HTTP代理，无需修改现有代理代码。它应用了五个受操作系统启发的调度原语：准入控制、速率限制跟踪、AIMD（加性增加乘性减少）背压与熔断、令牌预算管理以及优先级队列。该系统通过自动检测的提供商配置文件支持Anthropic、OpenAI和本地模型API。此外，研究还进行了消融实验以分析各原语的贡献。

Result: 在涵盖5到50个并发代理的七个场景评估中，无协调代理在资源争用下的失败率为72-100%，而HIVEMIND将失败率降低至0-18%，并消除了48-100%的浪费计算资源。消融研究表明，透明重试是唯一最关键的原语，但各原语的组合使用效果最佳。针对Ollama的验证显示，HIVEMIND每个请求增加的代理开销低于3ms。

Conclusion: HIVEMIND通过借鉴操作系统的进程调度策略，成功解决了多LLM代理并行访问API时的资源争用问题，显著提高了系统的稳定性和资源利用率，且具有低延迟和易部署（零代码修改）的优点。该系统以MIT许可开源。

Abstract: When multiple LLM coding agents share a rate-limited API endpoint, they exhibit resource contention patterns analogous to unscheduled OS processes competing for CPU, memory, and I/O. In a motivating incident, 3 of 11 parallel agents died from connection resets and HTTP 502 errors - a 27% failure rate - despite the API having sufficient aggregate capacity to serve all 11 sequentially. We present HIVEMIND, a transparent HTTP proxy that applies five OS-inspired scheduling primitives - admission control, rate-limit tracking, AIMD backpressure with circuit breaking, token budget management, and priority queuing - to eliminate the failure modes caused by uncoordinated parallel execution. The proxy requires zero modifications to existing agent code and supports Anthropic, OpenAI, and local model APIs via auto-detected provider profiles. Our evaluation across seven scenarios (5-50 concurrent agents) shows that uncoordinated agents fail at 72-100% rates under contention, while HIVEMIND reduces failures to 0-18% and eliminates 48-100% of wasted compute. An ablation study reveals that transparent retry - not admission control - is the single most critical primitive, but the primitives are most effective in combination. Real-world validation against Ollama confirms that HIVEMIND adds under 3ms of proxy overhead per request. The system is open-source under the MIT license.

</details>


### [27] [CCCL: In-GPU Compression-Coupled Collective Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17172)
*Chon Lam Lao,Zhiying Xu,Zhuang Wang,Ziming Mao,Delong Meng,Jia Zhen,Jun Wu,Ion Stoica,Yida Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CCCL，一个内置压缩的集合通信库，无需修改用户代码即可将LLM工作负载的端到端吞吐量提升高达10.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）工作负载中，集合通信（如allreduce、alltoall）会带来显著的性能开销。现有的应用层计算通信重叠策略通常需要大量代码修改，且对于张量并行和专家并行等场景并不实用，因此需要一种透明且高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出CCCL库，一种基于压缩的集合通信库。它通过紧密融合压缩内核以减少内存访问，并与NCCL集成以消除数据合并阶段，从而在不改变用户代码的前提下支持allreduce、alltoall和send/recv等操作。

Result: CCCL的压缩速度极快（可达NVLink带宽的3倍），足以支撑通信需求。在vLLM PD disaggregation工作负载中，端到端吞吐量提升了10.1%；在微基准测试中，吞吐量提升了30%。

Conclusion: CCCL作为一种无需用户干预的通信优化方案，能够有效降低LLM工作负载中的通信开销，显著提升系统吞吐量，具备良好的实用性和易用性。

Abstract: Collective communication incurs significant overhead in LLM workloads. Although overlapping communication with computation in application-level is a common strategy, it often requires substantial code modifications and is impractical for many workloads (e.g., tensor and expert parallelism). We present CCCL, a built-in compression-based collective communication library that supports operations such as allreduce, alltoall, and send/recv without requiring any user-side changes, thereby enabling seamless adoption in existing applications. CCCL tightly fuses compression kernels to minimize memory accesses and integrates with NCCL to eliminate the data coalescing stage, making it fast enough (up to 3x NVLink bandwidth) to sustain communication. Our evaluation shows that CCCL improves end-to-end throughput in vLLM PD disaggregation workloads by up to 10.1% and microbenchmark throughput by up to 30%.

</details>


### [28] [Cloud-native and Distributed Systems for Efficient and Scalable Large Language Models -- A Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17227)
*Minxian Xu,Jingfeng Wu,Shengye Song,Satish Narayana Srirama,Bahman Javad,Rajiv Ranjan,Devki Nandan Jha,Sa Wang,Wenhong Tian,Huanle Xu,Li Li,Zizhao Mo,Shuo Ren,Thomas Kunz,Petar Kochovski,Vlado Stankovski,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了云计算平台和分布式系统在支持大型语言模型（LLM）的可扩展性、效率优化及部署方面的关键作用，分析了微服务、自动缩放及云边协同等解决方案，并展望了无服务器推理、量子计算及联邦学习等未来趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的快速发展彻底改变了AI应用，但其训练和推理过程中的巨大计算需求给传统系统带来了严峻挑战。现有系统往往无法满足这些需求，迫切需要引入云原生和分布式架构来解决资源、扩展性及效率问题。

Method: 本文通过分析LLM部署的复杂性，探讨了云平台与分布式系统的集成方案，重点讨论了数据管理、资源优化、微服务架构、自动缩放机制以及混合云-边缘解决方案。同时，审视了无服务器推理、量子计算和联邦学习等前沿技术的研究现状。

Result: 研究表明，云原生架构（如微服务、自动缩放）和分布式系统能够有效支持LLM的规模化部署与优化；新兴技术如无服务器推理和联邦学习展现出推动LLM下一阶段创新的巨大潜力，能够进一步提升系统的灵活性与效率。

Conclusion: 为了维持LLM在科研与企业应用中的持续增长，未来需要制定明确的发展路线图，重点加强持续研究、推动技术标准化以及促进跨部门协作，以应对日益复杂的计算与部署需求。

Abstract: The rapid rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has revolutionized various artificial intelligence (AI) applications, from natural language processing to code generation. However, the computational demands of these models, particularly in training and inference, present significant challenges. Traditional systems are often unable to meet these requirements, necessitating the integration of cloud-native and distributed architectures. This paper explores the role of cloud platforms and distributed systems in supporting the scalability, efficiency, and optimization of LLMs. We discuss the complexities of LLM deployment, including data management, resource optimization, and the need for microservices, autoscaling, and hybrid cloud-edge solutions. Additionally, we examine emerging research trends, such as serverless inference, quantum computing, and federated learning, and their potential to drive the next phase of LLM innovation. The paper concludes with a roadmap for future developments, emphasizing the need for continued research, standardization, and cross-sector collaboration to sustain the growth of LLMs in both research and enterprise applications.

</details>


### [29] [Active Inference-Based Adaptive Routing for Heterogeneous Edge AI Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17373)
*Zihang Wang,Boris Sedlak,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了AIF-Router框架，利用主动推理（Active Inference）实现无需离线训练的边缘AI服务自适应路由编排，在不稳定的边缘环境中有效平衡了延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算虽然能降低AI推理的延迟和带宽成本，但在云边连续体中编排AI服务仍面临动态工作负载和基础设施异构性带来的挑战，现有方法难以实现自适应管理。

Method: 提出了AIF-Router框架，基于主动推理理论，通过贝叶斯状态推断和期望自由能最小化来指导路由决策；该方法利用可观测性驱动的实时指标进行在线学习，无需离线训练阶段。

Result: 在边缘节点存在设备不稳定性的情况下，AIF-Router展现出稳定的在线学习行为，证明了在不稳定边缘环境中应用主动推理进行自适应AI服务编排的可行性，有效平衡了延迟、吞吐量和资源利用率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，将主动推理应用于边缘AI系统的自适应决策具有潜力，但也揭示了在真实世界边缘AI系统中部署此类自适应性框架所面临的实际挑战。

Abstract: Edge computing enables AI inference closer to data sources, reducing latency and bandwidth costs. However, orchestrating AI services across the cloud-edge continuum remains challenging due to dynamic workloads and infrastructure variability. We present AIF-Router, an Active Inference--based routing framework that autonomously learns to balance latency, throughput, and resource utilization across multi-tier AI services without offline training. AIF-Router performs Bayesian state inference and expected free energy minimization to guide routing decisions based on observability-driven real-time metrics. Despite device instability on edge nodes, AIF-Router exhibits stable online learning behavior and demonstrates the feasibility of applying Active Inference for adaptive AI service orchestration in unreliable edge environments. Our findings highlight both the promise and practical challenges of deploying self-adaptive decision-making frameworks for real-world edge AI systems.

</details>


### [30] [Flint: Compiler Enabled Cluster-Free Design Space Exploration for Distributed ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17550)
*Jinsun Yoo,Meghan Cowan,Zheng Du,Changhai Man,Srinivas Sridharan,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Flint框架，利用机器学习框架编译器的中间表示（IR）来生成灵活的工作负载表示，从而支持跨栈的设计空间探索，解决未来分布式机器学习系统缺乏现成工作负载表示的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对未来分布式机器学习系统的设计空间探索缺乏现成的工作负载表示，这限制了跨栈的灵活探索。因此，需要一种方法来获取灵活的工作负载表示以支持设计空间探索。

Method: Flint框架利用机器学习框架编译器的中间表示（IR）来桥接这一空白。编译器负责理解和保留原始模型代码的行为。Flint在硬件执行之前与编译器接口，从而能够收集任意集群大小的工作负载表示。

Result: 通过将工作负载图与执行后跟踪数据进行对比验证，并通过设计空间探索案例研究展示了Flint的灵活性。

Conclusion: Flint框架成功解决了分布式机器学习系统设计空间探索中工作负载表示缺乏的问题，提供了一种灵活且经过验证的方法来支持未来的系统设计研究。

Abstract: Design space exploration for future distributed Machine Learning systems suffers from a lack of readily available workload representation that enables flexible exploration across the stack. We present Flint, a framework that bridges this gap by leveraging the Intermediate Representation of Machine Learning framework compilers. The compiler does the heavy weight lifting of understanding and preserving the behavior of the original model code. Flint can collect the workload representation of arbitrary cluster size because it interfaces with the compiler before hardware execution. We validate the workload graph against post-execution traces and show the flexibility of Flint through a design space exploration case study.

</details>


### [31] [EcoShift: Performance-Aware Power Management for Power-Constrained Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17635)
*Zhong Zheng,Michael E. Papka,Zhiling Lan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了EcoShift框架，这是一个面向异构CPU-GPU应用集群的性能感知功耗管理方案。通过在线性能预测和动态规划分配器，EcoShift将回收的功耗重新分配给最需要的应用，在保持集群功耗约束的前提下，相比现有最优策略平均性能提升高达6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功耗受限的高性能计算(HPC)系统需要在严格的集群级功耗限制下运行异构CPU-GPU应用。现有的集群级功耗管理策略依赖于公平共享或利用率启发式方法，未能捕捉不同应用对CPU和GPU功耗上限的特定敏感性，导致回收功耗的利用效率低下。

Method: EcoShift是一个性能感知的集群级功耗管理框架，它将在线性能预测与基于动态规划的分配器相结合，将回收的功耗分配给CPU-GPU应用，以最大化平均性能提升。该框架能够感知不同应用对功耗的敏感性，从而做出更优的分配决策。

Result: 在两个异构Intel CPU和NVIDIA A100/H100 GPU平台上，通过对多种CPU-GPU工作负载进行基于仿真的评估，EcoShift始终优于现有最优策略，实现了高达6%的平均性能提升，同时保持了集群级的功耗约束。

Conclusion: EcoShift通过捕获应用对功耗的特定敏感性并采用优化分配策略，有效解决了异构集群中功耗管理的低效问题，为功耗受限的HPC系统提供了更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Power-constrained HPC systems increasingly run heterogeneous CPU--GPU applications under strict cluster-wide power limits. Existing cluster-wide power management policies rely on fair-share or utilization heuristics and do not capture application-specific sensitivity to CPU and GPU power caps, leading to inefficient use of reclaimed power.
  We present EcoShift, a performance-aware cluster-wide power management framework. EcoShift combines online performance prediction with a dynamic-programming-based allocator to distribute reclaimed power across CPU--GPU applications for maximum average performance improvement.
  Through emulation-based evaluation on two heterogeneous Intel CPU and NVIDIA A100/H100 GPU platforms with diverse CPU--GPU workloads, EcoShift consistently outperforms state-of-the-art policies, achieving up to 6% average performance improvement while preserving the cluster-wide power constraint.

</details>


### [32] [Towards Energy Efficient Co-Scheduling in HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17640)
*Zhong Zheng,Michael E. Papka,Zhiling Lan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出EcoSched调度器，通过联合优化GPU数量选择和应用协同调度，在多GPU HPC系统上实现了最高14.8%的节能、30.1%的完工时间缩短和40.4%的EDP降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多GPU HPC系统虽然提供巨大算力，但低效的GPU分配常导致能源浪费和资源利用率不足。由于GPU应用呈现异构且非线性的扩展特性，使用所有可用GPU并不总是高效的选择，因此需要智能化的调度策略。

Method: EcoSched采用轻量级运行时性能分析来估计不同GPU数量下的相对性能，应用基于评分的策略来平衡能效和空闲资源，并整合NUMA感知的放置策略以减少干扰。系统在异构CPU-GPU平台上实现，支持H100、A100和V100等多种硬件。

Result: 在多种工作负载和硬件平台（H100、A100、V100）的评估中，EcoSched相比基准调度器实现了最高14.8%的能耗节省、30.1%的完工时间改善和40.4%的能耗延迟积（EDP）降低，且性能开销适中。

Conclusion: 联合优化GPU数量选择和协同调度策略对于实现高效的多GPU工作负载执行至关重要，证明了动态、自适应的资源管理在提升HPC系统能效方面的有效性。

Abstract: Modern multi GPU HPC systems expose substantial computational capacity, yet inefficient GPU allocation often leads to wasted energy and underutilization. In practice, GPU applications exhibit heterogeneous and nonlinear scaling, making it inefficient to always use all available GPUs. We present EcoSched, an online scheduler that jointly optimizes GPU count selection and application coscheduling to improve workload level efficiency on multi GPU systems. EcoSched uses lightweight runtime profiling to estimate relative performance across GPU counts, applies a score based policy to balance energy efficiency and idle resources, and incorporates NUMA aware placement to mitigate interference. We implement EcoSched on heterogeneous CPU GPU platforms and evaluate it with diverse workloads on H100, A100, and V100 systems. EcoSched achieves up to 14.8% energy savings, 30.1% makespan improvement, and 40.4% EDP reduction over baseline schedulers, with modest performance overhead. These results show that jointly selecting GPU counts and coscheduling actions is essential for efficient multi GPU workload execution.

</details>


### [33] [AsyncSparse: Accelerating Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on Asynchronous GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17834)
*Jie Liu,Huanzhi Pu,Zhiru Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次将GPU异步特性（TMA和warp specialization）应用于SpMM加速，提出的WCSR和BCSR内核分别在非结构化和结构化稀疏场景下显著超越现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpMM加速方案虽利用了Tensor Core，但未充分挖掘现代GPU架构的异步特性（如TMA和warp specialization）带来的性能潜力，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对结构化稀疏，设计了基于BCSR格式的warp专用生产者-消费者流水线，利用TMA异步传输与WGMMA计算重叠；针对非结构化稀疏，提出了WCSR内核，通过TMA加载稀疏操作数并将大行窗口拆分到不同线程块以实现负载均衡。

Result: WCSR内核在SuiteSparse矩阵上性能超越所有先前SpMM内核（相比AccSpMM加速1.47x，相比cuSPARSE加速6.24x）；BCSR内核在90%块稀疏度、64K tokens的Qwen2.5-7B prefill任务上，端到端性能相比cuDNN/cuBLAS加速2.66x。

Conclusion: 现代GPU异步特性（TMA与warp specialization）与SpMM内核协同设计可显著提升性能，本文提出的方案在非结构化与结构化稀疏场景下均取得了优越的实际加速效果。

Abstract: Sparse Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpMM) is a fundamental kernel across scientific computing and machine learning. While prior work accelerates SpMM using Tensor Cores, no existing sparse kernel exploits the asynchronous features of modern GPU architectures, such as NVIDIA's Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) and warp specialization. This work systematically studies how these features impact SpMM performance and introduces two co-designed kernels. For structured sparsity, we optimize a warp-specialized producer-consumer pipeline overlapping TMA data transfer with WGMMA computation using Block Compressed Sparse Row (BCSR) format. For irregular sparsity, we design a Window Compressed Sparse Row (WCSR) kernel that loads the sparse operand via TMA and splits large row-windows across thread blocks for load balancing. Our WCSR kernel outperforms all prior SpMM kernels on SuiteSparse matrices (1.47x over AccSpMM, 6.24x over cuSPARSE). Our BCSR kernel achieves a combined 2.66x end-to-end speedup on Qwen2.5-7B prefill at 90% block sparsity with 64K tokens over cuDNN/cuBLAS.

</details>


### [34] [Toward Optimality: A Tighter Analysis of Message Complexity for Leader Election in Diameter-Two Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18029)
*Abhijit Sadhukhan,Adri Bhattacharya,Anisur Rahaman Molla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过更紧致的分析，将Chatterjee等人提出的直径为2的同步网络领袖选举随机算法的消息复杂度从$O(n\log^3 n)$改进至$O(n\log n)$，同时保持$O(1)$轮复杂度和高概率正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直径为2的同步网络中，领袖选举的消息复杂度下界为$\Omega(n)$。现有算法（Chatterjee等，2020）实现了$O(1)$轮复杂度，但消息复杂度为$O(n\log^3 n)$，与下界存在多对数级别的差距。本文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: 对Chatterjee等人提出的随机化领袖选举算法进行了更精细、更紧致的理论分析，从而在保持原算法结构不变的情况下改进消息复杂度的上界。

Result: 证明了该算法的消息复杂度为$O(n\log n)$，相比原结果改善了$\log^2 n$因子；同时维持了$O(1)$的轮复杂度和高概率成功保证。

Conclusion: 本文显著缩小了领袖选举算法消息复杂度与理论下界之间的差距，表明对现有算法进行更深入的分析可以有效提升其理论性能界限。

Abstract: We study the message complexity of leader election in synchronous networks of diameter two. Our main contribution is a refined analysis of the randomized algorithm proposed by Chatterjee et al. [DC, 2020]. In their work, the authors established a lower bound of $Ω(n)$ messages ($n$ is the number of nodes in the network) and presented a randomized algorithm that elects a leader in ${O}(1)$ rounds using $O(n \log^3 n)$ messages with high probability.
  In this paper, we improve their $\polylog n$ gap in the message bound by providing a tighter analysis of their algorithm, reducing the message complexity to $O(n\log n)$, while preserving the $O(1)$-round complexity and high-probability correctness guarantee.

</details>

*Jonathan Bader,Edgar Blumenthal,Marten Eckardt,Justus Krebs,Joel Witzke,Xemena Wysokinska,Haci Ismail Aslan,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LightGBM和XGBoost集成的回归方法，用于预测高条件分位数，并结合乘法安全因子来优化分布式系统中的预测性内存分配，有效降低了欠分配和过分配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代分布式系统中，高效的资源分配对维持可扩展性、降低运营成本和确保快速执行至关重要。预测性内存分配面临非对称成本的挑战：欠分配会导致任务失败，而过度分配则浪费内存资源。

Method: 提出了一种基于LightGBM和XGBoost集成的回归方法，专门训练用于预测高条件分位数。为了应对欠分配的高昂代价，该方法还引入了一个乘法安全因子。

Result: 在SAP提供的真实构建作业数据集上，该方法将欠分配作业的比例从4.17%降低至2.89%，并将平均过分配从148%降低至44.51%。此外，研究还探索了欠分配优化与过分配优化之间的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效平衡预测性内存分配中的欠分配与过分配问题，显著减少了资源浪费，并通过帕累托前沿分析为实际应用提供了灵活的优化选择。

Abstract: In modern distributed systems, efficient resource allocation is a vital aspect to maintain scalability, reduce operational costs, and ensure fast execution even across heterogeneous workloads. Predictive models for resource usage are essential tools for optimizing allocation and preventing system bottlenecks. Predictive memory allocation has asymmetric costs as a key challenge: underallocation causes failures while overallocation wastes memory.
  We propose a regression method based on a LightGBM and XGBoost ensemble trained to predict high conditional quantiles. To further account for the high cost of underallocations we add a multiplicative safety factor. With our method we are able to reduce the number of under-allocated jobs from 4.17% to 2.89% and average overallocation from 148% to 44.51% on a real-world dataset of build jobs provided by SAP. We further explore the pareto frontier between optimization for underallocation and for overallocation.

</details>


### [36] [Trust, but Verify: ByzTwin-Range, a Digital Twin Cyber-Range for Byzantine Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18049)
*Tadeu Freitas,João Soares,Rolando Martins*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了ByzTwin-Range，一种双层架构，通过集成生产级BFT部署与数字孪生，在真实运行数据下实现对BFT协议的受控实验、压力测试和拜占庭故障注入，以识别同步性漏洞、配置弱点及对抗行为，从而支持关键信息物理系统的持续验证与自适应加固。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施日益依赖互联且软件驱动的信息物理系统（CPS），使其面临意外故障和复杂对抗行为的风险。拜占庭容错（BFT）协议虽能抵御任意故障，但在现实网络物理条件下评估其行为仍具挑战：传统网络靶场缺乏时间保真度，而在生产环境中测试又不安全。

Method: 引入ByzTwin-Range双层架构，将生产级BFT部署与数字孪生（DT）集成。DT镜像真实系统状态，通过联合仿真与仿真执行"假设"分析，并利用实时运行数据进行拜占庭故障注入。系统识别同步性漏洞（如超时配置错误、时间敏感的误判、对抗性延迟攻击）、配置弱点及对抗行为，并通过安全咨询通道将洞察反馈至运营部署。设计采用工业标准技术（OPC UA、TSN、FMU/H LA、QUIC/mTLS）以确保可行性与兼容性。

Result: ByzTwin-Range能够有效识别可能损害BFT保证的同步性漏洞、配置弱点及对抗行为，通过数字孪生实现对BFT协议在现实条件下的受控实验与压力测试，并将分析洞察反馈至生产环境以支持持续验证和自适应加固。

Conclusion: ByzTwin-Range为下一代感知BFT的网络靶场奠定了实用基础，通过持续测试、支持差分隐私的分析及未来的概念验证实现，为构建更具弹性的CPS系统开辟了道路。

Abstract: Critical infrastructures increasingly rely on interconnected and software-driven Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS), exposing operational processes to both accidental failures and sophisticated adversarial behavior. While Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) protocols offer robustness against arbitrary faults, evaluating their behavior under realistic cyber-physical conditions remains challenging: traditional cyber ranges lack timing fidelity, and testing in production environments is unsafe. This paper introduces ByzTwin-Range, a dual-layer architecture that integrates a production-grade BFT deployment with a Digital Twin (DT) to enable controlled experimentation, stress testing, and Byzantine fault injection using live operational data. The DT mirrors real system state, executes "What-if" analyses through co-simulation and emulation, and identifies synchrony vulnerabilities, i.e., misconfigured timeouts, timing-sensitive false suspicions, and adversarial delay exploits, configuration weaknesses, and adversarial behaviors that may undermine BFT guarantees. Insights from the twin are fed back into the operational deployment through a secure advisory channel, supporting continuous validation and adaptive hardening. The proposed design leverages industry-standard technologies (Open Platform Communications Unified Architecture, Time-Sensitive Networking, Functional Mock-up Unit/High-Level Architecture, QUIC/mutual TLS) to maximize feasibility and compatibility with existing industrial workflows. ByzTwin-Range establishes a practical foundation for next-generation, BFT-aware cyber ranges and paves the way for more resilient CPSs through continuous testing, differential-privacy-enabled analytics, and future proof-of-concept implementations.

</details>


### [37] [User Experiences with MPI RMA and ULFM in a Resilient Key-Value Store Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18098)
*Claudia Fohry,Rainer Fink*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在容错任务运行时系统中实现一个基于MPI RMA的内存键值存储组件的实践经验，用于应对节点故障。作者采用ULFM检测进程故障，并利用被动目标RMA实现数据冗余存储与恢复。由于ULFM在RMA方面的部分功能尚未实现，开发过程充满挑战，文中总结了缺失的功能及采用的变通方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件故障（如节点失效）日益常见，MPI程序员需要将易失数据保存到容错存储中。现有第三方存储方案（如Redis、Hazelcast IMap）难以深度集成和优化，因此作者希望建立一个定制化、基于MPI的内存存储，以便更好地融入任务运行时系统并满足应用特定需求。

Method: 实现了一个基于MPI的键值存储，在多个进程的主内存中以键值对形式保存冗余数据副本。由于读写操作天然具有单边特性，采用被动目标MPI RMA函数实现。利用Open MPI的ULFM扩展进行用户级故障检测；故障发生后，在存活进程上恢复并继续使用完好的数据副本。针对ULFM RMA功能未实现的问题，设计了相应的工作绕过方案。

Result: 由于ULFM在RMA方面的多项提议功能尚未实现，导致存储系统的实现较为困难。即使假设这些功能存在，编程复杂度仍然较高。作者总结了自己在开发过程中缺失的功能，并提出了实际采用的临时解决方法。

Conclusion: 基于MPI RMA和ULFM的容错内存存储在实践中面临接口不完善和实现难度大的问题。本文通过实践经验分析，列出了未来MPI标准或ULFM实现中需要补充的关键功能，为后续容错存储系统开发提供了参考。

Abstract: As hardware failures such as node losses become increasingly common, MPI programmers may want to save vulnerable data in a resilient store. While third-party storage solutions such as Redis or the Hazelcast IMap exist, a tailored, MPI-based store may be easier to integrate and can be optimized for particular application needs.
  This paper considers the implementation of such a store, which is intended as a component in a resilient task-based runtime system written in MPI. The store holds redundant data copies as key-value pairs in the main memories of multiple processes. Since store access operations, such as reads and writes, are naturally one-sided, we implemented the store with passive target MPI RMA functions. Process aborts are detected with the user-level failure mitigation (ULFM) extension of Open MPI. After failures, the program recovers on the surviving processes and continues with the intact data copies.
  Our implementation proved difficult, since several proposed ULFM functionalities for RMA have not yet been implemented. Even assuming their existence, we think that the programming task could be simplified. This paper describes our experiences, lists functionalities that we missed, and explains a workaround that we adopted in our implementation.

</details>
