### [38] [Conjunctive Prompt Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16543)
*Nokimul Hasan Arif,Qian Lou,Mengxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体LLM系统中存在的结构安全漏洞。攻击者通过在用户查询中植入触发键、在远程代理中插入对抗模板，使两者在路由汇聚时激活有害行为，而单独看每个组件均无害。现有防御手段无法有效阻止此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究主要聚焦于单智能体模型，然而许多实际应用依赖多智能体交互系统。这类系统中的提示分段和代理间路由创建了单智能体评估所忽略的攻击面。因此，研究多智能体系统中的新型攻击方式及其防御具有重要意义。

Method: 提出“合取提示攻击”方法，攻击者控制触发键的放置和对抗模板的插入，不修改模型权重或客户端代理。在星形、链式和DAG拓扑结构中，采用路由感知优化策略来提升攻击成功率。实验评估了该攻击在不同拓扑下的表现，并测试了现有防御机制的有效性。

Result: 路由感知优化攻击在多种拓扑结构下显著提升了攻击成功率，且误激活率较低。现有防御手段，包括PromptGuard、Llama-Guard变体及系统级控制（如工具限制），无法可靠阻止此类攻击，因为攻击的各个组件在隔离时均无恶意特征。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM管道存在结构性安全漏洞，现有防御机制因无法识别跨代理组合的恶意行为而失效。研究结果强调了需要开发能够推理路由和跨代理组合的新型防御策略。

Abstract: Most LLM safety work studies single-agent models, but many real applications rely on multiple interacting agents. In these systems, prompt segmentation and inter-agent routing create attack surfaces that single-agent evaluations miss. We study \emph{conjunctive prompt attacks}, where a trigger key in the user query and a hidden adversarial template in one compromised remote agent each appear benign alone but activate harmful behavior when routing brings them together. We consider an attacker who changes neither model weights nor the client agent and instead controls only trigger placement and template insertion. Across star, chain, and DAG topologies, routing-aware optimization substantially increases attack success over non-optimized baselines while keeping false activations low. Existing defenses, including PromptGuard, Llama-Guard variants, and system-level controls such as tool restrictions, do not reliably stop the attack because no single component appears malicious in isolation. These results expose a structural vulnerability in agentic LLM pipelines and motivate defenses that reason over routing and cross-agent composition. Code is available at https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/ConjunctiveAgents.

</details>


### [39] [Agentic AI for Education: A Unified Multi-Agent Framework for Personalized Learning and Institutional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16566)
*Arya Mary K J,Deepthy K Bhaskar,Sinu T S,Binu V P*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了代理统一学生支持系统（AUSS），这是一种新型多智能体架构，整合了学生、教育者和机构三个层面。实验结果显示推荐准确率达92.4%，评分效率达94.1%，退学预测F1分数为89.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于AI的教育系统呈现碎片化特征，缺乏跨利益相关者的多层次整合。代理人工智能代表了从反应式系统向主动、自主决策框架的范式转变，为解决上述问题提供了新的思路。

Method: 提出代理统一学生支持系统（AUSS），这是一种新颖的多智能体架构。该框架整合了学生层面的个性化、教育者层面的自动化以及机构层面的智能化，并利用大型语言模型（LLMs）、强化学习、预测分析和基于规则的推理等技术。

Result: 实验结果表明，该系统在推荐准确率上达到92.4%，评分效率提升至94.1%，退学预测的F1分数达到89.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统能够构建可扩展、自适应且智能的教育生态系统，为教育领域的代理AI应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) represents a paradigm shift from reactive systems to proactive, autonomous decision making frameworks. Existing AI-based educational systems remain fragmented and lack multi-level integration across stakeholders. This paper proposes the Agentic Unified Student Support System (AUSS), a novel multi-agent architecture integrating student-level personalization, educator-level automation, and institutional-level intelligence. The framework leverages Large Language Models (LLMs), reinforcement learning, predictive analytics, and rule-based reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate improvements in recommendation accuracy (92.4%), grading efficiency (94.1%), and dropout prediction (F1-score: 89.5%). The proposed system enables scalable, adaptive, and intelligent educational ecosystems.

</details>


### [40] [CogGen: A Cognitively Inspired Recursive Framework for Deep Research Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17072)
*Kuo Tian,Pengfei Sun,Zhen Wu,Junran Ding,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了CogGen框架，通过模拟认知写作的分层递归架构实现深度研究报告的自主生成，解决了现有线性工作流的错误累积和全局重组限制问题，在开源系统中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM研究报告生成方法依赖僵化的预定义线性工作流，导致错误累积、无法根据后续洞察进行全局重组，限制了深度多模态融合和报告质量。

Method: 提出CogGen框架：1) 采用分层递归架构模拟认知写作，实现灵活规划和全局重组；2) 引入抽象视觉表示（AVR），一种简洁的意图驱动语言，迭代优化视觉-文本布局而无需像素级重生成开销；3) 提出认知负荷评估框架（CLEF）并构建OWID基准。

Result: 在开源系统中达到最先进结果，生成的报告可媲美专业分析师输出，并超越Gemini Deep Research。

Conclusion: 受认知启发的递归框架能够有效提升LLM深度研究报告生成能力，实现灵活的信息编排和非线性叙事逻辑。

Abstract: The autonomous synthesis of deep research reports represents a critical frontier for Large Language Models (LLMs), demanding sophisticated information orchestration and non-linear narrative logic. Current approaches rely on rigid predefined linear workflows, which cause error accumulation, preclude global restructuring from subsequent insights, and ultimately limit in-depth multimodal fusion and report quality. We propose CogGen, a Cognitively inspired recursive framework for deep research report Generation. Leveraging a Hierarchical Recursive Architecture to simulate cognitive writing, CogGen enables flexible planning and global restructuring. To extend this recursivity to multimodal content, we introduce Abstract Visual Representation (AVR): a concise intent-driven language that iteratively refines visual-text layouts without pixel-level regeneration overhead. We further present CLEF, a Cognitive Load Evaluation Framework, and curate a new benchmark from Our World in Data (OWID). Extensive experiments show CogGen achieves state-of-the-art results among open-source systems, generating reports comparable to professional analysts' outputs and surpassing Gemini Deep Research. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/CogGen.

</details>


### [41] [Logic-Based Verification of Task Allocation for LLM-Enabled Multi-Agent Manufacturing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17142)
*Jonghan Lim,Mostafa Tavakkoli Anbarani,Rômulo Meira-Góes,Ilya Kovalenko*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）与时序逻辑和离散事件系统的控制架构，旨在解决制造业个性化生产中的安全性与灵活性问题，通过案例研究验证了其在多机器人装配场景中能有效阻止不安全任务的执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业面临产品个性化需求增长带来的产品变异性增加，频繁的系统重配置可能导致意外的危险行为，传统的基于预定义任务模型的多智能体控制架构难以在保证安全的同时适应新产品的任务规划；虽然引入大语言模型增强了适应性，但其可靠性问题限制了在安全关键场景中的应用。

Method: 提出一种控制架构，利用时序逻辑和离散事件系统对大语言模型生成的任务分配进行形式化验证，从而在任务执行前确保其安全性。该框架将LLM的灵活生成能力与基于模型的安全验证机制相结合，实现对制造车间现场的安全保障。

Result: 通过一个多机器人装配场景的案例研究，展示了所提框架能够识别并安全地处理不安全的任务分配，确保在任务执行前阻止潜在危险行为，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了将大语言模型与时序逻辑及离散事件系统相结合，可以在保留LLM灵活性的同时有效保障制造系统的安全性，为智能制造系统的安全控制提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Manufacturing industries are facing increasing product variability due to the growing demand for personalized products. Under these conditions, ensuring safety becomes challenging as frequent reconfigurations can lead to unintended hazardous behaviors. Multi-agent control architectures have been proposed to improve flexibility through decentralized decision-making and coordination. However, these architectures are based on predefined task models, which limit their ability to adapt task planning to new product requirements while preserving safety. Recently, large language models have been introduced into manufacturing systems to enhance adaptability, but reliability remains a key challenge. To address this issue, we propose a control architecture that leverages the flexibility of large language models while preserving safety on the manufacturing shop floor. Specifically, the proposed framework verifies large language model-enabled task allocations by using temporal logic and discrete event systems. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is demonstrated through a case study that involves a multi-robot assembly scenario, showing that unsafe tasks can be allocated safely before task execution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 实验结果表明，代理表现出短视和自利行为，这加剧了系统的低效性。然而，研究证实信息共享机制能够有效缓解这些负面影响。此外，该研究验证了LLM代理作为复杂运营环境中人类决策代理的潜力与局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究扩展了传统行为方法，为AI赋能组织的动态提供了新的见解。它强调了LLM代理在模拟复杂运营环境中人类决策方面的潜力，同时也指出了其局限性，为未来利用生成式代理研究供应链协调和决策偏差提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: Modeling coordination among generative agents in complex multi-round decision-making presents a core challenge for AI and operations management. Although behavioral experiments have revealed cognitive biases behind supply chain inefficiencies, traditional methods face scalability and control limitations. We introduce a scalable experimental paradigm using Large Language Models (LLMs) to simulate multi-stage supply chain dynamics. Grounded in a Hierarchical Reasoning Framework, this study specifically analyzes the impact of cognitive heterogeneity on agent interactions. Unlike prior homogeneous settings, we employ DeepSeek and GPT agents to systematically vary reasoning sophistication across supply chain tiers. Through rigorously replicated and statistically validated simulations, we investigate how this cognitive diversity influences collective outcomes. Results indicate that agents exhibit myopic and self-interested behaviors that exacerbate systemic inefficiencies. However, we demonstrate that information sharing effectively mitigates these adverse effects. Our findings extend traditional behavioral methods and offer new insights into the dynamics of AI-enabled organizations. This work underscores both the potential and limitations of LLM-based agents as proxies for human decision-making in complex operational environments.

### [43] [ARMove: Learning to Predict Human Mobility through Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17419)
*Chuyue Wang,Jie Feng,Yuxi Wu,Shenglin Yi,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ARMove提出了一种基于代理推理的人类移动预测框架，通过标准化特征管理和迭代优化解决了现有LLM方法在可解释性、迭代学习和可迁移性方面的局限，在全局数据集上显著优于现有方法并实现了跨区域、用户和尺度的强迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的人类移动预测方法存在三大局限：(1)黑盒推理导致可解释性有限；(2)缺乏从新数据中迭代学习的能力；(3)迁移性较差。这些限制阻碍了模型在不同人群和区域间的有效应用。

Method: ARMove采用代理推理框架，包含四个核心机制：(1)标准化特征管理，建立四个主要特征池作为基础知识和用户画像细分；(2)自动化生成机制整合LLM知识；(3)代理决策调整特征权重以最大化准确性并提供可解释决策路径；(4)大-小模型协同，将大LLM(如72B)的策略蒸馏到小模型(如7B)，降低成本并提升性能上限。

Result: 在四个全局数据集上的广泛实验表明：ARMove在12个指标中的6个上优于最先进基线(提升0.78%至10.47%)，其余4个指标也达到次优结果；迁移性测试验证了跨区域、用户群体和模型尺度的鲁棒性；可解释性分析突出了其决策透明性。

Conclusion: ARMove成功实现了完全可迁移的人类移动预测框架，通过代理推理机制有效解决了现有方法的可解释性、迭代学习和迁移性问题，为复杂多变的人类移动预测任务提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Human mobility prediction is a critical task but remains challenging due to its complexity and variability across populations and regions. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have made progress in zero-shot prediction, but existing methods suffer from limited interpretability (due to black-box reasoning), lack of iterative learning from new data, and poor transferability. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{ARMove}, a fully transferable framework for predicting human mobility through agentic reasoning. To address these limitations, ARMove employs standardized feature management with iterative optimization and user-specific customization: four major feature pools for foundational knowledge, user profiles for segmentation, and an automated generation mechanism integrating LLM knowledge. Robust generalization is achieved via agentic decision-making that adjusts feature weights to maximize accuracy while providing interpretable decision paths. Finally, large-small model synergy distills strategies from large LLMs (e.g., 72B) to smaller ones (e.g., 7B), reducing costs and enhancing performance ceilings. Extensive experiments on four global datasets show ARMove outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on 6 out of 12 metrics (gains of 0.78\% to 10.47\%), with transferability tests confirming robustness across regions, users, and scales. The other 4 items also achieved suboptimal results. Transferability tests confirm its 19 robustness across regions, user groups, and model scales, while interpretability 20 analysis highlights its transparency in decision-making. Our codes are available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ARMove-F847.

</details>


### [44] [Towards Self-Improving Error Diagnosis in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17658)
*Jiazheng Li,Emine Yilmaz,Bei Chen,Dieu-Thu Le*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ErrorProbe框架，用于LLM多智能体系统的语义故障归因，通过三阶段流程识别责任代理和错误起始步骤，无需人工标注即可实现自改进和跨域迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在解决复杂问题时引入了显著的调试挑战，包括长交互轨迹、智能体间依赖关系和错误延迟显现。现有诊断方法依赖昂贵的专家标注或"LLM-as-a-judge"范式，难以在长上下文中精确定位关键错误步骤。

Method: 提出ErrorProbe三阶段流水线：(1)操作化MAS故障分类法以检测局部异常；(2)执行症状驱动的后向追踪以剪枝无关上下文；(3)部署专门的多智能体团队（策略师、调查员、仲裁者）通过工具导向的执行验证错误假设。框架维护经可执行证据确认的情景记忆，无需标注即可更新。

Result: 在TracerTraj和Who&When基准测试上的实验表明，ErrorProbe显著优于基线方法，特别是在步骤级定位方面表现突出，同时验证记忆机制实现了无需重新训练的稳健跨域迁移。

Conclusion: ErrorProbe为LLM多智能体系统提供了一种有效的自改进故障诊断解决方案，通过消除对人工标注的依赖并实现跨域泛化，为复杂AI系统的调试和可靠性提升开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) enable complex problem-solving but introduce significant debugging challenges, characterized by long interaction traces, inter-agent dependencies, and delayed error manifestation. Existing diagnostic approaches often rely on expensive expert annotation or ''LLM-as-a-judge'' paradigms, which struggle to pinpoint decisive error steps within extended contexts. In this paper, we introduce ErrorProbe, a self-improving framework for semantic failure attribution that identifies responsible agents and the originating error step. The framework operates via a three-stage pipeline: (1) operationalizing the MAS failure taxonomy to detect local anomalies, (2) performing symptom-driven backward tracing to prune irrelevant context, and (3) employing a specialized multi-agent team (Strategist, Investigator, Arbiter) to validate error hypotheses through tool-grounded execution. Crucially, ErrorProbe maintains a verified episodic memory that updates only when error patterns are confirmed by executable evidence, without the need for annotation. Experiments across the TracerTraj and Who&When benchmarks demonstrate that ErrorProbe significantly outperforms baselines, particularly in step-level localization, while the verified memory enables robust cross-domain transfer without retraining.

</details>


### [45] [Multi-UAV Path Following using Vector-Field Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17995)
*Gautam Kumar,Amit Shivam,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多无人机路径跟随的分布式防碰撞框架，实现了无人机沿参考路径均匀分布并保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多无人机在路径跟随过程中需要同时实现防碰撞和保持均匀间距的协同控制需求，旨在解决现有方法难以兼顾收敛安全性与编队均匀性的问题。

Method: 利用矢量场引导律驱动无人机趋近参考路径；提出基于相对距离与方位角的旋转排斥机制以实现收敛过程中的防碰撞；设计基于机间间距误差的速度控制律以实现路径上的均匀分布。

Result: 建立了严格的解析保证，证明了在收敛过程中能够避免碰撞，且机间间距误差收敛至零，实现了沿路径的均匀分布；数值仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式框架能够有效解决多无人机路径跟随中的防碰撞与均匀分布问题，具备理论保证与实际可行性，为多无人机协同编队控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper presents a decentralized, collision-free framework for path following guidance of multiple uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs), while maintaining uniform spacing along a reference path. A vector field-based guidance law is employed to drive each UAV toward the reference path. A rotational repulsion mechanism, utilizing relative distance and bearing between UAVs, is proposed to avoid collisions during convergence to the path, and an inter-UAV spacing error-based velocity control law is presented to achieve uniform separation along the path. Analytical guarantees are established for collision avoidance and convergence of the inter-UAV spacing errors to zero, ensuring uniform separation along the path. Numerical simulations demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [46] [Diversity Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Systems: Structural Coupling and Collective Failure in Open-Ended Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18005)
*Nuo Chen,Yicheng Tong,Yuzhe Yang,Yufei He,Xueyi Zhang,Zou Qingyun,Qian Wang,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体系统在创意生成中存在多样性崩溃问题，主要源于交互结构而非模型能力不足。强模型存在效率悖论，权威驱动会抑制多样性，大群体和密集通信导致收敛，需在设计中保持智能体的独立性和分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统被广泛用于开放式创意生成，人们期望集体交互能拓宽探索的多样性，但目前尚不清楚这种协作在何时以及为何能真正扩展解空间。研究旨在系统探究MAS在创意任务中多样性扩展的失效机制。

Method: 对基于MAS的创意生成进行了系统性实证研究，从模型智能、智能体认知和系统动力学三个自下而上的层面分析多样性表现。通过控制实验设计，考察不同模型强度、群体权威结构、群体规模和通信拓扑对多样性的影响。

Result: 发现三个层面的关键问题：（1）模型层存在计算效率悖论，更强且高度对齐的模型在提高样本质量的同时，边际多样性递减；（2）认知层权威驱动的群体相比初级主导群体会抑制语义多样性；（3）系统层群体规模扩大产生边际效益递减，密集通信拓扑会加速过早收敛。这些现象被刻画为由结构耦合引发的集体失败，导致探索空间收缩和多样性崩溃。

Conclusion: 多样性崩溃主要源于交互结构而非模型固有能力不足，这强调了在为创意任务设计MAS时，保持智能体独立性和分歧的重要性。研究为如何有效利用多智能体协作进行创意探索提供了理论和实践指导。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly used for open-ended idea generation, driven by the expectation that collective interaction will broaden the exploration diversity. However, when and why such collaboration truly expands the solution space remains unclear. We present a systematic empirical study of diversity in MAS-based ideation across three bottom-up levels: model intelligence, agent cognition, and system dynamics. At the model level, we identify a compute efficiency paradox, where stronger, highly aligned models yield diminishing marginal diversity despite higher per-sample quality. At the cognition level, authority-driven dynamics suppress semantic diversity compared to junior-dominated groups. At the system level, group-size scaling yields diminishing returns and dense communication topologies accelerate premature convergence. We characterize these outcomes as collective failures emerging from structural coupling, a process where interaction inadvertently contracts agent exploration and triggers diversity collapse. Our analysis shows that this collapse arises primarily from the interaction structure rather than inherent model insufficiency, highlighting the importance of preserving independence and disagreement when designing MAS for creative tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/Xtra-Computing/MAS_Diversity.

</details>


### [47] [ConventionPlay: Capability-Limited Training for Robust Ad-Hoc Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18123)
*Abhishek Sriraman,Eleni Vasilaki,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ConventionPlay方法，通过在包含多样化自适应跟随者群体的认知层级框架中训练智能体，使其能够主动引导团队选择最优联合策略，在差异化回报的协调任务中实现了更优的协调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临时协作场景中，当存在多种可供遵循的惯例且回报不同时，智能体不能仅被动适应，而需要主动引导团队采用最有效的联合策略，这是现有方法未能有效解决的问题。

Method: 提出ConventionPlay强化学习方法，将认知层级框架扩展为包含具有不同能力限制的多样化自适应跟随者群体；智能体通过与这些伙伴训练，学习探测伙伴的行为 repertoire，从而在可能时主导团队、在必要时跟随。

Result: 在经典协调任务上的实验表明，ConventionPlay实现了更优的协调效率，尤其在惯例具有差异化回报的设置中表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过引入多样化自适应跟随者群体扩展认知层级框架，ConventionPlay使智能体具备主动引导团队协作的能力，有效解决了多惯例选择下的协调效率问题。

Abstract: Ad-hoc collaboration often relies on identifying and adhering to shared conventions. However, when partners can follow multiple conventions, agents must do more than simply adapt; they must actively steer the team toward the most effective joint strategy. We present ConventionPlay, a reinforcement learning-based approach that extends cognitive hierarchies to include a diverse population of adaptive followers. By training against partners with varied capability limits, our agent learns to probe its partner's repertoire, leading the team when possible and following when necessary. Our results in canonical coordination tasks show that ConventionPlay achieves superior coordination efficiency, particularly in settings where conventions have differentiated payoffs.

</details>


### [48] [Aether: Network Validation Using Agentic AI and Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18233)
*Jordan Auge,Sam Betts,Giovanna Carofiglio,Giulio Grassi,Martin Gysi,John Kenneth d'Souza*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Aether系统，结合生成式智能体AI与多功能网络数字孪生技术，实现网络变更验证工作流的自动化，在错误检测、诊断覆盖率和速度方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代网络运维中，网络变更验证至关重要但主要依赖人工，过程耗时且易出错。现有形式化网络验证方法通常应用于离线、部署前场景，难以适应持续变更和验证实时生产行为；当前运维方法涉及分散的测试工具，导致覆盖不全面且错误仅在部署后暴露。

Method: 提出Aether系统，采用智能体架构，包含五个专用网络运维AI智能体协同处理从意图分析到网络验证和测试的变更验证全生命周期；利用集成了建模、仿真和模拟的统一网络数字孪生技术，为验证和测试维护一致且最新的网络视图；通过编排智能体在数字孪生之上的协作实现自动化快速网络变更验证。

Result: 在覆盖主要网络变更类别的合成网络变更场景和某主要ISP运营网络的历史事故上进行评估，错误检测率达100%，诊断覆盖率达92-96%，处理速度为6-7分钟，相比传统方法显著提升。

Conclusion: Aether通过集成生成式智能体AI与网络数字孪生，成功实现了网络变更验证的自动化，有效降低了人工工作量、减少了错误、提升了运维敏捷性和成本效益，为网络运维领域提供了新的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Network change validation remains a critical yet predominantly manual, time-consuming, and error-prone process in modern network operations. While formal network verification has made substantial progress in proving correctness properties, it is typically applied in offline, pre-deployment settings and faces challenges in accommodating continuous changes and validating live production behavior. Current operational approaches typically involve scattered testing tools, resulting in partial coverage and errors that surface only after deployment. In this paper, we present Aether, a novel approach that integrates Generative Agentic AI with a multi-functional Network Digital Twin to automate and streamline network change validation workflows. It features an agentic architecture with five specialized Network Operations AI agents that collaboratively handle the change validation lifecycle from intent analysis to network verification and testing. Aether agents use a unified Network Digital Twin integrating modeling, simulation, and emulation to maintain a consistent, up-to-date network view for verification and testing. By orchestrating agent collaboration atop this digital twin, Aether enables automated, rapid network change validation while reducing manual effort, minimizing errors, and improving operational agility and cost-effectiveness. We evaluate Aether over synthetic network change scenarios covering main classes of network changes and on past incidents from a major ISP operational network, demonstrating promising results in error detection (100%), diagnostic coverage (92-96%), and speed (6-7 minutes) over traditional methods.

</details>


### [49] [QRAFTI: An Agentic Framework for Empirical Research in Quantitative Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18500)
*Terence Lim,Kumar Muthuraman,Michael Sury*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了QRAFTI，一个用于股票因子研究的多智能体框架，通过集成研究工具包与MCP服务器，支持大规模金融面板数据上的因子复制、新信号测试及自动化报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化研究流程在大规模金融面板数据上往往缺乏高效、可解释且可复现的自动化支持，特别是在多步骤实证任务中，单纯依赖动态代码生成难以满足性能与可解释性的双重需求。

Method: QRAFTI整合了面板数据研究工具包与MCP服务器，将数据访问、因子构建及自定义编码操作封装为可调用的工具；采用链式工具调用与基于反思的规划机制，构建多智能体系统来执行研究任务。

Result: 在多步骤实证任务中，相比于仅使用动态代码生成，该方法通过链式工具调用与反思规划展现出更优的性能表现与可解释性，并能生成附带叙述性分析与计算追溯的标准化研究报告。

Conclusion: 该框架有效模拟了量化研究团队的部分职能，为大规模金融面板数据上的因子研究提供了高性能、可解释且可追溯的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a multi-agent framework intended to emulate parts of a quantitative research team and support equity factor research on large financial panel datasets. QRAFTI integrates a research toolkit for panel data with MCP servers that expose data access, factor construction, and custom coding operations as callable tools. It can help replicate established factors, formulate and test new signals, and generate standardized research reports accompanied by narrative analysis and computational traces. On multi-step empirical tasks, using chained tool calls and reflection-based planning may offer better performance and explainability than dynamic code generation alone.

</details>

### [50] [Governing the Agentic Enterprise: A Governance Maturity Model for Managing AI Agent Sprawl in Business Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16338)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了代理AI治理成熟度模型（AAGMM），这是一个基于NIST和ISO标准的五级框架，涵盖12个治理领域，并通过仿真验证了其在控制代理蔓延和降低风险方面的显著有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI（能够规划、推理和执行多步骤工作流的自主系统）在企业中的快速应用导致了紧迫的治理危机，即不受控制的代理蔓延（Agent Sprawl）。现有学术文献缺乏正式且经实证验证的治理成熟度模型来连接治理能力与可衡量的业务成果。

Method: 提出了代理AI治理成熟度模型（AAGMM），这是一个基于NIST AI RMF和ISO/IEC 42001标准的五级框架，涵盖12个治理领域。同时提出了一种新颖的代理蔓延模式分类法，并将其与可量化的业务成本模型相关联。通过在五种企业场景和五个治理成熟度级别上进行750次仿真运行来验证该框架。

Result: 结果证实不同治理成熟度级别之间存在统计显著性差异（p < 0.001，大效应量 d > 2.0）。与1级组织相比，4-5级组织的代理蔓延指数降低了94.3%，风险事件减少了96.4%，有效任务完成率提高了32.6%。

Conclusion: AAGMM为从业者提供了一个可操作的路线图，用于治理自主AI代理，同时最大化业务回报。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of agentic AI in enterprise business operations--autonomous systems capable of planning, reasoning, and executing multi-step workflows--has created an urgent governance crisis. Organizations face uncontrolled agent sprawl: the proliferation of redundant, ungoverned, and conflicting AI agents across business functions. Industry surveys report that only 21% of enterprises have mature governance models for autonomous agents, while 40% of agentic AI projects are projected to fail by 2027 due to inadequate governance and risk controls. Despite growing acknowledgment of this challenge, academic literature lacks a formal, empirically validated governance maturity model connecting governance capability to measurable business outcomes. This paper introduces the Agentic AI Governance Maturity Model (AAGMM), a five-level framework spanning 12 governance domains, grounded in NIST AI RMF and ISO/IEC 42001 standards. We additionally propose a novel taxonomy of agent sprawl patterns--functional duplication, shadow agents, orphaned agents, permission creep, and unmonitored delegation chains--each linked to quantifiable business cost models. The framework is validated through 750 simulation runs across five enterprise scenarios and five governance maturity levels, measuring business outcomes including cost containment, risk incident rates, operational efficiency, and decision quality. Results demonstrate statistically significant differences (p < 0.001, large effect sizes d > 2.0) between all governance maturity levels, with Level 4-5 organizations achieving 94.3% lower sprawl indices, 96.4% fewer risk incidents, and 32.6% higher effective task completion rates compared to Level 1. The AAGMM provides practitioners with an actionable roadmap for governing autonomous AI agents while maximizing business returns.

</details>


### [51] [Semantic Consensus: Process-Aware Conflict Detection and Resolution for Enterprise Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16339)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义共识框架（SCF），通过过程感知中间件解决多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，实现100%工作流完成率，远超基线方法的25.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型系统在企业AI自动化中快速兴起，但生产部署失败率高达41%-86.7%，其中近79%的故障源于规范与协调问题而非模型能力限制。核心问题在于语义意图分歧——协作LLM智能体因孤立上下文和缺失过程模型而对共享目标形成不一致解释，这一根本原因尚未得到正式解决。

Method: 提出语义共识框架（SCF），一种包含六个组件的过程感知中间件：（1）过程上下文层提供共享操作语义；（2）语义意图图实现形式化意图表示；（3）冲突检测引擎实时识别矛盾、竞争及因果无效的意图组合；（4）共识解决协议采用政策-权威-时序层级机制；（5）漂移监控器检测渐进式语义分歧；（6）过程感知治理集成层执行组织策略。框架具有协议无关性，兼容MCP和A2A通信标准。

Result: 在跨三个多智能体框架（AutoGen、CrewAI、LangGraph）和四个企业场景的600次运行评估中，SCF是唯一实现100%工作流完成率的方法，相比最优基线的25.1%有显著提升；同时检测出65.2%的语义冲突，精确率达27.9%，并提供完整的治理审计轨迹。

Conclusion: SCF框架有效解决了企业多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，显著提升了工作流完成率并提供了冲突检测与治理审计能力，为企业AI自动化的可靠部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are rapidly emerging as the dominant architecture for enterprise AI automation, yet production deployments exhibit failure rates between 41% and 86.7%, with nearly 79% of failures originating from specification and coordination issues rather than model capability limitations. This paper identifies Semantic Intent Divergence--the phenomenon whereby cooperating LLM agents develop inconsistent interpretations of shared objectives due to siloed context and absent process models--as a primary yet formally unaddressed root cause of multi-agent failure in enterprise settings. We propose the Semantic Consensus Framework (SCF), a process-aware middleware comprising six components: a Process Context Layer for shared operational semantics, a Semantic Intent Graph for formal intent representation, a Conflict Detection Engine for real-time identification of contradictory, contention-based, and causally invalid intent combinations, a Consensus Resolution Protocol using a policy--authority--temporal hierarchy, a Drift Monitor for detecting gradual semantic divergence, and a Process-Aware Governance Integration layer for organizational policy enforcement. Evaluation across 600 runs spanning three multi-agent frameworks (AutoGen, CrewAI, LangGraph) and four enterprise scenarios demonstrates that SCF is the only approach to achieve 100% workflow completion--compared to 25.1% for the next-best baseline--while detecting 65.2% of semantic conflicts with 27.9% precision and providing complete governance audit trails. The framework is protocol-agnostic and compatible with MCP and A2A communication standards.

</details>


### [52] [Heterogeneous Self-Play for Realistic Highway Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16406)
*Jinkai Qiu,Alessandro Saviolo,Chaojie Wang,Mingke Wang,Xiaoyu Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了PHASE框架，通过自博弈方法生成逼真的高速公路仿真场景，在真实数据上实现96.3%成功率，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路交通场景生成面临三大挑战：需要覆盖广泛的速度和操控场景、可控生成罕见的安全关键场景、多智能体交互中的行为可信度。现有方法难以同时满足这些要求，且稀缺的交互数据限制了安全评估的可扩展性。

Method: 提出PHASE框架，采用上下文感知的自博弈方法。核心包括：(1) 显式单智能体条件化实现可控性；(2) 合成场景生成实现广泛高速公路覆盖；(3) 闭环多智能体训练实现真实交互动态。支持不同车辆类型（轿车和铰接卡车），通过车辆感知动力学和上下文条件化动作。引入早期终止、过错碰撞归因、高速公路感知奖励塑形、耦合课程学习和鲁棒策略优化来稳定训练。

Result: 仅用合成数据训练，PHASE在exiD数据集512个未见高交互真实场景中实现零样本迁移，成功率达96.3%。ADE/FDE从基线的6.57/12.07米降至2.44/5.25米。在轨迹嵌入空间中，相比IDM模型，Frechet轨迹距离降低13.1%，能量距离降低20.2%。

Conclusion: 合成自博弈方法可提供可扩展的、可控且逼真的高速公路场景生成路径，无需直接模仿专家日志数据，为自动驾驶安全评估提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic highway simulation is critical for scalable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles, particularly for interactions that are too rare to study from logged data alone. Yet highway traffic generation remains challenging because it requires broad coverage across speeds and maneuvers, controllable generation of rare safety-critical scenarios, and behavioral credibility in multi-agent interactions. We present PHASE, Policy for Heterogeneous Agent Self-play on Expressway, a context-aware self-play framework that addresses these three requirements through explicit per-agent conditioning for controllability, synthetic scenario generation for broad highway coverage, and closed-loop multi-agent training for realistic interaction dynamics. PHASE further supports different vehicle profiles, for example, passenger cars and articulated trailer trucks, within a single policy via vehicle-aware dynamics and context-conditioned actions, and stabilizes self-play with early termination of unrecoverable states, at-fault collision attribution, highway-aware reward shaping, coupled curricula, and robust policy optimization. Despite being trained only on synthetic data, PHASE transfers zero-shot to 512 unseen high-interaction real scenarios in exiD, achieving a 96.3% success rate and reducing ADE/FDE from 6.57/12.07 m to 2.44/5.25 m relative to a prior self-play baseline. In a learned trajectory embedding space, it also improves behavioral realism over IDM, reducing Frechet trajectory distance by 13.1% and energy distance by 20.2%. These results show that synthetic self-play can provide a scalable route to controllable and realistic highway scenario generation without direct imitation of expert logs.

</details>


### [53] [Evaluating Tool-Using Language Agents: Judge Reliability, Propagation Cascades, and Runtime Mitigation in AgentProp-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16706)
*Bhaskar Gurram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了工具使用LLM智能体的自动评估方法可靠性不足，并提出了AgentProp-Bench基准及运行时缓解方案，发现LLM集成评估可达到中等一致性，运行时拦截器能有效降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用的大语言模型（LLM）智能体的自动评估被广泛假设为可靠，但该假设缺乏与人类标注的验证对比，亟需系统研究自动评估的真实可靠性及其误差传播特性。

Method: 构建包含2000个任务、2300条轨迹的AgentProp-Bench基准，覆盖四个领域和九个生产LLM，包含100个人工标注验证子集；系统量化评估器可靠性，刻画误差传播机制，并提出运行时缓解方案进行实验验证。

Result: 基于子串的评估与人类标注的一致性仅达kappa=0.049（机遇水平），三LLM集成可达kappa=0.432（中等一致性）但存在保守偏差；参数级注入导致错误最终答案的概率约0.62；拒绝与恢复是独立能力（rho=0.126，p=0.747）；调优的运行时拦截器在GPT-4o-mini上降低幻觉23.0个百分点，但对激进拒绝的Gemini-2.0-Flash无显著效果。

Conclusion: 现有自动评估方法的可靠性有限，需采用多LLM集成评估；运行时缓解方案可部分降低幻觉，但其效果依赖于模型的固有拒绝策略；需重视拒绝与恢复能力的独立评估。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of tool-using large language model (LLM) agents is widely assumed to be reliable, but this assumption has rarely been validated against human annotation. We introduce AgentProp-Bench, a 2,000-task benchmark with 2,300 traces across four domains, nine production LLMs, and a 100-label human-validated subset. We quantify judge reliability, characterize error propagation, and evaluate a runtime mitigation. Substring-based judging agrees with human annotation at kappa=0.049 (chance-level); a three-LLM ensemble reaches kappa=0.432 (moderate) with a conservative bias. Under validated evaluation, a parameter-level injection propagates to a wrong final answer with human-calibrated probability approximately 0.62 (range 0.46-0.73 across models). Rejection (catching bad parameters) and recovery (correcting after acceptance) are independent model capabilities (Spearman rho=0.126, p=0.747). A tuned runtime interceptor reduces hallucination on GPT-4o-mini by 23.0 percentage points under a concurrent n=600 control, but shows no significant effect on Gemini-2.0-Flash, whose aggressive parameter rejection eliminates the target failure mode. All code, data, traces, and human labels are released at https://github.com/bhaskargurram-ai/agenthallu-bench.

</details>


### [54] [LLM-Guided Strategy Synthesis for Scalable Equality Saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17364)
*Chenyun Yin,Youwei Xiao,Yuze Luo,Yuyang Zou,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EggMind框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）自动合成可复用的等式饱和策略，通过领域特定语言EqSatL和新颖的技术（如证明驱动的重写模体缓存、可处理性指导），在向量化和张量编译等任务上显著优化了资源-质量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的等式饱和优化范式虽然强大，但其策略设计主要依赖人工，成为自动化编译器的主要障碍。尽管现有的规则合成框架能自动推断重写规则，但同时也扩大了搜索空间，加剧了e-graph爆炸问题。直接利用大语言模型演化后端代码在实践中效果不佳，缺乏可复用的策略抽象和可操作的反馈，容易触发e-graph爆炸或收敛至不良设计。

Method: 提出了EggMind，一个LLM引导的端到端框架。核心贡献包括：1) 引入领域特定语言EqSatL，将等式饱和策略表示为显式、可检查的制品；2) 提出LLM引导的智能体工作流，配备证明驱动的重写模体缓存和可处理性指导等新技术，在保持e-graph增长稳定的同时高效搜索高质量策略。

Result: 在向量化基准测试中，EggMind显著改善了资源-质量权衡，相对于完整的等式饱和，最终成本降低了45.1%，峰值RAM使用量降低了69.1%。此外，该方法有效地迁移到基于XLA的张量编译器，并在逻辑综合案例研究中展示了其实用潜力。

Conclusion: EggMind成功实现了等式饱和策略的自动化合成，通过显式的策略表示和智能的搜索机制，有效解决了e-graph爆炸问题，为编译器优化领域提供了一个可扩展、可复用的解决方案，具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Equality saturation (EqSat) is a powerful optimization paradigm that compactly represents many equivalent programs in an e-graph and delays commitment until extraction selects a lowest-cost program. Making EqSat effective, therefore, requires not only domain-specific rewrite rules but also domain-specific strategies. Today, much of this strategy design is still manual, making it a major obstacle to automating e-graph-based compilers. Recent rule-synthesis frameworks can automatically infer large rewrite vocabularies from semantic specifications, but they also enlarge the rewrite space and further exacerbate e-graph explosion. Although large language models (LLMs) make automated strategy synthesis plausible, directly evolving backend code remains ineffective in practice. The search lacks reusable strategy abstractions and actionable feedback, and can easily trigger e-graph explosion or converge to poor designs.
  We present EggMind, an LLM-guided, end-to-end framework for synthesizing reusable EqSat strategies. At its core, EggMind introduces a domain-specific language, EqSatL, to represent EqSat strategies as explicit and inspectable artifacts. It then proposes an LLM-guided agentic workflow, equipped with novel techniques including proof-derived rewrite motif caching and tractability guidance, to search efficiently for high-quality strategies while keeping synthesis stable under e-graph growth. Evaluation shows that EggMind substantially improves the resource-quality trade-off on vectorization benchmarks, reducing final cost by 45.1% and peak RAM by 69.1% relative to full EqSat. We further show that the same methodology transfers effectively to an XLA-based tensor compiler, and demonstrate its practical potential in a logic-synthesis case study with augmented rewrite spaces.

</details>


### [55] [SkillGraph: Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Collaboration with Multimodal Graph Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17503)
*Zheng Nie,Ruolin Shen,Xinlei Yu,Bo Yin,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGraph框架，通过联合优化智能体技能和通信拓扑结构，实现了视觉多智能体系统的动态自适应协作，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉多智能体系统存在两个相互制约的问题：一是通信拓扑在推理前被固定，无法感知视觉内容和查询上下文；二是智能体推理能力在部署期间保持静态。刚性拓扑无法充分利用智能体的专业知识，而静态智能体缺乏针对特定查询进行专业化的动力。

Method: 提出SkillGraph联合框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)多模态图Transformer（MMGT），编码视觉token、指令语义和技能嵌入，预测查询条件化的协作图，实现动态、内容感知的信息流；(2)技能设计器，从失败案例中提炼和优化推理启发式规则，构建自演化的多模态技能库。更新的技能嵌入反馈到MMGT中，使拓扑能随能力增长而自适应调整。

Result: 在四个基准测试、五种常见MAS结构和四个基础模型上，SkillGraph均取得了持续的性能提升，证明了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: SkillGraph成功实现了智能体专业知识与通信拓扑的协同演化，突破了静态系统和刚性拓扑的限制，为构建自适应、可演化的视觉多智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scaling vision-language models into Visual Multiagent Systems (VMAS) is hindered by two coupled issues. First, communication topologies are fixed before inference, leaving them blind to visual content and query context; second, agent reasoning abilities remain static during deployment. These issues reinforce each other: a rigid topology fails to leverage richer agent expertise, while static agents lack incentives to specialize for a given query. We address this with SkillGraph, a joint framework that evolves both agent expertise and communication topology. Within this framework, a Multimodal Graph Transformer (MMGT) encodes visual tokens, instruction semantics and active skill embeddings to predict a query-conditioned collaboration graph, replacing hand-crafted routing with dynamic, content-aware information flow. Complementing this, a Skill Designer distills and refines reasoning heuristics from failure cases, constructing a self-evolving multimodal Skill Bank. Crucially, updated skill embeddings are fed back into the MMGT, enabling the topology to adapt alongside capability growth. Experiments show that SkillGraph achieves consistent improvements across four benchmarks, five common MAS structures and four base models. Code is available at https://github.com/niez233/skillgraph.

</details>


### [56] [SafeAgent: A Runtime Protection Architecture for Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17562)
*Hailin Liu,Eugene Ilyushin,Jie Ni,Min Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SafeAgent，一种运行时安全架构，将智能体安全建模为基于演化交互轨迹的有状态决策问题，有效抵御大语言模型智能体中的提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）智能体对提示注入攻击存在脆弱性，攻击可通过多步骤工作流、工具交互和持久化上下文传播，使得单纯的输入输出过滤无法提供可靠的安全防护。

Method: 提出SafeAgent运行时安全架构，将执行治理与语义风险推理分离。该架构包含两个协调组件：1）运行时控制器，负责在智能体循环周围调解行动；2）上下文感知决策核心，基于持久会话状态运行。决策核心被形式化为上下文感知的高级机器智能，并通过风险编码、效用-成本评估、后果建模、策略仲裁和状态同步等算子实现。

Result: 在Agent Security Bench (ASB)和InjecAgent基准上的实验表明，SafeAgent在保持良性任务竞争性性能的同时，相比基线和文本级护栏方法，能够持续提升鲁棒性。消融研究进一步表明，恢复置信度和策略权重决定了不同的安全-效用操作点。

Conclusion: SafeAgent通过将有状态决策过程引入智能体安全防护，为解决LLM智能体的提示注入攻击问题提供了有效的解决方案，在安全性和效用之间实现了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks that propagate through multi-step workflows, tool interactions, and persistent context, making input-output filtering alone insufficient for reliable protection. This paper presents SafeAgent, a runtime security architecture that treats agent safety as a stateful decision problem over evolving interaction trajectories. The proposed design separates execution governance from semantic risk reasoning through two coordinated components: a runtime controller that mediates actions around the agent loop and a context-aware decision core that operates over persistent session state. The core is formalized as a context-aware advanced machine intelligence and instantiated through operators for risk encoding, utility-cost evaluation, consequence modeling, policy arbitration, and state synchronization. Experiments on Agent Security Bench (ASB) and InjecAgent show that SafeAgent consistently improves robustness over baseline and text-level guardrail methods while maintaining competitive benign-task performance. Ablation studies further show that recovery confidence and policy weighting determine distinct safety-utility operating points.

</details>


### [57] [TacticGen: Grounding Adaptable and Scalable Generation of Football Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18210)
*Sheng Xu,Guiliang Liu,Tarak Kharrat,Yudong Luo,Mohamed Aloulou,Javier López Peña,Konstantin Sofeikov,Adam Reid,Paul Roberts,Steven Spencer,Joe Carnall,Ian McHale,Oliver Schulte,Hongyuan Zha,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TacticGen，一个用于足球战术生成的多智能体扩散Transformer模型，既能高精度预测球员轨迹，又能根据规则、自然语言或神经网络等不同目标生成定制化的战术方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时空数据与深度学习进展主要集中在轨迹预测等预测性分析上，而战术设计的发展相对滞后。预测揭示的是“可能发生什么”，而战术生成决定的是“为了实现战略目标应该发生什么”，弥合这两者之间的差距对于专业足球战术规划至关重要。

Method: 将战术表述为以比赛上下文为条件的多智能体移动与交互序列，提出了一个多智能体扩散Transformer模型。该模型采用智能体级自注意力机制和上下文感知的交叉注意力机制，以捕捉球员与球之间的合作与竞争动态，并通过分类器引导机制支持多种推理时目标（规则、自然语言或神经模型）的战术生成。

Result: 模型使用来自顶级联赛的超过330万个事件和1亿帧追踪数据进行训练，在球员轨迹预测任务上取得了最先进的精度；同时能够生成适应不同目标的战术，其可扩展性也得到验证。专家案例研究证实，TacticGen生成的战术具有真实性和战略价值。

Conclusion: TacticGen成功地将轨迹预测与战术生成相结合，证明了其在专业足球战术规划中的实用价值，为从预测分析向战术设计的发展提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Success in association football relies on both individual skill and coordinated tactics. While recent advancements in spatio-temporal data and deep learning have enabled predictive analyses like trajectory forecasting, the development of tactical design remains limited. Bridging this gap is essential, as prediction reveals what is likely to occur, whereas tactic generation determines what should occur to achieve strategic objectives. In this work, we present TacticGen, a generative model for adaptable and scalable tactic generation. TacticGen formulates tactics as sequences of multi-agent movements and interactions conditioned on the game context. It employs a multi-agent diffusion transformer with agent-wise self-attention and context-aware cross-attention to capture cooperative and competitive dynamics among players and the ball. Trained with over 3.3 million events and 100 million tracking frames from top-tier leagues, TacticGen achieves state-of-the-art precision in predicting player trajectories. Building on it, TacticGen enables adaptable tactic generation tailored to diverse inference-time objectives through classifier guidance mechanism, specified via rules, natural language, or neural models. Its modeling performance is also inherently scalable. A case study with football experts confirms that TacticGen generates realistic, strategically valuable tactics, demonstrating its practical utility for tactical planning in professional football. The project page is available at: https://shengxu.net/TacticGen/.

</details>


### [58] [Training and Agentic Inference Strategies for LLM-based Manim Animation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18364)
*Ravidu Suien Rammuni Silva,Ahmad Lotfi,Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Golnaz Shahtahmassebi,Jordan J. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ManimTrainer训练流水线（结合SFT与GRPO强化学习）和ManimAgent推理流水线（含RITL策略），首次对Manim程序化动画生成进行了统一的训练与推理研究。实验表明SFT提升代码质量，GRPO改善视觉输出，Qwen 3 Coder 30B模型在GRPO与RITL-DOC配置下达到94%渲染成功率和85.7%视觉相似度，超越GPT-4.1基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用Manim等库生成程序化动画对大语言模型提出了独特挑战，包括空间推理、时序编排以及对通用预训练数据中代表性不足的领域特定API的熟悉程度。当前研究缺乏对训练与推理策略在此场景下如何交互的系统研究，这促使作者开展首个针对Manim文本到代码再到视频转换的统一训练与推理研究。

Method: 研究提出了两大核心组件：1）ManimTrainer训练流水线，结合监督微调（SFT）与基于强化学习的群组相对策略优化（GRPO），采用融合代码与视觉评估信号的统一奖励信号；2）ManimAgent推理流水线，包含循环内渲染器（RITL）和API文档增强的RITL（RITL-DOC）策略。研究使用ManimBench基准，对17个开源30B以下LLM在9种训练与推理策略组合上进行了系统评估。

Result: SFT普遍提升代码质量，GRPO增强视觉输出并提高模型在推理时自纠正过程中对外部信号的响应能力。Qwen 3 Coder 30B模型配合GRPO和RITL-DOC取得最佳整体性能，渲染成功率达94%，视觉相似度达85.7%，较GPT-4.1基线在视觉相似度上高出3个百分点。分析发现代码与视觉指标之间的相关性随SFT和GRPO增强，但随推理时增强策略而减弱。

Conclusion: 训练策略与代理式推理策略在Manim动画生成中扮演互补角色。SFT和GRPO强化了代码与视觉指标的相关性，而推理时增强策略则提供了独立的性能提升途径。该研究填补了程序化动画生成领域训练与推理策略交互研究的空白，为未来LLM在代码生成到视觉输出转换任务中的优化提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Generating programmatic animation using libraries such as Manim presents unique challenges for Large Language Models (LLMs), requiring spatial reasoning, temporal sequencing, and familiarity with domain-specific APIs that are underrepresented in general pre-training data. A systematic study of how training and inference strategies interact in this setting is lacking in current research. This study introduces ManimTrainer, a training pipeline that combines Supervised Fine-tuning (SFT) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) based Group Relative Policy Optimisation (GRPO) using a unified reward signal that fuses code and visual assessment signals, and ManimAgent, an inference pipeline featuring Renderer-in-the-loop (RITL) and API documentation-augmented RITL (RITL-DOC) strategies. Using these techniques, this study presents the first unified training and inference study for text-to-code-to-video transformation with Manim. It evaluates 17 open-source sub-30B LLMs across nine combinations of training and inference strategies using ManimBench. Results show that SFT generally improves code quality, while GRPO enhances visual outputs and increases the models' responsiveness to extrinsic signals during self-correction at inference time. The Qwen 3 Coder 30B model with GRPO and RITL-DOC achieved the highest overall performance, with a 94% Render Success Rate (RSR) and 85.7% Visual Similarity (VS) to reference videos, surpassing the baseline GPT-4.1 model by +3 percentage points in VS. Additionally, the analysis shows that the correlation between code and visual metrics strengthens with SFT and GRPO but weakens with inference-time enhancements, highlighting the complementary roles of training and agentic inference strategies in Manim animation generation.

</details>


### [59] [The Cognitive Penalty: Ablating System 1 and System 2 Reasoning in Edge-Native SLMs for Decentralized Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16913)
*Syed Muhammad Aqdas Rizvi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在对DAO治理提案的安全审查中，推理时计算扩展（System 2）相比自回归基线（System 1）导致准确率严重倒退、共识稳定性下降和延迟激增，因此在边缘原生SLM场景下System 1更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAOs拟采用边缘原生小语言模型（SLM）作为宪法防火墙以审查提案并缓解语义社会工程攻击。虽然推理时计算扩展（System 2）被认为能增强形式逻辑，但其在高度对抗性的加密经济治理环境中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究引入Sentinel-Bench框架，包含840次推理实验，在Qwen-3.5-9B模型上进行严格的模型内消融，通过切换冻结权重中的潜在推理，在对抗性Optimism DAO数据集上隔离推理时计算的影响。

Result: 自回归基线（System 1）实现了100%的对抗鲁棒性和司法一致性，最终性延迟低于13秒。相反，System 2推理引入了严重的计算-准确率倒置，由26.7%的推理非收敛（认知崩溃）率驱动，导致试验间共识稳定性降至72.6%，延迟增加17倍，并暴露于治理可提取价值（GEV）和硬件中心化风险。此外，在1.5%的对抗试验中观察到“推理诱导的阿谀奉承”现象。

Conclusion: 对于在拜占庭容错（BFT）约束下运行的边缘原生SLM，System 1的参数化直觉在结构和经济上均优于System 2的迭代审议，更适合去中心化共识场景。

Abstract: Decentralized Autonomous Organizations (DAOs) are inclined explore Small Language Models (SLMs) as edge-native constitutional firewalls to vet proposals and mitigate semantic social engineering. While scaling inference-time compute (System 2) enhances formal logic, its efficacy in highly adversarial, cryptoeconomic governance environments remains underexplored. To address this, we introduce Sentinel-Bench, an 840-inference empirical framework executing a strict intra-model ablation on Qwen-3.5-9B. By toggling latent reasoning across frozen weights, we isolate the impact of inference-time compute against an adversarial Optimism DAO dataset. Our findings reveal a severe compute-accuracy inversion. The autoregressive baseline (System 1) achieved 100% adversarial robustness, 100% juridical consistency, and state finality in under 13 seconds. Conversely, System 2 reasoning introduced catastrophic instability, fundamentally driven by a 26.7% Reasoning Non-Convergence (cognitive collapse) rate. This collapse degraded trial-to-trial consensus stability to 72.6% and imposed a 17x latency overhead, introducing critical vulnerabilities to Governance Extractable Value (GEV) and hardware centralization. While rare (1.5% of adversarial trials), we empirically captured "Reasoning-Induced Sycophancy," where the model generated significantly longer internal monologues (averaging 25,750 characters) to rationalize failing the adversarial trap. We conclude that for edge-native SLMs operating under Byzantine Fault Tolerance (BFT) constraints, System 1 parameterized intuition is structurally and economically superior to System 2 iterative deliberation for decentralized consensus.
  Code and Dataset: https://github.com/smarizvi110/sentinel-bench

</details>


### [60] [Hive: A Multi-Agent Infrastructure for Algorithm- and Task-Level Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17353)
*Zizhang Luo,Yuhao Luo,Youwei Xiao,Yansong Xu,Runlin Guo,Yun Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Hive，一个支持算法和任务级扩展的多智能体基础设施，通过Logits Cache减少冗余计算，通过Agent-Aware Scheduling优化资源分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体系统在模型和系统层面的扩展已被广泛研究，但在算法层面和任务层面的扩展尚未得到充分解决。算法层面上，增加推理时计算会引入跨路径冗余；任务层面上，任务分解与多智能体协作受限于现有基础设施调度机制对多智能体存在的不感知，无法优化资源分配。

Method: 提出了Hive多智能体基础设施，包含描述前端和后端两部分。前端捕获每个智能体的行为规范并支持测试时扩展算法。后端引入两个关键机制：Logits Cache，通过在冗余采样路径间复用中间logits来缓解算法层面的跨路径冗余；Agent-Aware Scheduling，根据智能体贡献高效分配计算和KV缓存资源，实现任务层面的优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，Logits Cache在重采样中实现了1.11倍至1.76倍的平均加速；Agent-Aware Scheduling将热点未命中率降低了33%至51%。

Conclusion: Hive通过在算法层面和任务层面的创新机制，成功解决了多智能体系统中的跨路径冗余和资源分配问题，有效提升了智能体系统的扩展性和性能。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as complex agentic systems that scale with task complexity. While prior work has extensively explored model- and system-level scaling, algorithm- and task-level scaling remain largely unaddressed, constraining the full potential of agentic systems. At the algorithm level, allocating additional inference-time computation can enhance workflow capacity but introduces cross-path redundancy: overlapping computations across multiple reasoning branches. At the task level, complex tasks can be decomposed into subproblems and delegated across multiple agents for improved scalability and parallelism. However, existing infrastructures' scheduling is unaware of the existence of multiple agents, missing opportunities to optimize resource allocation.
  We propose Hive, a multi-agent infrastructure that enables algorithm- and task-level scaling. Hive features a description frontend that captures per-agent behavior and supports test-time scaling algorithms. Leveraging this specification, our backend introduces two key mechanisms: Logits Cache that reuses intermediate logits across redundant sampling paths to mitigate cross-path redundancy at the algorithm level, and Agent-Aware Scheduling that efficiently allocates compute and KV-cache resources according to agent contributions at the task level. Experiments show that Logits Cache achieves an average speedup of $1.11\times$-$1.76\times$ for re-sampling, and Agent-Aware Scheduling reduces the hotspot miss rate by $33\%$-$51\%$.

</details>


### [61] [Computational Hermeneutics: Evaluating generative AI as a cultural technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16403)
*Cody Kommers,Ruth Ahnert,Maria Antoniak,Emmanouil Benetos,Steve Benford,Mercedes Bunz,Baptiste Caramiaux,Shauna Concannon,Martin Disley,James Dobson,Yali Du,Edgar Duéñez-Guzmán,Kerry Francksen,Evelyn Gius,Jonathan W. Y. Gray,Ryan Heuser,Sarah Immel,Richard Jean So,Sang Leigh,Dalaki Livingston,Hoyt Long,Meredith Martin,Georgia Meyer,Daniela Mihai,Ashley Noel-Hirst,Kirsten Ostherr,Deven Parker,Yipeng Qin,Jessica Ratcliff,Emily Robinson,Karina Rodriguez,Adam Sobey,Ted Underwood,Aditya Vashistha,Matthew Wilkens,Youyou Wu,Yuan Zheng,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出计算阐释学框架，主张GenAI系统是"语境机器"，评估应从标准化的准确性问题转向关于意义的语境问题，并提出迭代性、包含人、测量文化语境三个评估原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI评估框架将文化视为待测量的变量，而非系统运行的基础要素。作者认为GenAI系统本质上处理意义解释问题，面临情境性（意义仅在语境中产生）、多元性（多种有效解释并存）和模糊性（解释自然冲突）三个解释性挑战，需要新的评估范式。

Method: 借鉴人文学科的阐释学理论，提出计算阐释学框架，将GenAI系统理解为"语境机器"。该框架提供对GenAI系统功能的解释性说明，并据此制定三个阐释学评估原则：基准测试应是迭代的而非一次性的；应包含人而不仅是机器；应测量文化语境而不仅是模型输出。

Result: 建立了一个新兴的计算阐释学框架，为GenAI系统提供了阐释学视角的功能解释。提出了三个具体的阐释学评估原则，为设计和评估当代AI系统提供了新范式的理论基础。

Conclusion: 这一视角为当代AI系统的设计和评估提供了一个新兴范式：从关于准确性的标准化问题转向关于意义的语境化问题，实现了从技术评估向文化语境评估的根本性转变。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly recognized as cultural technologies, yet current evaluation frameworks often treat culture as a variable to be measured rather than fundamental to the system's operation. Drawing on hermeneutic theory from the humanities, we argue that GenAI systems function as "context machines" that must inherently address three interpretive challenges: situatedness (meaning only emerges in context), plurality (multiple valid interpretations coexist), and ambiguity (interpretations naturally conflict). We present computational hermeneutics as an emerging framework offering an interpretive account of what GenAI systems do, and how they might do it better. We offer three principles for hermeneutic evaluation -- that benchmarks should be iterative, not one-off; include people, not just machines; and measure cultural context, not just model output. This perspective offers a nascent paradigm for designing and evaluating contemporary AI systems: shifting from standardized questions about accuracy to contextual ones about meaning.

</details>


### [62] [Support Sufficiency as Consequence-Sensitive Compression in Belief Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16434)
*Mark Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统在承诺假设时的证据压缩需要动态调控，保留过少或过多支持信息都会导致控制失败，自适应分辨率控制器在累积效用上优于所有固定分辨率控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准观点认为系统承诺假设时只需保留选定内容和置信度，但本文指出这不足以下游控制。确定压缩中必须保留什么内容本身是一个后果敏感问题，当前理论缺乏对支持结构保留与压缩权衡的系统性理解。

Method: 提出循环仲裁架构，其中主动约束场联合确定候选假设的几何结构。系统将其压缩为支持感知的控制状态，其分辨率由当前后果几何、仲裁记忆和资源约束共同调节。通过有界目标函数形式化保留支持信息的权衡，并设计最小化重复交互仿真进行验证。

Result: 发现两种失败模式：保留支持过少会丧失策略相关区分，导致验证、弃权和恢复的错误路由；保留过多会导致跨过细情境的学习碎片化。自适应分辨率控制器在累积效用上超越所有固定分辨率控制器；固定高分辨率控制器虽承诺准确率最高，但资源成本和学习碎片化抵消了丰富保留的收益。

Conclusion: 支持充分性应理解为动态压缩准则而非静态表示阈值。稳健仲裁依赖于在当前后果景观下保留足够策略决策的最小支持结构，并在推理与行动的循环中随条件变化动态调节该结构。

Abstract: When a system commits to a hypothesis, much of the evidential structure behind that commitment is lost to compression. Standard accounts assume that selected content and scalar confidence suffice for downstream control. This paper argues that they do not, and that determining what must survive compression is itself a consequence-sensitive problem. We develop a recurrent arbitration architecture in which active constraint fields jointly determine a hypothesis geometry over candidates. Rather than carrying that geometry forward in full, the system compresses it into a support-aware control state whose resolution is regulated by current consequence geometry, arbitration memory, and resource constraints.
  A bounded objective formalizes the tradeoff. Too little retained support collapses policy-relevant distinctions, producing controllers that select content adequately while misrouting verification, abstention, and recovery. Too much retained support fragments learning across overly fine contexts, degrading adaptation even as discrimination improves. These failure modes yield ordered controller predictions confirmed by a minimal repeated-interaction simulation. Adaptive controllers that regulate support resolution outperform all fixed-resolution controllers in cumulative utility. Agile adaptive control outperforms sluggish adaptive control. Fixed high-resolution control achieves the best commitment accuracy but still trails adaptive controllers because resource cost and learning fragmentation offset the gains from richer retention.
  Support sufficiency should be understood not as a static representational threshold, but as a dynamic compression criterion. Robust arbitration depends on preserving the smallest support structure adequate for policy under the current consequence landscape, and on regulating that structure as conditions change across repeated cycles of inference and action.

</details>


### [63] [Healthcare AI for Automation or Allocation? A Transaction Cost Economics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16465)
*Ari Ercole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用O*NET数据库，基于交易成本经济学理论对医疗工作进行任务级分析，发现临床医生角色的交易成本强度显著高于非临床医生角色，主要源于信息搜索和决策协调的负担，揭示了医疗角色间协调工作的异质性，为数字和AI干预提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗生产力不仅受临床复杂性影响，还受不确定性下工作协调成本的影响。尽管交易成本经济学为这些协调摩擦提供了理论框架，但鲜有研究在跨医疗职业的任务层面进行操作化分析。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究不同医疗角色间协调工作的性质差异。

Method: 研究使用O*NET职业数据库中的任务陈述和频率权重，在任务粒度上刻画医疗工作。采用受限大语言模型对每个独特任务进行编码，将其归入一个主要的交易成本类别（信息搜索、决策与谈判、监督与执行、适应与协调）并给出总体交易成本强度评分，最后聚合至职业层面进行分析。

Result: 在聚合到职业层面后发现，临床医生角色的交易成本强度显著高于非临床医生角色，这主要由更大的信息搜索和决策相关协调负担所驱动，而职业内部的交易成本离散程度并无差异。研究揭示了不同医疗角色间协调工作性质存在系统性异质性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明医疗角色间协调工作存在系统性差异，数字和AI干预的机会分布不均，这种分布受潜在协调结构的影响大于受技术任务复杂性的影响，为优化医疗资源配置和针对性干预提供了重要依据。

Abstract: Healthcare productivity is shaped not only by clinical complexity but by the costs of coordinating work under uncertainty. Transaction-cost economics offers a theory of these coordination frictions, yet has rarely been operationalised at task level across health occupations. Using task statements and frequency weights from the O*NET occupational database, we characterised healthcare work at task granularity and coded each unique task using a constrained large language model into one dominant transaction-cost category (information search, decision and bargaining, monitoring and enforcement, or adaptation and coordination) together with an overall transaction-cost intensity score. Aggregating to the occupation level, clinician roles exhibited substantially higher transaction-cost intensity than non-clinician roles, driven primarily by greater burdens of information search and decision-related coordination, while dispersion of transaction costs within occupations did not differ. These findings demonstrate systematic heterogeneity in the nature of coordination work across healthcare roles and suggest that the opportunities for digital and AI interventions are unevenly distributed, shaped less by technical task complexity than by underlying coordination structure.

</details>


### [64] [Agentic Frameworks for Reasoning Tasks: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16646)
*Zeeshan Rasheed,Abdul Malik Sami,Muhammad Waseem,Kai-Kristian Kemell,Mika Saari,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对22个主流智能体框架进行了首次大规模实证对比研究，发现在推理性能相近的情况下，编排质量（特别是内存控制、故障处理和成本管理）是框架选择的决定性因素，且数学推理能力显著落后于其他推理任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管智能体框架在复杂推理和决策方面取得了进展，但缺乏对其推理性能、效率和实际适用性的系统性比较证据。为了填补这一空白，本研究旨在对主流智能体框架进行大规模实证评估，为框架选择提供数据支撑。

Method: 从2023年1月至2025年7月间收集的1,200个GitHub仓库中筛选出22个广泛使用的智能体框架，基于架构设计进行分类。在统一设置下，使用BBH、GSM8K和ARC三个推理基准测试，测量推理准确率、执行时间、计算成本和跨基准一致性。

Result: 22个框架中19个完成了全部基准测试，其中12个表现稳定，平均准确率74.6-75.9%，执行时间4-6秒/任务，成本0.14-0.18美分/任务。较差结果主要由编排问题导致：Camel因上下文失控11天未完成BBH，Upsonic因重试机制一天消耗1434美元，AutoGen和Mastra因迭代交互耗尽API配额。数学推理准确率（44.35%）显著低于BBH（89.80%）和ARC（89.56%）。

Conclusion: 框架选择应优先考虑编排质量，特别是内存控制、故障处理和成本管理机制，而非仅关注推理能力。这为推理密集型软件工程任务中的智能体框架选择提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic frameworks have enabled AI agents to perform complex reasoning and decision-making. However, evidence comparing their reasoning performance, efficiency, and practical suitability remains limited. To address this gap, we empirically evaluate 22 widely used agentic frameworks across three reasoning benchmarks: BBH, GSM8K, and ARC. The frameworks were selected from 1,200 GitHub repositories collected between January 2023 and July 2025 and organized into a taxonomy based on architectural design. We evaluated them under a unified setting, measuring reasoning accuracy, execution time, computational cost, and cross-benchmark consistency.
  Our results show that 19 of the 22 frameworks completed all three benchmarks. Among these, 12 showed stable performance, with mean accuracy of 74.6-75.9%, execution time of 4-6 seconds per task, and cost of 0.14-0.18 cents per task. Poorer results were mainly caused by orchestration problems rather than reasoning limits. For example, Camel failed to complete BBH after 11 days because of uncontrolled context growth, while Upsonic consumed USD 1,434 in one day because repeated extraction failures triggered costly retries. AutoGen and Mastra also exhausted API quotas through iterative interactions that increased prompt length without improving results.
  We also found a sharp drop in mathematical reasoning. Mean accuracy on GSM8K was 44.35%, compared with 89.80% on BBH and 89.56% on ARC. Overall, this study provides the first large-scale empirical comparison of agentic frameworks for reasoning-intensive software engineering tasks and shows that framework selection should prioritize orchestration quality, especially memory control, failure handling, and cost management.

</details>


### [65] [From Subsumption to Satisfiability: LLM-Assisted Active Learning for OWL Ontologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16672)
*Haoruo Zhao,Wenshuo Tang,Duncan Guthrie,Michele Sevegnani,David Flynn,Paul Harvey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种将大语言模型（LLM）作为第三组件集成到主动学习中的方法，用于本体建模中的成员查询（MQ），实验显示其在多个本体上的召回率保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动学习中，成员查询（MQ）允许学习者向教师提出是非问题（如“每个苹果都是水果吗？”），这可以被视为相对于目标本体的包含测试。受描述逻辑中将包含关系简化为可满足性的标准方法启发，作者旨在利用LLM来辅助本体建模过程。

Method: 研究者将候选公理重写为对应的反概念，并将其转化为受控自然语言后呈现给大语言模型（LLM）。LLM被引入作为第三组件，提供近似于反概念实例的现实世界示例。这种设计确保在本体建模中只可能出现II型错误，最坏情况下仅延迟构建过程而不引入不一致性。

Result: 在13个商业大语言模型上进行的实验结果表明，该方法在多个已建立的本体上的召回率（对应框架中的II型错误）保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将LLM集成到本体建模的主动学习框架中，通过提供现实世界示例来辅助成员查询，确保了系统的可靠性（仅可能产生II型错误）和稳定性（稳定召回率），为本体工程提供了一种新的有效工具。

Abstract: In active learning, membership queries (MQs) allow a learner to pose questions to a teacher, such as ''Is every apple a fruit?'', to which the teacher responds correctly with yes or no. These MQs can be viewed as subsumption tests with respect to the target ontology. Inspired by the standard reduction of subsumption to satisfiability in description logics, we reformulate each candidate axiom into its corresponding counter-concept and verbalise it in controlled natural language before presenting it to Large Language Models (LLMs). We introduce LLMs as a third component that provides real-world examples approximating an instance of the counter-concept. This design property ensures that only Type II errors may occur in ontology modelling; in the worst case, these errors merely delay the construction process without introducing inconsistencies. Experimental results on 13 commercial LLMs show that recall, corresponding to Type II errors in our framework, remains stable across several well-established ontologies.

</details>


### [66] [Agentic Risk-Aware Set-Based Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16687)
*Varun Kumar,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由大语言模型引导的多智能体框架，用于辅助工程设计的早期阶段。该框架采用人在回路的范式，通过多个专业智能体协同工作，并结合条件风险价值（CVaR）进行风险管理，在机翼设计案例中实现了对大量初始设计候选方案的系统性探索和筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程设计早期阶段通常具有参数空间巨大和固有不确定性的特点，需要进行大量繁琐的初始探索工作。传统方法依赖人工进行高强度的分析任务，效率低下。研究旨在利用AI技术自动化处理高容量分析任务，增强人类专家在决策过程中的能力，从而提升设计效率和质量。

Method: 提出一种基于集合设计理念的多智能体框架，包含编码助手、设计智能体、系统工程智能体和分析师智能体，由人类管理者协调。框架分为协作阶段（开发验证工具套件）和执行阶段（系统性探索和剪枝）。关键技术包括：1）整合形式化风险管理，采用条件风险价值（CVaR）作为量化指标筛选设计；2）分析师智能体执行全局敏感性分析，生成可操作的启发式规则；3）最终提供经高保真CFD仿真增强的精选设计候选集。

Result: 在典型的机翼翼型设计问题上验证了框架的有效性。框架成功实现了对大量初始设计候选的系统性探索和剪枝，能够自动识别并过滤掉高概率无法满足性能要求（特别是目标升力系数）的设计方案。最终向人类管理者呈现经过风险评估和高保真CFD仿真增强的精选设计候选集。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用AI处理高容量分析任务，显著增强了人类专家在最终设计选择中的决策能力。通过人在回路范式和多智能体协作，实现了工程设计早期阶段的自动化探索与形式化风险管理的有机结合，为工程设计领域提供了一种新的智能化辅助方法。

Abstract: This paper introduces a multi-agent framework guided by Large Language Models (LLMs) to assist in the early stages of engineering design, a phase often characterized by vast parameter spaces and inherent uncertainty. Operating under a human-in-the-loop paradigm and demonstrated on the canonical problem of aerodynamic airfoil design, the framework employs a team of specialized agents: a Coding Assistant, a Design Agent, a Systems Engineering Agent, and an Analyst Agent - all coordinated by a human Manager. Integrated within a set-based design philosophy, the process begins with a collaborative phase where the Manager and Coding Assistant develop a suite of validated tools, after which the agents execute a structured workflow to systematically explore and prune a large set of initial design candidates. A key contribution of this work is the explicit integration of formal risk management, employing the Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) as a quantitative metric to filter designs that exhibit a high probability of failing to meet performance requirements, specifically the target coefficient of lift. The framework automates labor-intensive initial exploration through a global sensitivity analysis conducted by the Analyst agent, which generates actionable heuristics to guide the other agents. The process culminates by presenting the human Manager with a curated final set of promising design candidates, augmented with high-fidelity Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) simulations. This approach effectively leverages AI to handle high-volume analytical tasks, thereby enhancing the decision-making capability of the human expert in selecting the final, risk-assessed design.

</details>


### [67] [The Query Channel: Information-Theoretic Limits of Masking-Based Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16689)
*Erciyes Karakaya,Ozgur Ercetin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于掩码的事后可解释性方法建模为通信问题，推导了解释准确性与查询次数之间的理论上下界，揭示了现有方法（如LIME和KernelSHAP）在特定查询预算下失效的根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于掩码的事后解释方法（如KernelSHAP和LIME）通过随机扰动黑盒模型来估计特征重要性，但缺乏统一的理论框架来理解其查询复杂度与解释可靠性之间的关系。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将解释过程建模为通信问题：潜在解释作为消息，每次掩码评估作为信道使用。利用信息论工具，将假设类的熵作为解释复杂度，推导查询接口的识别容量，并建立强逆定理（速率超容量时必然失败）和可达性结果（速率低于容量时稀疏最大似然解码器可实现可靠恢复）。提出蒙特卡洛互信息估计器作为非渐近查询基准。

Result: 理论分析表明，存在一个查询预算区间，信息论允许可靠解释，但标准凸代理方法（模仿LIME和KernelSHAP的Lasso和OLS）仍然失效。实验揭示了高斯噪声和非线性曲率会导致查询信道降级，产生瀑布效应和误码平层，使得高分辨率解释无法实现。超像素分辨率和分词可视为设定解释熵的信源编码选择。

Conclusion: 本研究为事后解释方法建立了坚实的理论基础，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并为设计更高效的解释算法提供了信息论指导原则。理论框架表明，解释的可靠性与查询预算、假设类复杂度以及查询信道特性之间存在严格的数学关系。

Abstract: Masking-based post-hoc explanation methods, such as KernelSHAP and LIME, estimate local feature importance by querying a black-box model under randomized perturbations. This paper formulates this procedure as communication over a query channel, where the latent explanation acts as a message and each masked evaluation is a channel use. Within this framework, the complexity of the explanation is captured by the entropy of the hypothesis class, while the query interface supplies information at a rate determined by an identification capacity per query. We derive a strong converse showing that, if the explanation rate exceeds this capacity, the probability of exact recovery necessarily converges to one in error for any sequence of explainers and decoders. We also prove an achievability result establishing that a sparse maximum-likelihood decoder attains reliable recovery when the rate lies below capacity. A Monte Carlo estimator of mutual information yields a non-asymptotic query benchmark that we use to compare optimal decoding with Lasso- and OLS-based procedures that mirror LIME and KernelSHAP. Experiments reveal a range of query budgets where information theory permits reliable explanations but standard convex surrogates still fail. Finally, we interpret super-pixel resolution and tokenization for neural language models as a source-coding choice that sets the entropy of the explanation and show how Gaussian noise and nonlinear curvature degrade the query channel, induce waterfall and error-floor behavior, and render high-resolution explanations unattainable.

</details>


### [68] [RankGuide: Tensor-Rank-Guided Routing and Steering for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16694)
*Jiayi Tian,Yupeng Su,Ryan Solgi,Souvik Kundu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RankGuide框架，通过张量秩信号引导的路由和转向机制，解决了小型推理模型(SRM)与大型推理模型(LRM)协作中的失败检测问题，在保持准确性的同时将延迟降低高达1.75倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型(LRM)虽然通过生成显式多步思维链(CoT)推理增强了问题解决能力，但会产生大量推理延迟和计算开销。现有小型推理模型(SRM)与LRM协作的方法在有效且高效地检测和缓解SRM失败方面仍面临关键挑战。

Method: 论文首先通过分析SRM在生成文本和隐藏状态空间的推理，识别出三种失败模式：过度自信、不确定性和重度重新验证。基于此提出RankGuide框架：1)利用连续隐藏状态导出的张量秩信号作为路由信号，检测SRM何时可能失败并选择性调用LRM；2)引入张量秩过滤的转向向量提取方法来调节SRM的推理轨迹，提高其生成质量。

Result: 在多个推理基准测试上的实验表明，RankGuide相比LRM能够将延迟降低高达1.75倍，同时相比先前方法保持竞争性的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过张量秩信号改进路由和转向机制，RankGuide使SRM-LRM协作系统能够以更少的步骤实现更高效的推理并提高准确性，为高效协作推理系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) enhance problem-solving capabilities by generating explicit multi-step chains of thought (CoT) reasoning; however, they incur substantial inference latency and computational overhead. To mitigate this issue, recent works have explored model collaboration paradigms, where small reasoning models (SRMs) generate intermediate reasoning steps to achieve a better accuracy--latency trade-off. Despite recent progress, effectively and efficiently detecting and mitigating SRM failures in collaborative systems remains a key challenge. To address this issue, we analyze SRM inference in both the generated text and hidden-state spaces, and identify three types of failure modes: \textit{overconfidence}, \textit{uncertainty}, and \textit{heavy revalidation}. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{RankGuide}, a framework that improves the efficiency and effectiveness of SRM--LRM collaboration through tensor-rank-guided routing and steering. Specifically, RankGuide leverages a routing signal that incorporates tensor-rank signals derived from consecutive hidden states to detect when SRMs are likely to fail and selectively invoke LRMs. In addition, we introduce a tensor-rank-filtered steering vector extraction method to modulate the reasoning trajectory of SRMs, thereby improving their generation quality. By improving both routing and steering through tensor-rank signals, RankGuide enables SRM--LRM collaborative systems to achieve more efficient reasoning with fewer steps and improved accuracy. Experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the efficacy of RankGuide in reducing latency by up to $1.75\times$ compared to LRM, while maintaining competitive accuracy relative to prior methods.

</details>


### [69] [Debate as Reward: A Multi-Agent Reward System for Scientific Ideation via RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16723)
*Moein Salimi,Babak Hosseini Mohtasham,Amin Aghakasiri,Mahdi Naieni,Amir Hossein Qeysarbeigi,Mohammad Masih Shalchian Nazer,Zahra Azar,Mahdi Jafari Siavoshani,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对科学创意生成的强化学习框架，通过引入多智能体奖励函数和改进的策略优化方法，有效解决了奖励黑客问题，显著提升了生成科学创意的新颖性、可行性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于迭代提示或多智能体架构的大语言模型科学创意自动化方法存在幻觉和计算效率低下的问题，且强化学习应用中存在奖励黑客这一关键瓶颈，即模型利用不完善的评估代理来最大化分数而不产生真正的科学创新。

Method: 提出了首个作为评判者的多智能体奖励函数，将方法论验证与实现细节解耦，提供严格且对奖励黑客具有鲁棒性的二元奖励；使用无偏的组相对策略优化变体来缓解人工长度偏差；基于从ICLR 2024论文集中提取的问题-解决方案对数据集ICLR-320进行训练。

Result: 实验表明，该框架在专家评估的新颖性、可行性和有效性指标上显著优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个专门用于高质量科学创意生成的强化学习框架，有效解决了奖励黑客问题，为科学创意自动化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential in automating scientific ideation, yet current approaches relying on iterative prompting or complex multi-agent architectures often suffer from hallucination or computational inefficiency. A critical bottleneck in applying Reinforcement Learning (RL) to this open-ended domain is reward hacking -- where models exploit imperfect evaluation proxies to maximize scores without producing genuine scientific innovation. To address these limitations, we propose an RL framework explicitly tailored for high-quality scientific idea generation. We propose the first multi-agent reward function designed to serve as a judge, decoupling methodological validation from implementation details while providing strict binary rewards that are robust to reward hacking. To effectively optimize against this sparse signal, we utilize an unbiased variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization to mitigate artificial length bias. We grounded our training in ICLR-320, a curated dataset of problem-solution pairs extracted from ICLR 2024 proceedings. Experiments demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across expert-evaluated metrics of novelty, feasibility, and effectiveness.

</details>


### [70] [When Agents Go Quiet: Output Generation Capacity and Format-Cost Separation for LLM Document Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16736)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Michael Agyare,Miriam Kobbinah,Nathaniel Agbugblah,Prosper Addo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM编程代理在生成大型格式密集文档时出现的"输出停滞"问题，提出了输出生成容量(OGC)理论框架，通过延迟模板渲染减少48-72%的token消耗并完全消除输出停滞，并开源了工具GEN-PILOT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编程代理在生成大型、格式密集文档时存在一个未被充分理解的失败模式——输出停滞，即代理在尝试生成此类文档时会静默产生空响应，这严重影响了代理的可靠性和实用性。

Method: 提出了三部分理论框架：(1)引入输出生成容量(OGC)概念，形式化度量代理在当前上下文状态下的有效输出能力；(2)证明格式成本分离定理，表明对于任何开销乘数μ_f > 1的格式，延迟模板渲染在token效率上始终优于直接生成；(3)形式化自适应策略选择框架，根据估计输出成本与可用OGC的比值选择最优生成策略（直接、分块或延迟）。基于此框架开发了开源MCP服务器GEN-PILOT。

Result: 在三个模型(Claude 3.5 Sonnet、GPT-4o、Llama 3.1 70B)、四种文档类型和消融研究上的对照实验验证表明：延迟渲染在所有条件下减少LLM生成token量48-72%，并完全消除了输出停滞现象；理论框架成功转化为实用工具GEN-PILOT。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从理论上解释并解决了LLM代理的输出停滞问题，证明了延迟模板渲染在token效率上的优越性，并提供了一套可操作的决策框架和实用工具，为提升LLM代理在复杂文档生成任务中的可靠性提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: LLM-powered coding agents suffer from a poorly understood failure mode we term output stalling: the agent silently produces empty responses when attempting to generate large, format-heavy documents. We present a theoretical framework that explains and prevents this failure through three contributions. (1) We introduce Output Generation Capacity (OGC), a formal measure of an agent's effective ability to produce output given its current context state - distinct from and empirically smaller than the raw context window. (2) We prove a Format-Cost Separation Theorem showing that deferred template rendering is always at least as token-efficient as direct generation for any format with overhead multiplier $μ_f > 1$, and derive tight bounds on the savings. (3) We formalize Adaptive Strategy Selection, a decision framework that maps the ratio of estimated output cost to available OGC into an optimal generation strategy (direct, chunked, or deferred). We validate the theory through controlled experiments across three models (Claude 3.5 Sonnet, GPT-4o, Llama 3.1 70B), four document types, and an ablation study isolating each component's contribution. Deferred rendering reduces LLM generation tokens by 48-72% across all conditions and eliminates output stalling entirely. We instantiate the framework as GEN-PILOT, an open-source MCP server, demonstrating that the theory translates directly into a practical tool.

</details>


### [71] [CT Open: An Open-Access, Uncontaminated, Live Platform for the Open Challenge of Clinical Trial Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16742)
*Jianyou Wang,Youze Zheng,Longtian Bao,Hanyuan Zhang,Qirui Zheng,Yuhan Chen,Yang Zhang,Matthew Feng,Maxim Khan,Aditya K. Sehgal,Christopher D. Rosin,Ramamohan Paturi,Umber Dube,Leon Bergen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CT Open平台，用于临床实验结果预测，通过自动化去污染流水线确保预测时结果未公开，推动AI在真实世界事件预测方面的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家长期致力于准确预测真实世界事件的结果，但临床实验结果预测对领域专家而言仍是高风险挑战。现有评估平台存在数据泄露问题，无法确保预测时结果未被公开，因此需要一个开放、公正的平台来推动AI预测能力的真实评估。

Method: 研究团队开发了CT Open开放平台，每年举办四次挑战赛。核心创新是提出了一种全自动去污染流水线，利用迭代式LLM驱动的网络搜索来识别试验结果的最早公开时间，确保每个被评估的试验在预测提交时结果未公开。平台发布训练集和两个时间戳测试基准（Winter 2025和Summer 2025）。

Result: 构建了一个开放访问的实时预测平台，任何人均可提交预测；通过人工专家标注验证了去污染流水线的质量和准确性；确保参与者可以使用任何方法和数据源进行预测；发布了训练数据集和时序测试基准数据集。

Conclusion: CT Open平台可作为推进AI在真实世界结果预测研究中的核心枢纽，同时为生物医学研究和临床试验设计改进提供参考。平台已在https://ct-open.net/上线运行。

Abstract: Scientists have long sought to accurately predict outcomes of real-world events before they happen. Can AI systems do so more reliably? We study this question through clinical trial outcome prediction, a high-stakes open challenge even for domain experts. We introduce CT Open, an open-access, live platform that will run four challenge every year. Anyone can submit predictions for each challenge. CT Open evaluates those submissions on trials whose outcomes were not yet public at the time of submission but were made public afterwards. Determining if a trial's outcome is public on the internet before a certain date is surprisingly difficult. Outcomes posted on official registries may lag behind by years, while the first mention may appear in obscure articles. To address this, we propose a novel, fully automated decontamination pipeline that uses iterative LLM-powered web search to identify the earliest mention of trial outcomes. We validate the pipeline's quality and accuracy by human expert's annotations. Since CT Open's pipeline ensures that every evaluated trial had no publicly reported outcome when the prediction was made, it allows participants to use any methodology and any data source. In this paper, we release a training set and two time-stamped test benchmarks, Winter 2025 and Summer 2025. We believe CT Open can serve as a central hub for advancing AI research on forecasting real-world outcomes before they occur, while also informing biomedical research and improving clinical trial design. CT Open Platform is hosted at $\href{https://ct-open.net/}{https://ct-open.net/}$

</details>


### [72] [Why Training-Free Token Reduction Collapses: The Inherent Instability of Pairwise Scoring Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16745)
*Yang Shanglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了无需训练的ViT token缩减方法在高压缩率下发生"悬崖式崩溃"的根本原因，并提出CATIS方法成功规避此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token缩减方法（ToMe、ToFu、PiToMe、MCTF）虽采用不同评分机制，但在高压缩率下均出现几乎一致的悬崖式性能崩溃，作者试图从理论上解释这一现象的内在原因。

Method: 提出诊断框架，使用排序一致性$ρ_s$和非对角相关性$ρ_{off}$两个工具分解崩溃原因；基于诊断结果推导三个设计原则，构建CATIS方法，采用一元信号替代成对相似性信号。

Result: 理论分析揭示了两个崩溃根源：(1)层式缩减固有的信号无关误差放大器；(2)成对相似性信号的排序一致性在深层网络中从0.88退化至0.27。CATIS在ViT-Large上以63% FLOPs缩减率保持96.9%的原精度（81.0%），而所有基线方法崩溃至43-65%。

Conclusion: 成对相似性信号固有的不稳定性（$O(N_p^2)$联合扰动）是崩溃的主因，而一元信号（$O(N_p)$扰动）具有更强的稳定性。通过一元信号提高触发阈值并使用分流机制抑制增益，可成功避免高压缩率下的性能崩溃。

Abstract: Training-free token reduction methods for Vision Transformers (ToMe, ToFu, PiToMe, and MCTF) employ different scoring mechanisms, yet they share a closely matched cliff-like collapse at high compression. This paper explains \emph{why}. We develop a diagnostic framework with two tools, ranking consistency $ρ_s$ and off-diagonal correlation $ρ_\text{off}$, that decomposes the collapse into (1)a signal-agnostic error amplifier inherent to layer-wise reduction, predicting convex Pareto curves and $r_{\text{crit}} \propto 1/L$; and (2)shared reliance on \emph{pairwise} similarity signals whose ranking consistency degrades from $ρ_s{=}0.88$ to $0.27$ in deep layers. Pairwise rankings are inherently unstable ($O(N_p^2)$ joint perturbations) while unary signals enjoy greater stability ($O(N_p)$ perturbations, CLT). From three design principles derived from this diagnosis, we construct CATIS as a constructive validation: unary signals raise the trigger threshold, triage suppresses the gain. On ViT-Large at 63% FLOPs reduction, CATIS retains 96.9% of vanilla accuracy (81.0%) on ImageNet-1K where all baselines collapse to 43--65%.

</details>


### [73] [Don't Start What You Can't Finish: A Counterfactual Audit of Support-State Triage in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16752)
*Eren Unlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SSTA-32诊断框架来评估智能体在任务被阻塞时的诊断能力，发现通过显式分类决策路径可激活模型潜在的分诊能力，实现91.7%的延迟决策准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体评估主要关注完全指定任务的执行，而关于澄清、能力感知、弃权和搜索终止的研究大多是孤立的。这留下了智能体是否能在行动前诊断任务为何被阻塞的开放问题。

Method: 引入Support-State Triage Audit (SSTA-32)匹配项诊断框架，通过最小反事实编辑将相同基础请求翻转为四种支持状态（完整、可澄清、支持阻塞、当前不支持）。在四种提示条件下评估前沿模型，并使用双角色自动审计(DPAA)进行确定性启发式评分。

Result: 默认执行在非完整任务上严重过度承诺(41.7%过度承诺率)。标量置信度映射避免了过度承诺但使三路延迟空间坍塌(58.3%类型延迟准确率)。相反，Action-Only和PSC都达到了91.7%的类型延迟准确率。针对性消融实验证实，移除支持充分性维度会选择性降低REQUEST SUPPORT准确率，而移除证据充分性维度会触发系统性的过度承诺。

Conclusion: 前沿模型具有强大的潜在分诊能力，但需要显式的分类决策路径才能安全激活。由于DPAA在单个上下文窗口内操作，这些结果代表了能力上限估计。

Abstract: Current agent evaluations largely reward execution on fully specified tasks, while recent work studies clarification [11, 22, 2], capability awareness [9, 1], abstention [8, 14], and search termination [20, 5] mostly in isolation. This leaves open whether agents can diagnose why a task is blocked before acting.
