### [181] [OGER: A Robust Offline-Guided Exploration Reward for Hybrid Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18530)
*Xinyu Ma,Mingzhou Xu,Xuebo Liu,Chang Jin,Qiang Wang,Derek F. Wong,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OGER框架，通过统一离线教师指导和在线强化学习，并利用多教师协作训练和熵感知的辅助探索奖励，有效提升了大语言模型的推理能力，特别是在数学推理上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）提升了大语言模型的推理能力，但模型仍难以探索初始潜在空间之外的新颖轨迹；现有的离线教师指导和熵驱动策略往往缺乏深度融合或受限于模型内在能力，亟需一种能有效结合两者优势的统一框架。

Method: 提出了OGER框架，通过专门的奖励建模视角将离线教师指导与在线强化学习相统一；采用多教师协作训练策略，并构建了一个辅助探索奖励，该奖励同时利用离线轨迹和模型自身的熵来激励自主探索。

Result: 在数学和通用推理基准测试上的大量实验表明，OGER显著优于竞争基线，在数学推理方面取得了大幅提升，同时对域外任务保持了良好的泛化能力；训练动态分析和消融实验验证了熵感知奖励调制的有效性。

Conclusion: OGER通过深度融合离线教师指导与熵驱动的在线探索，成功解决了LLM难以探索新颖轨迹的问题，在提升推理性能的同时保持了模型的泛化能力，为RLVR领域提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) have significantly improved Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, yet models often struggle to explore novel trajectories beyond their initial latent space. While offline teacher guidance and entropy-driven strategies have been proposed to address this, they often lack deep integration or are constrained by the model's inherent capacity. In this paper, we propose OGER, a novel framework that unifies offline teacher guidance and online reinforcement learning through a specialized reward modeling lens. OGER employs multi-teacher collaborative training and constructs an auxiliary exploration reward that leverages both offline trajectories and the model's own entropy to incentivize autonomous exploration. Extensive experiments across mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OGER significantly outperforms competitive baselines, achieving substantial gains in mathematical reasoning while maintaining robust generalization to out-of-domain tasks. We provide a comprehensive analysis of training dynamics and conduct detailed ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of our entropy-aware reward modulation. Our code is available at https://github.com/ecoli-hit/OGER.git.

</details>


### [182] [ClawEnvKit: Automatic Environment Generation for Claw-Like Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18543)
*Xirui Li,Ming Li,Derry Xu,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ClawEnvKit，一个从自然语言描述自动生成和验证claw-like智能体环境的流水线，构建了包含1,040个环境的Auto-ClawEval基准，成本降低13,800倍且质量相当，支持按需评估与自适应训练环境生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有claw-like智能体的训练与评估环境构建依赖人工流程，扩展性差，缺乏自动化、可验证且多样化的环境生成方法。

Method: 提出ClawEnvKit自动生成流水线，包含三模块：(1)解析器从自然语言提取结构化生成参数；(2)生成器产出任务规范、工具接口和评分配置；(3)验证器确保可行性、多样性、结构有效性与内部一致性。基于此构建Auto-ClawEval基准（1,040个环境，24类别）。

Result: Auto-ClawEval在连贯性与清晰度上匹配或超越人工环境，成本降低13,800倍。在4个模型家族和8个智能体框架上评估，框架工程提升性能达15.7个百分点，无模型达到饱和。支持按需实时评估与自适应训练环境生成。

Conclusion: ClawEnvKit实现了环境的自动化、规模化生成，显著降低成本并保持高质量，使评估从静态基准转向连续、用户驱动的动态过程，并为智能体提供按需、针对性训练环境。

Abstract: Constructing environments for training and evaluating claw-like agents remains a manual, human-intensive process that does not scale. We argue that what is needed is not just a dataset, but an automated pipeline capable of generating diverse, verified environments on demand. To this end, we introduce ClawEnvKit, an autonomous generation pipeline that instantiates this formalism from natural language descriptions. The pipeline comprises three modules: (1) a parser that extracts structured generation parameters from natural language input; (2) a generator that produces the task specification, tool interface, and scoring configuration; and (3) a validator that enforces feasibility, diversity, structural validity, and internal consistency across the generated environments. Using ClawEnvKit, we construct Auto-ClawEval, the first large-scale benchmark for claw-like agents, comprising 1,040 environments across 24 categories. Empirically, Auto-ClawEval matches or exceeds human-curated environments on coherence and clarity at 13,800x lower cost. Evaluated across 4 model families and 8 agent harness frameworks, we find that harness engineering boosts performance by up to 15.7 percentage points over a bare ReAct baseline, completion remains the primary axis of variation with no model saturating the benchmark, and automated generation enables evaluation at a scale previously infeasible. Beyond static benchmarking, ClawEnvKit enables live evaluation: users describe a desired capability in natural language and obtain a verified environment on demand, turning evaluation into a continuous, user-driven process. The same mechanism serves as an on-demand training environment generator, producing task distributions that adapt to an agent's current weaknesses rather than being bounded by existing user logs.

</details>


### [183] [Agentic Forecasting using Sequential Bayesian Updating of Linguistic Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18576)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLF（贝叶斯语言预测器），一个用于二元预测的智能体系统，在ForecastBench基准上达到了最先进性能，超越了包括GPT-5在内的所有顶尖公开方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测系统通常将所有检索到的证据追加到不断增长的上下文中，缺乏结构化的信念表示。本文旨在通过贝叶斯语言信念状态、分层多试验聚合和分层校准来解决这些问题，提升预测准确性。

Method: BLF系统基于三个核心思想：（1）贝叶斯语言信念状态：结合数值概率估计和自然语言证据摘要的半结构化表示，在迭代工具使用循环中由LLM更新；（2）分层多试验聚合：运行K个独立试验，使用logit空间收缩和数据相关先验进行组合；（3）分层校准：采用带分层先验的Platt缩放，避免对偏斜基率源的极端预测过度收缩。

Result: 在ForecastBench排行榜的400个回测问题上，BLF超越了所有顶尖公开方法，包括Cassi、GPT-5、Grok 4.20和Foresight-32B。消融研究表明结构化信念状态的影响力与网络搜索访问相当，收缩聚合和分层校准均提供了显著的额外增益。

Conclusion: BLF通过创新的信念状态表示、聚合和校准方法，显著提升了二元预测的性能。研究还开发了泄漏率低于1.5%的鲁棒回测框架，并使用严格的统计方法控制各种噪声源来比较不同方法。

Abstract: We present BLF (Bayesian Linguistic Forecaster), an agentic system for binary forecasting that achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ForecastBench benchmark. The system is built on three ideas. (1) A Bayesian linguistic belief state: a semi-structured representation combining numerical probability estimates with natural-language evidence summaries, updated by the LLM at each step of an iterative tool-use loop. This contrasts with the common approach of appending all retrieved evidence to an ever-growing context. (2) Hierarchical multi-trial aggregation: running $K$ independent trials and combining them using logit-space shrinkage with a data-dependent prior. (3) Hierarchical calibration: Platt scaling with a hierarchical prior, which avoids over-shrinking extreme predictions for sources with skewed base rates.
  On 400 backtesting questions from the ForecastBench leaderboard, BLF outperforms all the top public methods, including Cassi, GPT-5, Grok~4.20, and Foresight-32B. Ablation studies show that the structured belief state is as impactful as web search access, and that shrinkage aggregation and hierarchical calibration each provide significant additional gains.
  In addition, we develop a robust back-testing framework with a leakage rate below 1.5\%, and use rigorous statistical methodology to compare different methods while controlling for various sources of noise.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [ML and Smartphones Assisted Real-Time Uplink Performance Prediction in 5G Cellular System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16356)
*Md Mahfuzur Rahman,Jareen Shuva,Nishith Tripathi,Lingjia Liu,Jeffrey Reed*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于机器学习和商用智能手机的框架，能够利用实时测量数据有效预测真实5G专网的上行吞吐量和误块率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对真实5G蜂窝网络（尤其是上行链路）性能预测的实际需求，旨在探索一种仅利用商用现成（COTS）智能手机和通用机器学习方法即可实现可靠性能预测的实用路径，以辅助网络优化与评估。

Method: 构建了一个基于srsRAN 5G NR协议栈和Dell工作站的软件定义无线电（SDR）实验平台（gNB运行于3.4 GHz），利用两台商用Google Pixel 7a手机在室内外多种场景（静止、步行、移动，LOS与nLOS）下采集上行指标（CQI、MCS、吞吐量、TTI、BLER）。通过Ookla、iperf及YouTube业务生成流量，将包含多UE干扰条件的测量数据作为训练集，对线性回归、决策树、随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM五种监督回归模型进行基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，利用所采集的数据集训练模型，能够可靠地预测上行吞吐量和误块率；在对比的五种算法中验证了预测的准确性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅依靠商用智能手机和广泛可用的机器学习算法进行5G网络性能预测是切实可行的，为现实世界中的5G网络性能评估提供了一种低成本、实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a machine learning (ML) and smartphone-assisted framework for uplink performance prediction in a private, realistic 5G cellular system using real-time measurements in both indoor and outdoor settings. This work presents a comprehensive data-driven evaluation of 5G performance prediction using a controllable software-defined radio test environment. The experimental platform is built on srsRAN 5G NR stack running on a Dell workstation configured as a gNB and 5G core operating at 3.4 GHz. Two commercial Google Pixel 7a devices are instrumented to capture uplink metrics, including channel quality indicator (CQI), modulation and coding scheme (MCS), throughput, transmission time interval (TTI), and block error rate (BLER). Different types of traffic are generated using industry-standard tools such as Ookla and iperf, spanning stationary, pedestrian, and mobility cases under both line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (nLOS) propagation environments. Additional datasets include YouTube video sessions and global server endpoints to introduce variability in path characteristics. The resulting measurements, including multi-UE interference conditions, serve as training data for several supervised regression models. Five learning algorithms-linear regression, decision tree, random forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM-are benchmarked for prediction accuracy. The study shows that reliable forecasting of throughput and BLER is feasible using only COTS smartphones and widely available ML methods, offering a practical pathway for real-world 5G network performance estimation.

</details>


### [185] [End-to-End Performance of Video Streaming With MPEG-DASH Over Satellite 5G IAB Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16634)
*Muhammad Adeel Zahid,Ekram Hossain,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文通过ns-3仿真评估了LEO卫星5G IAB网络中MPEG-DASH视频流性能，发现QUIC-BBR在吞吐量、延迟、播放连续性和公平性之间取得最佳平衡，能提供最优的整体QoE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在现代集成卫星-地面网络（ISTN）环境下，传输层协议和拥塞控制算法如何影响自适应视频传输的质量体验（QoE），解决高延迟、吞吐量波动和播放连续性共同影响用户体验的问题。

Method: 在ns-3仿真平台中构建端到端仿真框架，集成5G无线接入网、LEO卫星回传、传输层协议和MPEG-DASH应用模块；对比评估TCP和QUIC协议下CUBIC、NewReno、BBR三种拥塞控制算法的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明没有单一配置在所有指标上均最优，但存在明确的性能权衡。QUIC-BBR组合在流媒体QoE角度表现最佳，在保证足够播放时长的同时，具有更少的中断次数和显著更低的延迟。

Conclusion: 在ISTN环境中评估自适应视频流时，必须联合考虑传输层设计和拥塞控制机制，这对优化卫星-地面集成网络的视频流服务质量具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end performance evaluation of MPEG-DASH video streaming over a Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite-based 5G Integrated Access and Backhaul (IAB) network. Our objective is to investigate how modern transport protocols and congestion control algorithms affect adaptive video delivery in an integrated satellite-terrestrial network (ISTN), where latency, throughput variation, and playback continuity jointly shape the user Quality-of-Experience (QoE). We implement a simulation framework in ns-3 by adapting open-source modules for the 5G radio access network, LEOS backhaul, transport layer protocols, and MPEG-DASH application behavior. Within this framework, TCP and QUIC are evaluated with multiple congestion control algorithms, including CUBIC, NewReno, and BBR. Performance is assessed using application-level and transport-level metrics, including playback duration, interruption duration, stall count, playback bitrate, throughput, latency, and fairness. The results show that no single configuration is uniformly optimal across all metrics. However, clear tradeoffs are observed among throughput, latency, playback continuity, and fairness. In particular, QUIC-BBR provides the most balanced overall behavior from a streaming QoE perspective, combining adequate playback duration with fewer interruptions and substantially lower latency than other alternatives. These findings highlight the importance of jointly considering transport design and congestion control when evaluating adaptive video streaming over ISTNs.

</details>


### [186] [Symphony: Taming Step Misalignments in the Network for Ring-based Collective Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16880)
*Yuze Jin,Xin Zhe Khooi,Ruyi Yao,Mun Choon Chan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Symphony，一种网络内解决方案，通过检测和缓解环形集合通信中的流水线步失调问题，在分布式AI训练中实现了高达54%的通信时间改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环形集合通信在分布式AI训练中被广泛使用，但其性能严重依赖于同步的逐步推进。在实际网络环境中，网络抖动和拥塞会导致流水线步失调，从而增加集合通信完成时间，这成为限制性能的关键问题。

Method: 提出Symphony系统，包含两个核心机制：(1)轻量级机制来跟踪每个作业的流水线进度；(2)创新性地使用拥塞信号选择性抑制超前进度流，使落后流能够追赶上来，无需全局协调。该方法通过Astra-Sim仿真验证，并在Intel Tofino2可编程交换机上进行了原型实现。

Result: 通过仿真和原型实验证明，Symphony有效缓解了环形集合通信中的步失调问题，实现了作业/集合通信时间高达54%的改善，并在实际硬件平台上验证了方案的可行性。

Conclusion: Symphony成功解决了环形集合通信中的流水线步失调问题，通过无需全局协调的选择性流控制机制，显著提升了分布式AI训练的通信效率，具有良好的实用性和部署价值。

Abstract: Ring-based collective operations are widely used in distributed AI training due to their efficient bandwidth utilization. While ring communication excels at pipelining, its performance is heavily dependent on having synchronized step-wise progression. This presents a mismatch to the underlying network conditions in practice: collective operations are vulnerable to network jitter and congestion, leading to step misalignment and increased collective completion time. To that end, we propose Symphony, an in-network solution that detects pipeline step misalignment and mitigates its impact. Symphony introduces (1) a lightweight mechanism to track per-job pipeline progress and (2) a novel use of congestion signals to selectively throttle outpacing flows, allowing lagging flows to catch up without global coordination. Through simulations using Astra-Sim, we show that Symphony effectively mitigates step misalignments in ring-based collectives, resulting in up to 54% improvement in job/collective communication time. Finally, we prototype and validate Symphony on an Intel Tofino2 programmable switch to demonstrate its practicality.

</details>


### [187] [Safety-Aware AoI Scheduling for LEO Satellite-Assisted Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17281)
*Kangkang Sun,Junyi He,Juntong Liu,Xiuzhen Chen,Jianhua Li,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对LEO卫星回传的自动驾驶车队场景，提出了SafeScale-MATD3框架，通过双时间尺度AoI建模和主动切换策略，将碰撞预警违规率降低4-5.5倍，是唯一满足严格1%违规预算的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车队穿越基础设施缺口时依赖LEO卫星回传进行安全关键更新，但现有框架无法同时解决三大挑战：卫星与车辆同时运动产生的复合多普勒效应、超过碰撞预警截止期限的子时隙切换中断、三类车辆优先级的异构新鲜度需求。核心难点在于时间尺度失配：粗粒度控制时隙掩盖了子时隙中断，导致AoI峰值分析和安全验证无法进行。

Method: 提出统一框架，将双时间尺度AoI模型与通过虚拟队列强制执行的分层时间平均安全约束相耦合。推导出乒乓切换AoI包络的闭式解，证明累积惩罚随振荡长度呈二次增长，为振荡抑制作为最高优先级安全机制提供了理论依据。基于此开发SafeScale-MATD3算法，采用漂移加惩罚模板，实现主动切换时序和多头双评论家MARL。

Result: 关键发现：抑制短暂但反复的乒乓切换比缩短任何单次中断产生更大的安全收益；时钟级AoI核算是在LEO切换下实现可验证碰撞预警保证的必要条件。实验表明，SafeScale-MATD3是唯一满足严格1%碰撞预警违规预算的方法，相比基线方法违规率降低4-5.5倍，碰撞预警AoI降低35%，并在能耗与新鲜度权衡中实现严格帕累托优势。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在LEO卫星回传场景下，抑制乒乓切换振荡比缩短单次中断更关键，时钟级AoI核算是实现碰撞预警保证的前提条件，为自动驾驶车队的安全通信提供了可验证的理论框架和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous platoons traversing infrastructure gaps increasingly depend on LEO satellite backhaul for safety-critical updates, yet no existing framework jointly addresses compound Doppler from simultaneous satellite and vehicle motion, sub-slot handover outages that exceed collision-alert deadlines, and heterogeneous freshness requirements across three vehicular priority classes. The core challenge is a \emph{timescale mismatch}: coarse control slots hide sub-slot outages, which makes both AoI spike analysis and safety verification ill-posed. Ping-pong handover oscillations further compound AoI cost in a way that purely reactive schedulers cannot mitigate. We address these challenges through a unified framework that couples a two-timescale AoI model with tiered time-average safety constraints enforced by virtual queues. A closed-form ping-pong AoI envelope reveals that cumulative penalty grows quadratically in oscillation length, analytically justifying oscillation suppression as the highest-leverage safety mechanism. The resulting drift-plus-penalty template is instantiated as SafeScale-MATD3 with proactive handover timing and multi-task dual-critic MARL. A key finding is that suppressing brief but repeated ping-pong oscillations yields larger safety returns than shortening any single outage, and that tick-level AoI accounting is a necessary condition for verifiable collision-alert guarantees under LEO handovers. Simulations show that SafeScale-MATD3 is the only method satisfying the strict 1 % collision-alert violation budget, reducing violation rate by 4 to 5.5 times versus baselines, while achieving 35 % lower collision-alert AoI and strict Pareto dominance on the energy and freshness tradeoff.

</details>


### [188] [Scheduling in Multi-Hop Wireless Networks With Deadlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17493)
*Nicholas Jones,Eytan Modiano*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了无线网络中满足即时吞吐量和硬分组截止时间保证的调度问题。研究发现链路调度策略对网络在干扰下满足硬截止时间的能力有重大影响。证明了在多流情况下队列稳定性不足以保证严格的截止时间，并提出了一个分布式多项式时间算法，能在实现近最优吞吐量的同时满足严格的端到端分组截止时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无线网络调度问题，旨在满足由即时吞吐量和硬分组截止时间定义的端到端服务保证。核心挑战在于理解在干扰环境下网络满足硬截止时间保证的能力，以及现有稳定策略为何无法保证严格的延迟约束。

Method: 1. 采用网络切片模型解耦流之间的排队动态。
2. 表征单流隔离运行时的吞吐量和截止时间最优策略。
3. 推导链路调度间隔与端到端分组延迟之间的条件关系。
4. 通过解决广义风车调度问题（pinwheel scheduling problem），在任意干扰模型下实现硬保证。
5. 设计分布式多项式时间调度算法。

Result: 1. 证明了在多流设置下，最坏情况稳定策略下的分组延迟可能无限增长，队列稳定性不足以保证严格截止时间。
2. 确定了多流情况下可行性的界限。
3. 提出的分布式算法能够满足严格的端到端分组截止时间，同时实现近最优吞吐量。

Conclusion: 链路调度策略是决定无线网络能否满足硬截止时间保证的关键因素。仅保证队列稳定性是不充分的。通过解决广义风车调度问题，可以在任意干扰模型下实现硬保证，所提出的分布式算法在延迟保证和吞吐量之间取得了有效的平衡。

Abstract: We analyze the problem of scheduling in wireless networks to meet end-to-end service guarantees, defined by instantaneous throughput and hard packet deadlines. Using a network slicing model to decouple the queueing dynamics between flows, we show that the network's ability to meet hard deadline guarantees under interference is largely influenced by the link scheduling policy. We characterize throughput- and deadline-optimal policies for a solitary flow operating in isolation, which provide bounds on feasibility in the general case with multiple flows. We prove that packet delays can grow arbitrarily large in the multi-flow setting under a worst-case stabilizing policy, showing that queue stability is not sufficient to guarantee tight deadlines. We derive conditions on end-to-end packet delays in terms of link inter-scheduling times, and show that it is possible to make hard guarantees under any interference model by solving a generalized version of the pinwheel scheduling problem. Finally, we introduce a decentralized polynomial-time algorithm which can meet tight end-to-end packet deadlines while achieving near-optimal throughput.

</details>


### [189] [Lagrange Index based Scheduling for Minimizing Age of Updates from Heterogeneous Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18077)
*Aniket Mukherjee,Joy Kuri,Chandramani Singh*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对异构传感系统，在严格接入约束和非抢占式传输条件下，提出了一种基于拉格朗日指数的启发式算法来最小化加权平均信息年龄（AoI）成本，数值结果显示其性能优于现有策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代传感系统生成的更新数据具有异构性（从小的状态包到大的数据对象不等），且在严格介质访问约束和非抢占式传输下，决策过程变得具有动作依赖性和随机性，现有策略难以有效处理此类场景下的信息新鲜度优化问题。

Method: 将该问题建模为具有半马尔可夫决策过程（SMDP）动态的无休止多臂赌博机（RMAB）问题，并开发了一种基于拉格朗日指数的启发式算法来最小化加权平均AoI成本；利用该启发式算法的结构特性实现了高效的指数计算。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的策略在加权AoI设置下，相较于现有的非抢占式调度策略具有一致的性能增益。

Conclusion: 该工作为异构感知系统提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案，能够显著提升信息新鲜度感知系统的性能。

Abstract: Modern sensing systems generate heterogeneous updates ranging from small status packets to large data objects. We study a single-hop wireless uplink network where sensors generate updates at will, each consisting of a sensor dependent number of packets. Under a strict medium-access constraint and non-preemptive (no-switching) transmissions, decision stages become action-dependent and stochastic. We formulate the problem as a restless multi-armed bandit (RMAB) with semi-Markov decision process (SMDP) dynamics and develop a Lagrange index based heuristic for minimizing weighted average AoI cost. For the weighted AoI setting, we utilize the structural properties of the heuristic to enable efficient index computation. Numerical results demonstrate consistent performance gains over existing non-preemptive scheduling policies, providing a practical solution for heterogeneous freshness-aware systems.

</details>


### [190] [Tabu Search for Tactical Wireless Network Design in Challenging Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18318)
*Wisssem Ahmed Zaid,Alain Hertz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对战术无线网络设计问题，提出了两种结合启发式子程序的禁忌搜索算法，能高效生成高质量网络设计方案，在信号强度、覆盖范围和干扰控制方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 战术无线网络在自然灾害等基础设施损坏场景中至关重要，但现有设计方法难以满足工业界特定约束条件下的优化需求（信号强度、覆盖范围、干扰控制），需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出两种禁忌搜索(tabu search)算法，通过集成多种启发式子程序来处理特定约束条件，实现网络设计的高效生成。

Result: 在综合测试中，该方法能快速有效地生成网络设计，相比现有方法展现出显著优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的禁忌搜索算法能有效解决战术无线网络设计问题，为工业界提供了快速、高质量的网络规划工具。

Abstract: Tactical wireless networks play a vital role in ensuring reliable connectivity in scenarios where conventional telecommunications infrastructure is unavailable or damaged, such as areas impacted by natural disasters. These networks are designed to operate efficiently in difficult and unpredictable environments by adapting to the unique characteristics of the terrain. This research addresses a real-world challenge from the communications industry: designing tactical wireless networks that meet the specific constraints defined by our industrial partner, with the goal of optimizing signal strength and coverage while minimizing interference. To this end, we propose two tabu search algorithms that incorporate several heuristic subroutines, enabling the efficient generation of high-quality network designs. Results from synthetic tests demonstrate that our approach produces networks rapidly and effectively, offering significant improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [191] [Sub-additive service curves in the Network Calculus analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18370)
*Anne Bouillard*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文反驳了Hamscher等人关于网络演算中允许负值函数的观点，证明传统非负假设在所有情况下均可进行分析，并指出了其在复杂反馈控制系统分析中的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络演算要求函数非负以表示数据量，但Hamscher等人提出允许负值函数可解决某些（特别是含反馈控制的）难题。本文旨在反驳这一观点，证明传统方法依然可行，并纠正其分析中的错误。

Method: 一是通过对次加性函数进行详细分析，证明在Hamscher等人提及的所有案例中，传统的非负函数假设下的分析依然可行；二是对其关于复杂反馈控制系统的第二篇论文进行分析，指出其使用负值函数的分析方法存在稳定性问题且理论基础不牢靠。

Result: 证明了使用常规非负假设可以对所有提及案例进行有效分析；发现Hamscher等人关于复杂反馈控制系统的分析是不合理的，存在稳定性问题；在可能的情况下，给出了使用常规假设的修正分析方案。

Conclusion: 在网络演算中引入负值函数是不必要的，且可能导致不合理的分析。传统的非负假设配合对次加性函数的深入分析足以处理包括反馈控制系统在内的复杂情况，确保了分析的正确性与稳定性。

Abstract: Network Calculus is a theoretical model that aims at providing upper bounds of worst-case performance (such as delay or buffer occupancy). This is a mathematical framework that handles both network modeling and network analysis. As such it has requirements regarding the space of functions needed for a safe analysis. Namely, the functions need to be non-negative, as they model a quantity of data. This results in some pitfall for the analysis, where hypothesis matter.
  A recent paper by Hamscher et al. states that allowing functions with negative values can also lead to a valid analysis, in cases that would be untractable with the non-negative assumption results, especially when feedback control is present in the system.
  In this paper, we show that, on the contrary, a more conventional analysis is possible in all the mentioned cases. The key is a detailed analysis of sub-additive functions. Second, we show that the analysis of complex feedback control systems, presented by Hamscher et al. in a second paper that uses functions with negative values, is unsound and has stability issues. We give a corrected analysis, when possible, with conventional hypotheses.

</details>


### [192] [Spectrum Configuration Framework for Throughput Maximization in Open Systems with Roll-Off-Based QoT Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18374)
*Peyman Pahlevanzadeh,Venkata Virajit Garbhapu,Agastya Raj,Dmitrii Briantcev,Dan Kilper,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对开放解聚光系统的频谱配置框架，通过联合优化收发器参数和WSS带宽，在固定频谱约束下最大化吞吐量并保证传输质量余量，实验验证了其在城域测试平台上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放和解聚的光网络架构中，如何在固定的频谱分配约束下实现频谱资源的高效利用，同时保证传输质量（QoT）余量并最大化吞吐量，是一个关键挑战。现有的配置方法往往难以在有限的收发器资源下实现最优的吞吐量与余量权衡。

Method: 提出一个频谱配置框架，联合优化多个收发器参数，包括调制格式、符号率、脉冲成型滚降因子和波长选择开关（WSS）带宽。首先通过实验评估滚降因子优化在级联WSS滤波影响下的效果；随后在“光学频谱即服务”（OSaaS）场景下，应用基于背包问题的优化算法，在固定频谱宽度和有限的收发器资源约束下选择最大化总吞吐量的服务配置。

Result: 实验结果表明，滚降因子优化在单通道和多通道传输中均能带来可测量的QoT增益。在城域级开放测试平台上的验证显示，该方法能够实现高效的频谱利用率，并在固定频谱约束下有效实现吞吐量与传输余量之间的自适应权衡。

Conclusion: 该频谱配置框架能够有效提升开放解聚光系统的频谱利用效率，在不牺牲传输质量余量的前提下最大化吞吐量，为光频谱即服务场景提供了一种实用的优化解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a spectrum-configuration framework for open and disaggregated optical systems that maximizes throughput while guaranteeing the quality of transmission (QoT) margins. The framework jointly optimizes transceiver parameters, including modulation format, symbol rate, pulse-shaping roll-off factor, and wavelength-selective switch (WSS) bandwidth, under fixed spectral allocation constraints. The impact of roll-off factor optimization is first experimentally evaluated in the presence of cascaded WSS filtering, demonstrating measurable QoT gains for both single- and multi-channel transmission. Building on these observations, a knapsack-based optimization is applied in the context of Optical Spectrum as a Service (OSaaS) to select service configurations that maximize aggregate throughput within a fixed spectrum width and limited transceiver resources. Experimental validation on a metro-scale open testbed confirms the effectiveness of the proposed approach in achieving efficient spectrum utilization and adaptive throughput-margin trade-offs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [When Spike Sparsity Does Not Translate to Deployed Cost: VS-WNO on Jetson Orin Nano](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17040)
*Jason Yoo,Shailesh Garg,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，尽管脉冲神经算子在算法层面具有稀疏性优势，但在Jetson Orin Nano边缘GPU上的实际部署中，稀疏性并未转化为延迟和能耗的降低，反而比密集算子表现更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经算子因其事件驱动的特性被认为在神经形态边缘计算中具有降低延迟和能耗的潜力，但这种优势是否能在商品化边缘GPU软件栈上保持尚不明确，需要实验验证。

Method: 在Jetson Orin Nano 8GB上部署五个预训练的变分脉冲小波神经算子(VS-WNO)和五个匹配的密集小波神经算子(WNO)检查点，在Darcy矩形基准测试上进行对比实验，使用Nsight Systems进行性能分析。

Result: VS-WNO在参考路径上表现出显著稀疏性，脉冲率从第一层的54.26%降至第四层的18.15%。但在部署路径上，VS-WNO的延迟(59.6ms)和能耗(228.0mJ)均高于密集WNO(53.2ms, 180.7mJ)，且误差略高(1.81% vs 1.77%)。性能分析显示CUDA运行时仍然是密集模式而非稀疏感知，cudaLaunchKernel占81.6%的CUDA API时间，密集卷积核占53.8%的GPU核时间。

Conclusion: 在Jetson级GPU栈上，脉冲稀疏性虽然可测量但未能降低部署成本，因为运行时系统并未随着脉冲活动减少而抑制密集计算工作。这表明脉冲神经网络的实际优势需要硬件-软件协同优化才能真正实现。

Abstract: Spiking neural operators are appealing for neuromorphic edge computing because event-driven substrates can, in principle, translate sparse activity into lower latency and energy. Whether that advantage survives deployment on commodity edge-GPU software stacks, however, remains unclear. We study this question on a Jetson Orin Nano 8 GB using five pretrained variable-spiking wavelet neural operator (VS-WNO) checkpoints and five matched dense wavelet neural operator (WNO) checkpoints on the Darcy rectangular benchmark. On a reference-aligned path, VS-WNO exhibits substantial algorithmic sparsity, with mean spike rates decreasing from 54.26% at the first spiking layer to 18.15% at the fourth. On a deployment-style request path, however, this sparsity does not reduce deployed cost: VS-WNO reaches 59.6 ms latency and 228.0 mJ dynamic energy per inference, whereas dense WNO reaches 53.2 ms and 180.7 mJ, while also achieving slightly lower reference-path error (1.77% versus 1.81%). Nsight Systems indicates that the request path remains launch-dominated and dense rather than sparsity-aware: for VS-WNO, cudaLaunchKernel accounts for 81.6% of CUDA API time within the latency window, and dense convolution kernels account for 53.8% of GPU kernel time; dense WNO shows the same pattern. On this Jetson-class GPU stack, spike sparsity is measurable but does not reduce deployed cost because the runtime does not suppress dense work as spike activity decreases.

</details>


### [194] [M100: An Orchestrated Dataflow Architecture Powering General AI Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17862)
*Yan Xie,Changkui Mao,Changsong Wu,Chao Lu,Chao Suo,Cheng Qian,Chun Yang,Danyang Zhu,Hengchang Xiong,Hongzhan Lu,Hongzhen Liu,Jiafu Liu,Jie Chen,Jie Dai,Junfeng Tang,Kai Liu,Kun Li,Lipeng Ge,Meng Sun,Min Luo,Peng Chen,Peng Wang,Shaodong Yang,Shibin Tang,Shibo Chen,Weikang Zhang,Xiao Ling,Xiaobo Du,Xin Wu,Yang Liu,Yi Jiang,Yihua Jin,Yin Huang,Yuli Zhang,Zhen Yuan,Zhiyuan Man,Zhongxiao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 理想汽车推出M100架构，采用数据流并行架构和软硬件协同设计，通过编译器管理数据流消除缓存，在自动驾驶和LLM推理任务中性能优于GPGPU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPGPU架构在AI计算中效率和成本效益不足，而领域专用架构难以扩展到更广泛的应用或适应快速发展的AI领域，缺乏面向自动驾驶、LLM和智能人机交互的高效通用AI计算架构。

Method: 采用数据流并行架构，通过编译器-架构协同设计优化计算和数据在时空上的流动；大幅消除缓存机制，采用编译器和运行时管理的数据流在计算单元与片上/片外存储器间传输；选择张量作为基本数据元素，在编译器、固件和硬件层面确定合适的操作粒度。

Result: M100在包括UniAD（自动驾驶）和LLaMA（大语言模型）等多种推理应用中展现出通用AI计算能力；基准测试显示在自动驾驶应用中性能优于GPGPU架构，具有更高的利用率。

Conclusion: M100代表了未来通用AI计算的一个有前景的方向，证明了数据流架构结合软硬件协同设计能够实现高效、低成本且具有良好扩展性的AI推理计算。

Abstract: As deep learning-based AI technologies gain momentum, the demand for general-purpose AI computing architectures continues to grow. While GPGPU-based architectures offer versatility for diverse AI workloads, they often fall short in efficiency and cost-effectiveness. Various Domain-Specific Architectures (DSAs) excel at particular AI tasks but struggle to extend across broader applications or adapt to the rapidly evolving AI landscape. M100 is Li Auto's response: a performant, cost-effective architecture for AI inference in Autonomous Driving (AD), Large Language Models (LLMs), and intelligent human interactions, domains crucial to today's most competitive automobile platforms. M100 employs a dataflow parallel architecture, where compiler-architecture co-design orchestrates not only computation but, more critically, data movement across time and space. Leveraging dataflow computing efficiency, our hardware-software co-design improves system performance while reducing hardware complexity and cost. M100 largely eliminates caching: tensor computations are driven by compiler- and runtime-managed data streams flowing between computing elements and on/off-chip memories, yielding greater efficiency and scalability than cache-based systems. Another key principle was selecting the right operational granularity for scheduling, issuing, and execution across compiler, firmware, and hardware. Recognizing commonalities in AI workloads, we chose the tensor as the fundamental data element. M100 demonstrates general AI computing capability across diverse inference applications, including UniAD (for AD) and LLaMA (for LLMs). Benchmarks show M100 outperforms GPGPU architectures in AD applications with higher utilization, representing a promising direction for future general AI computing.

</details>


### [195] [BASIS: Balanced Activation Sketching with Invariant Scalars for "Ghost Backpropagation"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16324)
*Vladimer Khasia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BASIS算法，通过平衡哈希和不变标量机制，将激活内存从O(L*BN)降至O(L*RN)，在GPT训练中实现与精确反向传播相当甚至更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确反向传播的激活内存随网络深度、序列长度和特征维度线性增长，形成O(L*BN)的空间瓶颈，严重制约深度神经网络的扩展；现有的随机自动微分方法虽试图缓解但存在灾难性方差问题。

Method: 提出BASIS（平衡激活草图与不变标量）算法：1）传播精确误差信号(dX)以保持完美梯度流；2）使用大幅压缩的秩-R张量计算权重更新(dW)；3）引入平衡哈希机制严格消除非对角碰撞方差；4）设计不变标量机制，通过有原则的偏差-方差权衡确定性保持空间几何的精确连续能量范数。

Result: 在GPT架构上训练50,000步验证：R=32时BASIS验证损失为6.575，优于精确反向传播的6.616，起到隐式正则化作用；即使在极端空间压缩(R=1)下，稳定的幅度轨迹使模型能平滑收敛，证明估计器的极端鲁棒性；理论证明激活内存降至O(L*RN)，大幅减少反向传播矩阵乘法开销。

Conclusion: BASIS成功解耦激活内存与批量和序列维度的依赖，在大幅压缩激活内存的同时保持甚至提升模型性能，为深度神经网络的高效训练提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: The activation memory required for exact backpropagation scales linearly with network depth, context length, and feature dimensionality, forming an O(L * BN ) spatial bottleneck (where B is the sequence-batch cardinality and N is the feature dimension). This constraint historically throttles the scaling of deep neural networks. While randomized automatic differentiation attempts to mitigate this, it historically suffers from catastrophic variance. In this paper, we introduce BASIS (Balanced Activation Sketching with Invariant Scalars), an efficient backpropagation algorithm that fully decouples activation memory from the batch and sequence dimensions. BASIS propagates the exact error signal (dX) to preserve flawless gradient flow, but computes the weight updates (dW) using massively compressed rank-R tensors. To solve the foundational instability of sketched gradients, we propose two novel mechanisms: Balanced Hashing, which strictly eliminates off-diagonal collision variance, and Invariant Scalars, a principled bias-variance tradeoff that deterministically preserves the exact continuous energy norm of the spatial geometry. Theoretically, BASIS reduces activation memory to O(L * RN ) and heavily decreases the backward pass matrix-multiplication footprint. Empirically, training a GPT architecture for 50,000 steps validates our theoretical guarantees: at R = 32, BASIS achieves parity with (and marginally outperforms) exact backpropagation validation loss (6.575 vs. 6.616), acting as an implicit regularizer. Remarkably, the stabilized magnitude trajectory allows the model to converge smoothly even under extreme spatial compression (R = 1), proving the extreme robustness of the estimator. The code is available at https://github.com/VladimerKhasia/basis

</details>


### [196] [UniMamba: A Unified Spatial-Temporal Modeling Framework with State-Space and Attention Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16325)
*Xingsheng Chen,Xianpei Mu,Deyu Yi,Yilin Yuan,Xingwei He,Bo Gao,Regina Zhang,Pietro Lio,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniMamba，一个结合状态空间模型与注意力机制的统一时空预测框架，在多元时间序列预测任务中实现了精度与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测面临复杂的时序依赖和跨变量交互挑战。现有的Transformer方法虽能捕捉时序相关性，但存在二次计算成本过高的问题；而Mamba等状态空间模型虽能高效建模长上下文，却缺乏显式的时序模式识别能力。

Method: 提出了UniMamba框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 增强的Mamba变量通道编码层，结合FFT-拉普拉斯变换和TCN捕捉全局时序依赖；2) 时空注意力层，联合建模变量间相关性和时序演变；3) 前馈时序动态层，融合连续和离散上下文以实现精准预测。

Result: 在八个公共基准数据集上的综合实验表明，UniMamba在预测精度和计算效率上均持续优于现有的最先进预测模型。

Conclusion: UniMamba成功地将高效的状态空间动态与基于注意力的依赖学习相结合，为长序列多元时间序列预测提供了一个可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting is fundamental to numerous domains such as energy, finance, and environmental monitoring, where complex temporal dependencies and cross-variable interactions pose enduring challenges. Existing Transformer-based methods capture temporal correlations through attention mechanisms but suffer from quadratic computational cost, while state-space models like Mamba achieve efficient long-context modeling yet lack explicit temporal pattern recognition. Therefore we introduce UniMamba, a unified spatial-temporal forecasting framework that integrates efficient state-space dynamics with attention-based dependency learning. UniMamba employs a Mamba Variate-Channel Encoding Layer enhanced with FFT-Laplace Transform and TCN to capture global temporal dependencies, and a Spatial Temporal Attention Layer to jointly model inter-variate correlations and temporal evolution. A Feedforward Temporal Dynamics Layer further fuses continuous and discrete contexts for accurate forecasting. Comprehensive experiments on eight public benchmark datasets demonstrate that UniMamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting models in both forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency, establishing a scalable and robust solution for long-sequence multivariate time-series prediction.

</details>


### [197] [AutoPPA: Automated Circuit PPA Optimization via Contrastive Code-based Rule Library Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18445)
*Chongxiao Li,Pengwei Jin,Di Huang,Guangrun Sun,Husheng Han,Jianan Mu,Xinyao Zheng,Jiaguo Zhu,Shuyi Xing,Hanjun Wei,Tianyun Ma,Shuyao Cheng,Rui Zhang,Ying Wang,Zidong Du,Qi Guo,Xing Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AutoPPA框架，通过自动生成优化规则来实现RTL设计中的PPA（性能、功耗、面积）优化，采用探索-评估-归纳（E²I）工作流从代码对中提取规则，优于人工优化和现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的PPA优化方法效率不足：基于反馈的方法缺乏先验知识，基于知识的方法严重依赖人工总结的优化规则。需要一种能够自动生成优化规则的高效框架。

Method: 提出AutoPPA框架，核心是自动生成优化规则。采用探索-评估-归纳（E²I）工作流，从多样化的生成代码对中对比和抽象规则，而非依赖人工定义的先验知识。同时采用自适应多步搜索框架，针对给定电路选择最有效的规则以提高泛化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，AutoPPA在PPA优化任务上优于人工优化以及当前最先进的方法SymRTLO和RTLRewriter。

Conclusion: 通过自动生成优化规则而非依赖人工规则，AutoPPA实现了更高效的PPA优化，为RTL设计自动化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Performance, power, and area (PPA) optimization is a fundamental task in RTL design, requiring a precise understanding of circuit functionality and the relationship between circuit structures and PPA metrics. Recent studies attempt to automate this process using LLMs, but neither feedback-based nor knowledge-based methods are efficient enough, as they either design without any prior knowledge or rely heavily on human-summarized optimization rules.
  In this paper, we propose AutoPPA, a fully automated PPA optimization framework. The key idea is to automatically generate optimization rules that enhance the search for optimal solutions. To do this, AutoPPA employs an Explore-Evaluate-Induce ($E^2I$) workflow that contrasts and abstracts rules from diverse generated code pairs rather than manually defined prior knowledge, yielding better optimization patterns. To make the abstracted rules more generalizable, AutoPPA employs an adaptive multi-step search framework that adopts the most effective rules for a given circuit. Experiments show that AutoPPA outperforms both the manual optimization and the state-of-the-art methods SymRTLO and RTLRewriter.

</details>


### [198] [Annotation Entropy Predicts Per-Example Learning Dynamics in LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16332)
*Brady Steele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在6个模型上发现LoRA微调存在对“争议样本”的遗忘现象，即标注分歧越大的样本在训练中损失越高，且全参数微调无此现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究参数高效微调（如LoRA）与全参数微调在学习行为上的差异，特别是模型对标注不一致样本的处理方式。

Method: 利用ChaosNLI数据集计算每个样本的标注熵，并在SNLI和MNLI数据集上计算样本损失曲线下面积（AULC），通过Spearman相关系数分析两者相关性；在6个模型（4个编码器、2个解码器）和25种条件下进行测试，并辅以偏相关控制和噪声注入实验。

Result: LoRA微调下标注熵与AULC在所有25种条件下均呈正相关（ρ=0.06-0.43），即标注分歧越大的样本损失越高；解码器模型相关性更强；该现象在全参数微调中基本不存在，且通过控制实验和数据集复现得到验证。

Conclusion: LoRA微调会独特地“遗忘”具有高标注歧义的争议样本，揭示了其与全参数微调在学习机制上的本质区别，这对理解参数高效微调的局限性和鲁棒性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We find that LoRA fine-tuning exhibits un-learning on contested examples: items with high annotator disagreement show increasing loss during training, a qualitatively distinct pattern largely absent under full fine-tuning and consistent across all six models tested (four encoder, two decoder-only). This discovery emerges from correlating annotation entropy, computed from ChaosNLI's 100 labels per example, with per-example area under the loss curve (AULC) on SNLI and MNLI. The correlation is positive in all 25 conditions tested (Spearman $ρ= 0.06$-$0.43$), with decoder-only models showing stronger correlations than encoders at matched LoRA rank. The effect survives partial-correlation controls and replicates across seeds and datasets. A preliminary noise-injection experiment is consistent with these findings.

</details>


### [199] [A Discordance-Aware Multimodal Framework with Multi-Agent Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16333)
*Pegah Ahadian,Mingrui Yang,Sixu Chen,Xiaojuan Li,Qiang Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种感知不一致性的多模态机器学习框架，结合预测模型与多智能体推理系统，用于解决膝骨关节炎中影像结构损伤与患者疼痛症状不匹配的临床难题，并生成个性化管理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 膝骨关节炎患者的影像学结构损伤与疼痛症状之间常存在不一致性（discordance），这种不匹配使得临床解释和患者分层变得复杂，且现有的临床决策支持系统未能充分建模这一现象。

Method: 基于FNIH骨关节炎生物标志物联盟的基线数据，构建多模态预测模型（集成CatBoost表格模型、MRI图像嵌入、X射线嵌入），预测关节间隙丢失进展和疼痛进展；通过基于残差的模型根据结构特征估算预期疼痛，计算疼痛-结构不一致评分；利用多智能体推理层解读信号，分配临床可解释的OA表型并生成表型特异性管理建议。

Result: 成功构建了一个能够同时预测结构进展与疼痛进展的多模态集成模型，并实现了疼痛-结构不一致性的量化评分，进而通过多智能体系统输出临床可解释的OA表型分类及针对性管理建议。

Conclusion: 该框架通过显式建模结构-症状不一致性并引入多智能体推理，为膝骨关节炎的精准分型和个性化管理提供了新的临床决策支持工具，有望改善患者分层和个体化治疗策略。

Abstract: Knee osteoarthritis frequently exhibits discordance between structural damage observed in imaging and patient-reported symptoms such as pain. This mismatch complicates clinical interpretation and patient stratification and remains insufficiently modeled in existing decision support systems. We propose a discordance aware multimodal framework that combines machine learning prediction models with a tool grounded multi agent reasoning system. Using baseline data from the FNIH Osteoarthritis Biomarkers Consortium, we trained multimodal models to predict two progression tasks, joint space loss only progression versus non progression, and pain only progression versus non progression. The predictive system integrates three modality specific experts: a CatBoost tabular model using demographic, radiographic, MRI-derived scalar, and biomarker features; MRI image embeddings extracted using a ResNet18 backbone; and Xray embeddings derived from the same architecture. Expert predictions are fused using a stacking ensemble. Residual based models estimate expected pain from structural features, enabling the computation of a pain structure discordance score between observed and expected symptoms. A multi-agent reasoning layer interprets these signals to assign clinically interpretable OA phenotypes and generate phenotype specific management recommendations.

</details>


### [200] [Preventing overfitting in deep learning using differential privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16334)
*Alizishaan Anwar Hussein Khatri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用差分隐私方法来提升深度神经网络的泛化能力，以解决有限数据场景下的过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络虽在各领域表现优异，但强大的拟合能力使其容易学习训练集中的噪声，导致过拟合和泛化能力差。在实际应用中，分析师常面临数据有限但需模型泛化至未见数据的挑战。

Method: 采用基于差分隐私的方法来改善深度神经网络的泛化性能。

Result: （摘要未明确给出具体实验结果，主要阐述了研究思路与方法。）

Conclusion: 差分隐私方法有望成为提升深度神经网络泛化能力的有效途径。

Abstract: The use of Deep Neural Network based systems in the real world is growing. They have achieved state-of-the-art performance on many image, speech and text datasets. They have been shown to be powerful systems that are capable of learning detailed relationships and abstractions from the data. This is a double-edged sword which makes such systems vulnerable to learning the noise in the training set, thereby negatively impacting performance. This is also known as the problem of \emph{overfitting} or \emph{poor generalization}. In a practical setting, analysts typically have limited data to build models that must generalize to unseen data. In this work, we explore the use of a differential-privacy based approach to improve generalization in Deep Neural Networks.

</details>


### [201] [Beyond Verifiable Rewards: Rubric-Based GRM for Reinforced Fine-Tuning SWE Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16335)
*Jiawei Huang,Qingping Yang,Renjie Zheng,Jiaze Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于评分标准的生成式奖励模型（GRM），为软件工程任务中的大模型智能体提供细粒度中间反馈，相比仅使用终端二元奖励，显著提升了强化微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型智能体在软件工程任务中的端到端微调依赖可验证的终端奖励（如单元测试是否通过），这种二元信号仅反映最终结果正确性，无法指导多步交互中的中间行为，限制了整体解决过程质量的提升。

Method: 引入基于评分标准的生成式奖励模型（GRM），通过人工设计的评分标准指明鼓励或抑制特定行为模式的标准，利用该反馈通过轨迹过滤收集高质量训练数据，并应用于强化微调（RFT）。

Result: 在软件工程任务的强化微调中，该方法优于仅使用终端分数的拒绝采样方法，能更有效地抑制不良行为模式并促进有益模式，最终提升了测试准确率。

Conclusion: 通过提供更丰富的中间学习信号，基于评分标准的GRM能够有效引导大模型智能体的行为优化，为软件工程任务中的强化学习训练提供了更精细的奖励机制。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in Large Language Model (LLM) Agents for Software Engineering (SWE) tasks, end-to-end fine-tuning typically relies on verifiable terminal rewards such as whether all unit tests pass. While these binary signals reflect whether the final solution is correct, they provide little guidance for shaping intermediate behaviors during multi-step interactions, thereby limiting improvements in the overall quality of the resolution process. To address this, we introduce a rubric-based Generative Reward Model (GRM) that provides richer learning signals. The GRM is equipped with human-designed rubrics that indicate criteria for encouraging or discouraging specific behavioral patterns, and we leverage this feedback for high-quality training data collection via trajectory filtration. When used for Reinforced Fine-Tuning (RFT) on SWE Tasks, our approach outperforms terminal-score-only rejection sampling: it more effectively suppresses undesirable patterns while promoting beneficial ones, as confirmed by case analyses, and it ultimately improves final test accuracy.

</details>


### [202] [SaFeR-Steer: Evolving Multi-Turn MLLMs via Synthetic Bootstrapping and Feedback Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16358)
*Haolong Hu,Hanyu Li,Tiancheng He,Huahui Yi,An Zhang,Qiankun Li,Kun Wang,Yang Liu,Zhigang Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SaFeR-Steer多轮对齐框架和TCSR指标，发布STEER数据集，显著提升MLLMs在单轮和多轮场景下的安全性，并使失效转向更晚轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）日益部署于多轮交互场景，攻击者可通过演进的视觉-文本历史逐步升级不安全意图，并利用长上下文中的安全性能衰减。然而，当前的安全对齐仍以单轮数据和固定模板对话为主，导致训练与实际部署之间存在显著差距。

Method: 提出SaFeR-Steer渐进式多轮对齐框架，结合分阶段合成自举与循环中导师GRPO方法，在自适应、基于策略的攻击下训练单一学生模型；引入TCSR（Trajectory-Constrained Safety Reward），利用轨迹最小/平均安全性将后期轮次的失败传播至更早轮次。发布STEER数据集，包含STEER-SFT（12,934对话）、STEER-RL（2,000对话）和STEER-Bench（3,227对话），跨越2~10轮。

Result: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B进行实验：单轮基准上，3B模型的安全性/有用性从48.30/45.86提升至81.84/70.77，7B模型从56.21/60.32提升至87.89/77.40；多轮基准上，3B模型从12.55/27.13提升至55.58/70.27，7B模型从24.66/46.48提升至64.89/72.35。该方法将失效转移至更晚轮次，并产生超越单纯参数规模扩展的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SaFeR-Steer框架有效弥合了多轮部署场景与单轮训练数据之间的对齐差距，显著提升了模型在多轮交互中的安全性与有用性，同时为多模态安全研究提供了新的数据集资源和评估基准。

Abstract: MLLMs are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings, where attackers can escalate unsafe intent through the evolving visual-text history and exploit long-context safety decay. Yet safety alignment is still dominated by single-turn data and fixed-template dialogues, leaving a mismatch between training and deployment.To bridge this gap, we propose SaFeR-Steer, a progressive multi-turn alignment framework that combines staged synthetic bootstrapping with tutor-in-the-loop GRPO to train a single student under adaptive, on-policy attacks. We also introduce TCSR, which uses trajectory minimum/average safety to propagate late-turn failures to earlier turns.I. Dataset. We release STEER, a multi-turn multimodal safety dataset with STEER-SFT (12,934), STEER-RL (2,000), and STEER-Bench (3,227) dialogues spanning 2~10 turns.II. Experiment. Starting from Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B, SaFeR-Steer substantially improves Safety/Helpfulness on both single-turn (48.30/45.86 -> 81.84/70.77 for 3B; 56.21/60.32 -> 87.89/77.40 for 7B) and multi-turn benchmarks (12.55/27.13 -> 55.58/70.27 for 3B; 24.66/46.48 -> 64.89/72.35 for 7B), shifting failures to later turns and yielding robustness beyond scaling alone.Codes are available at https://github.com/Ed-Bg/SaFeR-Steer

</details>


### [203] [SetFlow: Generating Structured Sets of Representations for Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16362)
*Nikola Jovišić,Milica Škipina,Vanja Švenda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SetFlow，一种基于流匹配和Set Transformer的生成架构，能够在表示空间直接对多示例学习(MIL)中的整个包进行建模并生成新样本，有效解决了数据稀缺问题，在乳腺X光检查基准上取得了与真实数据相当的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据稀缺和弱监督问题持续限制机器学习模型在乳腺X光检查等实际应用中的性能。虽然基础模型提供了强大的语义表示，但针对MIL数据的有效增强方法仍然有限，现有方法仅在实例层面操作，无法捕获包内依赖关系。

Method: 提出SetFlow生成架构，结合流匹配范式与Set Transformer设计，在表示空间直接对整个MIL包（即集合）进行建模。该方法能处理排列不变输入，同时捕获包内实例间的交互，并通过类别标签和输入尺度进行条件控制，生成语义一致的表示集合。

Result: 在大规模乳腺X光检查基准上，使用最先进的MIL-PF分类管道进行评估。生成的样本与原始数据分布高度匹配，用于数据增强时能提升下游性能。仅使用合成数据训练也能获得具有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: SetFlow证明了表示空间生成建模在数据稀缺和隐私敏感任务中的有效性，为MIL数据增强提供了新思路，生成质量足够高，可替代真实数据用于模型训练。

Abstract: Data scarcity and weak supervision continue to limit the performance of machine learning models in many real-world applications, such as mammography, where Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) often offers the best formulation. While recent foundation models provide strong semantic representations out of the box, effective augmentation of such representations of MIL data remains limited, as existing methods operate at the instance level and fail to capture intra-bag dependencies. In this work, we introduce SetFlow, a generative architecture that models entire MIL bags (i.e., sets) directly in the representation space. Our approach leverages the flow matching paradigm combined with a Set Transformer-inspired design, enabling it to handle permutation-invariant inputs while capturing interactions between instances within each bag. The model is conditioned on both class labels and input scale, allowing it to generate coherent and semantically consistent sets of representations. We evaluate SetFlow on a large-scale mammography benchmark using a state-of-the-art MIL-PF classification pipeline. The generated samples are shown to closely match the original data distribution and even improve downstream performance when used for augmentation. Furthermore, training on synthetic data alone shows competitive results, demonstrating the effectiveness of representation-space generative modeling for data-scarce and privacy-sensitive tasks.

</details>


### [204] [CAPO: Counterfactual Credit Assignment in Sequential Cooperative Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17693)
*Shripad Deshmukh,Jayakumar Subramanian,Raghavendra Addanki,Nikos Vlassis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeqAU（序贯贵族效用）和CAPO（反事实优势策略优化）算法，解决了序贯合作团队中个体贡献难以评估的问题，实现了无评论家的策略梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体按固定顺序行动且共享团队奖励的合作团队中，评估每个智能体的贡献非常困难，特别是在智能体逐个更新时，早期收集的数据不再反映新策略。

Method: 提出了序贯贵族效用（SeqAU），这是最大化每个智能体动作个体可学习性的唯一信号，并由此推导出CAPO算法。CAPO从团队奖励中拟合个体奖励分解，通过闭式计算个体优势，仅需少量策略前向传播，无需额外环境交互。

Result: 提供了分析性的偏差和方差界限，在受控序贯老虎机实验中验证了理论结果，CAPO相对于标准基线的优势随团队规模增大而增长。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性，多LLM流水线是自然的部署目标，为序贯合作场景下的个体信用分配问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In cooperative teams where agents act in a fixed order and share a single team reward, it is hard to know how much each agent contributed, and harder still when agents are updated one at a time because data collected earlier no longer reflects the new policies. We introduce the Sequential Aristocrat Utility (SeqAU), the unique per-agent learning signal that maximizes the individual learnability of each agent's action, extending the classical framework of Wolpert and Tumer (2002) to this sequential setting. From SeqAU we derive CAPO (Counterfactual Advantage Policy Optimization), a critic-free policy-gradient algorithm. CAPO fits a per-agent reward decomposition from group rewards and computes the per-agent advantage in closed form plus a handful of forward passes through the current policy, requiring no extra environment calls beyond the initial batch. We give analytic bias and variance bounds and validate them on a controlled sequential bandit, where CAPO's advantage over standard baselines grows with the team size. The framework is general; multi-LLM pipelines are a natural deployment target.

</details>


### [205] [Matched-Learning-Rate Analysis of Attention Drift and Transfer Retention in Fine-Tuned CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16410)
*Ruize Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在匹配学习率的公平比较下，LoRA在保持CLIP零样本迁移能力方面显著优于全量微调（平均零样本准确率45.47% vs 9.91%），揭示了以往因学习率设定差异导致的比较偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP适应方法比较中，全量微调（Full FT）与LoRA常采用不同的学习率惯例，导致比较结果存在混淆。作者旨在通过匹配学习率的对照实验，探究适应方法与优化尺度如何共同影响CLIP的注意力漂移和迁移保持能力。

Method: 在CLIP ViT-B/32模型上，针对EuroSAT和Oxford-IIIT Pets数据集，设计严格的匹配学习率对照实验。实验包含80次运行，跨越四个共享学习率（10^{-6}、5×10^{-6}、10^{-5}、5×10^{-5}）和五个随机种子，评估注意力漂移指标、最佳验证准确率和CIFAR-100零样本准确率。

Result: 学习率强烈调节结构变化：Full FT在高学习率下呈现明显的熵收缩，而LoRA在整个网格上保持熵正值。在匹配学习率下，LoRA保留的零样本迁移能力远超Full FT（EuroSAT上45.13% vs 11.28%，Pets上58.01% vs 8.54%）。低学习率下LoRA存在域内欠拟合的区间效应。注意力漂移作为描述性诊断有效，但难以作为迁移行为的因果解释。

Conclusion: 匹配学习率评估显著改变了对Full FT与LoRA性能差异的理解，LoRA在保持迁移能力方面优势明显，而注意力漂移更适合作为表征保持的描述性诊断工具而非因果解释。

Abstract: CLIP adaptation can improve in-domain accuracy while degrading out-of-domain transfer, but comparisons between Full Fine-Tuning (Full FT) and LoRA are often confounded by different learning-rate conventions. We study how adaptation method and optimization scale jointly shape attention drift and transfer retention in CLIP using a controlled matched-learning-rate comparison of Full FT and LoRA. The completed matrix contains 80 runs on CLIP ViT-B/32 across EuroSAT and Oxford-IIIT Pets, spanning four shared learning rates ($10^{-6}$, $5{\times}10^{-6}$, $10^{-5}$, $5{\times}10^{-5}$) and five seeds, and evaluates attention-drift metrics, best validation accuracy, and adapter-aware CIFAR-100 zero-shot accuracy. Learning rate strongly modulates structural change: on EuroSAT, Full FT moves from mild entropy broadening at $10^{-6}$ to marked contraction at $5{\times}10^{-5}$, whereas LoRA remains entropy-positive across the full matched grid. At matched learning rates, LoRA preserves substantially more zero-shot transfer than Full FT, averaging $45.13\%$ versus $11.28\%$ CIFAR-100 accuracy on EuroSAT and $58.01\%$ versus $8.54\%$ on Pets. Oxford-IIIT Pets also reveals a regime effect: low-learning-rate LoRA underfits in-domain, so method-only averages can obscure when LoRA becomes competitive. Supporting rollout, patch-to-patch, and CKA analyses are directionally consistent with the controlled matrix. Overall, matched-learning-rate evaluation materially changes the interpretation of Full FT versus LoRA, and attention drift is most useful as a descriptive diagnostic of representation preservation rather than a causal explanation of transfer behavior.

</details>


### [206] [CGCMA: Conditionally-Gated Cross-Modal Attention for Event-Conditioned Asynchronous Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16411)
*Yunxiang Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGCMA模型解决异步多模态对齐问题，在加密货币高频交易场景下，通过分离文本条件接地和延迟感知信任控制，有效融合连续市场状态与延迟网络情报，实现了显著的Sharpe比率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多模态基准假设结构同步性，但现实场景中密集主模态需与稀疏、延迟到达的外部上下文融合，要求模型显式推理信息的新鲜度和可信度。

Method: 提出CGCMA（条件门控跨模态注意力）：文本首先对价格序列进行注意力操作以识别事件相关的市场状态，随后条件门利用模态一致性、网络特征和延迟τ_lag调节残差注入，在外部上下文过时或矛盾时退回单模态预测。

Result: 构建CMI异步评估语料库（27,914条真实新闻样本）；CGCMA在新闻可用时间窗的零成本阈值交易评估中达到最高平均Sharpe比率（+0.449±0.257），增益不能仅由网络标量解释，也无法通过简单的新鲜度启发式方法恢复。

Conclusion: 验证了异步多模态对齐问题的有效性，在该压力测试场景下展示了有前景的异步多模态增益，为更广泛的异步多模态学习问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We study asynchronous alignment, a first-class multimodal learning setting in which a dense primary stream must be fused with sporadic external context whose value depends on when it arrives. Unlike standard multimodal benchmarks that assume structural synchrony, this setting requires models to reason explicitly about freshness and trust. We focus on the event-conditioned case in which continuous market states are paired with delayed web intelligence, and we use high-frequency cryptocurrency markets only as a timestamped, high-noise stress test for this broader problem. We propose CGCMA (Conditionally-Gated Cross-Modal Attention), whose central design principle is to separate text-conditioned grounding from lag-aware trust control. Text first attends over price sequences to identify event-relevant market states, after which a conditional gate uses modality agreement, web features, and lag $τ_{\mathrm{lag}}$ to regulate residual injection and fall back toward unimodal prediction when external context is stale or contradictory. We introduce CMI (Crypto Market Intelligence), an asynchronous evaluation corpus with 27,914 real-news samples pairing high-frequency price sequences with lagged web intelligence. On the current short real-news corpus, CGCMA attains the highest mean downstream Sharpe ratio ($+0.449 \pm 0.257$) among the evaluated baselines under a shared zero-cost threshold-trading evaluation on news-available bars. Additional controls show that the gain is not explained by web scalars alone and is not recovered by simple freshness heuristics. The resulting evidence supports problem validity and a promising asynchronous multimodal gain on this stress-test setting.

</details>


### [207] [Shifting the Gradient: Understanding How Defensive Training Methods Protect Language Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16423)
*Satchel Grant,Victor Gillioz,Jake Ward,Thomas McGrath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了两种大语言模型防御训练方法（正向预防性引导PPS和接种提示IP），发现它们虽然都通过在训练中添加特质诱导对象来防御模型获得特定特质，但通过截然不同的机制实现：PPS能主动减少特质的表达，而IP只能预防但不能消除已有的特质表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正向预防性引导（PPS）和接种提示（IP）这两种防御训练方法都展现出令人惊讶的相似结果：都在训练时向大语言模型添加特质诱导对象，都能防御模型获得该特质。这种成功带来了一个关键问题：它们如何工作？PPS和IP是否在做同样的事情？为了回答这些问题，作者需要对这两种方法进行行为和机制层面的比较分析。

Method: 作者以"邪恶性"作为案例研究特质，从行为和机制两个层面对比PPS和IP。行为层面：测试两种方法是否通过纯粹的联想机制运作，以及它们对已微调表达特质的模型的效果。机制层面：分析激活梯度变化、余弦相似度，以及梯度特征的定性和定量分析。同时研究了两种方法对特质表达数据的下一个token预测损失的影响。

Result: 行为上，PPS和IP都不通过纯粹的联想机制运作；PPS既能防御特质获得又能主动减少已有的特质表达，而IP对之前已微调表达特质的模型无效。机制上，PPS将激活梯度沿PPS向量轴向衰减方向移动，当PPS向量与特质表达轴对齐时可逆转梯度压力；IP表现出与PPS不同的梯度特征，梯度更加弥散，且会降低特质表达数据的预测损失。

Conclusion: PPS和IP通过截然不同的机制实现防御效果：PPS通过梯度方向操控主动抑制特质表达，而IP似乎通过"解释掉"训练数据中的特质表达来工作。研究揭示了每种方法的独特运作机制，并为理解IP的机制图景提出了开放性问题。

Abstract: Defensive training methods such as positive preventative steering (PPS) and inoculation prompting (IP) offer surprising results through seemingly similar processes: both add trait-inducing objects to large language models (LLMs) during training, and both defend the LLM against acquiring the trait. The surprising success of these methods comes with the question: how do they work? Are PPS and IP doing the same thing? We provide behavioral and mechanistic comparisons of these two methods using "evilness" as a case-study trait. Our central finding is that PPS and IP achieve their defensive benefits through distinct mechanisms. Behaviorally, we show that neither PPS nor IP operates through a purely associative mechanism; and PPS can both defend against trait acquisition and actively reduce pre-existing expression, whereas IP is ineffective in models that were previously finetuned to express the trait. This behavioral divergence is reflected mechanistically: PPS shifts the activation gradient towards an attenuating direction along the PPS vector axis. When the PPS vector is aligned with a trait-expressing axis, it can reverse the gradient pressure, reducing rather than increasing activation along that axis. In contrast, IP continues to resist a precise mechanistic account. Direct cosine similarity analyses reveal that IP has a characteristically different gradient signature than PPS, and qualitative analyses reveal IP's gradient to be more diffuse. Furthermore, IP reduces the next-token prediction loss on trait-expressing data where PPS need not, consistent with the notion that IP "explains away" the trait-expression in the training data. Taken together, our analyses reveal distinct mechanisms by which each method operates and highlight open questions about IP's mechanistic picture.

</details>


### [208] [Functional Similarity Metric for Neural Networks: Overcoming Parametric Ambiguity via Activation Region Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16426)
*Kutomanov Hennadii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经元激活区域拓扑的稳定规范表示方法和鲁棒的功能相似性度量，解决了ReLU网络因代数对称性导致的表示歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构随着复杂度的增加，由于神经元置换和正对角缩放等代数对称性，相同的函数映射可以通过完全不同的权重配置实现，导致传统的基于参数的比较方法在训练过程中对微小的权重扰动表现出极端的不稳定性，这成为模型可解释性和可靠融合的关键障碍。

Method: 提出一种数学上严谨的方法：首先通过L2归一化消除缩放歧义并进行层间补偿；然后将激活区域离散化为在数据样本上评估的二进制功能签名；采用局部敏感哈希（MinHash）快速近似计算Jaccard指数以解决大规模二进制向量比较的计算瓶颈；最后将跨网络神经元匹配建模为线性分配问题并使用匈牙利算法求解。

Result: 理论和实验证明，该方法有效缓解了神经元的“闪烁”效应，对微小权重扰动表现出卓越的鲁棒性，为模型融合、迁移学习、剪枝评估和可解释AI提供了可靠的基础。

Conclusion: 该框架通过从原始权重比较转向神经元激活区域拓扑分析，成功构建了稳定的神经网络规范表示，为模型合并、迁移学习、剪枝评估和可解释AI范式奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: As modern deep learning architectures grow in complexity, representational ambiguity emerges as a critical barrier to their interpretability and reliable merging. For ReLU networks, identical functional mappings can be achieved through entirely different weight configurations due to algebraic symmetries: neuron permutation and positive diagonal scaling. Consequently, traditional parameter-based comparison methods exhibit extreme instability to slight weight perturbations during training. This paper proposes a mathematically grounded approach to constructing a stable canonical representation of neural networks and a robust functional similarity metric. We shift focus from comparing raw weights to analyzing the topology of neuron activation regions. The algorithm first eliminates scaling ambiguity via L2-normalization of weight vectors with subsequent layer compensation. Next, discrete approximations of activation regions are generated as binary functional signatures evaluated over a data sample. To overcome the computational bottleneck of comparing large binary vectors, we adapt Locality-Sensitive Hashing, specifically MinHash, providing a fast and statistically precise approximation of the Jaccard index. The final cross-network neuron matching is formulated as a linear sum assignment problem solved via the Hungarian algorithm. We demonstrate theoretically and experimentally that our metric mitigates the neuron "flickering" effect and exhibits exceptional robustness to minor weight perturbations. This framework provides a solid foundation for model merging, transfer learning, objective assessment during pruning, and Explainable AI paradigms.

</details>


### [209] [Non-Stationarity in the Embedding Space of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16428)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Brad Shook,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了时间序列基础模型（TSFM）嵌入空间中非平稳性的可检测性，发现其对均值漂移、方差变化和线性趋势等分布性非平稳性具有线性可分性，但随着变化强度减弱，检测能力会平滑下降，且不同模型表现出独特的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将非平稳性与分布漂移混淆，忽视了经典时间序列分析中关于非平稳性的基本区分，以及统计过程控制（SPC）等长期方法论。在SPC中，非平稳性标志着过程偏离稳定状态，检测这种偏离是质量监控和变点分析的核心。为了理解TSFM嵌入空间中非平稳性的本质，本研究受到这一诊断传统的启发。

Method: 在受控条件下，研究不同形式的分布性非平稳性（均值漂移、方差变化和线性趋势）如何在TSFM嵌入空间中变得线性可分。同时考察由持续性引起的时序非平稳性，这种非平稳性源于长记忆或近单位根行为而非显式的分布漂移。通过扫描变化强度并探测多个TSFM模型来评估检测能力。

Result: 研究发现嵌入空间中对非平稳性的可检测性会平滑下降，且不同模型展现出独特的、特定于模型的失效模式。分布性非平稳性在嵌入空间中具有线性可分性，但检测效果随变化强度减弱而降低。

Conclusion: TSFM嵌入空间中的非平稳性检测能力存在局限性，不同模型在面对不同程度和类型的非平稳性时表现出差异化的失效模式，这为理解TSFM的表示学习能力及其在时间序列分析中的应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are widely used as generic feature extractors, yet the notion of non-stationarity in their embedding spaces remains poorly understood. Recent work often conflates non-stationarity with distribution shift, blurring distinctions fundamental to classical time-series analysis and long-standing methodologies such as statistical process control (SPC). In SPC, non-stationarity signals a process leaving a stable regime - via shifts in mean, variance, or emerging trends - and detecting such departures is central to quality monitoring and change-point analysis. Motivated by this diagnostic tradition, we study how different forms of distributional non-stationarity - mean shifts, variance changes, and linear trends - become linearly accessible in TSFM embedding spaces under controlled conditions. We further examine temporal non-stationarity arising from persistence, which reflects violations of weak stationarity due to long-memory or near-unit-root behavior rather than explicit distributional shifts. By sweeping shift strength and probing multiple TSFMs, we find that embedding-space detectability of non-stationarity degrades smoothly and that different models exhibit distinct, model-specific failure modes.

</details>


### [210] [(Sparse) Attention to the Details: Preserving Spectral Fidelity in ML-based Weather Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16429)
*Maksim Zhdanov,Ana Lucic,Max Welling,Jan-Willem van de Meent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Mosaic概率天气预报模型，通过学习功能扰动生成集合成员，使用块稀疏注意力在原生分辨率网格上操作，解决了ML天气预报中确定性训练和信息瓶颈导致的频谱退化问题，在1.5°分辨率下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的天气预报模型存在两个主要的频谱退化问题：(1)针对集合平均值的确定性训练，(2)压缩编码造成的信息瓶颈。这些问题导致模型无法很好地捕捉大气变量的频谱特性，影响了预报质量。

Method: 提出Mosaic模型，采用两个核心创新：(1)通过学习功能扰动生成集合成员，实现概率预报；(2)使用块稀疏注意力机制，在原生分辨率网格上操作，通过在空间相邻查询间共享键和值，以线性成本捕捉长程依赖关系。模型在1.5°分辨率下运行，参数量214M。

Result: Mosaic在1.5°分辨率下匹配或优于在6倍精细数据上训练的模型的主要高空变量性能，在1.5°分辨率模型中达到SOTA结果。生成的集合成员具有良好校准性，各成员在所有已分辨频率上展现出近乎完美的频谱对齐。计算效率高：24成员、10天预报在单张H100 GPU上仅需不到12秒。

Conclusion: Mosaic成功解决了ML天气预报中的频谱退化问题，证明了在粗分辨率下也能达到优异性能，同时保持计算效率。该方法为概率天气预报提供了一种有效的解决方案，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: We introduce Mosaic, a probabilistic weather forecasting model that addresses two principal sources of spectral degradation in ML-based weather prediction: (1) deterministic training against ensemble means and (2) compressive encoding creating an information bottleneck. Mosaic generates ensemble members through learned functional perturbations and operates on native-resolution grids via block-sparse attention, a hardware-aligned mechanism that captures long-range dependencies at linear cost by sharing keys and values across spatially adjacent queries. At 1.5$°$ resolution with 214M parameters, Mosaic matches or outperforms models trained on 6 times finer data on headline upper-air variables and achieves state-of-the-art results among 1.5$°$ models, producing well-calibrated ensembles whose individual members exhibit near-perfect spectral alignment across all resolved frequencies. A 24-member, 10-day forecast takes under 12 seconds on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [211] [Dimensional Criticality at Grokking Across MLPs and Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16431)
*Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TDU-OFC方法，通过梯度快照的级联统计发现深度神经网络在grokking（从记忆到泛化的突变）时刻，有效级联维度D(t)会穿越D=1基线，且穿越方向具有任务依赖性，揭示了向共享临界流形吸引的动力学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中的grokking现象（训练精度饱和后很久才出现从记忆到泛化的突变）是复杂系统的典型特征，但目前缺乏能够捕捉这一突变过程的宏观可观测信号。作者旨在寻找能够表征grokking转变的宏观动力学特征。

Method: 提出了TDU-OFC（阈值扩散更新-Olami-Feder-Christensen）离线雪崩探针方法，将梯度快照转化为级联统计数据，通过grokking对齐的有限尺寸标度提取时间分辨的有效级联维度D(t)作为宏观观测量。在模块加法任务的Transformer和XOR任务的MLP上进行实验验证，并设计了阴性对照和影子探针对照实验。

Result: 发现了grokking转变的宏观特征：(1)在泛化转变时刻，D(t)精确穿越高斯扩散基线D=1；(2)穿越方向具有任务依赖性——模块加法从D>1下降穿越，XOR从D<1上升穿越，表明向共享临界流形的吸引而非简单驻留在D≈1附近；(3)未grokked的运行保持超临界态(D>1)且从不进入转变后区域；(4)雪崩分布呈现重尾和有限尺寸标度特征；(5)grokked轨迹在行为转变前100-200个epoch就与ungrokked轨迹在D(t)上出现分离。

Conclusion: TDU-OFC方法成功揭示了grokking现象背后的宏观动力学特征，证明了存在向候选共享临界流形吸引的动力学机制，为理解深度神经网络中突变的泛化转变提供了新的宏观视角和定量工具。这一发现将神经网络训练动力学与复杂系统的临界现象理论联系起来。

Abstract: Abrupt transitions between distinct dynamical regimes are a hallmark of complex systems. Grokking in deep neural networks provides a striking example -- an abrupt transition from memorization to generalization long after training accuracy saturates -- yet robust macroscopic signatures of this transition remain elusive. Here we introduce \textbf{TDU--OFC} (Thresholded Diffusion Update--Olami-Feder-Christensen), an offline avalanche probe that converts gradient snapshots into cascade statistics and extracts a \emph{macroscopic observable} -- the time-resolved effective cascade dimension $D(t)$ -- via grokking-aligned finite-size scaling. Across Transformers trained on modular addition and MLPs trained on XOR, we discover a localized dynamical crossing of the Gaussian diffusion baseline $D=1$ precisely at the generalization transition. The crossing direction is task-dependent: modular addition descends through $D=1$ (approaching from $D>1$), while XOR ascends (from $D<1$). This opposite-direction convergence is consistent with attraction toward a candidate shared critical manifold, rather than trivial residence near $D \approx 1$. Negative controls confirm this picture: ungrokked runs remain supercritical ($D>1$) and never enter the post-transition regime. In addition, avalanche distributions exhibit heavy tails and finite-size scaling consistent with the dimensional exponent extracted from $D(t)$. Shadow-probe controls ($α_{\mathrm{train}}=0$) confirm that $D(t)$ is non-invasive, and grokked trajectories diverge from ungrokked ones in $D(t)$ some $100$--$200$ epochs before the behavioral transition.

</details>


### [212] [Sampling for Quality: Training-Free Reward-Guided LLM Decoding via Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16453)
*Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Wenhui Zhu,Bo Long,Zhipeng Wang,Suyash Gupta,Kayhan Behdin,Bee-Chung Chen,Deepak Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的概率框架，通过奖励引导的解码策略显著提升大模型在代码生成和数学推理任务上的表现，在HumanEval上提升高达54.9%，且优于强化学习方法GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解码方法通常优化token级别的似然概率，而非序列级别的整体质量，这限制了生成任务的效果，特别是在需要复杂推理或高精度输出的场景中。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的概率框架，定义结合模型转移概率与前缀依赖奖励势能的奖励增强目标分布。采用序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)算法进行采样，开发了高效的前缀变体与前瞻变体，并集成重采样-移动更新与Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation，支持块状生成。

Result: 在三个7B模型上的实验表明：(1)代码生成(HumanEval)性能提升高达54.9%，超越最强采样基线9.1%-15.3%，达87.8%准确率；(2)数学推理(MATH500)提升达8.8%，达78.4%准确率；(3)整体性能一致优于强化学习方法GRPO。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了推理时的性能提升而无需模型重训练，证明了通过奖励引导解码优化序列级质量的可行性和有效性，为大模型推理优化提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce a principled probabilistic framework for reward-guided decoding in large language models, addressing the limitations of standard decoding methods that optimize token-level likelihood rather than sequence-level quality. Our method defines a reward-augmented target distribution over complete sequences by combining model transition probabilities with prefix-dependent reward potentials. Importantly, the approach is training-free: it leaves model weights unchanged and instead modifies the inference distribution via reward potentials, with all gains arising purely from inference-time sampling. To sample from this distribution, we develop Sequential Monte Carlo algorithms, including a computationally efficient prefix-only variant and a lookahead variant whose intermediate targets match the exact marginals of the full sequence distribution. The framework also integrates resample-move updates with Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation and supports block-wise generation, subsuming common decoding strategies such as temperature sampling and power-tempered objectives. Empirical results across three 7B models show significant gains. On code generation (HumanEval), our method improves base performance by up to 54.9% and surpasses the strongest sampling baselines by 9.1%-15.3%. On mathematical reasoning (MATH500), it achieves gains of up to 8.8%. Notably, it reaches 87.8% on HumanEval and 78.4% on MATH500 with Qwen2.5-7B, consistently outperforming the reinforcement learning method GRPO.

</details>


### [213] [SLO-Guard: Crash-Aware, Budget-Consistent Autotuning for SLO-Constrained LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17627)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SLO-Guard，一种针对vLLM服务的崩溃感知自动调优器，通过将崩溃作为一等观察对象处理，在固定的调优预算下实现了比随机搜索更可预测、更一致的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟服务级目标（SLO）约束下服务大语言模型是一个配置繁重的系统问题，其搜索空间极易发生故障：许多看似合理的配置会直接崩溃或无法满足用户可见的延迟目标，而标准的黑盒优化器将这些失败视为浪费的试验，未能有效利用失败信息。

Method: SLO-Guard将崩溃视为一等观察对象，结合了可行优先的热预算退火（TBA）探索阶段和热启动的树形Parzen估计器（TPE）开发阶段；交接阶段重放所有探索历史，包括编码为极端约束违规的崩溃。此外，还贡献了配置修复过程、GPU感知的KV缓存内存保护和四类崩溃分类法。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100 40GB上使用vLLM 0.19服务Qwen2-1.5B进行评估，SLO-Guard在预指定的五种子研究中实现了75/75的可行性且零崩溃，与均匀随机搜索在最佳延迟上统计持平（Mann-Whitney双侧p=0.84）。SLO-Guard的优势在于预算一致性：在快速服务状态下有更多试验（15次中10.20次vs 7.40次；单侧p=0.014）和更高的交接后一致性（0.876 vs 0.539；p=0.010）。在并发负载下，SLO-Guard在最佳延迟上的跨种子标准差比随机搜索严格4.4倍（2.26 ms vs 10.00 ms）。

Conclusion: 核心主张并非SLO-Guard能找到更好的最终配置，而是一旦找到快速服务状态，它能在固定的调优预算下以更可预测的方式运行，具有更一致的性能表现。

Abstract: Serving large language models under latency service-level objectives (SLOs) is a configuration-heavy systems problem with an unusually failure-prone search space: many plausible configurations crash outright or miss user-visible latency targets, and standard black-box optimizers treat these failures as wasted trials. We present SLO-Guard, a crash-aware autotuner for vLLM serving that treats crashes as first-class observations. SLO-Guard combines a feasible-first Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA) exploration phase with a warm-started Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) exploitation phase; the handoff replays all exploration history, including crashes encoded as extreme constraint violations. We additionally contribute a configuration-repair pass, a GPU-aware KV-cache memory guard, and a four-category crash taxonomy.
  We evaluate SLO-Guard on Qwen2-1.5B served with vLLM 0.19 on an NVIDIA A100 40GB. Across a pre-specified five-seed study, both SLO-Guard and uniform random search attain 75/75 feasibility with zero crashes under the corrected concurrent harness, and are statistically tied on best-achieved latency (Mann-Whitney two-sided p=0.84). SLO-Guard's advantage is in budget consistency: more trials in the fast-serving regime (10.20 vs. 7.40 out of 15; one-sided p=0.014) and higher post-handoff consistency (0.876 vs. 0.539; p=0.010). Under concurrent load, SLO-Guard's cross-seed standard deviation on best latency is 4.4x tighter than random search's (2.26 ms vs. 10.00 ms). A harness-replication analysis shows that the consistency findings survive an independent sequential-dispatch measurement condition.
  The central claim is not that SLO-Guard finds a better final configuration, but that it spends a fixed tuning budget more predictably once the fast regime has been found.

</details>


### [214] [Multi-Label Phase Diagram Prediction in Complex Alloys via Physics-Informed Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16468)
*Eunjeong Park,Amrita Basak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络(GAT)作为CALPHAD的高效替代模型，用于快速预测Ag-Bi-Cu-Sn合金体系的相平衡，实现了高精度相预测并具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然CALPHAD提供了严谨的热力学框架，但探索多组分合金的成分-温度空间仍然计算成本高昂，通常局限于稀疏截面。为了实现快速相图映射和合金筛选，需要一种高效且物理一致的代理模型。

Method: 利用pycalphad生成约25,000个平衡态数据，将每个成分-温度点表示为四节点元素图（原子分数和元素描述符作为节点特征）。模型结合图注意力机制、全局池化和多层感知机来预测九种相关相。为提高物理一致性，引入热力学约束作为训练惩罚项或推理时投影。

Result: 在六个二元和三个三元子系统中，基线模型达到0.951的宏F1分数和93.98%的精确集合匹配率；物理信息解码将精确集合准确率提升至约96%。模型在未见过的三元截面上达到99.32%的精确集合准确率，在700°C的四元截面上达到91.78%的准确率。

Conclusion: 基于注意力的图学习结合热力学约束执行，为高分辨率相图映射和外推性合金筛选提供了一种有效且物理一致的代理模型方法。

Abstract: Accurate phase equilibria are foundational to alloy design because they encode the underlying thermodynamics governing stability, transformations, and processing windows. However, while the CALculation of Phase Diagrams (CALPHAD) provides a rigorous thermodynamic framework, exploring multicomponent composition-temperature space remains computationally expensive and is typically limited to sparse section. To enable rapid phase mapping and alloy screening, we propose a physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) that learns element-aware representations and couples them with thermodynamic constraints for multi-label phase-set prediction in the Ag-Bi-Cu-Sn alloy system. Using about 25,000 equilibrium states generated with pycalphad, each composition-temperature point is represented as a four-node element graph with atomic fractions and elemental descriptors as node features. The model combines graph attention, global pooling, and a multilayer perceptron to predict nine relevant phases. To improve physical consistency, we incorporate thermodynamic constraints, applied as training penalties or as an inference-time projection. Across six binary and three ternary subsystems, the baseline model achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.951 and 93.98% exact-set match, while physics-informed decoding improves robustness and raises exact-set accuracy to about 96% on dense in-domain grids. The surrogate also generalizes to an unseen ternary section with 99.32% exact-set accuracy and to a quaternary section at 700 °C with 91.78% accuracy. These results demonstrate that attention-based graph learning coupled with thermodynamic constraint enforcement provides an effective and physically consistent surrogate for high-resolution phase mapping and extrapolative alloy screening.

</details>


### [215] [Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16519)
*Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PODPO方法，一种用于在线强化学习的免似然、免梯度裁剪生成式策略，仅利用正优势样本进行策略更新，主动预防错误动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习领域中，传统高斯策略和基于流的方法存在表达能力受限（单峰分布）、梯度裁剪复杂或信任区域要求严格等问题，且都依赖事后惩罚负样本来纠正错误动作，缺乏主动预防机制。

Method: 提出正样本漂移策略优化（PODPO），一种免似然、免梯度裁剪的生成式方法。利用漂移模型，通过优势加权的局部对比漂移执行策略更新，仅依赖正优势样本，并利用生成模型固有的局部平滑性实现主动错误预防。

Result: PODPO能够优雅地将动作引导至高回报区域，同时利用生成模型的局部平滑特性主动预防错误动作的发生，而非依赖事后惩罚。

Conclusion: PODPO为在线强化学习中的生成式策略学习开辟了一条有前景的新路径，解决了传统方法的多个局限性。

Abstract: In the field of online reinforcement learning (RL), traditional Gaussian policies and flow-based methods are often constrained by their unimodal expressiveness, complex gradient clipping, or stringent trust-region requirements. Moreover, they all rely on post-hoc penalization of negative samples to correct erroneous actions. This paper introduces Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization (PODPO), a likelihood-free and gradient-clipping-free generative approach for online RL. By leveraging the drifting model, PODPO performs policy updates via advantage-weighted local contrastive drifting. Relying solely on positive-advantage samples, it elegantly steers actions toward high-return regions while exploiting the inherent local smoothness of the generative model to enable proactive error prevention. In doing so, PODPO opens a promising new pathway for generative policy learning in online settings.

</details>


### [216] [G-PARC: Graph-Physics Aware Recurrent Convolutional Neural Networks for Spatiotemporal Dynamics on Unstructured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16533)
*Jack T. Beerman,Tyler J. Abele,Mehdi Taghizadeh,Andrew Davis,Zoë J. Gray,Negin Alemazkoor,Xinfeng Gao,H. S. Udaykumar,Stephen S. Baek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Graph PARC (G-PARC)，一种结合物理感知与图神经网络的模型，通过移动最小二乘核在非结构化图上近似空间导数，解决了现有方法在处理非均匀网格和极端非线性问题上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理感知循环卷积网络（PARC）受限于静态均匀笛卡尔网格，难以高效追踪演化的局部结构；而基于图的物理感知深度学习（PADL）方法在处理极端非线性状态时表现不佳。需要一种能同时处理非结构化网格和极端非线性动态的方法。

Method: 提出Graph PARC (G-PARC)，利用移动最小二乘（MLS）核在非结构化图上近似空间导数，并将偏微分方程的导数嵌入神经网络的计算图中。该方法使用解析计算的微分算子替代传统的编码器-处理器-解码器框架。

Result: G-PARC在参数量减少2-3倍的情况下，准确率优于MeshGraphNet、MeshGraphKAN和GraphSAGE。实验证明其具备：(1)跨非均匀时空离散化的泛化能力；(2)处理结构变形所需的移动网格能力；(3)在河流水文、平面激波和弹塑性动力学等非线性基准测试中优于现有图PADL方法。

Conclusion: 通过将显式物理算子嵌入GNN的灵活性中，G-PARC实现了在复杂计算域上对极端非线性现象的精确建模，推动了物理感知深度学习超越理想化笛卡尔网格的限制。

Abstract: Physics-aware recurrent convolutional networks (PARC) have demonstrated strong performance in predicting nonlinear spatiotemporal dynamics by embedding differential operators directly into the computational graph of a neural network. However, pixel-based convolutions are restricted to static, uniform Cartesian grids, making them ill-suited to following evolving localized structures in an efficient manner. Graph neural networks (GNNs) naturally handle irregular spatial discretizations, but existing graph-based physics-aware deep learning (PADL) methods have difficulty handling extreme nonlinear regimes. To address these limitations, we propose Graph PARC (G-PARC), which uses moving least squares (MLS) kernels to approximate spatial derivatives on unstructured graphs, and embeds the derivatives of governing partial differential equations into the network's computational graph. G-PARC achieves better accuracy with 2-3x fewer parameters than MeshGraphNet, MeshGraphKAN, and GraphSAGE, replacing the traditional encoder-processor-decoder framework with analytically computed differential operators. We demonstrate that G-PARC (1) generalizes across nonuniform spatial and temporal discretizations; (2) handles moving meshes required for structural deformation; and (3) outperforms existing graph-based PADL methods on nonlinear benchmarks including fluvial hydrology, planar shock waves, and elastoplastic dynamics. By embedding explicit physical operators within the flexibility of GNNs, G-PARC enables accurate modeling of extreme nonlinear phenomena on complex computational domains, moving PADLbeyond idealized Cartesian grids.

</details>


### [217] [SCATR: Simple Calibrated Test-Time Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16535)
*Divya Shyamal,Marta Knežević,Lan Tran,Chanakya Ekbote,Vijay Lingam,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SCATR，一种轻量级的Best-of-N排序方法，通过利用基座模型的隐藏表示在小规模校准集上训练轻量级评分器。该方法在编码和数学推理基准测试中，相比先前的置信度基线提升高达9%，与强PRM基线相比在数学和编码任务上分别提升高达7.8%和4.2%，同时推理速度提升高达1000倍，实现了准确性与效率的优越平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
