Across the three temporal encodings, event order only and admission-relative RoPE match or exceed inserting time tokens on average while shortening sequences by 11%. CLIF remapping preserves downstream performance in our single-site setting while yielding a smaller, clinically interpretable token set compatible with multi-site use. Finer-than-decile quantization, reference-range anchoring, and soft discretization help in selective outcomes, while code-normalized xVal remains well below the discrete and soft families, consistent with near-median suppression that persists after the affine variant.

</details>


### [252] [Federation over Text: Insight Sharing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16778)
*Dixi Yao,Tahseen Rabbani,Tian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了文本联邦框架FoT，通过语义层面的多智能体协作构建共享元认知洞察库，在提升推理准确率24%的同时减少28%的推理token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在面对新问题时往往从头推理，缺乏将学习到的技能自动迁移到其他智能体的机制，且不存在自动化的知识共享方案。

Method: 提出类联邦学习的文本联邦框架FoT，在语义层面而非梯度层面进行联邦。各智能体独立完成本地思考和自我改进，将推理轨迹共享给中央服务器，服务器聚合并提炼成跨任务、跨领域的洞察库，供现有和未来智能体复用。

Result: 在数学问题求解、跨领域协作和机器学习研究洞察发现三大应用中验证有效。前两个应用中平均准确率提升24%，推理token减少28%；研究洞察发现应用中生成的洞察覆盖了后续论文超过90%的主要贡献。

Conclusion: FoT证明了在语义层面进行智能体联邦的可行性，能够有效促进跨任务知识迁移，显著提升推理效能和效率，为构建可持续学习的多智能体系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents often reason from scratch when presented with a new problem instance and lack automatic mechanisms to transfer learned skills to other agents. We propose a federated learning-like framework, Federation over Text (FoT), that enables multiple agents solving different tasks to collectively generate a shared library of metacognitive insights by iteratively federating their local reasoning processes. Instead of federation over gradients (e.g., as in distributed training), FoT operates at the semantic level without any gradient optimization or supervision signal. Iteratively, each agent does local thinking and self-improvement on their specific tasks independently, and shares reasoning traces with a central server, which aggregates and distills them into a cross-task (and cross-domain) insight library that existing and future agents can leverage to improve performance on related tasks. Experiments show that FoT improves reasoning effectiveness and efficiency across a wide range of challenging applications, including mathematical problem solving, cross-domain collaboration, and machine learning research insight discovery. Specifically, it improves average accuracies of downstream tasks by 24% while reducing the reasoning tokens by 28% across the first two applications. In the research insight discovery application, FoT is able to generate insights that cover over 90% of the major contributions in the subsequent papers.

</details>


### [253] [Continuous Limits of Coupled Flows in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16801)
*Zilin Li,Weiwei Xu,Xuchun Tong,Xuanbo Lu,Xuanqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢快动力学系统，证明了离散空间转移一致收敛到过阻尼Langevin SDE，揭示了表征权重无条件避免发散并与空间测度主特征空间对齐，并展示了全局耗散性以及正交解耦和线性可分特征在稳态极限下的自发涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表示学习严重依赖全局误差信号，而由局部交互驱动的去中心化算法提供了根本的分布式替代方案。然而，这些离散动力学在连续数据流形上的宏观收敛性质在理论上仍未解决，且长期存在参数爆炸问题。本研究旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢快动力学系统。首先利用测度论极限证明离散空间转移一致收敛到过阻尼Langevin随机微分方程；其次通过Itô-Poisson预解式和LaSalle不变性原理的随机扩展，建立表征权重的收敛性；最后为完全耦合的空间-参数流构建联合Lyapunov泛函。

Result: 证明了表征权重无条件避免发散并严格对齐于空间测度的主特征空间；通过联合Lyapunov泛函证明了全局耗散性；展示了正交解耦、线性可分的特征在稳态极限下自发涌现。

Conclusion: 该框架成功桥接了离散算法与连续随机分析，证明了去中心化学习动力学的全局收敛性质和特征自发解耦能力，为去中心化表示学习提供了形式化的理论基线。

Abstract: While modern representation learning relies heavily on global error signals, decentralized algorithms driven by local interactions offer a fundamental distributed alternative. However, the macroscopic convergence properties of these discrete dynamics on continuous data manifolds remain theoretically unresolved, notoriously suffering from parameter explosion. We bridge this gap by formalizing decentralized learning as a coupled slow-fast dynamical system on Riemannian manifolds. First, using measure-theoretic limits, we prove that the discrete spatial transitions converge uniformly to an overdamped Langevin stochastic differential equation. Second, via the Itô-Poisson resolvent and a stochastic extension of LaSalle's Invariance Principle, we establish that the representation weights unconditionally avoid divergence and align strictly with the principal eigenspace of the spatial measure. Finally, we construct a joint Lyapunov functional for the fully coupled spatial-parametric flow. This proves global dissipativity and demonstrates that orthogonally disentangled, linearly separable features emerge spontaneously at the stationary limit. Our framework bridges discrete algorithms with continuous stochastic analysis, providing a formal theoretical baseline for decentralized representation learning.

</details>


### [254] [AutoOR: Scalably Post-training LLMs to Autoformalize Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16804)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Chuan Du,Aleksander Petrov,Christopher Davis,Philip Torr,Antonio Papania-Davis,Weishi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AutoOR框架，通过合成数据生成和强化学习流程，训练大语言模型将自然语言描述的优化问题自动转化为求解器可处理的数学形式，在多个基准测试中取得了最先进或具有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制造、物流、调度等工业场景中，优化问题至关重要。然而，将复杂的问题描述转化为求解器可处理的形式需要专业的运筹学（OR）知识，这导致该过程难以规模化。为了解决这一瓶颈，研究旨在自动化这一翻译过程。

Method: 提出了AutoOR框架，包含可扩展的合成数据生成和强化学习（RL）流程。该方法首先从标准优化形式生成经过验证的训练数据，随后利用求解器执行反馈作为奖励信号，对大语言模型进行强化学习后训练。针对非线性问题，还引入了课程学习策略进行引导。

Result: 将AutoOR应用于8B参数模型，在六个已建立的运筹学基准测试中达到了最先进或具有竞争力的结果，表现媲美参数量显著更大的前沿模型。特别是在前沿模型得分接近0%的涉及物理动力学的非线性问题类别上，通过课程RL策略成功解决了该类别的训练难题。

Conclusion: AutoOR等方法能够利用AI显著加速工业决策过程，展示了大语言模型在自动化运筹学问题形式化方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Optimization problems are central to decision-making in manufacturing, logistics, scheduling, and other industrial settings. Translating complicated descriptions of these problems into solver-ready formulations requires specialized operations research (OR) expertise, making it hard to scale. We present AutoOR, a scalable synthetic data generation and reinforcement learning pipeline that trains LLMs to autoformalize optimization problems specified in natural language across linear, mixed-integer, and non-linear categories. AutoOR generates verified training data from standard optimization forms and uses solver execution feedback as the reward signal for RL post-training. AutoOR applied to an 8B model achieves state-of-the-art or competitive results across six established OR benchmarks, matching significantly larger frontier models. For a non-linear problem class involving physical dynamics, where frontier models score near 0%, we introduce a curriculum RL strategy that bootstraps from limited initial training data to make this class tractable for post-training. We believe that methods such as AutoOR can significantly accelerate industrial decision-making with AI.

</details>


### [255] [Self-Reinforcing Controllable Synthesis of Rare Relational Data via Bayesian Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16817)
*Chongsheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zelong Yu,Esteban Garces Arias,Julian Rodemann,Zhanshuo Zhang,Qilong Li,Gaojuan Fan,Krikamol Muandet,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RDDG框架，利用大语言模型(LLM)结合渐进式思维链和自增强反馈机制，生成高质量的表格关系数据，有效解决了非平衡数据分类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中普遍存在非平衡数据问题，虽然数据合成可以缓解稀有类别的数据稀缺问题，但LLM在关系/结构化表格数据合成领域的应用仍未被充分探索。此外，现有方法缺乏有效的反馈机制来引导LLM持续优化生成数据的质量。

Method: 提出RDDG（Relational Data generator with Dynamic Guidance）框架，采用渐进式思维链进行上下文学习。具体流程为：首先使用核心集选择从原始数据中识别代表性样本；然后利用上下文学习发现核心集内属性间的内在模式和相关性；接着在保持约束的前提下生成表格数据；最后引入自增强反馈机制，对生成数据质量进行自动评估，实现生成过程中的持续质量优化。

Result: 在多个真实和合成数据集上的实验结果表明，RDDG在数据保真度和下游非平衡分类性能方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RDDG为表格数据合成提供了一种有效的解决方案，证明了将LLM应用于关系数据生成并结合动态反馈机制的可行性，能够有效提升非平衡数据的分类性能。

Abstract: Imbalanced data is commonly present in real-world applications. While data synthesis can effectively mitigate the data scarcity problem of rare-classes, and LLMs have revolutionized text generation, the application of LLMs to relational/structured tabular data synthesis remains underexplored. Moreover, existing approaches lack an effective feedback mechanism that can guide LLMs towards continuously optimizing the quality of the generated data throughout the synthesis process. In this work, we propose RDDG, Relational Data generator with Dynamic Guidance, which is a unified in-context learning framework that employs progressive chain-of-thought (CoT) steps to generate tabular data for enhancing downstream imbalanced classification performance. RDDG first uses core set selection to identify representative samples from the original data, then utilizes in-context learning to discover the inherent patterns and correlations among attributes within the core set, and subsequently generates tabular data while preserving the aforementioned constraints. More importantly, it incorporates a self-reinforcing feedback mechanism that provides automatic assessments on the quality of the generated data, enabling continuous quality optimization throughout the generation process. Experimental results on multiple real and synthetic datasets demonstrate that RDDG outperforms existing approaches in both data fidelity and downstream imbalanced classification performance. We make our code available at https://github.com/cszhangLMU/RDDG.

</details>


### [256] [R&F-Inventory: A Large-Scale Dataset for Monotonic Inventory Estimation in Reach and Frequency Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16821)
*Yunshan Peng,Ji Wu,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Jinan Pang,Wenzheng Shu,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布了一个大规模的触达与频次（R&F）合约库存估算数据集，填补了现有公开广告数据集中缺乏预算-效果曲线刻画的空白，并定义了两个标准化基准任务和基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开广告数据集多基于独立样本，缺乏对R&F合约广告中核心的“预算-效果曲线”（包括UV和PV）的刻画。广告主在实际系统中需要实时查看不同预算水平下的UV、PV变化曲线，但缺乏相应的数据集支持相关研究。

Method: 以“定向-排期-频控”构成的R&F合约上下文为基础，构建大规模R&F合约库存估算数据集。数据集提供同一上下文中多个预算点对应的UV和PV观测值，形成完整的预算-效果曲线。数据集显式包含基于时间窗口的频控机制，并满足预算和排期维度上的单调性和边际收益递减特性。推导理论最大曝光上限用于数据质量评估和模型预测可行性检验。定义两个标准化基准任务：单点效果预测和预算-效果曲线重建，并提供可复现的基线方法和评估协议。

Result: 成功构建并发布了一个大规模R&F合约库存估算数据集，数据质量通过理论最大曝光上限的一致性检验得到验证。提供了两个标准化基准任务的可复现基线方法和评估协议，代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了R&F合约广告领域预算-效果曲线数据集的空白，可支持结构化约束学习、单调回归、曲线一致性建模和R&F合约规划等问题的系统研究，为相关算法研究和实际应用提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Reach and Frequency (R&F) contract advertising is an important form of widely used brand advertising. Unlike performance advertising, R&F contracts emphasize controllable delivery of UV and PV under given targeting, scheduling, and frequency control constraints. In practical systems, advertisers typically need to view the UV, PV change curves at different budget levels in real time when creating an R&F contract. However, most existing publicly available advertising datasets are based on independent samples, lacking a characterization of the core structure of the "budget-performance curve" (including UV and PV) in R&F contracts.This paper proposes and releases a large-scale R&F contract inventory estimation dataset. This dataset uses the R&F contract context consisting of "targeting-scheduling-frequency control" as the basic context, providing observations of UV and PV corresponding to multiple budget points within the same context, thus forming a complete budget-performance curve. The dataset explicitly includes a time-window-based frequency control mechanism (e.g.,"no more than 3 times within 5 days") and naturally satisfies the monotonicity and diminishing marginal returns characteristics in the budget and scheduling dimensions. We further derive the theoretical maximum exposure ceiling and use it as a consistency check to evaluate data quality and the feasibility of model predictions. Using this data set, this paper defines two standardized benchmark tasks: single-point performance prediction and reconstruction of budget-performance curves, and provides a set of reproducible baseline methods and evaluation protocols. This dataset can support systematic research on problems such as structural constraint learning, monotonic regression, curve consistency modeling, and R&F contract planning.The code for our experiments can be found at https://github.com/pengyunshan/RF-Inventory.

</details>


### [257] [The Illusion of Certainty: Decoupling Capability and Calibration in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16830)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Qinyuan Ye,Caiming Xiong,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了同策略蒸馏（OPD）中存在普遍的“错误校准缩放定律”：虽然OPD能有效提升任务准确率，但会系统性地导致模型过度自信。作者提出CaOPD框架，通过模型推演估计置信度并替换自报告置信度，在校准和能力之间实现帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现同策略蒸馏虽然提升了模型的任务准确性，但会系统性地导致严重的过度自信问题。研究追溯到信息不匹配的根源：教师监督在训练时的特权上下文中形成，而部署时的模型只能依赖部署时的信息来报告置信度。理论上证明，以教师为条件的成功通常不是部署时置信度的有效目标，有益的特权上下文会导致熵坍塌和系统性乐观偏差。

Method: 提出校准感知的同策略蒸馏框架（CaOPD）：1）从模型推演中估计经验置信度；2）用这种基于学生的目标替换自报告置信度；3）通过相同的自蒸馏流程蒸馏修正后的响应。

Result: 在多种模型和领域的实验中，CaOPD在保持竞争能力的同时实现了帕累托最优校准，在分布外和持续学习场景下表现出稳健的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，能力蒸馏并不意味着校准的置信度，置信度应被视为后训练中的关键目标。这挑战了以往假设能力提升会自然带来校准改善的观点，强调了在后训练中显式建模置信度的重要性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is an increasingly important paradigm for post-training language models. However, we identify a pervasive Scaling Law of Miscalibration: while OPD effectively improves task accuracy, it systematically traps models in severe overconfidence. We trace this failure to an information mismatch: teacher supervision is formed under privileged context available during training, whereas the deployed model must report confidence using only deployment-time information. We formalize this perspective theoretically, showing that teacher-conditioned success is generally not a valid target for deployment-time confidence and that helpful privileged context induces entropy collapse and a systematic optimism bias. To address this, we propose a calibration-aware OPD framework, CaOPD, that estimates empirical confidence from model rollouts, replaces self-reported confidence with this student-grounded target, and distills the revised response through the same self-distillation pipeline. Experiments across various models and domains show that CaOPD achieves Pareto-optimal calibration while maintaining competitive capability, generalizing robustly under out-of-distribution and continual learning. Our findings highlight that capability distillation does not imply calibrated confidence, and that confidence should be treated as an essential objective in post-training. Code: https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/CaOPD

</details>


### [258] [Applications of deep generative models to DNA reaction kinetics and to cryogenic electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16851)
*Chenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ViDa、Struc2mapGAN和CryoSAMU三种深度生成模型，分别用于DNA反应动力学可视化和冷冻电镜密度图分析与建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在生物学问题分析中的应用潜力巨大，但面临如何有效整合领域知识的挑战。本文旨在探索如何将生物物理先验知识与深度学习相结合，以解决DNA反应动力学解释和冷冻电镜密度图分析中的关键难题。

Method: 提出三个框架：1) ViDa：结合变分自编码器(VAE)和几何散射变换，生成符合生物物理规律的DNA反应动力学嵌入；2) Struc2mapGAN：利用生成对抗网络从蛋白质结构合成高保真冷冻电镜密度图；3) CryoSAMU：通过交叉注意力机制融合密度特征与蛋白质语言模型结构嵌入的结构感知多模态U-Net。

Result: ViDa成功将DNA杂交和立足点介导链置换反应降至二维空间可视化，聚类轨迹集成反应路径并揭示新机制；对冷冻电镜原子模型构建的深度学习方法进行了全面基准测试；Struc2mapGAN生成了高保真实验级密度图；CryoSAMU有效增强了中等分辨率冷冻电镜图谱。

Conclusion: 深度生成模型能够有效整合领域知识，在DNA反应机制解释和冷冻电镜密度图分析及蛋白质结构建模方面展现出巨大潜力，为生物物理学研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: This dissertation explores how deep generative models can advance the analysis of challenging biological problems by integrating domain knowledge with deep learning. It focuses on two areas: DNA reaction kinetics and cryogenic electron microscopy (cryo-EM). In the first part, we present ViDa, a biophysics-informed framework leveraging variational autoencoders (VAEs) and geometric scattering transforms to generate biophysically-plausible embeddings of DNA reaction kinetics simulations. These embeddings are reduced to a two-dimensional space to visualize DNA hybridization and toehold-mediated strand displacement reactions. ViDa preserves structure and clusters trajectory ensembles into reaction pathways, making simulation results more interpretable and revealing new mechanistic insights. In the second part, we address key challenges in cryo-EM density map interpretation and protein structure modeling. We provide a comprehensive review and benchmarking of deep learning methods for atomic model building, with improved evaluation metrics and practical guidance. We then present Struc2mapGAN, a generative adversarial network that synthesizes high-fidelity experimental-like cryo-EM density maps from protein structures. Finally, we present CryoSAMU, a structure-aware multimodal U-Net that enhances intermediate-resolution cryo-EM maps by integrating density features with structural embeddings from protein language models via cross-attention. Overall, these contributions demonstrate the potential of deep generative models to interpret DNA reaction mechanisms and advance cryo-EM density map analysis and protein structure modeling.

</details>


### [259] [CCAR: Intrinsic Robustness as an Emergent Geometric Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16861)
*Akash Samanta,Manish Pratap Singh,Debasis Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出类条件激活正则化（CCAR）方法，通过强制特征空间的块对角结构来实现几何解耦，从而在无需对抗训练的情况下显著提升模型对标签噪声和输入扰动的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准监督学习仅优化预测准确性而忽略学习特征的内部几何结构，导致学习到的表示往往纠缠且脆弱。作者认为通过显式设计特征空间的几何结构，可以引入内在的正则化机制来提升模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出类条件激活正则化（CCAR），通过软归纳偏置施加块对角结构约束，将不同类别的能量限制在正交子空间中。该方法在数学上等价于最大化Fisher判别比，建立了几何解耦与算法稳定性之间的形式化联系。

Result: 在标签噪声和输入扰动基准测试中显著优于基线方法，验证了鲁棒性是良好设计的特征空间的涌现属性。实验表明该方法能够自然过滤噪声和对抗性扰动。

Conclusion: 通过显式设计特征空间的几何结构，鲁棒性可以成为模型的固有属性而非需要专门训练的目标。这为构建更稳定、可解释的深度学习系统提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Standard supervised learning optimizes for predictive accuracy but remains agnostic to the internal geometry of learned features, often yielding representations that are entangled and brittle. We propose Class-Conditional Activation Regularization (CCAR) to explicitly engineer the feature space, imposing a block-diagonal structure via a soft inductive bias. By shaping the latent representation to confine class energy to orthogonal subspaces, we create an intrinsic geometric scaffold that naturally filters noise and adversarial perturbations. We provide theoretical analysis linking this structural constraint to the maximization of the Fisher Discriminant Ratio, establishing a formal connection between geometric disentanglement and algorithmic stability. Empirically, this approach demonstrates that robustness is an emergent property of a well-engineered feature space, significantly outperforming baselines on label noise and input corruption benchmarks.

</details>


### [260] [Learning to Trade Like an Expert: Cognitive Fine-Tuning for Stable Financial Reasoning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16862)
*Yuchen Pan,Soung Chang Liew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结构化框架，用于训练和评估作为自主交易代理的大语言模型。该框架包含一个经AI委员会验证、增强推理链和减少捷径学习的MCQ数据集，以及结合测试集评估和按时间顺序交易模拟的两阶段协议。实验表明，在该框架下训练的开源模型展现出具有竞争力的风险感知交易行为，优于开源基线并接近闭源前沿模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型作为自主交易代理的部署日益增多，人们开始质疑其金融决策能力是否能泛化到特定市场模式之外，以及在缺乏真实标签的噪声市场中应如何对其进行训练和评估。这促使研究者开发一个系统的框架来解决这些挑战。

Method: 该方法由三部分组成：(1) 构建一个经AI委员会验证、包含结构化推理链并进行数据增强以减少捷径学习的MCQ数据集；(2) 设计结合标准测试集评估与基于MCQ的按时间顺序交易模拟的两阶段评估协议；(3) 在不同市场机制下对模型进行广泛评估。

Result: 实验结果提供了统计上稳健的证据，表明使用该框架训练的开源模型随时间推移展现出具有竞争力的风险感知行为。这些模型的表现优于开源基线，并且在规模较小的情况下能够接近前沿模型的性能水平。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的框架能够有效地训练和评估金融领域的大语言模型。通过公开该数据集和评估框架，旨在支持该领域的进一步研究，解决LLM在金融决策中的泛化和评估难题。

Abstract: Recent deployments of large language models (LLMs) as autonomous trading agents raise questions about whether financial decision-making competence generalizes beyond specific market patterns and how it should be trained and evaluated in noisy markets lacking ground truth. We propose a structured framework for training and evaluating such models. Central to our approach is a curated, multiple-choice question (MCQ) dataset derived from classic textbooks and historical markets, verified by an AI committee, enriched with structured reasoning traces, and augmented to reduce shortcut learning. To evaluate whether performance on isolated MCQs generalizes to real-world trading, we introduce a two-stage protocol combining test-set evaluation with an MCQ-based chronological trading simulation. Extensive evaluations across market regimes provide statistically robust evidence that open models trained with our framework exhibit competitive, risk-aware behavior over time, outperform open-source baselines, and approach frontier-model performance at smaller scale. We release the dataset and evaluation framework to support further research.

</details>


### [261] [Untrained CNNs Match Backpropagation at V1: A Systematic RSA Comparison of Four Learning Rules Against Human fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16875)
*Nils Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现神经网络与人类视觉皮层的对齐程度取决于脑区：早期视觉区域（V1/V2）的对齐主要由网络架构决定，未训练的随机权重网络与反向传播效果无异；而在高级视觉区域（LOC/IT），学习规则才起关键作用，反向传播表现最佳，预测编码也能达到相似效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算神经科学中的一个核心问题是：用于训练神经网络的学习规则是否决定了其内部表征与人类视觉皮层表征的对齐程度。作者旨在系统比较不同学习规则（反向传播、反馈对齐、预测编码、STDP）对这种对齐关系的影响。

Method: 研究者在相同的卷积神经网络架构上应用了四种学习规则（BP、FA、PC、STDP），并引入了一个关键对照组——未训练的随机权重网络。利用THINGS-fMRI数据集（720个刺激，3名受试者），采用表征相似性分析（RSA）来评估模型与人类大脑活动的对齐程度。

Result: 早期视觉区域（V1/V2）的对齐主要由架构驱动：未训练的CNN（rho=0.071）与BP（rho=0.072）效果在统计上无显著差异（p=0.43）。学习规则仅在高级视觉区域产生差异化影响：BP在LOC/IT区域占主导地位，而具有局部赫布更新的PC在IT区域的对齐效果与BP在统计上无法区分（p=0.18）。FA在V1区域的表征甚至持续低于随机基线。

Conclusion: 学习规则与皮层对齐的关系具有区域特异性：网络架构决定了早期视觉区域的对齐，而监督学习目标驱动了高级视觉区域的对齐。这挑战了认为特定学习规则是模拟大脑视觉皮层关键因素的传统观点。

Abstract: A central question in computational neuroscience is whether the learning rule used to train a neural network determines how well its internal representations align with those of the human visual cortex. We present a systematic comparison of four learning rules -- backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), and spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP) -- applied to identical convolutional architectures and evaluated against human fMRI data from the THINGS-fMRI dataset (720 stimuli, 3 subjects) using Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA). Crucially, we include an untrained random-weights baseline that reveals the dominant role of architecture. We find that early visual alignment (V1/V2) is primarily architecture-driven: an untrained CNN achieves rho = 0.071, statistically indistinguishable from BP (rho = 0.072, p = 0.43). Learning rules only differentiate at higher visual areas: BP dominates at LOC/IT, and PC with local Hebbian updates achieves IT alignment statistically indistinguishable from BP (p = 0.18). FA consistently impairs representations below the random baseline at V1. Partial RSA confirms all effects survive pixel-similarity control. These results demonstrate that the relationship between learning rules and cortical alignment is region-specific: architecture determines early alignment, while supervised objectives drive late alignment.

</details>


### [262] [OC-Distill: Ontology-aware Contrastive Learning with Cross-Modal Distillation for ICU Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16878)
*Zhongyuan Liang,Junhyung Jo,Hyang-Jung Lee,Sang Kyu Kim,Irene Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OC-Distill框架，通过本体感知对比学习和跨模态知识蒸馏，仅用生命体征即在ICU预测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU临床恶化预测方法存在明显局限：对比预训练将所有患者视为同等负样本，忽视了临床诊断相似性；下游微调忽略了临床笔记等互补模态信息。

Method: 提出OC-Distill两阶段框架：第一阶段利用ICD层级结构量化患者相似性，设计本体感知对比目标学习临床表征；第二阶段通过跨模态知识蒸馏，将临床笔记中的互补信息迁移到模型中。训练时利用多模态监督，推理时仅需生命体征信号。

Result: 在MIMIC数据集的多个ICU预测任务上，OC-Distill展现了更高的标签效率，在仅使用生命体征进行推理的方法中达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: OC-Distill成功解决了现有方法的局限性，通过结合ICD本体知识和临床笔记信息，在保持推理便捷性的同时显著提升了ICU预测性能，为高急性医疗环境中的实时风险预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Early prediction of severe clinical deterioration and remaining length of stay can enable timely intervention and better resource allocation in high-acuity settings such as the ICU. This has driven the development of machine learning models that leverage continuous streams of vital signs and other physiological signals for real-time risk prediction. Despite their promise, existing methods have important limitations. Contrastive pretraining treats all patients as equally strong negatives, failing to capture clinically meaningful similarity between patients with related diagnoses. Meanwhile, downstream fine-tuning typically ignores complementary modalities such as clinical notes, which provide rich contextual information unavailable in physiological signals alone. To address these challenges, we propose OC-Distill, a two-stage framework that leverages multimodal supervision during training while requiring only vital signs at inference. In the first stage, we introduce an ontology-aware contrastive objective that exploits the ICD hierarchy to quantify patient similarity and learn clinically grounded representations. In the second stage, we fine-tune the pretrained encoder via cross-modal knowledge distillation, transferring complementary information from clinical notes into the model. Across multiple ICU prediction tasks on MIMIC, OC-Distill demonstrates improved label efficiency and achieves state-of-the-art performance among methods that use only vital signs at inference.

</details>


### [263] [SinkRouter: Sink-Aware Routing for Efficient Long-Context Decoding in Large Language and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16883)
*Junnan Liu,Xinyan Liu,Peifeng Gao,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zhang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni Bert Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SinkRouter，一种无需训练的选择性路由框架，通过识别注意力汇聚现象的固定点特性来跳过近似零输出的计算，结合硬件感知的Triton内核实现加速，在512K上下文长度下达到2.03倍加速且保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/LMM长上下文解码加速策略（如启发式剪枝）存在效率与准确性的权衡问题，且对注意力汇聚现象的机制理解不足，表现为：无差别保留高分token、视早期token为必需锚点、依赖启发式头路由。核心动机在于深入理解注意力汇聚现象的本质，并基于此设计更优的加速方案。

Method: 1. 理论层面：揭示注意力汇聚现象对应于训练过程中构建的稳定、可达且误差可控的固定点。
2. 算法层面：提出SinkRouter，一种无需训练的选择性路由框架，检测汇聚信号并跳过会产生近似零输出的计算。
3. 系统层面：开发硬件感知的Triton内核，采用块级分支和Split-K并行技术实现实际加速。

Result: 在LongBench、InfiniteBench、CVBench、MileBench、MMVP等多样化长上下文基准测试上，使用Llama-3.1-8B/70B、Yi-9B-200K、LLaVA-1.5-7B/13B等文本和多模态骨干模型进行评估，SinkRouter在保持竞争性准确率的同时持续提升解码效率，在512K上下文长度下实现2.03倍加速。

Conclusion: SinkRouter通过机制性理解注意力汇聚现象，成功将理论洞见转化为实际的硬件加速实现，为长上下文LLM/LMM解码提供了一个无需训练、保持准确率且显著提升效率的解决方案。

Abstract: In long-context decoding for LLMs and LMMs, attention becomes increasingly memory-bound because each decoding step must load a large amount of KV-cache data from GPU memory. Existing acceleration strategies often trade efficiency for accuracy by relying on heuristic pruning that may discard useful information. At a deeper level, they also tend to indiscriminately preserve all high-scoring tokens, treat early tokens as indispensable anchors, or rely on heuristic head routing, reflecting an insufficient mechanistic understanding of the attention sink phenomenon. In this paper, we show that the attention sink phenomenon corresponds to a stable, reachable, and error-controllable fixed point constructed during training. Based on this insight, we propose SinkRouter, a training-free selective routing framework that detects the sink signal and skips computations that would otherwise produce near-zero output. To translate this mechanism into real-world acceleration, we develop a hardware-aware Triton kernel with block-level branching and Split-K parallelism. We conduct extensive evaluations on a diverse suite of long-context benchmarks, including LongBench, InfiniteBench, CVBench, MileBench, and MMVP, using both text-only and multimodal backbones such as Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.1-70B, Yi-9B-200K, LLaVA-1.5-7B, and LLaVA-1.5-13B. Across these settings, SinkRouter consistently improves decoding efficiency while maintaining competitive accuracy, and reaches 2.03x speedup with a 512K context.

</details>


### [264] [Towards Fully Parameter-Free Stochastic Optimization: Grid Search with Self-Bounding Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16888)
*Yuheng Zhao,Yu-Hu Yan,Amit Attia,Tomer Koren,Lijun Zhang,Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRASP网格搜索框架，实现了完全无参数的随机优化方法，在非凸情况下达到近最优收敛率，在凸情况下具有竞争性能，并提供了一个更严格的模型集成保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无参数随机优化方法虽然不需要问题参数的具体数值，但仍然依赖于参数的下界或上界等先验知识，属于“部分无参数”方法。作者旨在实现“完全无参数”方法，即算法输入不需要满足任何与真实问题参数相关的不可验证条件。

Method: 提出了一个强大的通用网格搜索框架GRASP，结合新颖的自界定分析技术，能够有效地确定参数的搜索范围。该方法通过模型集成作为网格搜索的最后一步。

Result: 在非凸情况下，提出了完全无参数方法，达到近最优收敛率（对数因子内）；在凸情况下，无参数方法在加速和通用性方面具有强竞争力；在插值方差表征下，为模型集成提供了更严格的保证。

Conclusion: GRASP框架成功实现了完全无参数的随机优化，在理论保证上与最优调整方法具有竞争力，同时消除了对问题参数先验知识的依赖，为参数无关优化算法设计提供了新的通用范式。

Abstract: Parameter-free stochastic optimization aims to design algorithms that are agnostic to the underlying problem parameters while still achieving convergence rates competitive with optimally tuned methods. While some parameter-free methods do not require the specific values of the problem parameters, they still rely on prior knowledge, such as the lower or upper bounds of them. We refer to such methods as ``partially parameter-free''. In this work, we target achieving ``fully parameter-free'' methods, i.e., the algorithmic inputs do not need to satisfy any unverifiable condition related to the true problem parameters. We propose a powerful and general grid search framework, named \textsc{Grasp}, with a novel self-bounding analysis technique that effectively determines the search ranges of parameters, in contrast to previous work. Our method demonstrates generality in: (i) the non-convex case, where we propose a fully parameter-free method that achieves near-optimal convergence rate, up to logarithmic factors; (ii) the convex case, where our parameter-free methods are competitive with strong performance in terms of acceleration and universality. Finally, we contribute a sharper guarantee for the model ensemble, a final step of the grid search framework, under interpolated variance characterization.

</details>


### [265] [Covariance-Based Structural Equation Modeling in Small-Sample Settings with $p>n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16894)
*Hiroki Hasegawa,Aoba Tamura,Yukihiko Okada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对小样本高维场景下传统因子结构方程模型无法有效估计的问题，本文提出了一种基于自协方差和交叉协方差分量重构的新型估计框架，成功实现了小样本情况下结构参数符号与方向的稳定恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于因子的结构方程模型（SEM）依赖于似然估计，并假设样本协方差矩阵是非奇异的。然而，在小样本且变量维度$p$大于样本量$n$的设定下，样本协方差矩阵变得奇异，导致传统的似然估计失效，模型无法有效运行。这促使研究者寻找一种能在小样本高维场景下保持稳定的估计方法。

Method: 本文提出了一种新颖的估计原则，将协方差结构重构为自协方差和交叉协方差分量。基于此重构，构建了一个基于似然的可行集，并结合相对误差约束。该框架允许在$p>n$的小样本环境中，针对符号和方向进行稳定的参数估计。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，该方法在小样本设定下表现出更高的稳定性，特别是在恢复结构参数的符号和方向方面效果显著，弥补了传统方法在这一领域的不足。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将基于协方差的结构方程模型的应用范围扩展至小样本设定，为解决$p>n$时的估计难题提供了有效方案。该方法能为决策制定提供具有实际意义的方向性信息。

Abstract: Factor-based Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) relies on likelihood-based estimation assuming a nonsingular sample covariance matrix, which breaks down in small-sample settings with $p>n$. To address this, we propose a novel estimation principle that reformulates the covariance structure into self-covariance and cross-covariance components. The resulting framework defines a likelihood-based feasible set combined with a relative error constraint, enabling stable estimation in small-sample settings where $p>n$ for sign and direction. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show improved stability, particularly in recovering the sign and direction of structural parameters. These results extend covariance-based SEM to small-sample settings and provide practically useful directional information for decision-making.

</details>


### [266] [Noise-Adaptive Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems Without Task-Specific Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16919)
*Yingzhi Xia,Setthakorn Tanomkiattikun,Liangli Zhen,Zaiwang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了N-HMC和NA-NHMC方法，通过在初始噪声空间进行哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样，有效解决了扩散模型在求解反问题时面临的局部最优和流形不可行问题，在多个线性和非线性反问题任务上显著优于现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的反问题求解方法存在明显局限性：基于优化的方法虽速度快，但易陷入局部最优且存在噪声过拟合问题；贝叶斯方法虽能利用强先验，但在去噪过程中强制测量一致性会导致流形不可行问题。这些缺陷限制了扩散模型在反问题中的实际表现。

Method: 提出Noise-space Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (N-HMC)方法，将反向扩散过程视为从初始噪声到干净图像的确定性映射，把推理过程完全转移到初始噪声空间。通过在噪声空间进行哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样，实现对解空间的全面探索，同时保证采样结果始终位于学习到的数据流形上。进一步扩展为噪声自适应变体NA-NHMC，能够处理噪声类型和水平未知的反问题。

Result: 在四个线性和三个非线性反问题上的大量实验表明，NA-NHMC在重建质量上显著优于近期的SOTA方法。该方法在不同超参数和初始化条件下表现鲁棒，证明了其优越的泛化能力和稳定性。

Conclusion: N-HMC/NA-NHMC通过创新的噪声空间采样策略，成功解决了扩散模型求解反问题的关键挑战，为反问题求解提供了一个理论基础扎实、实际性能优越的新框架，特别是在噪声特性未知场景下展现出重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have recently shown remarkable performance on inverse problems (IPs). Optimization-based methods can fast solve IPs using DMs as powerful regularizers, but they are susceptible to local minima and noise overfitting. Although DMs can provide strong priors for Bayesian approaches, enforcing measurement consistency during the denoising process leads to manifold infeasibility issues. We propose Noise-space Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (N-HMC), a posterior sampling method that treats reverse diffusion as a deterministic mapping from initial noise to clean images. N-HMC enables comprehensive exploration of the solution space, avoiding local optima. By moving inference entirely into the initial-noise space, N-HMC keeps proposals on the learned data manifold. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of our approach and extend the framework to a noise-adaptive variant (NA-NHMC) that effectively handles IPs with unknown noise type and level. Extensive experiments across four linear and three nonlinear inverse problems demonstrate that NA-NHMC achieves superior reconstruction quality with robust performance across different hyperparameters and initializations, significantly outperforming recent state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/NA-HMC/NA-HMC.

</details>


### [267] [Test-Time Adaptation for EEG Foundation Models: A Systematic Study under Real-World Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16926)
*Gabriel Jason Lee,Jathurshan Pradeepkumar,Jimeng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了NeuroAdapt-Bench基准，系统评估了EEG基础模型在分布偏移下的测试时适应方法，发现优化无关方法比基于梯度的方法更稳定可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在跨临床环境、设备和人群时面临分布偏移问题，限制了其实际部署。测试时适应（TTA）是一种无需源数据即可适应目标数据的解决方案，特别适合隐私法规严格和标注数据稀缺的医疗场景，但其在EEG领域的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出NeuroAdapt-Bench基准，系统评估多种TTA方法在EEG基础模型上的表现。实验涵盖多个预训练基础模型、多种下游任务，以及分布内、分布外和极端模态偏移（如耳-EEG）等异构数据集。

Result: 标准TTA方法在EEG上效果不一致，常常导致性能下降，基于梯度的方法尤其容易出现严重退化。相比之下，优化无关的方法表现出更好的稳定性和可靠的性能提升。

Conclusion: 现有TTA技术在EEG领域存在局限性，需要开发针对EEG特性的领域特定适应策略，本文为未来研究提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have shown strong potential for learning generalizable representations from large-scale neural data, yet their clinical deployment is hindered by distribution shifts across clinical settings, devices, and populations. Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers a promising solution by enabling models to adapt to unlabeled target data during inference without access to source data, a valuable property in healthcare settings constrained by privacy regulations and limited labeled data. However, its effectiveness for EEG remains largely underexplored. In this work, we introduce NeuroAdapt-Bench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating test-time adaptation methods on EEG foundation models under realistic distribution shifts. We evaluate representative TTA approaches from other domains across multiple pretrained foundation models, diverse downstream tasks, and heterogeneous datasets spanning in-distribution, out-of-distribution, and extreme modality shifts (e.g., Ear-EEG). Our results show that standard TTA methods yield inconsistent gains and often degrade performance, with gradient-based approaches particularly prone to heavy degradation. In contrast, optimization-free methods demonstrate greater stability and more reliable improvements. These findings highlight the limitations of existing TTA techniques in EEG, provide guidance for future development, and underscore the need for domain-specific adaptation strategies.

</details>


### [268] [D-QRELO: Training- and Data-Free Delta Compression for Large Language Models via Quantization and Residual Low-Rank Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16940)
*Junlin Li,Shuangyong Song,Guodong Du,Ngai Wong,Xuebo Liu,Yongxiang Li,Min Zhang,Jing Li,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DQRELO方法，通过结合一比特量化与残差低秩近似，解决了大规模数据微调后的LLM增量压缩难题，显著优于现有方法并总结了压缩设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的增量压缩方法在处理大规模数据集微调的模型时失效。研究发现，更大的SFT数据规模会放大增量参数的幅度、奇异值和熵，从而加剧压缩误差，亟需一种无训练、无数据的新方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: 提出了DQRELO（Delta Compression via Quantization and Residual Low-Rank），这是一种无需训练和数据的增量压缩方法。它首先利用粗粒度的一比特量化捕获增量的主要结构，随后采用补偿性残差低秩近似从较小的残差误差中恢复细粒度细节。

Result: 在涵盖密集型和MoE架构的多种LLM及多个领域的挑战性设置下进行实验，结果表明DQRELO性能优于现有方法。此外，通过广泛的实证分析，建立了增量压缩的关键设计原则，揭示了任务难度、架构和层位置如何产生可预测的模式。

Conclusion: DQRELO有效解决了大规模微调场景下的增量压缩问题，验证了量化与低秩近似结合的有效性。总结出的设计原则可指导生产系统制定最优压缩策略，具有重要的实践意义。

Abstract: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) accelerates taskspecific large language models (LLMs) development, but the resulting proliferation of finetuned models incurs substantial memory overhead. Delta compression addresses this by retaining a single pre-trained LLM with multiple compressed delta weights. However, existing methods fail on models fine-tuned with largescale datasets. We find that larger SFT data scale amplifies delta parameter magnitude, singular values, and entropy, exacerbating compression errors. To tackle this, we propose DQRELO (Delta Compression via Quantization and Residual Low-Rank), a novel training- and data-free delta compression method. It combines coarse-grained one-bit quantization to capture the dominant structure of the delta, followed by compensated residual low-rank approximation to recover fine-grained details from the smaller residual error. Experiments on various LLMs spanning dense and MoE architectures across multiple domains under this challenging setting demonstrate that DQRELO outperforms existing methods. Moreover, we establish key design principles for delta compression through extensive empirical analysis, demonstrating how task difficulty, architecture, and layer positioning create predictable patterns that can guide optimal compression strategies in production systems.

</details>


### [269] [L1 Regularization Paths in Linear Models by Parametric Gaussian Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16949)
*Yun-Peng Li,Hans-Andrea Loeliger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于状态空间模型的L1正则化路径计算方法，通过参数化高斯消息传递算法，实现了对L1正则化卡尔曼平滑、线性SVM和LASSO等问题的有效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L1正则化路径计算在许多机器学习和统计推断问题中至关重要，如LASSO和线性SVM。然而，传统方法在计算效率和适用范围方面存在局限性，因此需要开发一种更加通用且高效的算法框架。

Method: 提出了两种互为对偶的算法，分别适用于独立变量和因变量的L1正则化。核心方法是利用因子图中的参数化高斯消息传递（即卡尔曼型前向-后向递归），算法主要涉及矩阵乘法运算。

Result: 所提算法具有广泛的适用性，在计算复杂度上能够与现有方法相竞争，且在大多数情况下仅需进行矩阵乘法运算，降低了计算复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法为L1正则化路径计算提供了一种高效、通用的解决方案，适用于多种状态空间模型，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: The paper considers the computation of L1 regularization paths in a state space setting, which includes L1 regularized Kalman smoothing, linear SVM, LASSO, and more. The paper proposes two new algorithms, which are duals of each other; the first algorithm applies to L1 regularization of independent variables while the second applies to L1 regularization of dependent variables. The heart of the proposed algorithms is parametric Gaussian message passing (i.e., Kalman-type forward-backward recursions) in the pertinent factor graphs. The proposed methods are broadly applicable, they (usually) require only matrix multiplications, and their complexity can be competitive with prior methods in some cases.

</details>


### [270] [Open-TQ-Metal: Fused Compressed-Domain Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16957)
*Sai Vegasena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Open-TQ-Metal首次在Apple Silicon上实现融合压缩域注意力，使单张64GB消费级Mac能运行128K上下文的Llama 3.1 70B模型，实现48倍注意力加速和3.2倍KV缓存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理框架无法在消费级硬件上支持大模型的长上下文推理。现有KV缓存量化方法需要反量化步骤，带来额外开销。作者旨在通过直接在压缩表示上计算注意力，突破内存和计算瓶颈，实现消费级设备上的长上下文推理。

Method: 提出Open-TQ-Metal系统，在Apple Silicon上实现融合压缩域注意力。核心技术包括：(1) 实时将KV缓存量化为int4；(2) 通过自定义Metal计算着色器直接在压缩表示上计算注意力，消除所有中间反量化矩阵；(3) 提供首个跨架构KV缓存量化方法分析。

Result: 在Gemma 4 31B和Llama 3.1 70B上的330项实验表明：(1) 融合sdpa_int4内核在128K上下文下比反量化-注意力基线快48倍；(2) KV缓存内存从40GB降至12.5GB（3.2倍压缩）；(3) 保持与FP16推理完全相同的top-1 token预测；(4) 发现注意力缩放因子（而非模型大小）决定角度量化方案的成败，Gemma 4的attn_scale=1.0使其方向误差比Llama标准1/sqrt(d)缩放大25-100倍。

Conclusion: Open-TQ-Metal成功证明了在消费级硬件上实现大模型长上下文推理的可行性。跨架构分析揭示了注意力缩放因子对量化方法性能的关键影响，为未来模型设计和量化策略选择提供了重要指导，特别是对于使用非标准注意力缩放的模型架构。

Abstract: We present Open-TQ-Metal, the first implementation of fused compressed-domain attention on Apple Silicon, enabling 128K-context inference for Llama 3.1 70B on a single 64GB consumer Mac -- a configuration impossible with all existing inference frameworks. Open-TQ-Metal quantizes the KV cache to int4 on the fly and computes attention directly on the compressed representation via custom Metal compute shaders, eliminating all intermediate dequantization matrices. Across 330 experiments spanning two model families (Gemma 4 31B and Llama 3.1 70B), the fused sdpa_int4 kernel achieves 48x attention speedup at 128K context over the dequantize-then-attend baseline, reduces KV cache memory from 40 GB to 12.5 GB (3.2x compression), and maintains identical top-1 token predictions to FP16 inference. We further provide the first cross-architecture analysis of KV cache quantization methods, revealing that the attention scale factor -- not model size -- determines whether angular quantization schemes like PolarQuant succeed or fail, with Gemma 4's attn_scale=1.0 amplifying directional error 25-100x more than Llama's standard 1/sqrt(d) scaling.

</details>


### [271] [Hyperbolic Enhanced Representation Learning for Incomplete Multi-view Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16959)
*Tianyi Chen,Haobo Wang,Kai Tang,Gengyu Lyu,Tianlei Hu,Gang Chen,Hong Ma,Meixiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HERL框架，通过在双曲空间中进行表示学习，解决了不完整多视图聚类中欧氏方法建模层次数据时的几何失配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视图聚类方法通常基于欧氏空间，难以有效建模现实数据中固有的层次结构，导致表示学习出现语义模糊现象——样本表示在空间上靠近但语义不同的邻居。为解决这一几何失配问题，需要引入更契合层次数据特性的双曲空间建模方法。

Method: 在庞加莱球（Poincaré ball）上构建结构感知的潜在空间，设计了双重约束的双曲对比机制：基于角度的损失函数通过方向对齐保持语义同一性，基于距离的损失函数强制层次紧凑性。同时引入双曲原型头来校正全局结构漂移，通过对齐跨视图的层次感知原型分布来增强表示学习。

Result: 实验结果表明，HERL在多个数据集上持续超越现有最先进的不完整多视图聚类方法，证明了双曲空间建模的有效性。

Conclusion: HERL成功地将双曲几何引入不完整多视图聚类，通过解耦细粒度语义关联强化了聚类边界，并对数据恢复过程施加几何约束，为处理具有层次结构的复杂数据提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Incomplete Multi-View Clustering (IMVC) faces the challenge of learning discriminative representations from fragmentary observations while maintaining robustness against missing views. However, prevalent Euclidean-based methods suffer from a geometric mismatch when modeling real-world data with intrinsic hierarchies, leading to semantic blurring where representations drift towards spatially proximal but semantically distinct neighbors. To bridge this gap, we propose HERL, a Hyperbolic Enhanced Representation Learning framework for IMVC. Operating within the Poincaré ball, HERL constructs a structure-aware latent space to enhance representation learning. Specifically, we design a dual-constraint hyperbolic contrastive mechanism optimizing: an angular-based loss to preserve semantic identity via directional alignment, and a distance-based loss to enforce hierarchical compactness. Furthermore, a hyperbolic prototype head is introduced to rectify global structural drift by aligning cross-view hierarchy-aware prototype distributions. Consequently, HERL disentangles fine-grained semantic correlations to sharpen cluster boundaries and imposes geometric constraints to rectify the data recovery process. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that HERL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [272] [Evaluating Multimodal LLMs for Inpatient Diagnosis: Real-World Performance, Safety, and Cost Across Ten Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16980)
*Bruce A. Bassett,Amy Rouillard,Sitwala Mundia,Michael Cameron Gramanie,Linda Camara,Ziyaad Dangor,Shabir A. Madhi,Kajal Morar,Marlvin T. Ncube,Ismail Kalla,Haroon Saloojee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在南非公立医院的真实世界多模态住院病例回顾性评估中，多模态大语言模型（LLM）的诊断表现显著优于常规病房诊断。尽管不同模型成本差异巨大，但其性能差异较小（<15%），低成本模型表现与顶级模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型（LLM）在诊断支持方面的评估很少使用真实世界的多模态住院数据，尤其是在中低收入国家（LMIC）的公立医院环境中。本研究旨在填补这一空白，评估LLM在资源受限的真实临床场景中的实际应用价值和可行性。

Method: 研究团队开展了VALID研究，回顾性评估了来自南非一家三级公立医院的539个多模态住院病例。输入数据包括放射影像（CT、MRI、CXR）及其报告、实验室结果、临床笔记和生命体征。专家组对300个病例进行裁决以建立基本事实诊断。研究测试了10个多模态LLM的零样本输出，并使用校准的三模型LLM陪审团对所有输出和常规病房诊断进行评分（超过10,000次评估），主要结果为复合评分和胜率。

Result: (1) LLM性能紧密聚集（变化<15%），低成本模型表现与顶级模型相当；(2) 所有LLM在平均诊断和安全评分上显著优于常规病房诊断；(3) GPT-5.1表现最佳，其次是Gemini模型；(4) 添加放射科报告使性能提升6%；(5) 诊断与推理评分高度相关（ρ=0.85）；(6) 由于输入限制，输出率在65-100%之间变化。

Conclusion: 在真实世界的LMIC数据集中，多模态LLM尽管成本差异巨大但表现出相似诊断性能，且在平均安全指标上优于常规护理。在LMIC环境中，可负担性、稳健性和部署约束可能比边际性能差异更为重要，为资源受限地区的AI医疗部署提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed for diagnostic support, but few evaluations use real-world multimodal inpatient data, particularly in low and middle-income country (LMIC) public hospitals.
  Methods: We conducted VALID, a retrospective evaluation of 539 multimodal inpatient cases from a tertiary public hospital in South Africa. Inputs included radiology imaging (CT, MRI, CXR) and reports, laboratory results, clinical notes, and vital signs. Expert panels adjudicated 300 cases (balanced and discordant subsets) to establish ground truth diagnoses, differentials, and reasoning. Ten multimodal LLMs generated zero-shot outputs. A calibrated three-model LLM Jury scored all outputs and routine ward diagnoses across diagnostic accuracy, differential quality, reasoning, and patient safety (>10,000 evaluations). Primary outcomes were composite scores ($S_3$, $S_4$) and win rates.
  Results: (i) LLM performance was tightly clustered (<15% variation) despite large cost differences; low-cost models performed comparably to top models. (ii) All LLMs significantly outperformed routine ward diagnoses on average diagnostic and safety scores. (iii) Top performance was achieved by GPT-5.1, followed by Gemini models. (vi) Adding radiology reports improved performance by 6%. (v) Diagnostic and reasoning scores were highly correlated ($ρ= 0.85$). (vi) Output rates varied (65-100%) due to input constraints. Results were robust across subsets and evaluation design.
  Conclusions: Across a real-world LMIC dataset, multimodal LLMs showed similar diagnostic performance despite large cost differences and outperformed routine care on average safety metrics. Affordability, robustness, and deployment constraints may outweigh marginal performance differences in LMIC settings.

</details>


### [273] [In-Context Learning Under Regime Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16988)
*Carson Dudley,Yutong Bi,Xiaofeng Liu,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer模型在非平稳环境下的变点检测与适应能力，通过理论构建证明了Transformer可以解决上下文变点检测问题，并在合成实验和实际应用中验证了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳序列在控制、预测和决策制定中普遍存在。随着基于Transformer的基础模型越来越多地依赖上下文学习进行时间序列预测，理解这些模型在非平稳环境中检测和适应机制变化的能力变得至关重要。

Method: 将问题形式化为上下文变点检测问题；理论构建并证明存在能够解决此问题的Transformer模型，分析模型复杂度（层数和参数）与变点位置信息量之间的依赖关系；通过合成线性回归和线性动力系统实验验证理论结果；将变点知识编码到预训练基础模型中，应用于真实场景。

Result: 在合成数据（线性回归和线性动力系统）上，训练的Transformer在各信息水平下均达到最优基线性能；在传染病预测和FOMC公告期间金融波动率预测等真实任务中，编码并引入变点知识确实提升了预训练基础模型的性能，且无需重新训练。

Conclusion: Transformer模型具备解决上下文变点检测问题的能力，其模型复杂度依赖于对变点位置的了解程度；编码变点知识可以有效提升模型在现实世界机制变化场景中的表现，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Non-stationary sequences arise naturally in control, forecasting, and decision-making. The data-generating process shifts at unknown times, and models must detect the change, discard or downweight obsolete evidence, and adapt to new dynamics on the fly. Transformer-based foundation models increasingly rely on in-context learning for time series forecasting, tabular prediction, and continuous control. As these models are deployed in non-stationary environments, understanding their ability to detect and adapt to regime shifts is important. We formalize this as an in-context change-point detection problem and formally establish the existence of transformer models that solve this problem. Our construction demonstrates that model complexity, in layers and parameters, depends on the level of information available about the change-point location, from no knowledge to knowing exact timing. We validate our results with experiments on synthetic linear regression and linear dynamical systems, where trained transformers match the performance of optimal baselines across information levels. We also show that encoding and incorporating changepoint knowledge indeed improves the real-world performance of a pretrained foundation models on infectious disease forecasting and on financial volatility forecasting around Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) announcements without retraining, demonstrating practical applicability to real-world regime changes.

</details>


### [274] [Reference-state System Reliability method for scalable uncertainty quantification of coherent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17066)
*Ji-Eun Byun,Hyeuk Ryu,Junho Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出参考态系统可靠性（RSR）方法，通过利用参考状态对蒙特卡洛样本进行分类而非空间分解，解决了传统方法在组件数量增加时扩展性差的问题，实现了大规模系统概率评估的高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干系统在基础设施网络、供应链等领域应用广泛，但其概率评估仍具挑战性。现有的基于分解的方法在组件数量增加时扩展性差，计算成本随参考状态数量急剧上升，无法满足大规模系统的实时风险评估需求。

Method: 提出参考态系统可靠性（RSR）方法。与现有方法不同，RSR不使用参考状态将状态空间分解为不相交的超立方体，而是利用参考状态对蒙特卡洛样本进行分类，使计算成本对参考状态数量的敏感度显著降低。通过将样本和参考状态存储为矩阵并使用批量矩阵运算进行比较，RSR能够充分利用现代机器学习驱动的高吞吐量矩阵计算技术。

Result: RSR在10秒内完成了119个节点、295条边的图的系统状态概率评估，展现了大规模系统实时风险评估的潜力。该方法可扩展至涉及数十万个参考状态的问题，远超现有方法的能力范围，并能自然地推广至多状态系统。

Conclusion: RSR方法有效解决了现有分解方法在大规模系统概率评估中的扩展性问题，大幅提升了计算效率。然而，当边界参考状态数量极庞大时，RSR的收敛速度会减慢，这一局限性也与现有基于参考状态的方法相同，为未来研究基于学习的系统状态边界表示方法提供了动力。

Abstract: Coherent systems are representative of many practical applications, ranging from infrastructure networks to supply chains. Probabilistic evaluation of such systems remains challenging, however, because existing decomposition-based methods scale poorly as the number of components grows. To address this limitation, this study proposes the Reference-state System Reliability (RSR) method. Like existing approaches, RSR characterises the boundary between different system states using reference states in the component-state space. Where it departs from these methods is in how the state space is explored: rather than using reference states to decompose the space into disjoint hypercubes, RSR uses them to classify Monte Carlo samples, making computational cost significantly less sensitive to the number of reference states. To make this classification efficient, samples and reference states are stored as matrices and compared using batched matrix operations, allowing RSR to exploit the advances in high-throughput matrix computing driven by modern machine learning. We demonstrate that RSR evaluates the system-state probability of a graph with 119 nodes and 295 edges within 10~seconds, highlighting its potential for real-time risk assessment of large-scale systems. We further show that RSR scales to problems involving hundreds of thousands of reference states -- well beyond the reach of existing methods -- and extends naturally to multi-state systems. Nevertheless, when the number of boundary reference states grows exceedingly large, RSR's convergence slows down, a limitation shared with existing reference-state-based approaches that motivates future research into learning-based representations of system-state boundaries.

</details>


### [275] [Tree of Concepts: Interpretable Continual Learners in Non-Stationary Clinical Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17089)
*Dongkyu Cho,Xiyue Li,Samrachana Adhikari,Rumi Chunara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Tree of Concepts框架，通过固定基于规则的浅层决策树作为概念接口，在持续学习过程中实现模型适应性与可解释性的平衡，在医疗表格数据基准测试中表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临适应性与可解释性的两难困境：适应性强的深度网络缺乏可解释性，而可解释的决策树在分布漂移下表现脆弱，高风险领域（如医疗）亟需同时满足这两种特性。

Method: 提出Tree of Concepts框架，使用浅层决策树定义固定的基于规则的概念接口，训练概念瓶颈模型从原始特征预测这些概念；持续更新仅作用于概念提取器和标签头，保持概念语义随时间稳定。

Result: 在多个持续学习协议下的表格医疗基准测试中，该方法实现了比现有基线（包括重放增强变体）更强的稳定性-可塑性权衡。

Conclusion: 结构化概念接口能够支持持续适应，同时在非平稳、高风险领域保持一致的审计接口。

Abstract: Continual learning aims to update models under distribution shift without forgetting, yet many high-stakes deployments, such as healthcare, also require interpretability. In practice, models that adapt well (e.g., deep networks) are often opaque, while models that are interpretable (e.g., decision trees) are brittle under shift, making it difficult to achieve both properties simultaneously. In response, we propose Tree of Concepts, an interpretable continual learning framework that uses a shallow decision tree to define a fixed, rule-based concept interface and trains a concept bottleneck model to predict these concepts from raw features. Continual updates act on the concept extractor and label head while keeping concept semantics stable over time, yielding explanations that do not drift across sequential updates. On multiple tabular healthcare benchmarks under continual learning protocols, our method achieves a stronger stability-plasticity trade-off than existing baselines, including replay-enhanced variants. Our results suggest that structured concept interfaces can support continual adaptation while preserving a consistent audit interface in non-stationary, high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [276] [Live LTL Progress Tracking: Towards Task-Based Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17106)
*Noel Brindise,Cedric Langbort,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Live LTL Progress Tracking框架，通过跟踪向量实时更新有限线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范的满足状态，解决强化学习中非马尔可夫目标的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中常面临非马尔可夫目标的挑战，现有方法难以有效追踪和表示智能体在复杂多阶段任务中的执行进度。

Method: 提出一个基于有限线性时序逻辑（LTL）的框架，构建一个"跟踪向量"（tracking vector），在轨迹展开的每个时间步进行更新。该向量将规范状态标记为真、假或"开放"（未确定），应用于LTL公式树以编码任务执行的详细信息。

Result: 形式化地提出了Live LTL Progress Tracking框架及算法，提供了简单的工作示例，并展示了将其集成到强化学习模型中的多种途径。

Conclusion: 该框架为新的性能指标、多样化探索和奖励塑形提供了潜在工具，未来工作将应用于强化学习中的任务空间探索和多样化解决方案寻找等问题。

Abstract: Motivated by the challenge presented by non-Markovian objectives in reinforcement learning (RL), we present a novel framework to track and represent the progress of autonomous agents through complex, multi-stage tasks. Given a specification in finite linear temporal logic (LTL), the framework establishes a 'tracking vector' which updates at each time step in a trajectory rollout. The values of the vector represent the status of the specification as the trajectory develops, assigning true, false, or 'open' labels (where 'open' is used for indeterminate cases). Applied to an LTL formula tree, the tracking vector can be used to encode detailed information about how a task is executed over a trajectory, providing a potential tool for new performance metrics, diverse exploration, and reward shaping. In this paper, we formally present the framework and algorithm, collectively named Live LTL Progress Tracking, give a simple working example, and demonstrate avenues for its integration into RL models. Future work will apply the framework to problems such as task-space exploration and diverse solution-finding in RL.

</details>


### [277] [The Topological Trouble With Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17121)
*Michael C. Mozer,Shoaib Ahmed Siddiqui,Rosanne Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出前馈Transformer在动态状态跟踪方面的局限性，提出应通过循环架构实现隐式激活动态，并对循环和连续思维Transformer进行了系统分类，展望了增强状态空间模型等研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈Transformer的架构限制了其动态状态跟踪能力，随着输入步数增加，状态表示被推至更深层，导致浅层信息不可访问并最终耗尽模型深度。现有的动态深度或显式思维方法存在计算和内存效率低下的问题，因此需要重新关注循环架构中的隐式激活动态。

Method: 提出了一种循环和连续思维Transformer的分类体系，根据循环轴（深度vs步数）和输入token与循环步数的比率进行分类。

Result: 建立了一个系统的分类框架，用于理解和组织不同的循环Transformer架构，并识别出增强状态空间模型和粗粒度循环等有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 时间扩展认知需要从显式思维轨迹转向通过循环架构实现的隐式激活动态，这为将状态跟踪更好地集成到现代基础模型中提供了路径。

Abstract: Transformers encode structure in sequences via an expanding contextual history. However, their purely feedforward architecture fundamentally limits dynamic state tracking. State tracking -- the iterative updating of latent variables reflecting an evolving environment -- involves inherently sequential dependencies that feedforward networks struggle to maintain. Consequently, feedforward models push evolving state representations deeper into their layer stack with each new input step, rendering information inaccessible in shallow layers and ultimately exhausting the model's depth. While this depth limit can be bypassed by dynamic depth models and by explicit or latent thinking that externalizes state representations, these solutions are computationally and memory inefficient. In this article, we argue that temporally extended cognition requires refocusing from explicit thought traces to implicit activation dynamics via recurrent architectures. We introduce a taxonomy of recurrent and continuous-thought transformer architectures, categorizing them by their recurrence axis (depth versus step) and their ratio of input tokens to recurrence steps. Finally, we outline promising research directions, including enhanced state-space models and coarse-grained recurrence, to better integrate state tracking into modern foundation models.

</details>


### [278] [BOIL: Learning Environment Personalized Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17137)
*Rohan Patil,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了黑盒预言机信息学习（BOIL）过程，利用Pagerank算法和公共信息最大化，从复杂环境结构中高效提取有价值信息，以指导多智能体系统的长期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在复杂环境中导航面临挑战，需要从有限信息中高效提取洞察，以解决覆盖、巡逻和随机可达性等问题。

Method: 提出BOIL过程，结合Pagerank算法与公共信息最大化技术，从环境结构中提取信息，用于指导智能体的长期行为策略。

Result: 实验表明，BOIL生成的策略分布在长期时间范围内优于启发式方法，在复杂环境中显著提升了系统性能。

Conclusion: BOIL是一种可扩展的有效方法，能够从环境结构中提取关键信息，改善多智能体系统在复杂环境中的长期表现。

Abstract: Navigating complex environments poses challenges for multi-agent systems, requiring efficient extraction of insights from limited information. In this paper, we introduce the Blackbox Oracle Information Learning (BOIL) process, a scalable solution for extracting valuable insights from the environment structure. Leveraging the Pagerank algorithm and common information maximization, BOIL facilitates the extraction of information to guide long-term agent behavior applicable to problems such as coverage, patrolling, and stochastic reachability. Through experiments, we demonstrate the efficacy of BOIL in generating strategy distributions conducive to improved performance over extended time horizons, surpassing heuristic approaches in complex environments.

</details>


### [279] [SeekerGym: A Benchmark for Reliable Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17143)
*Remy Kim,Minseung Lee,Shuo Li,Osbert Bastani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeekerGym基准测试，用于评估AI代理信息检索的完整性及不确定性量化能力，实验显示最佳方法在Wikipedia和ML Surveys上的检索完整率仅为42.5%和29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI代理在信息检索方面虽然取得了成功，但在可信度方面仍面临根本性挑战。特别是深度研究代理在检索信息时，无法保证信息的完整性，检索信息的缺口可能导致偏差从而误导用户，即使提供的信息本身是正确且相关的。

Method: 引入SeekerGym基准测试来评估AI代理检索信息的完整性。每个任务是一个文档（如维基百科文章或机器学习综述论文），AI代理需要发出查询来从文档中检索段落。由于文档全面覆盖了某个主题，直接检索其章节的能力可以衡量信息检索的完整性。此外，该基准还测量代理对其信息完整性不确定性的量化能力。

Result: 对多个模型和算法进行基准测试，最佳方法在Wikipedia上的段落检索率为42.5%，在ML Surveys上为29.2%，表明仍有很大的改进空间。

Conclusion: 当前AI代理在信息检索完整性方面表现不佳，存在显著的改进空间，SeekerGym为评估和改进这一问题提供了有效的基准测试框架。

Abstract: Despite their substantial successes, AI agents continue to face fundamental challenges in terms of trustworthiness. Consider deep research agents, tasked with searching for information relevant to a given topic-while AI agents can perform effective information retrieval, there is little guarantee regarding the completeness of this information. Gaps in retrieved information can leave biases that mislead users even if the information they are given is correct and relevant. We introduce SeekerGym, a benchmark designed to evaluate the completeness of information retrieved by AI agents. In addition, SeekerGym also measures how well agents quantify their uncertainty in the completeness of their information; if an agent fails to retrieve all relevant information, it is useful for it to at least quantify how much might be missing. At a high level, each task in SeekerGym is a document (e.g., a Wikipedia article), and the AI agent must issue queries to retrieve passages from that document. Intuitively, the document comprehensively covers a topic, so the ability to retrieve its sections directly measures completeness of information retrieval. In addition to Wikipedia, we also consider machine learning survey papers, where the goal is to retrieve relevant sections of a survey paper. We benchmark several models and algorithms; the best approaches retrieve 42.5% of passages on Wikipedia and 29.2% on ML Surveys, leaving substantial room for improvement.

</details>


### [280] [Uncertainty Quantification in PINNs for Turbulent Flows: Bayesian Inference and Repulsive Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17156)
*Khemraj Shukla,Zongren Zou,Theo Kaeufer,Michael Triantafyllou,George Em Karniadakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了三种物理信息神经网络(PINNs)的概率扩展方法，用于湍流建模中的不确定性量化，发现贝叶斯PINNs提供最一致的不确定性估计，而函数空间排斥集成方法则在计算效率和精度间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理信息神经网络(PINNs)大多是确定性的，无法可靠地量化认知不确定性，这对于数据驱动的雷诺平均纳维-斯托克斯(RANS)建模等不适定问题至关重要。

Method: 提出了三种概率扩展框架：(i) 结合哈密顿蒙特卡洛采样和温度化多分量似然的贝叶斯PINNs，(ii) 蒙特卡洛Dropout方法，(iii) 在函数空间强制多样性的排斥深度集成方法。重点研究了集成多样性和似然温度化在改善PDE约束逆问题不确定性校准中的作用。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器、Re=3900的圆周绕流直接数值模拟数据和Re=10000的实验粒子图像测速数据等测试案例上进行评估。结果表明：贝叶斯PINNs在所有推断量中提供最一致的不确定性估计；函数空间排斥集成方法提供了计算高效的近似，在主要流场变量上具有竞争性精度。

Conclusion: 该研究为物理信息学习中的精度、计算成本和不确定性校准之间的权衡提供了定量见解，为数据驱动湍流建模中的不确定性量化提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have emerged as a promising framework for solving inverse problems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs), including the reconstruction of turbulent flow fields from sparse data. However, most existing PINN formulations are deterministic and do not provide reliable quantification of epistemic uncertainty, which is critical for ill-posed problems such as data-driven Reynolds-averaged Navier-Stokes (RANS) modeling. In this work, we develop and systematically evaluate a set of probabilistic extensions of PINNs for uncertainty quantification in turbulence modeling. The proposed framework combines (i) Bayesian PINNs with Hamiltonian Monte Carlo sampling and a tempered multi-component likelihood, (ii) Monte Carlo dropout, and (iii) repulsive deep ensembles that enforce diversity in function space. Particular emphasis is placed on the role of ensemble diversity and likelihood tempering in improving uncertainty calibration for PDE-constrained inverse problems. The methods are assessed on a hierarchy of test cases, including the Van der Pol oscillator and turbulent flow past a circular cylinder at Reynolds numbers Re=3,900 (direct numerical simulation data) and Re = 10,000 (experimental particle image velocimetry data). The results demonstrate that Bayesian PINNs provide the most consistent uncertainty estimates across all inferred quantities, while function-space repulsive ensembles offer a computationally efficient approximation with competitive accuracy for primary flow variables. These findings provide quantitative insight into the trade-offs between accuracy, computational cost, and uncertainty calibration in physics-informed learning, and offer practical guidance for uncertainty quantification in data-driven turbulence modeling.

</details>


### [281] [RosettaSearch: Multi-Objective Inference-Time Search for Protein Sequence Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17175)
*Meghana Kshirsagar,Allen Nie,Ching-An Cheng,Fanglei Xue,Rahul Dodhia,Juan Lavista Ferres,Kevin K. Yang,Frank DiMaio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RosettaSearch，一种基于大语言模型的推理时多目标优化方法，通过搜索算法和结构预测模型反馈优化蛋白质序列，成功率和结构保真度显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的先进蛋白质序列设计模型（如LigandMPNN）在单次解码时难以生成高保真度的设计，存在优化瓶颈。研究旨在利用大语言模型的推理能力，在推理阶段进行多目标优化，以突破单次解码的限制。

Method: 提出RosettaSearch框架，将大语言模型（LLM）作为生成式优化器嵌入搜索算法中。该方法通过RosettaFold3结构预测模型计算奖励，实现可控的探索与利用平衡。进一步扩展为多模态版本，利用视觉语言模型将预测的蛋白质结构图像作为反馈信号指导序列生成。

Result: 在400个次优序列的大规模评估中，相比LigandMPNN单次解码，结构保真度指标提升18%至68%，设计成功率提高2.5倍。该结果在独立结构预测模型（Chai-1）验证下表现稳健，并在两个不同LLM家族（o4-mini和Gemini-3）上均具泛化性，性能随模型推理能力增强而提升。在计算生成的骨架上同样验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: RosettaSearch证明了在推理阶段结合LLM与结构预测反馈进行优化的有效性，能够显著提升蛋白质序列设计质量。生成的序列轨迹可作为训练数据资源，代码与数据集将公开发布，为蛋白质设计领域提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: We introduce RosettaSearch, an inference-time multi-objective optimization approach for protein sequence optimization. We use large language models (LLMs) as a generative optimizer within a search algorithm capable of controlled exploration and exploitation, using rewards computed from RosettaFold3, a structure prediction model. In a large-scale evaluation, we apply RosettaSearch to 400 suboptimal sequences generated by LigandMPNN (a state-of-the-art model trained for protein sequence design), recovering high-fidelity designs that LigandMPNN's single-pass decoding fails to produce. RosettaSearch's designs show improvements in structural fidelity metrics ranging between 18\% to 68\%, translating to a 2.5$\times$ improvement in design success rate. We observe that these gains in success rate are robust when RosettaSearch-designed sequences are evaluated with an independent structure prediction oracle (Chai-1) and generalize across two distinct LLM families (o4-mini and Gemini-3), with performance scaling consistently with reasoning capability. We further demonstrate that RosettaSearch improves sequence fidelity for ProteinMPNN-designed sequences on \textit{de novo} backbones from the Dayhoff atlas, showing that the approach generalizes beyond native protein structures to computationally generated backbones. We also demonstrate a multi-modal extension of RosettaSearch with vision-language models, where images of predicted protein structures are used as feedback to incorporate structural context to guide protein sequence generation. The sequence trajectories generated by our approach can be used as training data in sequence design models or in post-training and will be released along with the code and datasets upon publication.

</details>


### [282] [Decomposing the Depth Profile of Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17177)
*Jayadev Billa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对240次微调实验的系统分析，发现表征变化集中在输出邻近层是普遍现象。通过逐层控制实验揭示了架构类型（顺序块vs并行块）、模型规模和任务目标对深度分布剖面的差异化影响，将深度方向的表征变化梯度视为多种因素共同作用的复合现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调能够使预训练网络适应新目标，但表征变化的深度分布剖面究竟反映了模型的内在属性还是梯度流的大小，此前未被直接测试。作者旨在系统性探究这一基本问题。

Method: 在四种架构族（编码器和解码器Transformer、状态空间模型、RNN）的15个模型上进行了240次微调实验，参数规模从125M到6.9B。除标准训练外，还设计了逐层控制实验，在每个优化器步骤后均衡各层的‖ΔW‖/‖W‖，以分离梯度流大小的影响。

Result: 标准训练中，除一次运行外，表征变化均集中在输出邻近层。在逐层控制下，该剖面在特定条件下保持或消失：125M-350M规模下，顺序块架构（BERT、OPT、GPT-2）在各目标中保持斜率，而并行块架构（Pythia、CodeGen）仅在因果语言建模目标中保持；该架构差异在1.3B-1.4B规模时缩小。标准训练中，剖面陡峭度与初始化时的无训练目标距离相关，剖面宽度主要由架构决定。

Conclusion: 深度方向的表征变化梯度（局部性梯度）是一个复合现象，其组成成分依赖于模型规模。架构类型、规模和目标函数共同决定了微调过程中表征变化的深度分布剖面。

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts pretrained networks to new objectives. Whether the resulting depth profile of representational change reflects an intrinsic property of the model or the magnitude of gradient flow has not been tested directly. We measure this profile across 240 fine-tuning runs spanning 15 models in four architecture families (encoder and decoder transformers, a state-space model, and an RNN) at scales from 125M to 6.9B parameters. Representational change concentrates in output-proximal layers in every standard-training run except one. We apply a per-layer control that equalizes $\|ΔW\|/\|W\|$ across layers after each optimizer step. Under this control, the profile persists in some conditions and collapses in others. At 125M--350M, sequential-block architectures (BERT, OPT, GPT-2) retain the slope across tested objectives while parallel-block architectures (Pythia, CodeGen) retain it only for causal-language-modeling objectives. This architectural distinction narrows at 1.3B--1.4B, where both block types show positive equal-step slopes for CausalLM. Under standard training, profile shape is described by two additional axes: steepness tracks a training-free objective distance at initialization, and profile width is dominated by architecture. We treat the locality gradient, the depthwise slope of representational change, as a composite phenomenon whose components are scale-dependent.

</details>


### [283] [Do LLM-derived graph priors improve multi-agent coordination?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17191)
*Nikunj Gupta,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次验证了利用大语言模型(LLM)生成的图先验能够有效提升多智能体强化学习(MARL)中的协调性能，仅需1.5B参数的小型模型即可生效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在协作型多智能体强化学习中，现有协调图构建方法存在显著缺陷：人工指定拓扑缺乏灵活性，基于距离的启发式方法语义信息不足，而完全从交互中学习结构则数据需求大且脆弱。研究旨在探索是否可以利用LLM从自然语言描述中推断潜在的协调模式，生成有用的协调图先验。

Method: 1. 利用轻量级开源LLM，基于智能体观测的自然语言描述推断潜在协调模式，生成协调图先验。
2. 将生成的图先验集成到基于图神经网络(GNN)的MARL流程中，通过图卷积层实现。
3. 在Multi-Agent Particle Environment (MPE)基准的四个协作场景上进行评估，对比范围涵盖独立学习器到最先进的图方法。
4. 对五种紧凑型开源LLM进行消融实验，评估模型选择对先验质量的敏感性。

Result: 1. 首次提供定量证据证明LLM生成的图先验可以增强动态多智能体环境中的协调能力和适应性。
2. 证明参数量仅为1.5B的小型模型足以进行有效的先验生成，无需依赖超大模型。

Conclusion: LLM具备从自然语言中提取语义信息以辅助MARL协调决策的能力，为解决协调图构建难题提供了新范式。该方法兼具语义理解与数据效率优势，且可在资源受限的小型模型上实现，具有良好的实用前景。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is crucial for AI systems that operate collaboratively in distributed and adversarial settings, particularly in multi-domain operations (MDO). A central challenge in cooperative MARL is determining how agents should coordinate: existing approaches must either hand-specify graph topology, rely on proximity-based heuristics, or learn structure entirely from environment interaction; all of which are brittle, semantically uninformed, or data-intensive. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate useful coordination graph priors for MARL by using minimal natural language descriptions of agent observations to infer latent coordination patterns. These priors are integrated into MARL algorithms via graph convolutional layers within a graph neural network (GNN)-based pipeline, and evaluated on four cooperative scenarios from the Multi-Agent Particle Environment (MPE) benchmark against baselines spanning the full spectrum of coordination modeling, from independent learners to state-of-the-art graph-based methods. We further ablate across five compact open-source LLMs to assess the sensitivity of prior quality to model choice. Our results provide the first quantitative evidence that LLM-derived graph priors can enhance coordination and adaptability in dynamic multi-agent environments, and demonstrate that models as small as 1.5B parameters are sufficient for effective prior generation.

</details>


### [284] [Demystifying the unreasonable effectiveness of online alignment methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17207)
*Enoch Hyunwook Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从决策中心的温度零后悔准则出发，证明标准贪婪在线对齐方法（如在线RLHF和在线DPO）能实现常数级累积后悔，从而解释了其在实践中的优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于KL正则化的后悔分析结果为O(log T)，与纯贪婪更新对齐方法在实践中的优异表现存在理论上的悲观性 mismatch，主要原因是KL正则化后悔将学习代价与探索性随机化混淆。

Method: 引入温度零后悔准则，即在推理时仅评估排名第一的响应；在该决策中心性能框架下分析标准贪婪在线对齐方法（如在线RLHF和在线DPO）的理论性能。

Result: 证明标准贪婪在线对齐方法在温度零后悔准则下可达到常数(O(1))累积后悔。

Conclusion: 通过分离识别最优响应的代价与正则化引入的随机性，为贪婪对齐的实际高效性提供了更精确的理论解释。

Abstract: Iterative alignment methods based on purely greedy updates are remarkably effective in practice, yet existing theoretical guarantees of \(O(\log T)\) KL-regularized regret can seem pessimistic relative to their empirical performance. In this paper, we argue that this mismatch arises from the regret criterion itself: KL-regularized regret conflates the statistical cost of learning with the exploratory randomization induced by the softened training policy. To separate these effects, we study the traditional temperature-zero regret criterion, which evaluates only the top-ranked response at inference time. Under this decision-centric notion of performance, we prove that standard greedy online alignment methods, including online RLHF and online DPO, achieve constant \((O(1))\) cumulative regret. By isolating the cost of identifying the best response from the stochasticity induced by regularization, our results provide a sharper theoretical explanation for the practical superb efficiency of greedy alignment.

</details>


### [285] [Guardrails in Logit Space: Safety Token Regularization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17210)
*Thong Bach,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了安全token正则化（STR）方法，通过在微调过程中约束安全相关token的logits，以轻量级的方式有效保持大模型的安全对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全对齐技术主要关注预训练阶段，而将大模型在新领域上进行微调（即使使用良性数据集）往往会降低其安全对齐性能，导致模型行为发生不良偏移。

Method: 提出安全token正则化（STR），一种轻量级方法。该方法是识别来自对齐良好的模型的拒绝模板中的显著token，并在训练过程中约束其相关的logits，从而防止关键安全行为的丢失。与强化学习或偏好优化方法不同，STR计算开销极小，且能与LoRA等参数高效微调技术无缝集成。

Result: 综合实验表明，该方法在安全性能上与最先进的方法相当，同时保留了特定任务的实用性，且实现开销极小。此外，STR还能增强训练稳定性并提升整体性能。

Conclusion: 这项工作为微调大模型提供了一种实用、易部署且低开销的持续安全对齐策略。

Abstract: Fine-tuning well-aligned large language models (LLMs) on new domains often degrades their safety alignment, even when using benign datasets. Existing safety alignment techniques primarily focus on pretraining, leaving fine-tuned models vulnerable to behavioral shifts. In this work, we introduce safety token regularization (STR), a lightweight method designed to preserve safety properties during fine-tuning. Our approach identifies salient tokens from rejection templates of well-aligned models and constrains their associated logits during training, preventing the loss of critical safety behaviors. Unlike reinforcement learning or preference optimization methods, STR requires minimal additional computation and seamlessly integrates with parameter-efficient fine-tuning techniques such as LoRA. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves safety performance on par with state-of-the-art methods, while preserving task-specific utility and requiring minimal implementation overhead. Furthermore, we show that safety token regularization enhances training stability and overall performance beyond safety considerations alone. This work offers a practical and readily deployable strategy for continual safety alignment in fine-tuned LLMs.

</details>


### [286] [Continual Safety Alignment via Gradient-Based Sample Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17215)
*Thong Bach,Dung Nguyen,Thao Minh Le,Truyen Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现高梯度样本会导致安全对齐严重退化，提出了一种基于梯度的样本选择方法，通过过滤高梯度样本来在微调过程中有效保持模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在持续适应新任务时需要保持安全对齐，然而即使是在良性数据上进行微调，也往往会损害模型的安全行为（如拒绝有害请求、真实性和常识推理能力）。作者旨在探究哪些训练样本会导致对齐漂移，以解决这一关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于梯度的样本选择方法，在微调过程中识别并过滤掉高梯度样本。该方法基于实证分析发现：高梯度样本会导致更大的安全退化并推动模型趋向预训练分布，而中等梯度样本则能在最小化对齐损失的同时实现任务学习。方法无需精心策划的安全数据或架构修改。

Result: 在多个模型系列的持续领域任务上，该方法显著改善了对齐保持能力，同时保持了具有竞争力的任务性能。实验表明该方法在选择比例、任务顺序和多种攻击基准测试中均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过数据中心的视角分析发现样本对安全对齐的贡献不均等，基于梯度的样本选择策略能够有效平衡任务学习与安全保持，为大语言模型的持续适应提供了一个简单而有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models require continuous adaptation to new tasks while preserving safety alignment. However, fine-tuning on even benign data often compromises safety behaviors, including refusal of harmful requests, truthfulness, and commonsense reasoning. We investigate which training samples cause alignment drift through a data-centric lens. Our empirical analysis shows samples contribute unequally: high-gradient samples cause greater safety degradation and drive models toward pretrained distributions, while moderate-gradient samples enable task learning with minimal alignment loss. We propose gradient-based sample selection that filters high-gradient samples during fine-tuning. Across multiple model families on continual domain tasks, our method substantially improves alignment preservation while maintaining competitive task performance, without requiring curated safe data or architectural modifications. Our method is robust across selection ratios, task orderings, and diverse attack benchmarks.

</details>


### [287] [LASER: Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17224)
*Ege Çakar,Ketan Ali Raghu,Lia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了递归模型(TRMs)推理轨迹的激活流形位于低维线性子空间，并据此提出LASER框架，在无精度损失的情况下实现约60%的激活内存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归架构（如TRMs）通过迭代隐计算执行隐式推理，但其推理轨迹的几何结构尚未被充分理解。本文旨在探究TRMs在递归展开过程中的激活流形结构，以揭示其内在的计算机制并探索优化潜力。

Method: 通过分析TRMs的激活流形，发现激活占据有效的低维线性子空间，其主方向可通过低成本的幂迭代动态追踪。基于此发现，提出了LASER（Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion）框架，该框架通过无矩阵子空间追踪技术维护演化的低秩基，并结合保真度触发的重置机制进行动态压缩。

Result: 研究发现权重共享将迭代计算集中在少数主导特征方向上，且这种集中程度在不同计算位置差异显著。LASER框架成功实现了约60%的激活内存节省，且未造成统计学上显著的精度下降。

Conclusion: 本文不仅揭示了递归架构在隐式推理过程中表征容量分配的内在规律（集中于少数主导特征方向），还证明了利用该结构可有效提升潜在计算的效率与稳定性，为未来递归网络的高效设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recursive architectures such as Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) perform implicit reasoning through iterative latent computation, yet the geometric structure of these reasoning trajectories remains poorly understood. We investigate the activation manifold of TRMs during recursive unrolling and find that activations occupy an effectively linear, low-dimensional subspace whose principal directions can be tracked dynamically with cheap power iterations. This suggests that weight-sharing concentrates iterative computation along a small number of dominant eigendirections, and we find that this concentration varies sharply across computational sites. We exploit this structure through LASER (Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion), a dynamic compression framework that maintains an evolving low-rank basis via matrix-free subspace tracking with a fidelity-triggered reset mechanism, achieving ${\sim}60\%$ activation memory savings with no statistically significant accuracy degradation. Our analysis raises questions about how recursive architectures allocate representational capacity during implicit reasoning, and whether this concentration can be exploited to improve the efficiency and stability of latent computation.

</details>


### [288] [Revisiting Auxiliary Losses for Conditional Depth Routing: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17228)
*Qingwei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在条件深度执行的门控训练中，常用的oracle效用回归与排序监督（util/rank）实际上是有害的；移除它们后，简单的MLP门控与复杂的JEPA引导门控性能相当，且训练成本降低约39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件深度执行通过门控机制使部分token走轻量级FFN，其余走完整FFN以节省计算。然而门控训练存在核心困难：门控决策需传播多层才影响语言建模损失，导致梯度微弱且噪声大。虽然常用辅助损失来稳定训练，但预测性辅助损失与显式分数监督之间的相互作用尚未在受控条件下系统比较。

Method: 在157.5M参数的decoder-only模型上评估两种门控设计：(1) MLP门控(G1)，将隐藏状态映射为效用分数；(2) JEPA引导门控(G3)，增加动作条件预测器，在低维潜在空间预测执行完整/廉价路径的结果。采用50%完整路径预算，在fineweb-edu子集上进行3次不同种子的实验。

Result: 关键发现：联合移除util/rank辅助损失后，两种门控在3/3种子上均获得更优的LM损失和更快的阈值命中速度，G3相对于G1的早期-中期优势消失。原因是oracle标签假设后续层均执行完整路径，而门控执行只让部分token走完整路径，导致util/rank在当前方案下净负面影响。移除后训练FLOPs代理从~1.53x降至~1.07x（V100-32GB上从2.87h降至1.75h，节省约39%）。

Conclusion: 在所研究的实验条件下，广泛使用的oracle风格效用回归与成对排序监督在条件深度执行的门控训练中实际上是负面的，移除它们可同时提升性能并大幅降低训练成本。结论仅适用于本研究所考察的实验设定。

Abstract: Conditional depth execution routes a subset of tokens through a lightweight cheap FFN while the remainder execute the standard full FFN at each controlled layer. The central difficulty is gate training: the gate decision must propagate through many layers before it influences the language modeling (LM) loss, so the resulting gradients are weak and noisy. Auxiliary losses are commonly stacked to stabilise training, yet the interactions among them -- particularly between a predictive auxiliary and explicit score supervision -- have not been systematically compared under controlled conditions.
