  We evaluate two gate designs under a 157.5M-parameter decoder-only model with controller-only training, 50% full-path budget, and 3-seed runs on a fineweb-edu subset. The MLP gate (G1) maps the current hidden state to a utility score; the JEPA-guided gate (G3) adds an action-conditional predictor that forecasts, in a low-dimensional latent space, the outcome of executing full vs. cheap per token, aligned against a fixed target head. Under the standard recipe with oracle-style utility regression and pairwise rank supervision (util/rank), G3 improves early-to-mid optimisation over G1 in 3/3 seeds (lower avg LM, faster threshold hits, ~10.3x lower grad norms), with 20k-step endpoint LM within a 0.005 heuristic reference.
  A key finding (ablation A3): jointly removing util/rank improves best/avg LM and threshold-hit speed in 3/3 seeds for both gates, and the early-to-mid advantage of G3 over G1 disappears. We trace this to an off-policy oracle label that assumes all subsequent layers execute full, whereas gated execution routes only a fraction through full -- making util/rank net-negative under the current recipe. Removing util/rank also cuts the training FLOPs proxy from ~1.53x to ~1.07x full-only (2.87h to 1.75h on a V100-32GB, ~39%). Conclusions are scoped to the studied regime.

</details>


### [289] [Fully Analog Resonant Recurrent Neural Network via Metacircuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17277)
*Zixin Zhou,Tianxi Jiang,Menglong Yang,Zhihua Feng,Qingbo He,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于耦合电学局部谐振器的全模拟谐振循环神经网络（R²NN），通过元电路架构和改进的力电类比实现了训练模型到物理硬件的精确映射，在触觉感知、语音识别和状态监测等任务中验证了其跨领域通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理神经网络相比传统数字架构在边缘智能应用中具有推理速度快和能效高的优势，但现有技术难以将训练好的模型精确映射到物理硬件上，导致可扩展、端到端的全模拟循环神经网络在时序信息处理中的实现面临挑战。

Method: 提出了一种由耦合电学局部谐振器组成的元电路架构来实现全模拟谐振循环神经网络（R²NN）。通过改进的力电类比建立了R²NN模型与元电路元件之间的直接映射关系。该架构集成了可全局训练的电阻耦合和局部谐振（产生有效的频率依赖负电阻），塑造了一个阻抗景观，引导电流沿频率选择性路径流动，从而直接提取判别性频谱特征。

Result: 该机制实现了对原始模拟输入的实时时间分类，并绕过了模数转换过程。通过集成硬件在触觉感知、语音识别和状态监测三个不同领域的应用，成功展示了该框架的跨领域通用性。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一种可扩展的、全模拟的智能时序处理范式，为低延迟、资源高效的物理神经硬件铺平了道路，推动了边缘智能的发展。

Abstract: Physical neural networks offer a transformative route to edge intelligence, providing superior inference speed and energy efficiency compared to conventional digital architectures. However, realizing scalable, end-to-end, fully analog recurrent neural networks for temporal information processing remains challenging due to the difficulty of faithfully mapping trained network models onto physical hardware. Here we present a fully analog resonant recurrent neural network (R$^2$NN) implemented via a metacircuit architecture composed of coupled electrical local resonators. A reformulated mechanical-electrical analogy establishes a direct mapping between the R$^2$NN model and metacircuit elements, enabling accurate physical implementation of trained neural network parameters. By integrating jointly trainable global resistive coupling and local resonances, which generate effective frequency-dependent negative resistances, the architecture shapes an impedance landscape that steers currents along frequency-selective pathways. This mechanism enables direct extraction of discriminative spectral features, facilitating real-time temporal classification of raw analog inputs while bypassing analog-to-digital conversion. We demonstrate the cross-domain versatility of this framework using integrated hardware for tactile perception, speech recognition, and condition monitoring. This work establishes a scalable, fully analog paradigm for intelligent temporal processing and paves the way for low-latency, resource-efficient physical neural hardware for edge intelligence.

</details>


### [290] [REALM: Reliable Expertise-Aware Language Model Fine-Tuning from Noisy Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17289)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Mark Beliaev,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REALM方法，通过无监督学习同时估计模型参数和标注者专业度，解决了标注者能力异质性导致的监督微调噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督微调流程聚合多个标注者标签时采用多数投票或简单平均，丢弃了标注者身份信息，使模型直接将不可靠标注者的错误吸收到参数中，忽视了标注者能力的异质性。

Method: 提出REALM方法，将每个观测标签建模为模型预测和均匀随机猜测的混合，以标注者的学习专业度加权，无监督地联合学习模型参数和每个标注者的专业度标量值；在多任务设置中扩展为学习专业度矩阵，捕获每个标注者在不同任务上的可靠性。

Result: 在五个问答基准上，使用三种规模的Flan-T5模型在模拟噪声标注下进行微调，该方法在绝大多数单任务和多任务设置中均一致优于朴素噪声SFT，在最具对抗性的场景下准确率提升高达50%，且增益随模型规模增加而增长。

Conclusion: REALM能够有效识别并降低低质量标注者的影响，提升模型在噪声标注场景下的鲁棒性，且性能优势随模型规模扩大更加显著，为处理异质标注者群体提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning of large language models relies on human-annotated data, yet annotation pipelines routinely involve multiple crowdworkers of heterogeneous expertise. Standard practice aggregates labels via majority vote or simple averaging, discarding annotator identity and causing the model to absorb the errors of unreliable annotators directly into its parameters. We propose REALM, a method that jointly learns the model parameters and a scalar expertise value for each annotator entirely unsupervised, requiring no supervision beyond annotator identity. The key idea is to model each observed label as a mixture between the model's prediction and a uniform random guess, weighted by the annotator's learned expertise. We extend REALM to a multi-task setting via a learned expertise matrix that captures per-annotator reliability across tasks. We evaluate on five question answering benchmarks, fine-tuning three sizes of Flan-T5 under simulated noisy annotations. The proposed algorithm consistently outperforms the naive noisy SFT in the large majority of single- and multi-task settings, across datasets, model sizes, and noise types, with accuracy improvements of up to $50\%$ in the most adversarial regime and gains that grow with model capacity.

</details>


### [291] [Interpolating Discrete Diffusion Models with Controllable Resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17310)
*Marcel Kollovieh,Sirine Ayadi,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了IDDM（插值离散扩散模型），通过可控重采样机制减少对中间潜在状态的依赖，解决了现有离散扩散模型中掩码扩散的不可逆错误和均匀扩散的样本质量低的问题，在分子图生成和文本生成任务中展现出竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型存在根本性局限：掩码扩散模型因过早去掩码会导致不可逆错误；均匀扩散模型虽然允许自我纠正，但由于过度依赖中间潜在状态，往往产生低质量样本。需要一种既能减少误差累积又能有效纠正标记的方法。

Method: 提出IDDM（插值离散扩散模型），其核心是可控重采样机制，部分地将概率质量重置为边缘分布，从而减轻误差累积并实现更有效的标记纠正。IDDM指定了一个生成过程，其转移在保持当前状态、从先验重采样和向目标状态翻转之间进行插值，同时强制边缘一致性并完全解耦训练与推理。

Result: 在分子图生成和文本生成任务上与最先进的离散扩散模型进行基准测试，证明了IDDM具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: IDDM通过引入可控重采样机制和插值生成过程，成功解决了现有离散扩散模型的关键局限性，为离散数据的生成建模提供了一个更有效的框架。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models form a powerful class of generative models across diverse domains, including text and graphs. However, existing approaches face fundamental limitations. Masked diffusion models suffer from irreversible errors due to early unmasking, while uniform diffusion models, despite enabling self-correction, often yield low-quality samples due to their strong reliance on intermediate latent states. We introduce IDDM, an Interpolating Discrete Diffusion Model, that improves diffusion by reducing dependence on intermediate latent states. Central to IDDM is a controllable resampling mechanism that partially resets probability mass to the marginal distribution, mitigating error accumulation and enabling more effective token corrections. IDDM specifies a generative process whose transitions interpolate between staying at the current state, resampling from a prior, and flipping toward the target state, while enforcing marginal consistency and fully decoupling training from inference. We benchmark our model against state-of-the-art discrete diffusion models across molecular graph generation as well as text generation tasks, demonstrating competitive performance.

</details>


### [292] [A Survey of Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models under Data Scarcity: Challenges and Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17312)
*Zhiyin Yu,Yuchen Mou,Juncheng Yan,Junyu Luo,Chunchun Chen,Xing Wei,Yunhui Liu,Hongru Sun,Yuxing Zhang,Jun Xu,Yatao Bian,Ming Zhang,Wei Ye,Tieke He,Jie Yang,Guanjie Zheng,Zhonghai Wu,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai,Xiao Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是首篇针对数据稀缺条件下大语言模型强化学习的系统综述，提出了一个包含数据、训练和框架三个视角的分层框架，对现有方法进行了分类和总结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的重要后训练范式，但面临着高质量外部监督数据有限和模型生成经验受限等数据稀缺挑战，使得数据高效的强化学习成为关键研究方向。

Method: 提出了一种自下而上的分层框架，包含三个互补视角：以数据为中心的视角、以训练为中心的视角和以框架为中心的视角；基于此开发了现有方法的分类法，并总结和分析了每个类别中的代表性方法。

Result: 建立了一个系统性的方法分类体系，总结了各类方法的代表性工作，分析了它们的优势和局限性，为理解数据高效强化学习的设计空间提供了清晰的概念基础。

Conclusion: 本综述为未来的研究提供了全面的路线图，旨在指导该新兴领域的研究者，并激发面向大语言模型更高效、可扩展的强化学习后训练的新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, reinforcement learning for LLMs faces substantial data scarcity challenges, including the limited availability of high-quality external supervision and the constrained volume of model-generated experience. These limitations make data-efficient reinforcement learning a critical research direction. In this survey, we present the first systematic review of reinforcement learning for LLMs under data scarcity. We propose a bottom-up hierarchical framework built around three complementary perspectives: the data-centric perspective, the training-centric perspective, and the framework-centric perspective. We develop a taxonomy of existing methods, summarize representative approaches in each category, and analyze their strengths and limitations. Our taxonomy aims to provide a clear conceptual foundation for understanding the design space of data-efficient RL for LLMs and to guide researchers working in this emerging area. We hope this survey offers a comprehensive roadmap for future research and inspires new directions toward more efficient and scalable reinforcement learning post-training for LLMs.

</details>


### [293] [SigGate-GT: Taming Over-Smoothing in Graph Transformers via Sigmoid-Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17324)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SigGate-GT通过在GraphGPS框架中引入sigmoid门控机制，有效缓解了图变换器中的过平滑和注意力熵退化问题，在分子预测任务上达到了新的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现图变换器中的过平滑（节点表示随深度增加而坍塌）和注意力熵退化问题的根源与大语言模型中的注意力汇聚（attention sinks）现象相同：softmax注意力的归一化约束迫使每个节点必须关注某些位置，即使不存在有信息的信号。受最近关于逐元素sigmoid门控能消除大语言模型中注意力汇聚现象的发现启发，作者提出了SigGate-GT。

Method: SigGate-GT在GraphGPS框架内，对注意力输出应用可学习的、每个注意力头独立的sigmoid门控。每个门控可以将激活值抑制向零，使注意力头能够选择性地屏蔽无信息的连接，从而避免强制关注无意义节点。该方法仅增加约1%的参数开销。

Result: 在五个标准基准测试中，SigGate-GT在ZINC数据集上匹配了先前最优结果（MAE 0.059），在ogbg-molhiv数据集上创造了新的最优记录（ROC-AUC 82.47%），在所有五个数据集上相对于GraphGPS均获得统计显著性提升（p < 0.05）。消融实验表明，门控机制将过平滑程度降低了30%（4-16层的平均相对MAD增益），增加了注意力熵，并在10倍学习率范围内稳定了训练。

Conclusion: SigGate-GT通过sigmoid门控机制成功解决了图变换器中的关键问题，实现了性能提升和训练稳定性改善，且参数开销极小，为图变换器的设计提供了新的有效方向。

Abstract: Graph transformers achieve strong results on molecular and long-range reasoning tasks, yet remain hampered by over-smoothing (the progressive collapse of node representations with depth) and attention entropy degeneration. We observe that these pathologies share a root cause with attention sinks in large language models: softmax attention's sum-to-one constraint forces every node to attend somewhere, even when no informative signal exists. Motivated by recent findings that element-wise sigmoid gating eliminates attention sinks in large language models, we propose SigGate-GT, a graph transformer that applies learned, per-head sigmoid gates to the attention output within the GraphGPS framework. Each gate can suppress activations toward zero, enabling heads to selectively silence uninformative connections. On five standard benchmarks, SigGate-GT matches the prior best on ZINC (0.059 MAE) and sets new state-of-the-art on ogbg-molhiv (82.47% ROC-AUC), with statistically significant gains over GraphGPS across all five datasets ($p < 0.05$). Ablations show that gating reduces over-smoothing by 30% (mean relative MAD gain across 4-16 layers), increases attention entropy, and stabilizes training across a $10\times$ learning rate range, with about 1% parameter overhead on OGB.

</details>


### [294] [Rethinking the Comparison Unit in Sequence-Level Reinforcement Learning: An Equal-Length Paired Training Framework from Loss Correction to Sample Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17328)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang,Sibo wang,Linglin Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EqLen方法，通过主动构建等长、对齐且可比的训练片段来解决序列级相对强化学习中的长度问题，而非传统的损失缩放或归一化校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽部分缓解了长度相关现象，但训练中使用的比较单元缺乏内在可比性这一根本问题未被充分刻画。作者认为长度问题不应仅视为损失缩放或归一化偏差，而应视为比较单元构建问题。

Method: 提出基于样本构建的训练框架，通过双轨同步生成、前缀继承和片段掩码，主动构建等长、对齐且可比的训练片段，而非对不等长响应进行事后校正。该方法适用于GRPO、GSPO、RLOO等群组相对比较算法。

Result: EqLen能够高效收集有效的等长训练片段，实现稳定训练（摘要未提供具体实验数据）。

Conclusion: 将长度问题重新定义为比较单元构建问题，并通过主动构建等长训练片段的方式为序列级相对强化学习提供了新视角和解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the length problem in sequence-level relative reinforcement learning. We observe that, although existing methods partially alleviate length-related phenomena, a more fundamental issue remains insufficiently characterized: the comparison units used during training lack inherent comparability. Building on this observation, we propose a new perspective: the length problem should not be viewed merely as a loss-scaling or normalization bias, but rather as a \emph{comparison unit construction} problem. We further establish a sample-construction-based training framework that, instead of applying post-hoc corrections to unequal-length responses, proactively constructs equal-length, alignable, and comparable training segments during generation. Within this framework, we propose EqLen, a concrete method applicable to group-relative comparison algorithms such as GRPO, GSPO, and RLOO. Through dual-track synchronous generation, prefix inheritance, and segment masking, EqLen efficiently collects effective equal-length training segments and enables stable

</details>


### [295] [FLARE: Task-agnostic embedding model evaluation through a normalization process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17344)
*Jingzhou Jiang,Yixuan Tang,Yi Yang,Kar Yan Tam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归一化流的标签无关嵌入评估方法（FLARE），通过直接利用对数似然估计信息充分性，在高维空间实现了稳定且准确的嵌入模型排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏任务特定标签的情况下，现有的基于核估计或高斯混合模型的标签无关评估方法在高维空间中会失效，导致嵌入模型排序不稳定。

Method: 提出FLARE（Flow-based Labelless Representation Embedding Evaluation），利用归一化流（normalized flows）直接从对数似然估计信息充分性，避免了基于距离的密度估计，并给出了估计误差依赖于数据流形内在维度而非原始嵌入维度的有限样本边界。

Result: 在11个数据集和8个嵌入模型上，FLARE在有监督基准下达到了Spearman's ρ = 0.90的相关性，且在高维嵌入（d ≥ 3,584）情况下保持稳定，而现有的标签无关基线方法则失效。

Conclusion: FLARE成功解决了高维嵌入空间中标签无关评估的稳定性问题，提供了一种可靠的无监督嵌入模型选择方法，具有重要的理论和实践价值。

Abstract: When task-specific labels are not available, it becomes difficult to select an embedding model for a specific target corpus. Existing labelless measures based on kernel estimators or Gaussian mixes fail in high-dimensional space, resulting in unstable rankings. We propose a flow-based labelless representation embedding evaluation (FLARE), which utilizes normalized streams to estimate information sufficiency directly from log-likelihood and avoid distance-based density estimation. We give a finite sample boundary, indicating that the estimation error depends on the intrinsic dimension of the data manifold rather than the original embedding dimension. On 11 datasets and 8 embedders, FLARE reached Spearman's $ρ$ of 0.90 under the supervised benchmark and remained stable in high-dimensional embeddings ($d \geq 3{,}584$) as the existing labelless baseline collapsed.

</details>


### [296] [Towards a Data-Parameter Correspondence for LLMs: A Preliminary Discussion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17384)
*Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“数据-参数对应”统一框架，揭示了数据中心化与模型中心化优化在统计流形上的几何对偶性，并证明了联合优化在效率、鲁棒性和隐私性上优于孤立方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM优化方法长期分裂为孤立的数据中心化（如样本选择、增强、投毒）和模型中心化（如掩码、量化、LoRA）范式，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解这两种看似迥异操作之间的内在联系。

Method: 基于统计流形$\mathcal{M}$上的Fisher-Rao度量$g_{ij}(θ)$与自然参数$θ$和期望参数$η$之间的勒让德对偶性，建立统一的几何理论框架，推导出贯穿模型生命周期的三个基本对应关系。

Result: 发现三个核心对应关系：1）几何对应：数据剪枝与参数稀疏化通过双重坐标约束等效减少流形体积；2）低秩对应：上下文学习(ICL)与LoRA在格拉斯曼流形上探索相同子空间，k-shot样本几何等价于秩-r更新；3）安全隐私对应：对抗攻击中数据投毒与参数后门呈协同放大，保护机制中数据压缩与参数隐私呈级联衰减。

Conclusion: 该框架为跨社区的方法迁移提供了数学形式化基础，证明了整合数据与参数模态的协同优化在效率、鲁棒性和隐私维度上可超越孤立方法，为LLM全生命周期优化提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Large language model optimization has historically bifurcated into isolated data-centric and model-centric paradigms: the former manipulates involved samples through selection, augmentation, or poisoning, while the latter tunes model weights via masking, quantization, or low-rank adaptation. This paper establishes a unified \emph{data-parameter correspondence} revealing these seemingly disparate operations as dual manifestations of the same geometric structure on the statistical manifold $\mathcal{M}$. Grounded in the Fisher-Rao metric $g_{ij}(θ)$ and Legendre duality between natural ($θ$) and expectation ($η$) parameters, we identify three fundamental correspondences spanning the model lifecycle: 1. Geometric correspondence: data pruning and parameter sparsification equivalently reduce manifold volume via dual coordinate constraints; 2. Low-rank correspondence: in-context learning (ICL) and LoRA adaptation explore identical subspaces on the Grassmannian $\mathcal{G}(r,d)$, with $k$-shot samples geometrically equivalent to rank-$r$ updates; 3. Security-privacy correspondence: adversarial attacks exhibit cooperative amplification between data poisoning and parameter backdoors, whereas protective mechanisms follow cascading attenuation where data compression multiplicatively enhances parameter privacy. Extending from training through post-training compression to inference, this framework provides mathematical formalization for cross-community methodology transfer, demonstrating that cooperative optimization integrating data and parameter modalities may outperform isolated approaches across efficiency, robustness, and privacy dimensions.

</details>


### [297] [Back to Repair: A Minimal Denoising Network\ for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17388)
*Kadir-Kaan Özer,René Ebeling,Markus Enzweiler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JuRe是一个极简的时间序列异常检测去噪网络，证明了在训练目标正确实现流形投影原理时，架构复杂性是不必要的。该模型在多变量和单变量基准测试中均排名第二，且在AUC-PR和VUS-PR上领先所有神经基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法往往依赖复杂的架构设计（如注意力机制、潜变量、对抗组件等），但本文旨在探索是否在正确的训练目标下，简单的网络架构也能达到甚至超越复杂模型的性能，从而证明架构复杂性并非必要。

Method: 提出JuRe（Just Repair）模型，仅包含一个隐藏维度为128的深度可分离卷积残差块。模型通过修复被破坏的时间序列窗口进行训练，在推理时使用固定的、无参数的结构差异函数进行评分。核心设计包括训练时的数据破坏策略和基于流形投影原理的去噪目标。

Result: 在TSB-AD多变量基准上排名第二（AUC-PR 0.404，180个序列，17个数据集），在UCR单变量数据集上同样排名第二（AUC-PR 0.198，250个序列），在AUC-PR和VUS-PR指标上领先所有神经基线。消融实验显示训练时的破坏策略是主导因素（移除后ΔAUC-PR = 0.047），证明去噪目标而非网络容量决定检测质量。成对Wilcoxon符号秩检验表明在TSB-AD上对25个基线中的21个具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 当训练目标正确实现流形投影原理时，架构复杂性是不必要的。去噪目标本身驱动检测质量，而非网络容量。JuRe以极简架构实现了与复杂模型相当甚至更优的性能，为时间序列异常检测领域提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: We introduce JuRe (Just Repair), a minimal denoising network for time series anomaly detection that exposes a central finding: architectural complexity is unnecessary when the training objective correctly implements the manifold-projection principle. JuRe consists of a single depthwise-separable convolutional residual block with hidden dimension 128, trained to repair corrupted time series windows and scored at inference by a fixed, parameter-free structural discrepancy function. Despite using no attention, no latent variable, and no adversarial component, JuRe ranks second on the TSB-AD multivariate benchmark (AUC-PR 0.404, 180 series, 17 datasets) and second on the UCR univariate archive by AUC-PR (0.198, 250 series), leading all neural baselines on AUC-PR and VUS-PR. Component ablation on TSB-AD identifies training-time corruption as the dominant factor ($Δ$AUC-PR $= 0.047$ on removal), confirming that the denoising objective, not network capacity, drives detection quality. Pairwise Wilcoxon signed-rank tests establish statistical significance against 21 of 25 baselines on TSB-AD. Code is available at the URL https://github.com/iis-esslingen/JuRe.

</details>


### [298] [On the Generalization Bounds of Symbolic Regression with Genetic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17402)
*Masahiro Nomura,Ryoki Hamano,Isao Ono*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于遗传规划的符号回归（SR）推导了一个泛化误差界，将其分解为结构选择项和常数拟合项，为该领域的常用实践提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于遗传规划（GP）的符号回归（SR）在经验上取得了巨大成功，但理论界对其为何能超越训练数据进行泛化的理解仍然有限。现有研究缺乏对GP-SR泛化性质的学习理论分析，无法从理论上解释许多广泛使用的经验性做法为何有效。

Method: 作者针对以表达式树形式表示的SR模型进行了学习理论分析。在约束树的大小、深度和可学习常数的前提下，推导了GP风格SR的泛化界。该推导将泛化差距分解为两个可解释的部分：反映选择表达式树结构组合复杂性的“结构选择项”，以及捕捉在固定结构内优化数值常数复杂性的“常数拟合项”。

Result: 研究得到的泛化界将泛化差距明确分解为结构选择项和常数拟合项。通过将简洁性压力、深度限制、数值稳定算子和区间算术等实际设计选择与泛化界中的显式复杂度项联系起来，作者证明了结构限制能减少假设类的增长，而稳定性机制能控制预测对参数扰动的敏感度。

Conclusion: 本文通过建立实际设计选择与泛化界中复杂度项的理论联系，为GP-SR中常见的经验行为提供了原理性解释，例如为何引入结构限制和稳定性机制能提升泛化能力。这项工作不仅填补了GP-SR理论理解的空白，也为其泛化特性提供了更严格的理论基础，有助于指导未来的算法设计。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) with genetic programming (GP) aims to discover interpretable mathematical expressions directly from data. Despite its strong empirical success, the theoretical understanding of why GP-based SR generalizes beyond the training data remains limited. In this work, we provide a learning-theoretic analysis of SR models represented as expression trees. We derive a generalization bound for GP-style SR under constraints on tree size, depth, and learnable constants. Our result decomposes the generalization gap into two interpretable components: a structure-selection term, reflecting the combinatorial complexity of choosing an expression-tree structure, and a constant-fitting term, capturing the complexity of optimizing numerical constants within a fixed structure. This decomposition provides a theoretical perspective on several widely used practices in GP, including parsimony pressure, depth limits, numerically stable operators, and interval arithmetic. In particular, our analysis shows how structural restrictions reduce hypothesis-class growth while stability mechanisms control the sensitivity of predictions to parameter perturbations. By linking these practical design choices to explicit complexity terms in the generalization bound, our work offers a principled explanation for commonly observed empirical behaviors in GP-based SR and contributes towards a more rigorous understanding of its generalization properties.

</details>


### [299] [Reward Score Matching: Unifying Reward-based Fine-tuning for Flow and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17415)
*Jeongjae Lee,Jinho Chang,Jeongsol Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了奖励分数匹配（RSM）框架，统一了现有的基于奖励的扩散/流模型微调方法，揭示了方法间的差异主要在于价值引导估计器的构造和时间步优化强度，并据此设计了更简单高效的改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于奖励的扩散模型微调方法（如Soft RL、GFlowNets等）看似分散且基于不同视角，缺乏统一理解，作者希望找到一个共同框架来理清这些方法之间的关系与设计权衡。

Method: 提出奖励分数匹配（RSM）统一框架，将奖励对齐问题建模为向奖励引导目标的分数匹配，通过分析不同方法在价值引导估计器构造和时间步优化强度上的差异，揭示了偏差-方差-计算量的权衡关系，并基于此设计了更简化的改进方案。

Result: RSM框架成功统一了多种现有方法，明确了其核心优化组件与辅助机制的区别，所提出的简化设计在可微分奖励和黑盒奖励的代表性设置中均实现了对齐效果和计算效率的提升。

Conclusion: RSM框架将分散的奖励微调方法整合为一个更小、更可解释、更具可操作性的设计空间，为未来的方法设计提供了清晰的理论指导。

Abstract: Reward-based fine-tuning aims to steer a pretrained diffusion or flow-based generative model toward higher-reward samples while remaining close to the pretrained model. Although existing methods are motivated by different perspectives such as Soft RL, GFlowNets, etc., we show that many can be written under a common framework, which we call reward score matching (RSM). Under this view, alignment becomes score matching toward a reward-guided target, and the main differences across methods reduce to the construction of the value-guidance estimator and the effective optimization strength across timesteps. This unification clarifies the bias--variance--compute tradeoffs of existing designs and distinguishes core optimization components from auxiliary mechanisms that add complexity without clear benefit. Guided by this perspective, we develop simpler redesigns that improve alignment effectiveness and compute efficiency across representative settings with differentiable and black-box rewards. Overall, RSM turns a seemingly fragmented collection of reward-based fine-tuning methods into a smaller, more interpretable, and more actionable design space.

</details>


### [300] [TransXion: A High-Fidelity Graph Benchmark for Realistic Anti-Money Laundering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17420)
*Keyang Chen,Mingxuan Jiang,Yongsheng Zhao,Zeping Li,Zaiyuan Chen,Weiqi Luo,Zhixin Li,Sen Liu,Yinan Jing,Guangnan Ye,Xihong Wu,Hongfeng Chai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TransXion，一个更具挑战性和真实性的反洗钱基准数据集，通过引入丰富的节点属性和非模板化的异常生成机制，解决了现有数据集语义稀疏和评估过于乐观的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反洗钱交易图基准数据集存在两大局限：一是节点缺乏语义信息，仅有匿名标识符；二是依赖模板驱动的异常注入，导致基准偏向静态结构模式，对模型鲁棒性的评估过于乐观，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 提出了TransXion基准生态系统。该方法将感知档案的正常活动模拟与随机、非模板的非法子图合成相结合。它联合建模持久化的实体档案和条件交易行为，从而能够评估观测活动与实体社会经济背景相矛盾的“反常行为”异常。

Result: 构建了包含5万个实体、约300万笔交易的数据集，实体具有丰富的人口统计和行为属性。实验表明，TransXion重现了支付网络的关键结构特性。在多种检测模型上，TransXion的检测性能显著低于广泛使用的基准，证明了其更高的难度和真实性。

Conclusion: TransXion提供了一个更忠实于现实的测试平台，能够有效促进上下文感知和鲁棒的反洗钱检测方法的开发，数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Money laundering poses severe risks to global financial systems, driving the widespread adoption of machine learning for transaction monitoring. However, progress remains stifled by the lack of realistic benchmarks. Existing transaction-graph datasets suffer from two pervasive limitations: (i) they provide sparse node-level semantics beyond anonymized identifiers, and (ii) they rely on template-driven anomaly injection, which biases benchmarks toward static structural motifs and yields overly optimistic assessments of model robustness. We propose TransXion, a benchmark ecosystem for Anti-Money Laundering (AML) research that integrates profile-aware simulation of normal activity with stochastic, non-template synthesis of illicit subgraphs.TransXion jointly models persistent entity profiles and conditional transaction behavior, enabling evaluation of "out-of-character" anomalies where observed activity contradicts an entity's socio-economic context. The resulting dataset comprises approximately 3 million transactions among 50,000 entities, each endowed with rich demographic and behavioral attributes. Empirical analyses show that TransXion reproduces key structural properties of payment networks, including heavy-tailed activity distributions and localized subgraph structure. Across a diverse array of detection models spanning multiple algorithmic paradigms, TransXion yields substantially lower detection performance than widely used benchmarks, demonstrating increased difficulty and realism. TransXion provides a more faithful testbed for developing context-aware and robust AML detection methods. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/chaos-max/TransXion.

</details>


### [301] [A unified convergence theory for adaptive first-order methods in the nonconvex case, including AdaNorm, full and diagonal AdaGrad, Shampoo and Muo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17423)
*S. Gratton,Ph. L. Toint*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的一阶优化算法框架，涵盖AdaGrad、AdaNorm、Shampoo等多种方法，并提供了在合理假设下的随机全局收敛速率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应预条件优化方法（如AdaGrad、Shampoo等）缺乏统一的收敛性分析框架，特别是在处理不同变量组的异质几何结构和包含动量项时。本文旨在建立一个统一框架，整合这些方法并提供完整的收敛性保证。

Method: 提出了一个基于自适应预条件梯度的统一框架，该框架：(1)包含完整的和对角的AdaGrad、AdaNorm以及Shampoo和Muon的自适应变体；(2)支持跨不同变量组组合异质几何结构；(3)对框架内所有方法（含两种动量和不含动量）进行完全随机全局收敛速率分析；(4)仅依赖梯度预言的方差合理假设，无需假设有界随机梯度或足够小的步长。

Result: 在合理的假设条件下，为框架内的所有优化方法建立了随机全局收敛速率的理论保证，包括有动量和无动量的情况。分析不需要有界随机梯度假设或对小步长的限制，使得结果更具一般性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功整合了多种主流自适应优化方法，并通过统一的收敛性分析证明了其理论保证，为非凸无约束优化问题的算法设计和分析提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: A unified framework for first-order optimization algorithms fornonconvex unconstrained optimization is proposed that uses adaptivelypreconditioned gradients and includes popular methods such as full anddiagonal AdaGrad, AdaNorm, as well as adpative variants of Shampoo andMuon. This framework also allows combining heterogeneous geometriesacross different groups of variables while preserving a unifiedconvergence analysis. A fully stochastic global rate-of-convergenceanalysis is conducted for all methods in the framework, with andwithout two types of momentum, using reasonable assumptions on thevariance of the gradient oracle and without assuming boundedstochastic gradients or small enough stepsize.

</details>


### [302] [Neural Adjoint Method for Meta-optics: Accelerating Volumetric Inverse Design via Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17425)
*Chanik Kang,Hyewon Suk,Haejun Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经伴随方法，利用傅里叶神经算子预测梯度场，将超光学器件设计时间从数小时缩短至秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超光学器件的高性能结构设计是一个高维优化问题，需要迭代求解计算昂贵的麦克斯韦方程组。即使使用伴随优化，宽带设计也需要数千次麦克斯韦求解，使得工业规模优化缓慢且成本高昂。

Method: 提出神经伴随方法(Neural Adjoint Method)，这是一种求解器监督的代理模型。使用傅里叶神经算子(FNO)从体素化介电常数体积预测3D伴随梯度场。引入分阶段FNO，通过逐步细化残差误差并增加对高频分量的重视来更好地保持灵敏度峰值。使用配对的前向/伴随FDTD仿真构建超光学数据集进行训练。

Result: 在光谱分选(颜色路由器)、消色差聚焦(超透镜)和波导模式转换三个任务上进行评估，设计时间从数小时减少到数秒。

Conclusion: 该研究结果为AI加速科学计算支持的快速、大规模体素化超光学设计提供了一条实用途径。

Abstract: Meta-optics promises compact, high-performance imaging and color routing. However, designing high-performance structures is a high-dimensional optimization problem: mapping a desired optical output back to a physical 3D structure requires solving computationally expensive Maxwell's equations iteratively. Even with adjoint optimization, broadband design can require thousands of Maxwell solves, making industrial-scale optimization slow and costly. To overcome this challenge, we propose the Neural Adjoint Method, a solver-supervised surrogate that predicts 3D adjoint gradient fields from a voxelized permittivity volume using a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO). By learning the dense, per-voxel sensitivity field that drives gradient-based updates, our method can replace per-iteration adjoint solves with fast predictions, greatly reducing the computational cost of full-wave simulations required during iterative refinement. To better preserve sensitivity peaks, we introduce a stage-wise FNO that progressively refines residual errors with increasing emphasis on higher-frequency components. We curate a meta-optics dataset from paired forward/adjoint FDTD simulations and evaluate it across three tasks: spectral sorting (color routers), achromatic focusing (metalenses), and waveguide mode conversion. Our method reduces design time from hours to seconds. These results suggest a practical route toward fast, large-scale volumetric meta-optical design enabled by AI-accelerated scientific computing.

</details>


### [303] [Machine Learning Hamiltonian Dynamical Systems with Sparse and Noisy Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17470)
*Vedanta Thapar,Abhinav Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应辛循环神经网络（ASRNNs），通过将哈密顿物理结构融入模型架构，成功解决了在极端数据稀缺（每条轨迹仅两个数据点）、噪声干扰和非规则采样条件下学习动力学系统的难题，并能进一步发现精确的符号化控制方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器学习方法在从数据中发现动力学系统规律时，严重依赖于数据的密度和质量。当观测数据稀疏、含有噪声或采样不规则时，这些方法的性能会急剧下降。因此，迫切需要一种能够处理极端数据稀缺并保持物理结构的学习架构。

Method: 提出了自适应辛循环神经网络（ASRNNs）。该方法将哈密顿学习与辛循环积分相结合，构建了一个参数感知且保持结构特性的模型。这种设计避免了时间导数的估计，从而能够在噪声环境下稳定学习。在训练完成后，利用ASRNNs作为保持结构的数据生成器，结合独立的回归方法（SINDy和PySR）进行符号化发现。

Result: 实验表明，ASRNNs在极端数据稀缺（每条训练轨迹仅包含两个不规则间隔的时间点）和相关噪声干扰的情况下，仍能准确预测长期动力学行为。此外，该方法能够恢复多项式系统的精确符号方程，并为非多项式哈密顿量提供一致的多项式近似。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过显式地将物理结构融入学习架构，可以为从稀疏和噪声数据中发现可解释的哈密顿动力学提供一条鲁棒的途径。

Abstract: Machine learning has become a powerful tool for discovering governing laws of dynamical systems from data. However, most existing approaches degrade severely when observations are sparse, noisy, or irregularly sampled. In this work, we address the problem of learning symbolic representations of nonlinear Hamiltonian dynamical systems under extreme data scarcity by explicitly incorporating physical structure into the learning architecture. We introduce Adaptable Symplectic Recurrent Neural Networks (ASRNNs), a parameter-cognizant, structure-preserving model that combines Hamiltonian learning with symplectic recurrent integration, avoiding time derivative estimation, and enabling stable learning under noise. We demonstrate that ASRNNs can accurately predict long-term dynamics even when each training trajectory consists of only two irregularly spaced time points, possibly corrupted by correlated noise. Leveraging ASRNNs as structure-preserving data generators, we further enable symbolic discovery using independent regression methods (SINDy and PySR), recovering exact symbolic equations for polynomial systems and consistent polynomial approximations for non-polynomial Hamiltonians. Our results show that such architectures can provide a robust pathway to interpretable discovery of Hamiltonian dynamics from sparse and noisy data.

</details>


### [304] [Trustworthy deep domain adaptation for wearable photoplethysmography signal analysis with decision-theoretic uncertainty quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17480)
*Ciaran Bench*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于决策论的不确定性量化框架，用于评估生成模型在域适应任务中生成输出的可信度，并通过光电容积脉搏波时间序列去噪用于房颤分类的案例研究进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型在域适应任务中可能产生幻觉和伪影，降低下游判别模型的性能。现有的不确定性量化评估方法在生成模型中面临缺乏真实标签的困难，且评估结果与下游任务效用脱节，无法有效指导生成输出的使用。

Method: 提出决策论不确定性量化框架，将下游任务性能与不确定性评估相结合，形式化了使用下游分类器评估生成输出质量的启发式方法。以光电容积脉搏波（PPG）时间序列去噪用于房颤分类为案例进行研究。

Result: 该框架能够有效评估生成输出的可信度，并为域适应任务中生成数据的不确定性提供与下游任务相关的可靠性分析。在PPG信号去噪用于房颤分类的案例中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 决策论不确定性量化为生成模型在域适应任务中的输出质量评估提供了理论基础和实用框架，能够更好地反映不确定性对下游任务的实际价值。

Abstract: In principle, deep generative models can be used to perform domain adaptation; i.e. align the input feature representations of test data with that of a separate discriminative model's training data. This can help improve the discriminative model's performance on the test data. However, generative models are prone to producing hallucinations and artefacts that may degrade the quality of generated data, and therefore, predictive performance when processed by the discriminative model. While uncertainty quantification can provide a means to assess the quality of adapted data, the standard framework for evaluating the quality of predicted uncertainties may not easily extend to generative models due to the common lack of ground truths (among other reasons). Even with ground truths, this evaluation is agnostic to how the generated outputs are used on the downstream task, limiting the extent to which the uncertainty reliability analysis provides insights about the utility of the uncertainties with respect to the intended use case of the adapted examples. Here, we describe how decision-theoretic uncertainty quantification can address these concerns and provide a convenient framework for evaluating the trustworthiness of generated outputs, in particular, for domain adaptation. We consider a case study in photoplethysmography time series denoising for Atrial Fibrillation classification. This formalises a well-known heuristic method of using a downstream classifier to assess the quality of generated outputs.

</details>


### [305] [A Probabilistic Consensus-Driven Approach for Robust Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17494)
*Marcin Kostrzewa,Maciej Zięba,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件归一化流和模型集成的反事实解释生成方法，通过单一可解释参数控制鲁棒性，无需重训练即可灵活调整，实现了在模型变化下稳定且合理的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释对于解释黑箱模型至关重要，但在模型发生微小变化时往往会失效。现有的鲁棒反事实解释生成方法通常局限于特定模型类型、需要昂贵的调参过程或缺乏灵活的鲁棒性控制机制。

Method: 提出一种联合建模数据分布和合理模型决策空间的新方法，通过模型集成的概率共识训练条件归一化流，捕获不同分类器一致性水平下的数据密度。在推理时，使用单一可解释参数控制鲁棒性水平，该参数指定目标类别上达成一致的最小模型比例，无需重新训练生成模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在实现卓越实证鲁棒性的同时，在其他评估指标上也保持了良好的性能表现。方法能够有效地将反事实解释推向既合理又在模型变化下稳定的区域。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了反事实解释在模型变化下的失效问题，提供了一种灵活、高效的鲁棒性控制机制，为黑箱模型解释提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations (CFEs) are essential for interpreting black-box models, yet they often become invalid when models are slightly changed. Existing methods for generating robust CFEs are often limited to specific types of models, require costly tuning, or inflexible robustness controls. We propose a novel approach that jointly models the data distribution and the space of plausible model decisions to ensure robustness to model changes. Using a probabilistic consensus over a model ensemble, we train a conditional normalizing flow that captures the data density under varying levels of classifier agreement. At inference time, a single interpretable parameter controls the robustness level; it specifies the minimum fraction of models that should agree on the target class without retraining the generative model. Our method effectively pushes CFEs toward regions that are both plausible and stable across model changes. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves superior empirical robustness while also maintaining good performance across other evaluation measures.

</details>


### [306] [Contraction and Hourglass Persistence for Learning on Graphs, Simplices, and Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17548)
*Mattie Ji,Indradyumna Roy,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hourglass Persistence，一种通过交错包含和收缩操作的新型拓扑描述符，提升了图神经网络的性能，并在多个基准数据集上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持久同调方法在图神经网络中通常通过遍历递增的子图序列进行操作，但这种方法存在表达能力的局限性。作者发现这种包含过程存在缺陷，需要寻找更有效的拓扑操作方式来提升图表示学习的效果。

Method: 作者首先分析了收缩操作作为一种原理性拓扑操作，研究收缩序列的持久性，称为收缩同调。然后提出了Hourglass Persistence，这是一类通过交错包含和收缩序列来提升表达能力、可学习性和稳定性的拓扑描述符。同时设计了高效的算法，可以插入端到端可微分的GNN流程中。

Result: 理论分析表明前向PH和CH在表达能力上存在差异。在标准真实世界图数据集上的实验显示，所提出的Hourglass Persistence方法在多个PH方法中实现了持续的实证改进。作者还开发了开源代码供研究使用。

Conclusion: Hourglass Persistence框架成功扩展了持久同调在图神经网络中的应用，通过结合包含和收缩操作提升了拓扑描述符的表达能力和稳定性，为图表示学习提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。该框架还可扩展到单纯复形和细胞网络。

Abstract: Persistent homology (PH) encodes global information, such as cycles, and is thus increasingly integrated into graph neural networks (GNNs). PH methods in GNNs typically traverse an increasing sequence of subgraphs. In this work, we first expose limitations of this inclusion procedure. To remedy these shortcomings, we analyze contractions as a principled topological operation, in particular, for graph representation learning. We study the persistence of contraction sequences, which we call Contraction Homology (CH). We establish that forward PH and CH differ in expressivity. We then introduce Hourglass Persistence, a class of topological descriptors that interleave a sequence of inclusions and contractions to boost expressivity, learnability, and stability. We also study related families parametrized by two paradigms. We also discuss how our framework extends to simplicial and cellular networks. We further design efficient algorithms that are pluggable into end-to-end differentiable GNN pipelines, enabling consistent empirical improvements over many PH methods across standard real-world graph datasets. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/Aalto-QuML/Hourglass}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [307] [SVL: Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning as Survival Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17551)
*Franki Nguimatsia Tiofack,Fabian Schramm,Théotime Le Hellard,Justin Carpentier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了生存价值学习（SVL），一种用于目标条件强化学习的概率方法。该方法将到达目标的时间建模为概率分布，通过生存概率的折现和来估计价值函数。实验表明，SVL在复杂的长期任务上表现优异，匹配或超越了强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于时序差分（TD）学习的目标条件强化学习（GCRL）方法由于自举（bootstrapping）机制存在不稳定性和样本效率低的问题。尽管近期研究探索了对比学习和监督学习方案以提高稳定性，但仍需一种更稳定、更高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出生存价值学习（SVL），将GCRL重构为生存学习问题。核心是将从每个状态到达目标的时间建模为概率分布。这种结构化的分布式蒙特卡洛视角产生了一个闭式恒等式，将目标条件价值函数表示为生存概率的折现和。该方法通过在事件轨迹和右删失轨迹上进行最大似然训练的风险模型来实现价值估计。引入了三种实用的价值估计器：有限视界截断和两种分箱无限视界近似方法。

Result: 在离线GCRL基准测试上的实验表明，SVL结合分层智能体能够匹配或超越强大的分层TD和蒙特卡洛基线方法。该方法在复杂的、长期视界的任务中表现尤为出色。

Conclusion: SVL通过将GCRL重构为生存学习问题并提供结构化的分布式蒙特卡洛视角，成功解决了传统TD方法的不稳定性和样本效率问题。该方法为长期视界任务提供了一种有效且稳定的替代方案，在离线GCRL场景中展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Standard approaches to goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) that rely on temporal-difference learning can be unstable and sample-inefficient due to bootstrapping. While recent work has explored contrastive and supervised formulations to improve stability, we present a probabilistic alternative, called survival value learning (SVL), that reframes GCRL as a survival learning problem by modeling the time-to-goal from each state as a probability distribution. This structured distributional Monte Carlo perspective yields a closed-form identity that expresses the goal-conditioned value function as a discounted sum of survival probabilities, enabling value estimation via a hazard model trained via maximum likelihood on both event and right-censored trajectories. We introduce three practical value estimators, including finite-horizon truncation and two binned infinite-horizon approximations to capture long-horizon objectives. Experiments on offline GCRL benchmarks show that SVL combined with hierarchical actors matches or surpasses strong hierarchical TD and Monte Carlo baselines, excelling on complex, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [308] [Diverse Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17568)
*Yujia Zheng,Zijian Li,Shunxing Fan,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“多样化字典学习”问题，证明了在无法完全识别的通用设定下，潜在变量的交集、补集、对称差及潜在-观测依赖结构仍可在较弱假设下被识别，并提出一种简单的归纳偏置以实现这些理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅从观测数据X=g(Z)中恢复潜在变量Z是病态问题。现有方法通常依赖线性假设或辅助监督，这些假设在实践中难以验证，且在轻微违规下理论保证会失效。作者旨在解决在无法实现完全识别的一般场景中，如何可靠地理解潜在世界的问题。

Method: 引入多样化字典学习问题，从集合论视角出发，证明了潜在变量的交集、补集、对称差以及潜在到观测的依赖结构，在无需强假设的情况下仍具有可识别性（至适当的不确定性）。这些结果可通过集合代数组合以构建结构化的潜在世界视图，并提出一种简单的归纳偏置，可集成到大多数模型中。

Result: 理论证明在通用设定下，潜在变量的集合关系和依赖结构是可识别的；当存在足够的结构多样性时，可进一步实现所有潜在变量的完全识别。实验验证了该理论及归纳偏置在合成数据和真实数据上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为部分可识别性提供了理论基础和实践方法，使得在无法完全识别潜在变量的情况下，仍能通过集合论关系构建对潜在世界的可靠理解，为无监督学习中的可识别性问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Given only observational data $X = g(Z)$, where both the latent variables $Z$ and the generating process $g$ are unknown, recovering $Z$ is ill-posed without additional assumptions. Existing methods often assume linearity or rely on auxiliary supervision and functional constraints. However, such assumptions are rarely verifiable in practice, and most theoretical guarantees break down under even mild violations, leaving uncertainty about how to reliably understand the hidden world. To make identifiability actionable in the real-world scenarios, we take a complementary view: in the general settings where full identifiability is unattainable, what can still be recovered with guarantees, and what biases could be universally adopted? We introduce the problem of diverse dictionary learning to formalize this view. Specifically, we show that intersections, complements, and symmetric differences of latent variables linked to arbitrary observations, along with the latent-to-observed dependency structure, are still identifiable up to appropriate indeterminacies even without strong assumptions. These set-theoretic results can be composed using set algebra to construct structured and essential views of the hidden world, such as genus-differentia definitions. When sufficient structural diversity is present, they further imply full identifiability of all latent variables. Notably, all identifiability benefits follow from a simple inductive bias during estimation that can be readily integrated into most models. We validate the theory and demonstrate the benefits of the bias on both synthetic and real-world data.

</details>


### [309] [Recovery Guarantees for Continual Learning of Dependent Tasks: Memory, Data-Dependent Regularization, and Data-Dependent Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17578)
*Liangzu Peng,Uday Kiran Reddy Tadipatri,Ziqing Xu,Eric Eaton,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对持续学习理论发展滞后的问题，提出通过显式建模任务间的非线性依赖关系，为经验回放、知识蒸馏等主流持续学习范式提供了非平凡的统计恢复保证和误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管持续学习在实证研究上取得了显著进展，但其理论基础仍处于起步阶段。核心挑战在于不同任务间的数据分布存在差异，现有研究未能很好地处理这种任务间的依赖关系变化，导致理论分析往往只能给出无意义的界限。因此，迫切需要建立能够显式建模任务依赖性的理论框架，以提供有意义的性能保证。

Method: 作者考虑非线性回归任务，提出关键假设：任务间存在依赖性，具体表现为当前任务的数据是前序任务数据的非线性变换。基于此模型，在合理的自然假设下，利用统计学习方法，针对几种主流持续学习范式进行理论分析，包括：1）带有数据无关正则化的经验回放；2）使用数据无关权重平衡任务损失；3）使用数据依赖权重的回放；4）带有数据依赖正则化的持续学习（如知识蒸馏）。

Result: 研究成功证明了上述持续学习范式的统计恢复保证，即给出了估计误差的界限。最重要的是，在先前研究只能给出无意义界限的情况下，本文推导出的界限具有信息量且非平凡，填补了理论空白。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式建模任务间的非线性依赖关系，成功弥合了持续学习实证成功与理论空白之间的差距。研究结果为实践中广泛使用的持续学习算法提供了坚实的理论基础，证明了这些方法在特定假设下的有效性，推动了持续学习理论的发展。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is concerned with learning multiple tasks sequentially without forgetting previously learned tasks. Despite substantial empirical advances over recent years, the theoretical development of CL remains in its infancy. At the heart of developing CL theory lies the challenge that the data distribution varies across tasks, and we argue that properly addressing this challenge requires understanding this variation--dependency among tasks. To explicitly model task dependency, we consider nonlinear regression tasks and propose the assumption that these tasks are dependent in such a way that the data of the current task is a nonlinear transformation of previous data. With this model and under natural assumptions, we prove statistical recovery guarantees (more specifically, bounds on estimation errors) for several CL paradigms in practical use, including experience replay with data-independent regularization and data-independent weights that balance the losses of tasks, replay with data-dependent weights, and continual learning with data-dependent regularization (e.g., knowledge distillation). To the best of our knowledge, our bounds are informative in cases where prior work gives vacuous bounds.

</details>


### [310] [How Much Data is Enough? The Zeta Law of Discoverability in Biomedical Data, featuring the enigmatic Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17581)
*Paul M. Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于数据协方差算子谱结构的跨模态可发现性缩放定律框架（Zeta Law），用于预测生物医学数据集中何时增加数据能显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生物医学数据集扩展至百万级样本且AI模型容量不断增长，迫切需要预测何时增加数据能显著提升性能。当前模型开发依赖经验性缩放曲线，缺乏关于性能何时改进、饱和或出现交叉行为的理论指导。

Method: 提出了一个基于数据协方差算子谱结构、任务对齐信号投影和学习表示的缩放定律框架。在温和假设下，性能指标（如AUC）可表示为累积信噪能量，遵循由协方差谱幂律衰减控制的类Zeta缩放定律，自然引出黎曼Zeta函数。表示学习方法通过将有用信号集中到早期稳定模式来提高样本效率。

Result: 该框架成功预测了交叉区域：简单模型在小样本时表现最佳，而高容量或多模态编码器在数据充足时表现更优。应用于多模态疾病分类、影像遗传学、功能MRI和拓扑数据分析，为预测何时扩展数据、改进表示或添加模态最能加速发现提供了原则性方法。

Conclusion: Zeta定律为预测何时扩展数据、改进表示或添加模态最能加速科学发现提供了理论框架，为生物医学AI模型开发提供了原则性的缩放指导。

Abstract: How much data is enough to make a scientific discovery? As biomedical datasets scale to millions of samples and AI models grow in capacity, progress increasingly depends on predicting when additional data will substantially improve performance. In practice, model development often relies on empirical scaling curves measured across architectures, modalities, and dataset sizes, with limited theoretical guidance on when performance should improve, saturate, or exhibit cross-over behavior.
  We propose a scaling-law framework for cross-modal discoverability based on spectral structure of data covariance operators, task-aligned signal projections, and learned representations. Many performance metrics, including AUC, can be expressed in terms of cumulative signal-to-noise energy accumulated across identifiable spectral modes of an encoder and cross-modal operator. Under mild assumptions, this accumulation follows a zeta-like scaling law governed by power-law decay of covariance spectra and aligned signal energy, leading naturally to the appearance of the Riemann zeta function. Representation learning methods such as sparse models, low-rank embeddings, and multimodal contrastive objectives improve sample efficiency by concentrating useful signal into earlier stable modes, effectively steepening spectral decay and shifting scaling curves.
  The framework predicts cross-over regimes in which simpler models perform best at small sample sizes, while higher-capacity or multimodal encoders outperform them once sufficient data stabilizes additional degrees of freedom. Applications include multimodal disease classification, imaging genetics, functional MRI, and topological data analysis. The resulting zeta law provides a principled way to anticipate when scaling data, improving representations, or adding modalities is most likely to accelerate discovery.

</details>


### [311] [STEP-PD: Stage-Aware and Explainable Parkinson's Disease Severity Classification Using Multimodal Clinical Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17611)
*Md Mezbahul Islam,John Michael Templeton,Christian Poellabauer,Ananda Mohan Mondal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STEP-PD框架，利用PPMI队列数据实现帕金森病严重程度的三分类预测（健康、轻度、中重度），XGBoost模型准确率达94.14%，并提供了临床可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病（PD）是一种症状负担和功能损害随时间演变的进行性疾病，严重程度分期对临床监测和治疗规划至关重要。然而，现有计算研究多侧重于二分类PD检测，未能充分利用重复随访评估进行分期预测。

Method: 提出STEP-PD严重程度感知机器学习框架，利用PPMI数据集中所有访问数据，整合主观问卷和客观临床评估指标。基于Hoehn和Yahr分期定义三个临床类别（健康、轻度PD、中重度PD），采用分层交叉验证和不平衡感知训练，使用XGBoost进行分类，并通过SHAP提供全局解释和局部患者级解释。

Result: XGBoost在所有任务中表现最佳：健康vs轻度准确率95.48%，健康vs中重度准确率99.44%，轻度vs中重度准确率96.78%；三分类严重程度任务准确率94.14%，Macro-F1为0.8775。可解释性分析揭示了从早期运动特征向进展相关轴性和平衡障碍的转变。

Conclusion: 研究表明，PPMI队列中的多模态临床评估可以支持准确且可解释的访问级别PD严重程度分层，为临床决策提供有价值的参考。

Abstract: Parkinson's disease (PD) is a progressive disorder in which symptom burden and functional impairment evolve over time, making severity staging essential for clinical monitoring and treatment planning. However, many computational studies emphasize binary PD detection and do not fully use repeated follow-up clinical assessments for stage-aware prediction. This study proposes STEP-PD, a severity-aware machine learning framework to classify PD severity using clinically interpretable boundaries. It leverages all available visits from the Parkinson's Progression Markers Initiative (PPMI) and integrates routinely collected subjective questionnaires and objective clinician-assessed measures. Disease severity is defined using Hoehn and Yahr staging and grouped into three clinically meaningful categories: Healthy, Mild PD (stages 1-2), and Moderate-to-Severe PD (stages 3-5). Three binary classification problems and a three-class severity task were evaluated using stratified cross-validation with imbalance-aware training. To enhance interpretability, SHAP was used to provide global explanations and local patient-level waterfall explanations. Across all tasks, XGBoost achieved the strongest and most stable performance, with accuracies of 95.48% (Healthy vs. Mild), 99.44% (Healthy vs. Moderate-to-Severe), and 96.78% (Mild vs. Moderate-to-Severe), and 94.14% accuracy with 0.8775 Macro-F1 for three-class severity classification. Explainability results highlight a shift from early motor features to progression-related axial and balance impairments. These findings show that multimodal clinical assessments within the PPMI cohort can support accurate and interpretable visit-level PD severity stratification.

</details>


### [312] [Conditional Attribution for Root Cause Analysis in Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17616)
*Shashank Mishra,Karan Patil,Cedric Schockaert,Didier Stricker,Jason Rambach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种条件归因框架用于时间序列异常检测的根因分析，通过在潜在空间中检索上下文相关的正常实例作为基准，解决了现有方法依赖不切实际的特征扰动和忽略依赖关系的问题，在SWaT和MSDS数据集上显著提升了根因识别准确性和时序定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列异常检测根因分析方法存在两个关键缺陷：1) 依赖不切实际的特征扰动，导致归因不可靠；2) 忽略时间依赖和跨特征依赖关系。这些方法通常使用边际分布或随机采样的基准，无法提供具有操作意义的解释。

Method: 提出条件归因框架：1) 基于异常观测条件检索具有上下文相似性的正常实例作为基准，保持依赖关系且具有操作意义；2) 利用变分自编码器潜在空间和UMAP流形嵌入在低维表示中进行上下文检索，支持高维时间序列数据；3) 该策略基于系统学习的流形进行检索，避免分布外伪影，确保归因保真度和计算效率；4) 引入置信度感知和时序评估指标来评估解释的可靠性和响应性。

Result: 在SWaT和MSDS基准数据集上的实验表明：1) 在多种异常检测模型上持续提升根因识别准确性；2) 改善时序定位能力；3) 增强鲁棒性。结果验证了条件归因在复杂时间序列系统可解释异常诊断中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 条件归因框架通过在学习的流形上检索上下文相关的正常实例，有效解决了传统方法的局限性，为复杂时间序列系统提供了可靠且可解释的异常诊断方案，具有重要的实际应用价值。代码和模型将公开发布。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) for time-series anomaly detection is critical for the reliable operation of complex real-world systems. Existing explanation methods often rely on unrealistic feature perturbations and ignore temporal and cross-feature dependencies, leading to unreliable attributions. We propose a conditional attribution framework that explains anomalies relative to contextually similar normal system states. Instead of using marginal or randomly sampled baselines, our method retrieves representative normal instances conditioned on the anomalous observation, enabling dependency-preserving and operationally meaningful explanations. To support high-dimensional time-series data, contextual retrieval is performed in learned low-dimensional representations using both variational autoencoder latent spaces and UMAP manifold embeddings. By grounding the retrieval process in the system's learned manifold, this strategy avoids out-of-distribution artifacts and ensures attribution fidelity while maintaining computational efficiency. We further introduce confidence-aware and temporal evaluation metrics for assessing explanation reliability and responsiveness. Experiments on the SWaT and MSDS benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach consistently improves root-cause identification accuracy, temporal localization, and robustness across multiple anomaly detection models. These results highlight the practical utility of conditional attribution for explainable anomaly diagnosis in complex time-series systems. Code and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [313] [STRIKE: Additive Feature-Group-Aware Stacking Framework for Credit Default Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17622)
*Swattik Maiti,Ritik Pratap Singh,Fardina Fathmiul Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STRIKE框架，通过语义特征分组和堆叠集成策略，解决了高维异构信贷数据中的过拟合和鲁棒性问题，在三个真实数据集上AUC-ROC优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信贷风险违约预测数据具有高维、异构、噪声大的特点，传统单一模型容易过拟合且在分布偏移下鲁棒性差；亟需一种既能保持可解释性又能提升泛化能力的建模框架。

Method: 提出STRIKE框架：首先将特征空间按语义划分为若干连贯的组；其次在每个组内独立训练基学习器；最后通过元学习器聚合各组的预测信号。该框架基于风险建模的可加性视角，将不同特征源的互补证据进行结构化融合。

Result: 在涵盖企业破产和消费者信贷的三组真实数据集上进行评估，STRIKE在AUC-ROC指标上持续优于强树模型基线和传统堆叠方法；消融实验验证性能提升源于有意义的特征分解而非单纯增加模型复杂度。

Conclusion: STRIKE是一种稳定、可扩展且可解释的信贷违约预测框架，能够有效处理高维异构数据，兼具性能优势与结构化建模的可解释性。

Abstract: Credit risk default prediction remains a cornerstone of risk management in the financial industry. The task involves estimating the likelihood that a borrower will fail to meet debt obligations, an objective critical for lending decisions, portfolio optimization, and regulatory compliance. Traditional machine learning models such as logistic regression and tree-based ensembles are widely adopted for their interpretability and strong empirical performance. However, modern credit datasets are high-dimensional, heterogeneous, and noisy, increasing overfitting risk in monolithic models and reducing robustness under distributional shift. We introduce STRIKE (Stacking via Targeted Representations of Isolated Knowledge Extractors), a feature-group-aware stacking framework for structured tabular credit risk data. Rather than training a single monolithic model on the complete dataset, STRIKE partitions the feature space into semantically coherent groups and trains independent learners within each group. This decomposition is motivated by an additive perspective on risk modeling, where distinct feature sources contribute complementary evidence that can be combined through a structured aggregation. The resulting group-specific predictions are integrated through a meta-learner that aggregates signals while maintaining robustness and modularity. We evaluate STRIKE on three real-world datasets spanning corporate bankruptcy and consumer lending scenarios. Across all settings, STRIKE consistently outperforms strong tree-based baselines and conventional stacking approaches in terms of AUC-ROC. Ablation studies confirm that performance gains stem from meaningful feature decomposition rather than increased model complexity. Our findings demonstrate that STRIKE is a stable, scalable, and interpretable framework for credit risk default prediction tasks.

</details>


### [314] [ATLAS: Constitution-Conditioned Latent Geometry and Redistribution Across Language Models and Neural Perturbation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17663)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATLAS方法，追踪宪法条件训练在模型中引发的潜在几何结构，证明该结构可在不同模型和生物基质间迁移复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宪法条件后训练可被视为对模型学习表征几何的结构化扰动，现有方法将分析单元视为单一行为、神经元、向量或补丁，缺乏对局部结构及其在系统变化下稳定性的系统研究。

Method: 引入ATLAS几何优先程序，测试局部图表（local chart），分析其切向结构、占据分布和行为耦合在系统变化下的表现。将分析单元从单一元素扩展为源定义的更广泛家族（source-defined family），并在Gemma模型、未适配的Phi模型以及ALM8小鼠额叶皮层扰动数据中进行验证。

Result: 在Gemma模型中，锚定源局部图表捕获310/320个源行和全部84/84个分数翻转行；在Phi模型中，目标局部实现的确认对比达到AUC 0.984，平均间隔5.50；在ALM8小鼠数据中，源定义家族在5折交叉验证中获得支持，平均留出AUC 0.72，平均折间隔4.50。

Conclusion: 书面宪法可诱导可恢复的潜在几何结构，其组织形式在模型和基质变化下仍可检测，但局部坐标、占据和行为表达会发生变化，这是一种几何递归重分布现象，而非坐标同一性、位点同一性或目标侧中介定理。

Abstract: Constitution-conditioned post-training can be analysed as a structured perturbation of a model's learned representational geometry. We introduce ATLAS, a geometry-first program that traces constitution-induced hidden-state structure across charts, models, and substrates. Instead of treating the relevant unit as a single behaviour, neuron, vector, or patch, ATLAS tests a local chart whose tangent structure, occupancy distribution, and behavioural coupling can be measured under system change. On Gemma, the anchored source-local chart captures 310 / 320 reviewed source rows and all 84 / 84 reviewed score-flip rows, but compact exact-patch sufficiency does not close, so the exportable unit is the broader source-defined family. Freezing that family, we re-identify a target-local realisation in an unadapted Phi model, where the fully adjudicated confirmatory contrast separates with AUC 0.984 and mean gap 5.50. In held-out ALM8 mouse frontal-cortex perturbation data, the same source-defined family receives support across 5/5 folds, with mean held-out AUC 0.72 and mean fold gap 4.50. A multiple-choice analysis provides the main boundary: nearby target-local signals can appear without source-faithful closure. The resulting correspondence is not coordinate identity, site identity, or a target-side mediation theorem. It is geometric recurrence under redistribution: written constitutions can induce recoverable latent geometry whose organisation remains detectable across model and substrate changes while its local coordinates, occupancy, and behavioural expression shift.

</details>


### [315] [Prior-Fitted Functional Flow: In-Context Generative Models for Pharmacokinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17670)
*César Ojeda,Niklas Hartung,Wilhelm Huisinga,Tim Jahn,Purity Kamene Kavwele,Marian Klose,Piyush Kumar,Ramsés J. Sánchez,Darius A. Faroughy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Prior-Fitted Functional Flows的药代动力学生成基础模型，能够实现零样本群体合成和个体预测，无需手动调参，并在真实数据集上达到了最先进的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药代动力学分析通常面临数据稀疏、观测不规则以及需要手动参数调整等挑战，缺乏能够同时处理群体合成和个体预测的通用模型。本文旨在开发一个生成式基础模型，利用文献语料库构建先验知识，以解决上述问题。

Method: 提出Prior-Fitted Functional Flows模型，学习显式以整个研究人群的稀疏、不规则数据为条件的功能向量场。构建了一个新的开放获取文献语料库来指导模型先验，从而能够生成连贯的虚拟队列并对部分观测的患者轨迹进行预测。

Result: 该模型能够在无需手动参数调整的情况下实现零样本群体合成和个体轨迹预测，并提供校准的不确定性估计。在广泛的真实世界数据集上展示了最先进的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将基础模型范式引入药代动力学领域，通过学习功能向量场和利用文献先验，实现了对稀疏不规则数据的有效建模，为药物开发和临床预测提供了强有力的新工具。

Abstract: We introduce Prior-Fitted Functional Flows, a generative foundation model for pharmacokinetics that enables zero-shot population synthesis and individual forecasting without manual parameter tuning. We learn functional vector fields, explicitly conditioned on the sparse, irregular data of an entire study population. This enables the generation of coherent virtual cohorts as well as forecasting of partially observed patient trajectories with calibrated uncertainty. We construct a new open-access literature corpus to inform our priors, and demonstrate state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on extensive real-world datasets.

</details>


### [316] [Grokking of Diffusion Models: Case Study on Modular Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17673)
*Joon Hyeok Kim,Yong-Hyun Park,Mattis Dalsætra Østby,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型在模加法任务上表现出“顿悟”现象，并通过机制分析发现模型通过组合周期性表示和分阶段采样过程来实现算术推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型取得了巨大的经验成功，但从机制角度理解其泛化能力仍存在空白，特别是关于模型如何桥接连续像素生成与离散符号推理尚未被探索。

Method: 基于流匹配目标训练扩散模型，利用模加法任务诱导“顿悟”现象以进行可控分析。研究分为两个数据层面：单一图像层面进行机制解剖，以及高类内变化的多图像层面进行动态采样分析。

Result: 在单一图像层面，发现模型通过组合单个操作数的周期性表示来实现模加法；在多图像层面，发现模型利用迭代采样过程将任务分解为两个阶段：算术计算阶段和视觉去噪阶段，两者由一个关键时间步阈值分隔。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了扩散模型中算法学习机制分解，揭示了模型如何通过分阶段计算和周期性表示构建，成功桥接连续像素空间生成与离散符号推理。

Abstract: Despite their empirical success, how diffusion models generalize remains poorly understood from a mechanistic perspective. We demonstrate that diffusion models trained with flow-matching objectives exhibit grokking--delayed generalization after overfitting--on modular addition, enabling controlled analysis of their internal computations. We study this phenomenon across two levels of data regime. In a single-image regime, mechanistic dissection reveals that the model implements modular addition by composing periodic representations of individual operands. In a diverse-image regime with high intraclass variability, we find that the model leverages its iterative sampling process to partition the task into an arithmetic computation phase followed by a visual denoising phase, separated by a critical timestep threshold. Our work provides the mechanistic decomposition of algorithmic learning in diffusion models, revealing how these models bridge continuous pixel-space generation and discrete symbolic reasoning.

</details>


### [317] [SafeAnchor: Preventing Cumulative Safety Erosion in Continual Domain Adaptation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17691)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeAnchor框架，通过识别低秩安全子空间并约束梯度更新方向，有效解决了大语言模型在多领域持续微调过程中安全对齐逐渐丧失的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现大语言模型的安全对齐非常脆弱且集中在输出层的前几个token，仅通过100个对抗样本微调即可逆转。在现实部署中，模型需要在不同领域（如医学、法律、代码）进行序列化适应，导致安全防护栏累积性侵蚀。然而现有安全保护方法仅针对单任务微调，多领域序列化设置尚未被解决。

Method: SafeAnchor框架包含三个核心步骤：1）通过Fisher Information特征分解在LoRA参数空间中识别低秩安全子空间；2）将领域特定的梯度更新约束在这些子空间的正交补空间中；3）通过阈值触发的纠正性回放监控残余安全偏移。该方法在整个持续适应过程中锚定安全对齐。

Result: 在Llama-2-7B-Chat和Mistral-7B-Instruct模型上进行评估，涵盖三领域流水线和八个基准测试。SafeAnchor保留了93.2%的原始安全对齐性能，相比所有基线方法提升18-42个百分点，同时在领域任务上的性能与无约束微调相比仅下降1.5个百分点以内。

Conclusion: SafeAnchor成功解决了多领域持续适应场景下的安全对齐保护问题，证明了通过在参数空间中识别并保护安全子空间，可以在保持模型领域适应能力的同时有效防止安全性能的累积性衰退，为大语言模型的安全部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models is remarkably shallow: it is concentrated in the first few output tokens and reversible by fine-tuning on as few as 100 adversarial examples. This fragility becomes critical in real-world deployment, where models undergo sequential adaptation across domains such as medicine, law, and code, causing safety guardrails to erode cumulatively. Yet all existing safety-preserving methods target only single-task fine-tuning, leaving the multi-domain sequential setting entirely unaddressed.
  We introduce SafeAnchor, a framework that anchors safety in place throughout continual adaptation. SafeAnchor first identifies low-rank safety subspaces in LoRA parameter space via Fisher Information eigendecomposition, then constrains domain-specific gradient updates to the orthogonal complement of these subspaces, and finally monitors for residual safety drift with threshold-triggered corrective replay. Evaluated on Llama-2-7B-Chat and Mistral-7B-Instruct across a three-domain pipeline and eight benchmarks, SafeAnchor retains 93.2% of original safety alignment, outperforming all baselines by 18-42 points, while matching unconstrained fine-tuning to within 1.5 points on domain tasks.

</details>


### [318] [MoE-nD: Per-Layer Mixture-of-Experts Routing for Multi-Axis KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17695)
*Libo Sun,Peixiong He,Po-Wei Harn,Xiao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MoE-nD框架，通过异构的逐层KV缓存压缩策略（混合token驱逐与量化），在14倍压缩率下保持与未压缩基线相当的精度，显著优于现有同质化压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法对所有层采用相同的压缩策略，忽视了不同层对压缩操作的敏感性差异。这种同质化处理导致精度损失，因为不同层对token驱逐和量化的响应截然不同，最优的逐层压缩组合远非均匀。

Method: 提出MoE-nD混合专家框架，为每一层路由到其最优的（驱逐率、K位数、V位数）元组。采用离线校准的贪心求解器，在全局内存预算约束下选择最小化预测质量损失的路由方案；推理时通过单一注意力补丁联合应用逐层异构驱逐与量化。

Result: 在LongBench-v1子集（16k输入）上，MoE-nD异构变体在14倍压缩（136MB）下匹配1.9GB未压缩基线，而其他压缩基线在相当或更小内存下得分均低于8/100。在AIME推理基准上，相比最强逐层量化基线提升6-27分。两个无效结果（MATH-500、LongBench TREC）因短输入导致求解器在多数层选择keep=1.0，清晰刻画了逐层驱逐路由的适用边界。

Conclusion: 逐层异构压缩策略显著优于同质化方法，通过精细化分配不同层间的驱逐与量化配置，可在大幅压缩内存的同时保持模型精度。研究还明确了该方法的适用条件：对短输入任务，驱逐操作空间有限。

Abstract: KV cache memory is the dominant bottleneck for long-context LLM inference. Existing compression methods each act on a single axis of the four-dimensional KV tensor -- token eviction (sequence), quantization (precision), low-rank projection (head dimension), or cross-layer sharing -- but apply the same recipe to every layer. We show that this homogeneity leaves accuracy on the table: different layers respond very differently to each compression operation, and the optimal per-layer mix of eviction and quantization is far from uniform. We propose MoE-nD, a mixture-of-experts framework that routes each layer to its own (eviction-ratio, K-bits, V-bits) tuple under a global memory budget. An offline-calibrated greedy solver chooses the routing that minimizes predicted quality loss; at inference time, per-layer heterogeneous eviction and quantization are applied jointly through a single attention patch. On a 4-task subset of LongBench-v1 (16k inputs, n=50 per task, adapted reasoning-model protocol; see section Experiments), MoE-nD's hetero variant matches our uncompressed 1.9~GB baseline at 14x compression (136~MB) while every other compressed baseline we tested (1d, 2d_uniform, 2d) at comparable or smaller memory stays under 8/100. The gains hold on AIME reasoning benchmarks (+6 to +27 pts over the strongest per-layer-quantization baseline across eight configurations). Two null results -- MATH-500 and LongBench's TREC -- share a principled cause (short inputs, solver picks keep=1.0 on most layers), cleanly characterizing when per-layer eviction routing has headroom to help.

</details>


### [319] [The Geometric Canary: Predicting Steerability and Detecting Drift via Representational Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17698)
*Prashant C. Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何稳定性概念，发现其监督变体可近乎完美预测模型的可控性，而无监督变体则在漂移检测上表现优异，两者共同构成了LLM部署生命周期中的互补诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的可靠部署需要两项关键能力：预测模型是否接受行为控制以及检测其内部结构退化。尽管这两项能力看似不同，但作者发现它们拥有共同的几何基础，旨在寻找一个统一的几何指标来解决这两个问题。

Method: 作者提出了几何稳定性概念，即表征成对距离结构的一致性，并设计了监督（Shesha变体，任务对齐）和无监督两种测量方法。研究在35-69个嵌入模型和三个NLP任务上进行了实验，并将该方法与CKA、Procrustes等基线进行对比。

Result: 监督的任务对齐几何稳定性能精准预测线性可控性（ρ=0.89-0.97），捕获了类别可分性之外的独特方差；无监督稳定性虽然在可控性预测上失效（ρ≈0.10），但在漂移检测上表现卓越，其测量几何变化的能力是CKA的近2倍（Llama中高达5.23倍），误报率比Procrustes低6倍，并在73%的模型中提供更早的预警。

Conclusion: 监督和无监督的几何稳定性形成了互补的诊断工具：前者适用于部署前的可控性评估，后者适用于部署后的监控。这种关键分离表明，任务对齐对于可控性预测至关重要，而无监督方法则更适合检测模型结构的退化。

Abstract: Reliable deployment of language models requires two capabilities that appear distinct but share a common geometric foundation: predicting whether a model will accept targeted behavioral control, and detecting when its internal structure degrades. We show that geometric stability, the consistency of a representation's pairwise distance structure, addresses both. Supervised Shesha variants that measure task-aligned geometric stability predict linear steerability with near-perfect accuracy ($ρ= 0.89$-$0.97$) across 35-69 embedding models and three NLP tasks, capturing unique variance beyond class separability (partial $ρ= 0.62$-$0.76$). A critical dissociation emerges: unsupervised stability fails entirely for steering on real-world tasks ($ρ\approx 0.10$), revealing that task alignment is essential for controllability prediction. However, unsupervised stability excels at drift detection, measuring nearly $2\times$ greater geometric change than CKA during post-training alignment (up to $5.23\times$ in Llama) while providing earlier warning in 73\% of models and maintaining a $6\times$ lower false alarm rate than Procrustes. Together, supervised and unsupervised stability form complementary diagnostics for the LLM deployment lifecycle: one for pre-deployment controllability assessment, the other for post-deployment monitoring.

</details>


### [320] [Modeling Higher-Order Brain Interactions via a Multi-View Information Bottleneck Framework for fMRI-based Psychiatric Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17713)
*Kunyu Zhang,Qiang Li,Vince D. Calhoun,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于O信息的多视图信息瓶颈框架，用于fMRI精神疾病诊断，能够显式区分高阶脑连接中的协同与冗余信息，显著提升了诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的静息态fMRI诊断方法主要依赖成对脑连接，忽略了高阶交互（HOIs）。虽然超图方法可以编码高阶交互，但其构建依赖启发式相似性度量，无法显式区分交互是协同主导还是冗余主导。

Method: 引入O信息作为有符号度量来表征高阶交互的信息性质；将三阶和四阶O信息整合到统一的多视图信息瓶颈框架中，系统融合成对、三重和四重脑交互，并显式惩罚冗余；开发了高斯解析近似和基于随机矩阵的Rényi熵估计器两种加速策略，实现超过30倍的加速。

Result: 在四个基准数据集（REST-meta-MDD, ABIDE, UCLA, ADNI）上进行了广泛评估，性能优于包括最先进图神经网络（GNN）和超图方法在内的11种基线方法；揭示了可解释的区域级协同-冗余模式。

Conclusion: 该方法有效克服了传统超图方法的局限性，能够更全面地捕捉脑连接信息并区分协同与冗余，为fMRI精神疾病诊断提供了更准确且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has emerged as a cornerstone for psychiatric diagnosis, yet most approaches rely on pairwise brain cortical or sub-cortical connectivities that overlooks higher-order interactions (HOIs) central to complex brain dynamics. While hypergraph methods encode HOIs through predefined hyperedges, their construction typically relies on heuristic similarity metrics and does not explicitly characterize whether interactions are synergy- or redundancy-dominated. In this paper, we introduce $O$-information, a signed measure that characterizes the informational nature of HOIs, and integrate third- and fourth-order $O$-information into a unified multi-view information bottleneck framework for fMRI-based psychiatric diagnosis. To enable scalable $O$-information estimation, we further develop two independent acceleration strategies: a Gaussian analytical approximation and a randomized matrix-based Rényi entropy estimator, achieving over a 30-fold computational speedup compared with conventional estimators. Our tri-view architecture systematically fuses pairwise, triadic, and tetradic brain interactions, capturing comprehensive brain connectivity while explicitly penalizing redundancy. Extensive evaluation across four benchmark datasets (REST-meta-MDD, ABIDE, UCLA, ADNI) demonstrates consistent improvements, outperforming 11 baseline methods including state-of-the-art graph neural network (GNN) and hypergraph based approaches. Moreover, our method reveals interpretable region-level synergy-redundancy patterns which are not explicitly characterized by conventional hypergraph formulations.

</details>


### [321] [FlashFPS: Efficient Farthest Point Sampling for Large-Scale Point Clouds via Pruning and Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17720)
*Yuzhe Fu,Hancheng Ye,Cong Guo,Junyao Zhang,Qinsi Wang,Yueqian Lin,Changchun Zhou,Hai,Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashFPS通过识别并消除最远点采样（FPS）中的三类冗余，实现了PNN推理显著加速（GPU上最高5.16倍），且精度损失可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最远点采样（FPS）是点云神经网络（PNN）的核心操作，但其带来的巨大推理延迟成为主要瓶颈。现有的CUDA和硬件级优化未能根本解决问题，因为PNN多层结构中的FPS计算存在大量冗余，限制了模型的可扩展性。

Method: 提出FlashFPS框架，包含FPS-Prune和FPS-Cache两个组件。FPS-Prune引入候选修剪和迭代修剪，减少不必要的全点云计算和后期冗余迭代；FPS-Cache通过缓存复用机制消除层间冗余，利用层间输出的可预测性避免重复计算。

Result: FlashFPS在GPU上相比标准CUDA基线实现5.16倍加速，在PNN加速器上实现2.69倍加速，同时保持极小的精度损失。该方法具有硬件无关性，可作为即插即用模块集成到现有CUDA库和PNN加速器中。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析识别FPS中的三类冗余并提出针对性优化策略，FlashFPS成功解决了PNN中的FPS瓶颈问题，实现了高效且可扩展的点云推理，为大规模点云处理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Point-based Neural Networks (PNNs) have become a key approach for point cloud processing. However, a core operation in these models, Farthest Point Sampling (FPS), often introduces significant inference latency, especially for large-scale processing. Despite existing CUDA- and hardware-level optimizations, FPS remains a major bottleneck due to exhaustive computations across multiple network layers in PNNs, which hinders scalability. Through systematic analysis, we identify three substantial redundancies in FPS, including unnecessary full-cloud computations, redundant late-stage iterations, and predictable inter-layer outputs that make later FPS computations avoidable. To address these, we propose \textbf{\textit{FlashFPS}}, a hardware-agnostic, plug-and-play framework for FPS acceleration, composed of \textit{FPS-Prune} and \textit{FPS-Cache}. \textit{FPS-Prune} introduces candidate pruning and iteration pruning to reduce redundant computations in FPS while preserving sampling quality, and \textit{FPS-Cache} eliminates layer-wise redundancy via cache-and-reuse. Integrated into existing CUDA libraries and state-of-the-art PNN accelerators, \textit{FlashFPS} achieves 5.16$\times$ speedup over the standard CUDA baseline on GPU and 2.69$\times$ on PNN accelerators, with negligible accuracy loss, enabling efficient and scalable PNN inference. Codes are released at https://github.com/Yuzhe-Fu/FlashFPS.

</details>


### [322] [Tool Learning Needs Nothing More Than a Free 8B Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17739)
*Chenming Tang,Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Weijie Liu,Junqiang Zheng,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TRUSTEE方法，利用免费开源小模型（如8B）完全模拟动态环境，结合自适应课程学习，在无需标注数据或昂贵外部资源的情况下训练工具调用Agent，并取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的工具调用Agent训练方法存在明显局限：要么依赖带有真值标注的训练数据，要么需要昂贵的商业大模型来合成环境，且环境一旦生成就保持固定，缺乏灵活性，资源成本高。

Method: 提出TRUSTEE框架，一种无需外部数据的方法。核心是使用免费开源的小型语言模型（可小至8B参数）完全模拟动态环境，包括任务生成、用户模拟、工具模拟和轨迹评估四个模块。同时引入自适应课程学习机制，在训练过程中动态控制任务难度的各个方面。

Result: 实验结果表明，TRUSTEE在多个领域均带来一致的性能提升，并且优于所有需要额外外部资源的基线方法。这证实了仅用本地8B模型作为骨干构建的模拟环境，即可为工具学习设定强有力的基线。

Conclusion: 通过精巧的设计，即使使用8B参数的小型本地模型模拟环境，也能在无需昂贵标注数据、真实人类交互、可执行工具或人类专家/商业大模型提供的可验证环境的情况下，实现强有力的工具学习性能。该范式有望启发未来在有限资源下进行环境扩展的研究。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a prevalent paradigm for training tool calling agents, which typically requires online interactive environments. Existing approaches either rely on training data with ground truth annotations or require advanced commercial language models (LMs) to synthesize environments that keep fixed once created. In this work, we propose TRUSTEE, a data-free method training tool calling agents with dynamic environments fully simulated by free open-source LMs that can be as small as 8B, including task generation, user simulation, tool simulation and trajectory evaluation, paired with an adaptive curriculum learning mechanism that controls various aspects of the task difficulty dynamically during training. Our empirical results show that TRUSTEE brings consistent improvements across various domains and outperforms all the baselines which require extra external resources for training. These confirm that, with a sufficiently sophisticated design, even simulated environments with a local 8B LM as the backbone could set a strong baseline for tool learning, without expensive annotated data, realistic human interactions, executable tools or costly verifiable environments from human experts or commercial LMs. We hope our proposed paradigm could inspire future research on environment scaling with limited resources.

</details>


### [323] [Efficient Federated RLHF via Zeroth-Order Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17747)
*Deyi Wang,Qining Zhang,Lei Ying*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Par-S²ZPO算法，一种高效的联邦强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）方法，通过零阶优化和二进制扰动设计，实现了低通信、计算和内存开销，在样本复杂度上与集中式方法相当，但在策略更新迭代上收敛更快，并在MuJoCo任务上超越了FedAvg-based RLHF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决资源受限设备（如边缘设备）在联邦学习环境下进行强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）时所面临的通信、计算和内存资源限制问题，旨在设计一种适应这些约束的高效算法。

Method: 提出了一种名为Partitioned, Sign-based Stochastic Zeroth-order Policy Optimization (Par-S²ZPO) 的联邦RLHF算法。该方法基于带有二进制扰动的零阶优化技术进行构建，从算法设计层面确保了低通信、计算和内存复杂度。

Result: 理论分析建立了Par-S²ZPO收敛速率的上界，证明其在样本复杂度方面与集中式对应方法一样高效，但在策略更新迭代次数方面收敛更快；实验结果表明，在四个MuJoCo强化学习任务上，该算法优于基于FedAvg的RLHF方法。

Conclusion: Par-S²ZPO算法成功解决了资源受限环境下的联邦RLHF问题，在保持样本效率的同时实现了更快的迭代收敛速度和更优的实际性能，为边缘设备上的RLHF应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper considers reinforcement learning from human feedback in a federated learning setting with resource-constrained agents, such as edge devices. We propose an efficient federated RLHF algorithm, named Partitioned, Sign-based Stochastic Zeroth-order Policy Optimization (Par-S$^2$ZPO). The algorithm is built on zeroth-order optimization with binary perturbation, resulting in low communication, computation, and memory complexity by design. Our theoretical analysis establishes an upper bound on the convergence rate of Par-S$^2$ZPO, revealing that it is as efficient as its centralized counterpart in terms of sample complexity but converges faster in terms of policy update iterations. Our experimental results show that it outperforms a FedAvg-based RLHF on four MuJoCo RL tasks.

</details>


### [324] [HiP-LoRA: Budgeted Spectral Plasticity for Robust Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17751)
*Lixian Chen,Jianhong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HiP-LoRA框架，通过谱分解将更新分为主通道和残差通道，并利用奇异值加权稳定性预算来缓解LoRA的谱干扰问题，在资源预算受限的情况下有效平衡预训练能力保留与任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LoRA方法存在谱干扰问题：低秩更新往往将能量集中在预训练权重的主奇异方向上，这会扰动模型的通用能力，导致灾难性遗忘和多适配器合并时的脆弱性。

Method: HiP-LoRA利用预训练层缓存的SVD分解，将更新分解为两个通道：(1)在主导奇异子空间内的主通道；(2)在正交补空间中的残差低秩通道。在主通道上应用奇异值加权的稳定性预算，持续平衡预训练行为保留与任务特定可塑性。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B上的实验表明，在相同预算条件下，HiP-LoRA显著减少了预训练退化，大幅降低了多适配器MergeFail率，在持续调优和知识编辑等对干扰敏感的任务中稳健地超越了基线方法。

Conclusion: HiP-LoRA成功解决了LoRA的谱干扰问题，通过谱感知的适配框架有效平衡了模型的能力保留与任务适配，为资源受限场景下的基础模型适配提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Adapting foundation models under resource budgets relies heavily on Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), with LoRA being a standard modular solution. However, LoRA suffers from spectral interference. Low-rank updates often concentrate energy on the leading singular directions of pretrained weights, perturbing general capabilities and causing catastrophic forgetting and fragile multi-adapter merging. To resolve this, we propose HiP-LoRA, a spectrum-aware adaptation framework. Utilizing the cached singular value decomposition (SVD) of pretrained layers, HiP-LoRA decomposes updates into two channels: a principal channel within the dominant singular subspace, and a residual low-rank channel in the orthogonal complement. A singular-value-weighted stability budget on the principal channel continuously balances pretrained behavior preservation with task-specific plasticity. Experiments on Llama-3.1-8B demonstrate that under matched budgets, HiP-LoRA drastically reduces pretraining degradation and multi-adapter MergeFail, robustly outperforming baselines in interference-sensitive tasks like continual tuning and knowledge editing.

</details>


### [325] [LLM-AUG: Robust Wireless Data Augmentation with In-Context Learning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17770)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Manan Tiwari,Sayanta Seth,Vijay K. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLM-AUG框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习在嵌入空间生成合成样本，解决了无线通信深度学习中的数据稀缺问题，在低样本场景下显著优于传统和深度生成基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在无线通信应用中面临数据稀缺的根本性瓶颈，特别是在采集标注射频数据成本高、耗时长或受操作限制的场景下，迫切需要高效的数据增强方法。

Method: 提出LLM-AUG框架，利用大语言模型的上下文学习能力，通过结构化提示直接在学习的嵌入空间生成合成训练样本，无需训练特定任务模型，实现低样本场景的快速适应。

Result: 在RadioML 2016.10A和干扰分类数据集上，LLM-AUG仅用15%标注数据即接近oracle性能；在低SNR下相比扩散模型有29.4%的相对增益，在两个数据集上分别获得67.6%和35.7%的相对提升；t-SNE可视化验证生成样本更好地保留了类别结构。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可作为无线机器学习的有效实用数据增强器，在演进的无线环境中实现鲁棒和数据高效的学习。
