Abstract: Data scarcity remains a fundamental bottleneck in applying deep learning to wireless communication problems, particularly in scenarios where collecting labeled Radio Frequency (RF) data is expensive, time-consuming, or operationally constrained. This paper proposes LLM-AUG, a data augmentation framework that leverages in-context learning in large language models (LLMs) to generate synthetic training samples directly in a learned embedding space. Unlike conventional generative approaches that require training task-specific models, LLM-AUG performs data generation through structured prompting, enabling rapid adaptation in low-shot regimes. We evaluate LLM-AUG on two representative tasks: modulation classification and interference classification using the RadioML 2016.10A dataset, and the Interference Classification (IC) dataset respectively. Results show that LLM-AUG consistently outperforms traditional augmentation and deep generative baselines across low-shot settings and reaches near oracle performance using only 15% labeled data. LLM-AUG further demonstrates improved robustness under distribution shifts, yielding a 29.4% relative gain over diffusion-based augmentation at a lower SNR value. On the RadioML and IC datasets, LLM-AUG yields a relative gain of 67.6% and 35.7% over the diffusion-based baseline. The t-SNE visualizations further validate that synthetic samples generated by better preserve class structure in the embedding space, leading to more consistent and informative augmentations. These results demonstrate that LLMs can serve as effective and practical data augmenters for wireless machine learning, enabling robust and data-efficient learning in evolving wireless environments.

</details>


### [326] [TeleEmbedBench: A Multi-Corpus Embedding Benchmark for RAG in Telecommunications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17778)
*Pranshav Gajjar,Vijay K Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleEmbedBench，这是首个专为电信领域设计的大规模多语料嵌入模型基准，涵盖O-RAN规范、3GPP文档和srsRAN代码库，共9000个问答对。实验表明LLM类嵌入模型（如Qwen3、EmbeddingGemma）在检索准确性和鲁棒性上显著优于传统句子转换器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在电信领域关键任务中日益依赖检索增强生成（RAG）技术，但现有通用基准无法评估电信语料的特殊性——密集、缩写多、交叉引用频繁。缺乏针对电信领域的嵌入模型评估基准是本研究要解决的核心问题。

Method: 构建TeleEmbedBench基准，包含三个异构语料库（O-RAN Alliance规范、3GPP发布文档、srsRAN开源代码库），共9000个问题-文本块对，覆盖三种标准分块大小（512、1024、2048 tokens）。采用自动化流水线构建数据集：一个LLM从文本块生成特定查询，另一个LLM按严格标准验证。评估8种嵌入模型，包括传统句子转换器和LLM类嵌入器。同时引入TeleEmbedBench-Clean评估模型对噪声、不完整查询的鲁棒性。

Result: 1）LLM类嵌入模型（Qwen3、EmbeddingGemma）在检索准确性和抗跨域干扰鲁棒性上持续且显著优于传统句子转换器；2）领域特定任务指令能提升原始源代码的嵌入性能，但反而会降低自然语言电信规范的检索性能。

Conclusion: TeleEmbedBench填补了电信领域嵌入模型评估的空白，证明了LLM类嵌入模型在该领域的优越性，同时揭示了任务指令对不同类型电信文本的差异化影响，为电信领域RAG系统的嵌入模型选择和优化提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in the telecommunications domain for critical tasks, relying heavily on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to adapt general-purpose models to continuously evolving standards. However, a significant gap exists in evaluating the embedding models that power these RAG pipelines, as general-purpose benchmarks fail to capture the dense, acronym-heavy, and highly cross-referential nature of telecommunications corpora. To address this, we introduce TeleEmbedBench, the first large-scale, multi-corpus embedding benchmark designed specifically for telecommunications. The benchmark spans three heterogeneous corpora: O-RAN Alliance specifications, 3GPP release documents, and the srsRAN open-source codebase, comprising 9,000 question-chunk pairs across three standard chunk sizes (512, 1024, and 2048 tokens). To construct this dataset at scale without manual annotation bottlenecks, we employ a novel automated pipeline where one LLM generates specific queries from text chunks and a secondary LLM validates them across strict criteria. We comprehensively evaluate eight embedding models, spanning standard sentence-transformers and LLM-based embedders. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based embedders, such as Qwen3 and EmbeddingGemma, consistently and significantly outperform traditional sentence-transformers in both retrieval accuracy and robustness against cross-domain interference. Additionally, we introduce TeleEmbedBench-Clean to evaluate model robustness against noisy, incomplete user queries. Finally, our analysis reveals that while domain-specific task instructions improve embedder performance for raw source code, they paradoxically degrade retrieval performance for natural language telecommunications specifications.

</details>


### [327] [Ranking Abuse via Strategic Pairwise Data Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17805)
*Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于MLE的成对排序系统（如Bradley-Terry模型）面对对抗性扰动时的脆弱性，提出了一种自适应子集选择攻击方法（ASSA），发现这类系统存在急剧的相变行为，少量策略性投票者即可显著改变全局排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最大似然估计（MLE）的成对排序系统（如Bradley-Terry模型）被广泛用于聚合成对比较的偏好数据，但这类系统在面临策略性数据操纵时的鲁棒性尚未得到充分理解。研究旨在揭示MLE排序机制在面对对抗性扰动时的内在脆弱性。

Method: 将操纵任务建模为约束组合优化问题，并提出自适应子集选择攻击方法（Adaptive Subset Selection Attack, ASSA），以高效识别高影响的扰动。该方法在合成数据和真实选举数据集上进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验发现基于MLE的排序系统表现出急剧的相变行为：在超过一个小扰动预算后，有限数量的策略性投票者就能显著改变全局排名。ASSA方法在约束预算下持续优于随机和贪婪基线方法。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了MLE排序机制对结构化扰动存在根本性的敏感性，突显了在集体决策系统中开发更鲁棒聚合方法的必要性。

Abstract: Pairwise ranking systems based on Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE), such as the Bradley-Terry model, are widely used to aggregate preferences from pairwise comparisons. However, their robustness under strategic data manipulation remains insufficiently understood.
  In this paper, we study the vulnerability of MLE-based ranking systems to adversarial perturbations. We formulate the manipulation task as a constrained combinatorial optimization problem and propose an Adaptive Subset Selection Attack (ASSA) to efficiently identify high-impact perturbations.
  Experimental results on both synthetic data and real-world election datasets show that MLE-based rankings exhibit a sharp phase-transition behavior: beyond a small perturbation budget, a limited number of strategic voters can significantly alter the global ranking. In particular, our method consistently outperforms random and greedy baselines under constrained budgets.
  These findings reveal a fundamental sensitivity of MLE-based ranking mechanisms to structured perturbations and highlight the need for more robust aggregation methods in collective decision-making systems.

</details>


### [328] [Efficient Diffusion Models under Nonconvex Equality and Inequality constraints via Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17838)
*Kijung Jeon,Michael Muehlebach,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对非凸可行集的约束扩散模型统一框架，通过高效的着陆机制替代昂贵的投影操作，结合欠阻尼动力学加速收敛，在保证样本质量的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学和工程应用（如分子生成、机器人学）中，生成模型需要满足物理、几何或安全等约束条件。现有的约束扩散方法通常依赖昂贵且常定义不明的投影操作，导致计算成本高且容易失败。因此需要一种既能保证约束可行性，又能高效计算的解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个适用于通用非凸可行集Σ的约束扩散模型统一框架。核心创新点包括：(1) 同时在扩散过程中强制执行等式和不等式约束；(2) 集成过阻尼和欠阻尼动力学进行前向和后向采样；(3) 设计了一种计算高效的着陆机制，替代了传统的牛顿迭代求解投影，避免了投影失败问题；(4) 利用欠阻尼动力学加速向先验分布的混合。

Result: 在包含等式约束和混合约束的基准测试中，该方法在样本质量上与最先进的基线方法相当，但在训练和推理阶段的函数评估次数和内存使用量显著减少。实验证明了该方法在非凸可行集扩散问题上的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为非凸可行集上的扩散模型提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案，成功解决了传统约束扩散方法中计算成本高和投影操作困难的问题，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Generative modeling within constrained sets is essential for scientific and engineering applications involving physical, geometric, or safety requirements (e.g., molecular generation, robotics). We present a unified framework for constrained diffusion models on generic nonconvex feasible sets $Σ$ that simultaneously enforces equality and inequality constraints throughout the diffusion process. Our framework incorporates both overdamped and underdamped dynamics for forward and backward sampling. A key algorithmic innovation is a computationally efficient landing mechanism that replaces costly and often ill-defined projections onto $Σ$, ensuring feasibility without iterative Newton solves or projection failures. By leveraging underdamped dynamics, we accelerate mixing toward the prior distribution, effectively alleviating the high simulation costs typically associated with constrained diffusion. Empirically, this approach reduces function evaluations and memory usage during both training and inference while preserving sample quality. On benchmarks featuring equality and mixed constraints, our method achieves comparable sample quality to state-of-the-art baselines while significantly reducing computational cost, providing a practical and scalable solution for diffusion on nonconvex feasible sets.

</details>


### [329] [LEPO: \underline{L}atent R\underline{e}asoning \underline{P}olicy \underline{O}ptimization for Large Language~Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17892)
*Yuyan Zhou,Jiarui Yu,Hande Dong,Zhezheng Hao,Hong Wang,Jianqing Zhang,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEPO框架，通过Gumbel-Softmax将可控随机性注入潜在推理，解决了现有潜在推理方法因缺乏随机采样而退化为确定性推理的问题，在连续潜在表示上直接应用强化学习，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引入潜在推理的大语言模型方法因缺乏随机采样而不可避免地退化为确定性推理，无法发现多样化的推理路径，且与强化学习的兼容性不足。

Method: 通过Gumbel-Softmax将可控随机性注入潜在推理以恢复LLM的探索能力；提出LEPO框架，在rollout阶段保持随机性以实现多样化轨迹采样，在优化阶段构建统一的梯度估计用于潜在表示和离散token。

Result: 大量实验表明，LEPO显著优于现有的用于离散推理和潜在推理的强化学习方法。

Conclusion: LEPO成功地将强化学习直接应用于连续潜在表示，通过引入可控随机性解决了潜在推理的确定性退化问题，为LLM的潜在推理能力提升提供了有效的新框架。

Abstract: Recently, latent reasoning has been introduced into large language models (LLMs) to leverage rich information within a continuous space. However, without stochastic sampling, these methods inevitably collapse to deterministic inference, failing to discover diverse reasoning paths. To bridge the gap, we inject controllable stochasticity into latent reasoning via Gumbel-Softmax, restoring LLMs' exploratory capacity and enhancing their compatibility with Reinforcement Learning (RL). Building on this, we propose \textbf{\underline{L}}atent R\textbf{\underline{e}}asoning \textbf{\underline{P}}olicy \textbf{\underline{O}}ptimization~(\textbf{LEPO}), a novel framework that applies RL directly to continuous latent representations. Specifically, in rollout stage, LEPO maintains stochasticity to enable diverse trajectory sampling, while in optimization stage, LEPO constructs a unified gradient estimation for both latent representations and discrete tokens. Extensive experiments show that LEPO significantly outperforms existing RL methods for discrete and latent reasoning.

</details>


### [330] [Can Explicit Physical Feasibility Benefit VLA Learning? An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17896)
*Yubai Wei,Chen Wu,Hashem Haghbayan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的训练中引入显式的物理可行性监督，通过一个简单的几何可行性目标函数增强扩散策略的训练。实验表明，该方法在障碍物感知操作任务中提高了物理可靠性和整体性能，并在低数据场景下提升了学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型通常通过大规模模仿学习进行训练，但并未显式监督硬物理约束（如避障或运动学可行性），导致模型只能从演示中隐式推断物理可行行为的几何结构。这种隐式学习可能导致物理可靠性不足。

Method: 本文制定了一个简单的基于几何的可行性目标，并将其集成到基于扩散的VLA策略的训练阶段。通过障碍物感知操作任务作为探针，系统地评估几何依赖的物理可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，在VLA训练中增加可行性监督不仅提高了物理可靠性和整体任务性能，还在低数据环境下提升了学习效率。

Conclusion: 显式的可行性信号可以有效补充基于模仿学习的VLA训练，为开发更可靠的VLA策略提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models map multimodal inputs directly to robot actions and are typically trained through large-scale imitation learning. While this paradigm has shown strong performance, prevailing VLA training procedures do not explicitly supervise hard physical constraints such as obstacle avoidance or kinematic feasibility. As a result, the geometric structure underlying physically feasible behavior must be inferred only implicitly from demonstrations. In this paper, we study whether introducing explicit feasibility supervision can provide effective structured guidance for VLA policies. We formulate a simple geometry-grounded feasibility objective and integrate it into the training stage of a diffusion-based VLA policy. To evaluate this idea systematically, we use obstacle-aware manipulation as a controlled probe of geometry-dependent physical feasibility. Empirical results show that augmenting VLA training with feasibility supervision improves both physical reliability and overall task performance, while also enhancing learning efficiency in the low-data regime. These findings indicate that explicit feasibility signals can effectively complement imitation-based VLA learning, highlighting their potential for developing more reliable VLA policies.

</details>


### [331] [LoReC: Rethinking Large Language Models for Graph Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17897)
*Hongyu Zhan,Qixin Wang,Yusen Tan,Haitao Yu,Jingbo Zhou,Shuai Chen,Jia Li,Xiao Tan,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoReC方法，通过“观察、记忆、对比”三阶段增强LLM对图数据的理解，显著提升GraphLLM性能并超越传统GNN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphLLM范式直接利用LLM处理图任务时效果有限，甚至逊于传统GNN方法。分析发现，这是由于LLM处理图数据能力不足，且倾向于忽略图信息所致。

Method: 提出一种新颖的即插即用方法LoReC，包含三个阶段：(1) Look：重新分配注意力至图结构；(2) Remember：将图信息重新注入前馈网络(FFN)；(3) Contrast：校正解码过程中的原始logits。

Result: 大量实验表明，LoReC在多种数据集上显著优于现有GraphLLM方法，并且在性能上超越了传统的GNN方法。

Conclusion: LoReC有效解决了LLM在处理图数据时的局限性，证明了通过增强LLM对图信息的关注和整合能力，可以显著提升图学习任务的性能。

Abstract: The advent of Large Language Models (LLMs) has fundamentally reshaped the way we interact with graphs, giving rise to a new paradigm called GraphLLM. As revealed in recent studies, graph learning can benefit from LLMs. However, we observe limited benefits when we directly utilize LLMs to make predictions for graph-related tasks within GraphLLM paradigm, which even yields suboptimal results compared to conventional GNN-based approaches. Through in-depth analysis, we find this failure can be attributed to LLMs' limited capability for processing graph data and their tendency to overlook graph information. To address this issue, we propose LoReC (Look, Remember, and Contrast), a novel plug-and-play method for GraphLLM paradigm, which enhances LLM's understanding of graph data through three stages: (1) Look: redistributing attention to graph; (2) Remember: re-injecting graph information into the Feed-Forward Network (FFN); (3) Contrast: rectifying the vanilla logits produced in the decoding process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LoReC brings notable improvements over current GraphLLM methods and outperforms GNN-based approaches across diverse datasets. The implementation is available at https://github.com/Git-King-Zhan/LoReC.

</details>


### [332] [Learning to Correct: Calibrated Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Attempt Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17912)
*Muhammed Emrullah Ildiz,Halil Alperen Gozeten,Ege Onur Taga,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CAL-GRPO算法，通过设计校准的尝试级别加权策略，解决了在长链式思维（CoT）设置下优化Verification@K奖励时的梯度偏差问题，有效提升了模型在多次尝试后的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的推理模型利用长链式思维解决复杂问题，在多次尝试（K次）且每次尝试接收硬验证器反馈的设置下，需要强化学习方法来有效利用每次尝试的奖励。作者发现简单地根据每次尝试的通过/失败结果进行加权会导致梯度偏差，因此需要设计一种无偏且低方差的加权策略。

Method: 提出了校准尝试级别（CAL）GRPO算法。该算法通过设计一种特定的加权策略，在保持较小方差的同时获得无偏梯度，从而正确优化Verification@K奖励（即模型在第K次尝试内成功）。理论分析揭示了如何结合每次尝试的奖励来影响训练过程及最终的Verification@K性能。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，CAL-GRPO相比普通GRPO以及朴素加权方法具有显著优势。实验结果、基线对比和消融研究均验证了理论分析的正确性以及CAL-GRPO方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究从理论和实践上证明了在长CoT设置下，正确处理尝试级别奖励对于模型训练至关重要。CAL-GRPO通过校准加权策略成功解决了梯度偏差问题，为利用测试时计算资源提升推理模型性能提供了有效的方法。

Abstract: State-of-the-art reasoning models utilize long chain-of-thought (CoT) to solve increasingly complex problems using more test-time computation. In this work, we explore a long CoT setting where the model makes up to K successive attempts at solving a problem, in which each attempt is allowed to build on earlier ones after the model receives a hard verifier feedback. This motivates RL methods that can harness per-attempt rewards by carefully weighting individual attempts. We study optimizing the Verification@K reward (the model succeeds by the K-th attempt) and show that naively weighing the attempts by their pass/fail results in biased gradients. We introduce Calibrated Attempt-Level (CAL) GRPO by devising a weighing strategy to obtain unbiased gradients while maintaining small variance. Our theory reveals how incorporating per-attempt rewards influence the training and the eventual Verification@K performance. Experiments, baselines, and ablations on synthetic and real data corroborate our theory and the benefits of CAL-GRPO over vanilla GRPO as well as naive weighting.

</details>


### [333] [Fisher Decorator: Refining Flow Policy via A Local Transport Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17919)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Haoyu Wang,Wenxuan Yuan,Ziyan Wang,Zonghao Chen,Li Zeng,Zhuo Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何视角的离线强化学习框架，通过Fisher信息矩阵导出的各向异性优化替代传统的各向同性L2正则化，解决了流策略中的优化方向错位问题，在多个基准测试中取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的离线强化学习方法面临表达能力、最优性和效率之间的关键权衡。特别是现有流策略将L2正则化解释为2-Wasserstein距离的上界，这在离线设置中存在问题：行为策略流形本质上是各向异性的，而L2（或W2上界）正则化是各向同性且对密度不敏感的，导致优化方向系统性错位。

Method: 从几何角度重新审视离线RL，将策略改进形式化为局部传输映射：初始流策略加上残差位移。通过分析诱导密度变换，推导出由Fisher信息矩阵控制的KL约束目标的局部二次近似，实现可处理的各向异性优化。利用嵌入在流速度中的分数函数获得相应的二次约束以提高优化效率。

Result: 研究结果揭示了先前方法中的最优性差距源于其各向同性近似。相比之下，本文框架在最优解的可证明邻域内实现了可控的近似误差。大量实验表明该方法在多种离线RL基准测试中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过几何视角和Fisher信息矩阵引导的各向异性优化，成功解决了流策略离线强化学习中的优化方向错位问题，为离线RL提供了理论上更严格、实践中更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow-based offline reinforcement learning (RL) have achieved strong performance by parameterizing policies via flow matching. However, they still face critical trade-offs among expressiveness, optimality, and efficiency. In particular, existing flow policies interpret the $L_2$ regularization as an upper bound of the 2-Wasserstein distance ($W_2$), which can be problematic in offline settings. This issue stems from a fundamental geometric mismatch: the behavioral policy manifold is inherently anisotropic, whereas the $L_2$ (or upper bound of $W_2$) regularization is isotropic and density-insensitive, leading to systematically misaligned optimization directions. To address this, we revisit offline RL from a geometric perspective and show that policy refinement can be formulated as a local transport map: an initial flow policy augmented by a residual displacement. By analyzing the induced density transformation, we derive a local quadratic approximation of the KL-constrained objective governed by the Fisher information matrix, enabling a tractable anisotropic optimization formulation. By leveraging the score function embedded in the flow velocity, we obtain a corresponding quadratic constraint for efficient optimization. Our results reveal that the optimality gap in prior methods arises from their isotropic approximation. In contrast, our framework achieves a controllable approximation error within a provable neighborhood of the optimal solution. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across diverse offline RL benchmarks. See project page: https://github.com/ARC0127/Fisher-Decorator.

</details>


### [334] [HEALing Entropy Collapse: Enhancing Exploration in Few-Shot RLVR via Hybrid-Domain Entropy Dynamics Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17928)
*Zhanyu Liu,Qingguo Hu,Ante Wang,Chenqing Liu,Zhishang Xiang,Hui Li,Delai Qiu,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HEAL框架解决低资源场景下RLVR的熵崩塌问题，通过引入通用领域数据和熵动态对齐奖励机制，仅用32个样本即可达到或超越使用1000个样本训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）方法主要假设高资源场景，在低资源场景下容易出现严重的熵崩塌问题，限制了探索能力并降低了推理性能。

Method: 提出混合域熵动态对齐（HEAL）框架：1）选择性引入高价值通用领域数据促进多样化探索；2）设计熵动态对齐（EDA）奖励机制，对齐目标域和通用域之间的轨迹级熵动态，同时捕获熵幅度和细粒度变化。

Result: HEAL在多个领域持续提升少样本RLVR性能。仅使用32个目标域样本，HEAL能够达到甚至超越使用1K个目标域样本训练的全样本RLVR效果。

Conclusion: HEAL框架有效缓解了低资源场景下的熵崩塌问题，通过跨域知识迁移显著提升了少样本强化学习推理能力，为资源受限场景下的大语言模型推理训练提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward (RLVR) has proven effective for training reasoning-oriented large language models, but existing methods largely assume high-resource settings with abundant training data. In low-resource scenarios, RLVR is prone to more severe entropy collapse, which substantially limits exploration and degrades reasoning performance. To address this issue, we propose Hybrid-domain Entropy dynamics ALignment (HEAL), a framework tailored for few-shot RLVR. HEAL first selectively incorporates high-value general-domain data to promote more diverse exploration. Then, we introduce Entropy Dynamics Alignment (EDA), a reward mechanism that aligns trajectory-level entropy dynamics between the target and general domains, capturing both entropy magnitude and fine-grained variation. Through this alignment, EDA not only further mitigates entropy collapse but also encourages the policy to acquire more diverse exploration behaviors from the general domain. Experiments across multiple domains show that HEAL consistently improves few-shot RLVR performance. Notably, using only 32 target-domain samples, HEAL matches or even surpasses full-shot RLVR trained with 1K target-domain samples.

</details>


### [335] [How Much Cache Does Reasoning Need? Depth-Cache Tradeoffs in KV-Compressed Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17935)
*Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了Transformer推理中KV缓存的压缩极限，给出了k跳指针追踪任务中模型深度的下界猜想、带宽障碍以及自适应与遗忘缓存的错误率差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是Transformer推理的主要内存瓶颈，但现有研究缺乏对压缩极限的理论分析，特别是压缩到何种程度会导致多步推理能力下降尚不清楚。

Method: 在共享KV缓存（大小为s）、注意力维度m、头数H、精度p位以及满足局部性缓存的设定下，研究k跳指针追踪任务的理论性质，通过概率分析、构造上界算法（窗口化指针倍增）以及计数论证等方法进行理论推导。

Result: (1) 提出深度下界猜想L=Ω(⌈k/s⌉·⌈log₂n/(Hmp)⌉)，并证明了匹配的上界和最大下界；(2) 发现带宽障碍：当Hmp≥log₂n时，基于窗口可区分性计数的下界无法超过⌈k/s⌉；(3) 证明自适应缓存相比遗忘缓存有Ω((n/s)^{T-1})的错误率优势，解释了重击驱逐策略的优势。

Conclusion: 本文为KV缓存压缩提供了严格的理论框架，揭示了压缩极限、带宽约束和缓存策略选择的基本规律，为实际系统设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the dominant memory bottleneck during Transformer inference, yet little is known theoretically about how aggressively it can be compressed before multi-step reasoning degrades. We study this through $k$-hop pointer chasing on $n$ tokens under a shared KV cache of size $s$, attention dimension $m$, $H$ heads, $p$-bit precision, and a locality-respecting cache controller (satisfied by all standard KV-compression methods). We give three results.
  (1) Product depth lower bound (conjectured). We conjecture that any such Transformer ($n \geq 4k$, $s \leq \sqrt{n}/4$) requires depth $L = Ω(\lceil k/s \rceil \cdot \lceil \log_2 n/(Hmp) \rceil)$, and isolate the sole remaining gap as a probabilistic step on the joint distribution of cache trace and pointer chain. Unconditionally, we prove a matching upper bound $L = O(\min(k, \lceil k/s \rceil \log s) \cdot \log n/(mp))$ via windowed pointer doubling, and a max-bound $L = Ω(\max(\lceil k/s \rceil, \log n/(Hmp)))$. Closing the conjecture amounts to upgrading max to product.
  (2) Bandwidth barrier. The product bound binds only when $Hmp \lesssim \log n$. Any lower bound provable via per-window distinguishability counting -- including reachability, bandwidth, and combinations -- cannot exceed $\lceil k/s \rceil$ once $Hmp \geq \log_2 n$. Breaking this requires lifting unconditional communication-complexity bounds for pointer chasing to Cache-Transformer depth.
  (3) Adaptive vs oblivious error scaling. Under random cache over $T = \lceil \log_2 k \rceil$ doubling stages, oblivious caches give $\Pr[\mathcal{E}] \leq (s/(n-T))^T + 2T^3/n$ (exponential in $T$), while adaptive locality-respecting caches achieve $\Pr[\mathcal{E}] = s/n$ exactly, independent of $T$. The $Ω((n/s)^{T-1})$ separation explains why heavy-hitter eviction empirically dominates random eviction for multi-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [336] [Federated Rule Ensemble Method in Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17956)
*Ke Wan,Kensuke Tanioka,Toshio Shimokawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联邦RuleFit框架，通过结合差分隐私直方图、梯度提升决策树和L1正则化优化，在保护隐私的前提下实现了跨机构的可解释性全局模型构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在医学领域的应用日益广泛，但单一机构的数据规模和多样性有限；跨机构数据整合虽可解决此问题，却受隐私法规和数据所有权限制；现有联邦学习方法多依赖复杂架构且缺乏可解释性，限制了其临床适用性。

Method: 提出联邦RuleFit框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)基于差分隐私直方图的预处理，估计共享截断值以实现一致的规则定义并降低客户端间异质性；(2)使用共享截断值的梯度提升决策树进行局部规则生成；(3)通过联邦对偶平均算法进行L1正则化优化，实现稀疏且一致的变量选择。

Result: 仿真研究表明，该方法性能与集中式RuleFit相当，且优于现有联邦学习方法；真实世界数据分析显示，该方法能在保持竞争性预测准确性的同时提供可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: 该框架为联邦学习环境下的可解释性建模提供了实用且有效的解决方案，在保护隐私的同时确保了模型的临床适用性。

Abstract: Machine learning has become integral to medical research and is increasingly applied in clinical settings to support diagnosis and decision-making; however, its effectiveness depends on access to large, diverse datasets, which are limited within single institutions. Although integrating data across institutions can address this limitation, privacy regulations and data ownership constraints hinder these efforts. Federated learning enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data; however, most methods rely on complex architectures that lack interpretability, limiting clinical applicability. Therefore, we proposed a federated RuleFit framework to construct a unified and interpretable global model for distributed environments. It integrates three components: preprocessing based on differentially private histograms to estimate shared cutoff values, enabling consistent rule definitions and reducing heterogeneity across clients; local rule generation using gradient boosting decision trees with shared cutoffs; and coefficient estimation via $\ell_1$-regularized optimization using a Federated Dual Averaging algorithm for sparse and consistent variable selection. In simulation studies, the proposed method achieved a performance comparable to that of centralized RuleFit while outperforming existing federated approaches. Real-world analysis demonstrated its ability to provide interpretable insights with competitive predictive accuracy. Therefore, the proposed framework offers a practical and effective solution for interpretable and reliable modeling in federated learning environments.

</details>


### [337] [Online Conformal Prediction with Adversarial Semi-bandit Feedback via Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17984)
*Junyoung Yang,Kyungmin Kim,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在部分反馈（强盗反馈）设置下的在线保形预测方法，解决了真实标签仅在预测集内时才能被观测到的挑战，并在理论和实验上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线保形预测方法通常假设完全反馈设置（即总能观测到真实标签），但在实际应用中，往往只能获得部分反馈，即仅当真实标签落在预测集内时才能被揭示。这种部分反馈设置更具挑战性，因此需要新的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者将在线保形预测问题建模为对抗性强盗（adversarial bandit）问题，将每个候选预测集视为一个臂（arm）。基于现有的对抗性强盗算法，通过显式建立与学习者遗憾（regret）的联系，实现了长期覆盖保证。

Result: 所提出的方法在理论上实现了长期覆盖保证，在实验上（包括i.i.d.和非i.i.d.设置）证明了该方法能够成功控制误覆盖率，同时保持合理的预测集大小。

Conclusion: 本研究成功将在线保形预测扩展到了部分反馈设置，通过将其与对抗性强盗问题相结合，为安全关键系统中的在线不确定性量化提供了一个理论上可靠且实践中有效的新方法。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in safety-critical systems, where decisions must be made under uncertainty. In particular, we consider the problem of online uncertainty quantification, where data points arrive sequentially. Online conformal prediction is a principled online uncertainty quantification method that dynamically constructs a prediction set at each time step. While existing methods for online conformal prediction provide long-run coverage guarantees without any distributional assumptions, they typically assume a full feedback setting in which the true label is always observed. In this paper, we propose a novel learning method for online conformal prediction with partial feedback from an adaptive adversary-a more challenging setup where the true label is revealed only when it lies inside the constructed prediction set. Specifically, we formulate online conformal prediction as an adversarial bandit problem by treating each candidate prediction set as an arm. Building on an existing algorithm for adversarial bandits, our method achieves a long-run coverage guarantee by explicitly establishing its connection to the regret of the learner. Finally, we empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in both independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and non-i.i.d. settings, showing that it successfully controls the miscoverage rate while maintaining a reasonable size of the prediction set.

</details>


### [338] [Causally-Constrained Probabilistic Forecasting for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17998)
*Pooyan Khosravinia,João Gama,Bruno Veloso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果引导的Transformer模型（CGT），通过融合时滞因果图先验与深度序列建模，实现了多变量时间序列异常检测的高准确率与可解释根因定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习（如图神经网络和Transformer）的多变量时间序列异常检测方法主要依赖相关性分析，缺乏因果解释能力且难以定位根本原因。工业监测中的故障往往源于复杂的时序动态和传感器间交互，亟需能够提供因果解释和根因定位的检测方法。

Method: 提出因果约束的概率预测框架CGT模型：(1)利用因果发现得到的硬父节点掩码约束预测路径；(2)引入高斯分布头捕获预测不确定性；(3)设计带有停止梯度隔离和安全门控混合机制的影子辅助路径，在不损害因果表示的前提下利用残差相关信息；(4)采用负对数似然分数与自适应流式阈值进行异常识别；(5)通过逐维度概率归因和反事实钳制定位根因变量。

Result: 在ASD和SMD基准数据集上达到最先进检测性能，F1分数分别为96.19%和95.32%，同时显著提升了变量级别的归因质量。

Conclusion: 因果结构先验能够有效提升多变量传感器系统中深度异常检测的鲁棒性和可解释性，为工业监测提供了兼具高性能和可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a central challenge in industrial monitoring, as failures frequently arise from complex temporal dynamics and cross-sensor interactions. While recent deep learning models, including graph neural networks and Transformers, have demonstrated strong empirical performance, most approaches remain primarily correlational and offer limited support for causal interpretation and root-cause localization. This study introduces a causally-constrained probabilistic forecasting framework which is a Causally Guided Transformer (CGT) model for multivariate time-series anomaly detection, integrating an explicit time-lagged causal graph prior with deep sequence modeling. For each target variable, a dedicated forecasting block employs a hard parent mask derived from causal discovery to restrict the main prediction pathway to graph-supported causes, while a latent Gaussian head captures predictive uncertainty. To leverage residual correlational information without compromising the causal representation, a shadow auxiliary path with stop-gradient isolation and a safety-gated blending mechanism is incorporated to suppress non-causal contributions when reliability is low. Anomalies are identified using negative log-likelihood scores with adaptive streaming thresholding, and root-cause variables are determined through per-dimension probabilistic attribution and counterfactual clamping. Experiments on the ASD and SMD benchmarks indicate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, with F1-scores of 96.19% on ASD and 95.32% on SMD, and enhances variable-level attribution quality. These findings suggest that causal structural priors can improve both robustness and interpretability in detecting deep anomalies in multivariate sensor systems.

</details>


### [339] [Neural Garbage Collection: Learning to Forget while Learning to Reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18002)
*Michael Y. Li,Jubayer Ibn Hamid,Emily B. Fox,Noah D. Goodman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经垃圾回收（NGC）方法，使语言模型在链式思维推理过程中自主学习丢弃KV cache条目，在保持高准确率的同时实现2-3倍的内存压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理显著提升了语言模型能力，但每个推理步骤都会增加KV cache，成为扩展该范式的瓶颈。现有方法依赖人工设计的标准来管理内存约束，而深度学习的广泛模式表明，端到端学习应该能够完全取代这种设计选择——既然模型能学会推理，为什么不能学会遗忘？

Method: 引入神经垃圾回收（NGC），让语言模型在学习推理的同时学习遗忘，仅基于任务结果的奖励进行端到端训练。模型在推理过程中周期性暂停，决定驱逐哪些KV cache条目，然后基于剩余缓存继续推理。通过将链式思维中的token和缓存驱逐决策视为从语言模型采样的离散动作，使用强化学习联合优化模型的推理方式和内存管理：驱逐的内容影响记忆，记忆影响推理，推理的正确性决定奖励。

Result: 在Countdown、AMC和AIME任务上，NGC在2-3倍峰值KV cache大小压缩下，相对于全缓存上界保持了强劲的准确率，并显著优于驱逐基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文结果是迈向更广阔愿景的第一步，即端到端优化同时驱动语言模型的能力和效率，展示了让模型自主学习内存管理的可行性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning has driven striking advances in language model capability, yet every reasoning step grows the KV cache, creating a bottleneck to scaling this paradigm further. Current approaches manage these constraints on the model's behalf using hand-designed criteria. A more scalable approach would let end-to-end learning subsume this design choice entirely, following a broader pattern in deep learning. After all, if a model can learn to reason, why can't it learn to forget? We introduce Neural Garbage Collection (NGC), in which a language model learns to forget while learning to reason, trained end-to-end from outcome-based task reward alone. As the model reasons, it periodically pauses, decides which KV cache entries to evict, and continues to reason conditioned on the remaining cache. By treating tokens in a chain-of-thought and cache-eviction decisions as discrete actions sampled from the language model, we can use reinforcement learning to jointly optimize how the model reasons and how it manages its own memory: what the model evicts shapes what it remembers, what it remembers shapes its reasoning, and the correctness of that reasoning determines its reward. Crucially, the model learns this behavior entirely from a single learning signal - the outcome-based task reward - without supervised fine-tuning or proxy objectives. On Countdown, AMC, and AIME tasks, NGC maintains strong accuracy relative to the full-cache upper bound at 2-3x peak KV cache size compression and substantially outperforms eviction baselines. Our results are a first step towards a broader vision where end-to-end optimization drives both capability and efficiency in language models.

</details>


### [340] [Neural Shape Operator Surrogates -- Expression Rate Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18012)
*Helmut Harbrecht,Christoph Schwab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在参数化形状族上的偏微分方程和边界积分方程解算子的神经算子和谱算子逼近的存在性，并给出了误差界和收敛速率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在经验上已被证实能够学习跨不同几何形状的PDE解算子，但缺乏严格的数学理论支撑。本文旨在为形状到解映射的算子代理模型提供严格的误差分析和收敛性保证。

Method: 通过仿射参数化形状编码将参考域上的PDE拉回到参数化PDE族；建立参数化解族的量化全纯性；利用全纯性导出有限维离散逼近及其收敛速率；构造性地证明神经算子和谱算子代理的存在性。

Result: 在满足抽象假设的前提下，形状到解映射的神经算子和谱算子代理存在，且误差界和收敛速率在允许形状族上一致成立；适用于椭圆型、抛物型PDE及边界积分方程；支持主成分形状编码器和框架解码器。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为神经算子跨形状泛化能力提供了严格的数学基础，解释并支持了其在椭圆型和抛物型PDE及边界积分方程中的经验成功。

Abstract: We prove error bounds for operator surrogates of solution operators for partial differential and boundary integral equations on families of domains which are diffeomorphic to one common reference (or latent) domain $D_{ref}$. The pullback of the PDE to $D_{ref}$ via affine-parametric shape encoding produces a collection of holomorphic parametric PDEs on $D_{ref}$. Sufficient conditions for (uniformly with respect to the parameter) well-posedness are given, implying existence, uniqueness and stability of parametric solution families on $D_{ref}$. We illustrate the abstract hypotheses by reviewing recent holomorphy results for a suite of elliptic and parabolic PDEs.
  Quantified parametric holomorphy implies existence of finite-parametric, discrete approximations of the parametric solution families with convergence rates in terms of the number $N$ of parameters. We obtain constructive proofs of existence of Neural and Spectral Operator surrogates for the shape-to-solution maps with error bounds and convergence rate guarantees uniform on the collection of admissible shapes. We admit principal-component shape encoders and frame decoders.
  Our results support in particular the (empirically reported) ability of neural operators to realize data-to-solution maps for elliptic and parabolic PDEs and BIEs that generalize across parametric families of shapes.

</details>


### [341] [Clusterability-Based Assessment of Potentially Noisy Views for Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18024)
*Mudi Jiang,Jiahui Zhou,Xinying Liu,Zengyou He,Zhikui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多视图聚类性评分（MVCS），用于在聚类前评估视图质量并检测噪声视图，从而避免低质量视图对聚类性能的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多视图聚类中，不同视图的质量差异显著，低质量或退化视图会严重损害整体聚类性能。现有研究主要通过视图加权或噪声鲁棒优化在聚类过程中解决此问题，却忽视了聚类前的数据层面评估。

Method: 提出多视图聚类性评分（MVCS），通过三个互补组件量化多视图数据中潜在聚类相关结构的强度：单视图结构聚类性、联合空间聚类性以及跨视图邻域一致性。利用该评分在聚类前进行潜在噪声视图分析和检测。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，噪声视图会显著降低聚类性能；与现有的单视图聚类性度量相比，该方法能更有效地支持噪声视图的分析与检测。

Conclusion: MVCS是首个专为多视图数据设计的聚类性评分，能够有效识别噪声视图，为多视图聚类前的视图质量评估提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In multi-view clustering, the quality of different views may vary substantially, and low-quality or degraded views can impair overall clustering performance. However, existing studies mainly address this issue within the clustering process through view weighting or noise-robust optimization, while paying limited attention to data-level assessment before clustering. In this paper, we study the problem of pre-clustering noisy-view analysis in multi-view data from a clusterability perspective. To this end, we propose a Multi-View Clusterability Score (MVCS), which quantifies the strength of latent cluster-related structures in multi-view data through three complementary components: per-view structural clusterability, joint-space clusterability, and cross-view neighborhood consistency. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first clusterability score specifically designed for multi-view data. We further use it to perform potentially noisy view analysis and noisy-view detection before clustering. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that noisy views can significantly degrade clustering performance, and that, compared with existing clusterability measures designed for single-view data, the proposed method more effectively supports noisy-view analysis and detection.

</details>


### [342] [RASP-Tuner: Retrieval-Augmented Soft Prompts for Context-Aware Black-Box Optimization in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18026)
*Enze Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RASP-Tuner，一种针对非稳态黑盒系统的在线优化方法，通过检索历史上下文和低维提示子空间自适应，在保持性能的同时显著降低了计算成本（比滑动窗口GP-UCB快8-12倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际部署系统的优化目标会随外部上下文变化而漂移。当上下文重复访问少量潜在状态时，丢弃历史的优化器会重复付出适应成本；而在每步必须保持低开销的要求下，高观测数量下的完整高斯过程(GP)重拟合难以持续。

Method: 将在线调优建模为上下文条件化的遗憾最小化问题。RASP-Tuner采用三阶段分解：(i)通过检索相似历史上下文识别状态代理；(ii)使用混合专家代理预测短视界损失，输入拼接参数、上下文和检索的软提示；(iii)主要在低维提示子空间中自适应，仅在标量化误差或分歧激增时调用完整代理更新。RealErrorComposer通过EMA稳定的逻辑分数将异构流指标映射到[0,1]。

Result: 在9个合成非稳态基准、对抗性上下文健全性检查和3个表格化真实流上验证。在配对测试下（视界T=100），RASP-Tuner在9个合成任务中的7个上改善或匹配累积遗憾（相对于GP-UCB和CMA-ES实现），同时在相同硬件上每步挂钟时间比滑动窗口GP-UCB低8-12倍。

Conclusion: 在聚类分离的强凸状态模型(RA-GD)中的理想化分析提供了动态遗憾有界的充分条件；实际部署流水线违反了部分前提，文中明确了哪些差距仍然开放。

Abstract: Many deployed systems expose black-box objectives whose minimizing configuration shifts with an externally observed context. When contexts revisit a small set of latent regimes, an optimizer that discards history pays repeated adaptation cost; when each step must remain inexpensive, full Gaussian-process (GP) refits at high observation counts are difficult to sustain. We cast online tuning as context-conditioned regret minimization and present RASP-Tuner, which instantiates a decomposition motivated by first principles: (i) identify a regime proxy by retrieving similar past contexts; (ii) predict short-horizon loss with a mixture-of-experts surrogate whose input concatenates parameters, context, and a retrieved soft prompt; (iii) adapt chiefly in a low-dimensional prompt subspace, invoking full surrogate updates only when scalarized error or disagreement spikes. A RealErrorComposer maps heterogeneous streaming metrics to [0,1] via EMA-stabilized logistic scores, supplying a single differentiable training target. On nine synthetic non-stationary benchmarks, an adversarial-context sanity check, and three tabular real-world streams (Section on real-world experiments), RASP-Tuner improves or matches cumulative regret relative to our GP-UCB and CMA-ES implementations on seven of nine synthetic tasks under paired tests at horizon T=100, while recording 8-12 times lower wall-clock per step than sliding-window GP-UCB on identical hardware. Idealized analysis in a cluster-separated, strongly convex regime model (RA-GD) supplies sufficient conditions for bounded dynamic regret; the deployed pipeline violates several of these premises, and we articulate which gaps remain open.

</details>


### [343] [Variational Autoencoder Domain Adaptation for Cross-System Generalization in ML-Based SOP Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18035)
*Leyla Sadighi,Stefan Karlsson,Carlos Natalino,Mojtaba Eshghie,Fehmida Usmani,Eoin Kenny,Lena Wosinska,Paolo Monti,Marija Furdek,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于变分自编码器（VAE）的域适应框架，通过学习共享表示来解决光缆系统间机器学习模型迁移失败的问题，跨系统检测准确率提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对不同光纤系统（波长、光纤属性、网络架构差异）间机器学习模型迁移时性能急剧下降的问题，旨在提升物理层威胁检测模型的跨系统泛化能力。

Method: 提出基于VAE的域适应框架。首先在两个不同光系统（21公里O波段暗光纤与63.4公里C波段城域环网）的联合数据上训练共享编码器，学习捕获系统间共有的事件特征；随后冻结编码器，使用单个系统的标签训练分类器。

Result: 从系统1迁移至系统2的跨系统准确率达95.3%，反向迁移达73.5%，相比仅在单系统训练的深度神经网络（DNN）基线分别提升83.4%和51%，且保持系统内性能不降低。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了光纤物理层威胁检测中的域偏移问题，显著提升了模型在不同光纤系统间的迁移性能，证明了无监督域适应在跨系统场景下的实用价值。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) models trained to detect physical-layer threats on one optical fiber system often fail catastrophically when applied to a different system, due to variations in operating wavelength, fiber properties, and network architecture. To overcome this, we propose a Domain Adaptation (DA) framework based on a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) that learns a shared representation capturing event signatures common to both systems while suppressing system-specific differences. The shared encoder is first trained on the combined data from two distinct optical systems: a 21 km O-band dark-fiber testbed (System 1) and a 63.4 km C-band live metro ring (System 2). The encoder is then frozen, and a classifier is trained using labels from an individual system. The proposed approach achieves 95.3% and 73.5% cross-system accuracy when moving from System 1 to System 2 and vice versa, respectively. This corresponds to gains of 83.4% and 51% over a fully supervised Deep Neural Network (DNN) baseline trained on a single system, while preserving intra-system performance.

</details>


### [344] [Sonata: A Hybrid World Model for Inertial Kinematics under Clinical Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18058)
*Blaise Delaney,Salil Patel,Yuji Xing,Dominic Dootson,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Sonata，一个紧凑的潜在世界模型，用于临床数据稀缺场景下的六轴躯干IMU表征学习。该模型在739名受试者的数据上预训练，相比基线在临床判别、跌倒风险预测等方面表现更优，且参数量仅3.77M，适合可穿戴设备推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床队列通常仅包含数十至数百名患者，这使得网络规模的掩码重建目标难以匹配该问题，因此需要一种专门针对临床数据稀缺场景设计的模型来有效学习IMU表征。

Method: 提出了Sonata，一个3.77M参数的混合模型，在九个公共数据集的协调语料库（739名受试者，190k窗口）上进行预训练。核心是使用潜在世界模型目标，即预测未来状态，而非重建原始传感器轨迹。

Result: 在14组评估中，与匹配的自回归预测基线（MAE）相比，Sonata在冻结探针临床判别、前瞻性跌倒风险预测和跨队列迁移方面表现一致更强，同时产生更高秩、结构更清晰的潜在表征。

Conclusion: Sonata作为一个仅3.77M参数的模型，不仅性能优越，而且兼容可穿戴设备上的推理，为神经评估的通用运动学世界模型迈出了一步。

Abstract: We introduce Sonata, a compact latent world model for six-axis trunk IMU representation learning under clinical data scarcity. Clinical cohorts typically comprise tens to hundreds of patients, making web-scale masked-reconstruction objectives poorly matched to the problem. Sonata is a 3.77 M-parameter hybrid model, pre-trained on a harmonised corpus of nine public datasets (739 subjects, 190k windows) with a latent world-model objective that predicts future state rather than reconstructing raw sensor traces. In a controlled comparison against a matched autoregressive forecasting baseline (MAE) on the same backbone, Sonata yields consistently stronger frozen-probe clinical discrimination, prospective fall-risk prediction, and cross-cohort transfer across a 14-arm evaluation suite, while producing higher-rank, more structured latent representations. At 3.77 M parameters the model is compatible with on-device wearable inference, offering a step toward general kinematic world models for neurological assessment.

</details>


### [345] [Towards a Foundation-Model Paradigm for Aerodynamic Prediction in Three-dimensional Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18062)
*Yunjia Yang,Babak Gholami,Caglar Gurbuz,Mohammad Rashed,Nils Thuerey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练和微调的高效代理模型构建方法AeroTransformer，在跨音速机翼预测任务中仅用450个样本即实现0.36%的误差，相比从头训练误差降低84.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对复杂三维构型的空气动力学预测，传统机器学习模型需要高昂的训练数据生成成本，难以满足形状优化对精确预测模型的需求。

Method: 开发了一个面向大规模训练的Transformer架构AeroTransformer；首先在包含近30000个样本、几何多样性广泛的SuperWing数据集上进行预训练，然后使用少量任务特定样本进行微调，以高效构建精确的代理模型。

Result: 在跨音速机翼预测任务中，使用450个任务特定样本微调后，表面流动预测误差达到0.36%，相比从头训练误差降低了84.2%。同时系统研究了模型配置和训练策略的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的预训练加微调方法能够在有限数据和计算预算下有效构建高精度代理模型，显著降低了数据需求，为工程实际应用提供了可行方案。研究还开源了数据集、预训练模型并提供了在线交互设计工具。

Abstract: Accurate machine-learning models for aerodynamic prediction are essential for accelerating shape optimization, yet remain challenging to develop for complex three-dimensional configurations due to the high cost of generating training data. This work introduces a methodology for efficiently constructing accurate surrogate models for design purposes by first pre-training a large-scale model on diverse geometries and then fine-tuning it with a few more detailed task-specific samples. A Transformer-based architecture, AeroTransformer, is developed and tailored for large-scale training to learn aerodynamics. The methodology is evaluated on transonic wings, where the model is pre-trained on SuperWing, a dataset of nearly 30000 samples with broad geometric diversity, and subsequently fine-tuned to handle specific wing shapes perturbed from the Common Research Model. Results show that, with 450 task-specific samples, the proposed methodology achieves 0.36% error on surface-flow prediction, reducing 84.2% compared to training from scratch. The influence of model configurations and training strategies is also systematically studied to provide guidance on effectively training and deploying such models under limited data and computational budgets. To facilitate reuse, we release the datasets and the pre-trained models at https://github.com/tum-pbs/AeroTransformer. An interactive design tool is also built on the pre-trained model and is available online at https://webwing.pbs.cit.tum.de.

</details>


### [346] [Towards Real-Time ECG and EMG Modeling on $μ$ NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18067)
*Josh Millar,Ashok Samraj Thangarajan,Soumyajit Chatterjee,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PhysioLite，一个面向微控制器级NPU的轻量级模型，在ECG/EMG信号分析上达到与Transformer基座模型相当的性能，模型大小仅约370KB（8位量化），并在实际μNPU硬件上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经处理单元（NPU）的小型化使其能够集成到微控制器级的可穿戴设备中，实现近实时、离线且隐私保护的推理。然而，生理信号分析在此类硬件上仍然不可行，因为最新的基于Transformer的模型虽然性能优异，但体量过大、功耗过高，且其动态注意力操作与μNPU不兼容。

Method: 提出PhysioLite模型架构与训练框架，采用可学习小波滤波器组、CPU卸载的位置编码以及硬件感知的层设计，使模型兼容资源受限的NPU硬件并进行8位量化。

Result: 在ECG和EMG基准测试中，PhysioLite性能与最先进的Transformer基座模型相当，但模型大小仅为其不到10%（约370KB，8位量化）。在MAX78000和HX6538 WE2 μNPU上完成了组件级延迟与资源消耗的评测，证明了其在电池供电受限硬件上的可行性。

Conclusion: PhysioLite成功填补了高性能生理信号分析与资源受限可穿戴硬件之间的空白，使低功耗设备上的离线隐私保护信号分析成为可能，并已开源模型与训练框架。

Abstract: The miniaturisation of neural processing units (NPUs) and other low-power accelerators has enabled their integration into microcontroller-scale wearable hardware, supporting near-real-time, offline, and privacy-preserving inference. Yet physiological signal analysis has remained infeasible on such hardware; recent Transformer-based models show state-of-the-art performance but are prohibitively large for resource- and power-constrained hardware and incompatible with $μ$ NPUs due to their dynamic attention operations. We introduce PhysioLite, a lightweight, NPU-compatible model architecture and training framework for ECG/EMG signal analysis. Using learnable wavelet filter banks, CPU-offloaded positional encoding, and hardware-aware layer design, PhysioLite reaches performance comparable to state-of-the-art Transformer-based foundation models on ECG and EMG benchmarks, while being <10% of the size ($\sim$370KB with 8-bit quantization). We also profile its component-wise latency and resource consumption on both the MAX78000 and HX6538 WE2 $μ$ NPUs, demonstrating its viability for signal analysis on constrained, battery-powered hardware. We release our model(s) and training framework at: https://github.com/j0shmillar/physiolite.

</details>


### [347] [Implicit neural representations as a coordinate-based framework for continuous environmental field reconstruction from sparse ecological observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18083)
*Agnieszka Pregowska,Hazem M. Kalaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了隐式神经表示（INRs）作为一种基于坐标的建模框架，用于直接从稀疏、不规则的环境观测数据中学习连续的时空场，并在物种分布重建、物候动态和形态分割三个场景中验证了其有效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏且不规则的观测数据中重建连续环境场仍是环境建模和生物多样性信息学的一个核心挑战。由于许多生态数据集在空间和时间上具有异质性，基于网格的方法难以跨领域扩展或推广，因此需要一种更具扩展性和通用性的建模方法。

Method: 采用隐式神经表示（INRs）作为基于坐标的建模框架，直接根据坐标输入学习连续的空间和时空场。研究分析了该框架在物种分布重建、物候动态和形态分割三个代表性建模场景中的表现，并考察了其插值行为、空间连贯性以及计算特性（包括可扩展性、分辨率无关查询和架构归纳偏置）。

Result: 结果表明，神经场能够提供稳定的连续表示，且计算成本可预测，是对经典平滑器和基于树的方法的有效补充。这种方法在处理大型、不规则采样数据集时展现出良好的空间连贯性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 基于坐标的神经场可作为一种灵活的表示层，整合到环境建模流程和探索性分析框架中，特别适用于处理大型、不规则采样的数据集，为环境建模提供了一种新的有效工具。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous environmental fields from sparse and irregular observations remains a central challenge in environmental modelling and biodiversity informatics. Many ecological datasets are heterogeneous in space and time, making grid-based approaches difficult to scale or generalise across domains. Here, we evaluate implicit neural representations (INRs) as a coordinate-based modelling framework for learning continuous spatial and spatio-temporal fields directly from coordinate inputs. We analyse their behaviour across three representative modelling scenarios: species distribution reconstruction, phenological dynamics, and morphological segmentation derived from open biodiversity data. Beyond predictive performance, we examine interpolation behaviour, spatial coherence, and computational characteristics relevant for environmental modelling workflows, including scalability, resolution-independent querying, and architectural inductive bias. Results show that neural fields provide stable continuous representations with predictable computational cost, complementing classical smoothers and tree-based approaches. These findings position coordinate-based neural fields as a flexible representation layer that can be integrated into environmental modelling pipelines and exploratory analysis frameworks for large, irregularly sampled datasets.

</details>


### [348] [Predicting LLM Compression Degradation from Spectral Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18085)
*Mingxue Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 发现交互项$γ\cdot \barρ_s$（压缩率×稳定秩）可高度准确预测大模型低秩压缩导致的性能下降（注意力层相关系数0.890，MLP层0.839），从而实现无需昂贵试错的“先预测后压缩”工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的矩阵级低秩压缩虽能降低成本，但实际执行压缩并在语言任务上评估模型性能的计算开销极其昂贵。研究者希望找到一种方法，在实际投入大量计算资源之前，就能预测压缩可能带来的性能退化。

Method: 系统分析了Qwen3和Gemma3模型家族在四种代表性低秩压缩方法（vanilla SVD、两种ASVD变体和SVD-LLM）下的表现。研究识别出稳定秩和信息密度是影响性能退化的主导因素，构建了交互项$γ\cdot \barρ_s$（压缩率乘以稳定秩）作为预测指标，并将其与标准SVD截断界限及Transformer层中的误差组合机制建立理论联系。

Result: 提出的预测因子$γ\cdot \barρ_s$在预测准确率退化方面表现优异：在留一法交叉验证中，针对注意力层达到Pearson相关系数0.890，针对MLP层达到0.839。稳定秩和以每参数比特数衡量的信息密度主导了性能退化程度。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了压缩导致的退化是可预测的，从而提出了一种“先预测后压缩”的工作流程：先根据模型权重计算$γ\cdot \barρ_s$，估算退化程度，最后仅对理想的配置投入计算资源进行实际压缩。

Abstract: Matrix-level low-rank compression is a promising way to reduce the cost of large language models, but running compression and evaluating the resulting models on language tasks can be prohibitively expensive. Can compression-induced degradation be predicted before committing to this compute? We systematically analyze the Qwen3 and Gemma3 model families across four representative low-rank compression methods: vanilla SVD, two ASVD variants, and SVD-LLM. We find that stable rank and information density, measured in bits per parameter, dominate performance degradation. The interaction term $γ\cdot \barρ_s$, defined as compression ratio times stable rank, is a robust predictor of accuracy degradation, achieving leave-one-out cross-validation Pearson correlations of $0.890$ for attention layers and $0.839$ for MLP layers. We provide theoretical intuition for why this predictor succeeds by connecting it to standard SVD truncation bounds and error composition mechanisms in transformer layers. These findings enable a predict-then-compress workflow: compute $γ\cdot \barρ_s$ from weights, estimate degradation, and invest compute only in desirable configurations.

</details>


### [349] [Towards E-Value Based Stopping Rules for Bayesian Deep Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18089)
*Emanuel Sommer,Rickmer Schulte,Sarah Deubner,Julius Kobialka,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于E值的停止规则，用于解决贝叶斯深度集成（BDE）中采样过程的何时终止问题，实验表明通常仅需完整链预算的一小部分即可获得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯深度集成结合了深度集成与MCMC采样的优势，但长时间采样成本过高。虽然已有研究表明在优化的DE基础上增加采样能带来改进，但关键的实际问题在于：采样过程应持续多久才能相比初始DE基线产生显著提升？目前缺乏原则性的方法来决定何时停止采样。

Method: 将集成构建过程形式化为序贯Anytime-Valid假设检验问题，提出基于E值的停止规则。该方法提供了一种原则性的方式来决定是否拒绝“MCMC相比强基线无改进”的零假设，从而实现采样的早停。

Result: 在多种设置下的实证研究表明，该方法能够有效决定停止时机，且往往只需要完整MCMC链预算的一小部分即可达到满意的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于E值的停止规则为贝叶斯深度集成提供了一种高效、原则性的采样终止机制，显著降低了计算成本同时保持了性能优势。

Abstract: Bayesian Deep Ensembles (BDEs) represent a powerful approach for uncertainty quantification in deep learning, combining the robustness of Deep Ensembles (DEs) with flexible multi-chain MCMC. While DEs are affordable in most deep learning settings, (long) sampling of Bayesian neural networks can be prohibitively costly. Yet, adding sampling after optimizing the DEs has been shown to yield significant improvements. This leaves a critical practical question: How long should the sequential sampling process continue to yield significant improvements over the initial optimized DE baseline? To tackle this question, we propose a stopping rule based on E-values. We formulate the ensemble construction as a sequential anytime-valid hypothesis test, providing a principled way to decide whether or not to reject the null hypothesis that MCMC offers no improvement over a strong baseline, to early stop the sampling. Empirically, we study this approach for diverse settings. Our results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach and reveal that only a fraction of the full-chain budget is often required.

</details>


### [350] [Generalization Boundaries of Fine-Tuned Small Language Models for Graph Structural Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18092)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了微调后的小型语言模型在图属性估计任务中的泛化边界，发现模型在超出训练范围的图尺寸和未见过的图族分布上仍能保持较强的结构推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的小型语言模型在图属性估计任务上虽然在分布内表现优异，但其在训练条件之外的泛化能力——特别是针对图尺寸变化和图族分布变化这两个维度的泛化——仍未被充分理解，缺乏对其可靠性边界的实证研究。

Method: 采用受控实验设置，使用三个参数量在3B-4B之间的指令微调模型和两种图序列化格式，系统地评估模型在两个泛化轴上的表现：一是大幅超出训练范围的图尺寸，二是留出的随机图族分布，并进一步在真实世界图基准上评估领域学习能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，微调后的模型在结构上不同的图族之间保持较强的序一致性（ordinal consistency），并且能够在输入尺寸远超训练数据的情况下继续根据结构属性对图进行排序，同时展现出明显的架构特异性退化特征。

Conclusion: 本研究明确界定了微调后的小型语言模型能够可靠泛化的边界，为将其应用于基于图的推理任务提供了实证依据，有助于指导未来图相关任务中语言模型的选用和部署策略。

Abstract: Small language models fine-tuned for graph property estimation have demonstrated strong in-distribution performance, yet their generalization capabilities beyond training conditions remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically investigate the boundaries of structural inference in fine-tuned small language models along two generalization axes - graph size and graph family distribution - and assess domain-learning capability on real-world graph benchmarks. Using a controlled experimental setup with three instruction-tuned models in the 3-4B parameter class and two graph serialization formats, we evaluate performance on graphs substantially larger than the training range and across held-out random graph families. Our results show that fine-tuned models maintain strong ordinal consistency across structurally distinct graph families and continue to rank graphs by structural properties on inputs substantially larger than those seen during training, with distinct architecture-specific degradation profiles. These findings delineate where fine-tuned small language models generalize reliably, providing empirical grounding for their use in graph-based reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [351] [LoRaQ: Optimized Low Rank Approximation for 4-bit Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18117)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Sophie Yáng Shen,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LoRaQ方法，实现了首个全子16位精度的后训练量化流程，在无需数据校准的情况下，通过优化量化误差补偿，在保持相同内存开销的前提下超越了现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化方法在处理4位量化时性能显著下降，而当前最先进的低秩近似方法假设辅助分支必须保持高精度（W16A16）且依赖大量数据相关校准，这限制了其在资源受限硬件上的应用潜力。

Method: 提出LoRaQ（低秩近似量化）方法，这是一种简单、无数据的校准方法，通过优化量化误差补偿来克服对高精度分支的依赖，实现了低秩分支本身的量化，支持混合精度配置（如W8A8、W6A6、W4A8等）。

Result: 在Pixart-Σ和SANA模型上，在相同内存开销下，LoRaQ在原生实现中优于现有最先进方法；混合精度配置（低秩分支W8A8/W6A6/W4A8配合W4主层）在保持全量化架构的同时取得了更优结果。

Conclusion: LoRaQ成功挑战了低秩近似方法必须保持高精度分支的传统假设，首次实现了完全量化的流程，为大规模扩散模型在资源受限硬件上的部署提供了更有效的解决方案，且与现代混合精度硬件兼容。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large diffusion transformers on resource-constrained hardware, but aggressive 4-bit quantization significantly degrades generative performance. Low-rank approximation methods have emerged as a promising solution by appending auxiliary linear branches to restore performance. However, current state-of-the-art approaches assume these branches must retain high precision (W16A16) and rely on heavy, data-dependent calibration for initialization. We challenge both limitations with LoRaQ (Low-Rank Approximated Quantization), a simple, data-free calibration approach that optimizes quantization error compensation. By overcoming the need for high-precision branches, LoRaQ enables the first fully sub-16 bit pipeline, allowing the low-rank branch itself to be quantized. We demonstrate that, at equal memory overhead, LoRaQ outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in their native implementations on Pixart-$Σ$ and SANA. We also analyze mixed-precision configurations, showing that setups such as W8A8, W6A6, and W4A8 for the low-rank branch, alongside a W4 main layer, yield superior results while maintaining a fully quantized architecture compatible with modern mixed-precision hardware.

</details>


### [352] [An `Inverse' Experimental Framework to Estimate Market Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18130)
*Thomas Asikis,Heinrich Nax*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量化归一化技术的预测框架，能够仅利用订单簿数据（买卖报价和成交价格）在交易早期预测市场配置效率，为数字市场的算法治理提供决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字化市场作为社会技术生态系统的关键基础设施，缺乏原则性的测量框架来指导关于市场效率和公平性的算法治理决策。由于订单簿中的买卖报价并不代表真实的最大购买意愿和最小出售意愿，仅依靠订单簿数据难以评估市场的实际配置效率表现。

Method: 采用"反转"标准诱导值方法的实验设计，提出基于分位数的归一化技术处理高度非结构化、非平稳和非线性的订单簿数据。开发了包括线性回归和梯度提升树在内的多种预测模型，利用来自供需模型的分位数输入进行训练，仅使用买卖报价和价格实现数据（而非诱导保留价值）预测配置效率。

Result: 模型能够在最早的买卖报价阶段就以合理的准确度预测配置效率，并且随着额外实现价格数据的加入，预测性能得到改善。预测技术的性能因目标变量和市场类型而异。

Conclusion: 该框架具有应用于现实世界市场数据的巨大潜力，即使在交易实现之前也能为市场效率和表现提供有价值的洞察，为市场效率和公平性相关的算法治理决策提供重要支撑。

Abstract: Digital marketplaces processing billions of dollars annually represent critical infrastructure in sociotechnical ecosystems, yet their performance optimization lacks principled measurement frameworks that can inform algorithmic governance decisions regarding market efficiency and fairness from complex market data. By looking at orderbook data from double auction markets alone, because bids and asks do not represent true maximum willingnesses to buy and true minimum willingnesses to sell, there is little an economist can say about the market's actual performance in terms of allocative efficiency. We turn to experimental data to address this issue, `inverting' the standard induced value approach of double auction experiments. Our aim is to predict key market features relevant to market efficiency, particularly allocative efficiency, using orderbook data only -- specifically bids, asks and price realizations, but not the induced reservation values -- as early as possible. Since there is no established model of strategically optimal behavior in these markets, and because orderbook data is highly unstructured, non-stationary and non-linear, we propose quantile-based normalization techniques that help us build general predictive models. We develop and train several models, including linear regressions and gradient boosting trees, leveraging quantile-based input from the underlying supply-demand model. Our models can predict allocative efficiency with reasonable accuracy from the earliest bids and asks, and these predictions improve with additional realized price data. The performance of the prediction techniques varies by target and market type. Our framework holds significant potential for application to real-world market data, offering valuable insights into market efficiency and performance, even prior to any trade realizations.

</details>


### [353] [Does "Do Differentiable Simulators Give Better Policy Gradients?'' Give Better Policy Gradients?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18161)
*Ku Onoda,Paavo Parmas,Manato Yaguchi,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对策略梯度中不连续动态导致的一阶梯度估计偏差问题，提出了两种轻量级解决方案：DDCG用于检测并切换估计器，IVW-H用于稳定方差。研究发现，在实际应用中，精细的方差控制往往比处理偏差更为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略梯度强化学习中，可微模型提供的一阶梯度估计比零阶估计更高效，但不连续动态会导致偏差并削弱一阶估计器的效果。现有方法基于噪声较大的REINFORCE估计器构建置信区间来检测不连续性，但这需要针对特定任务调参且样本效率低。本文旨在探究偏差是否为主要障碍，以及何种最小化修复能够解决问题。

Method: 提出两种方法：（1）DDCG（Discontinuity Detection with Confidence Gradient）：一种轻量级测试方法，在非光滑区域切换估计器，仅需单个超参数即可实现鲁棒性能；（2）IVW-H（Inverse-Variance Weighting with Heuristic）：一种逐步逆方差实现方法，无需显式的不连续性检测即可稳定方差。DDCG专注于检测并处理不连续区域，而IVW-H专注于方差控制。

Result: 在标准不连续设置中，DDCG仅需单个超参数即可实现鲁棒性能，且在小样本情况下保持可靠。在可微机器人控制任务中，IVW-H无需显式不连续检测即可稳定方差并取得优异结果。两种方法相比先前需要任务特定调参的方法均有显著改进。

Conclusion: 研究表明，虽然估计器切换在受控研究中能提升鲁棒性，但在实际部署中，精细的方差控制往往占据主导地位。这为策略梯度方法在不连续环境中的应用提供了新的见解和实践指导。

Abstract: In policy gradient reinforcement learning, access to a differentiable model enables 1st-order gradient estimation that accelerates learning compared to relying solely on derivative-free 0th-order estimators. However, discontinuous dynamics cause bias and undermine the effectiveness of 1st-order estimators. Prior work addressed this bias by constructing a confidence interval around the REINFORCE 0th-order gradient estimator and using these bounds to detect discontinuities. However, the REINFORCE estimator is notoriously noisy, and we find that this method requires task-specific hyperparameter tuning and has low sample efficiency. This paper asks whether such bias is the primary obstacle and what minimal fixes suffice. First, we re-examine standard discontinuous settings from prior work and introduce DDCG, a lightweight test that switches estimators in nonsmooth regions; with a single hyperparameter, DDCG achieves robust performance and remains reliable with small samples. Second, on differentiable robotics control tasks, we present IVW-H, a per-step inverse-variance implementation that stabilizes variance without explicit discontinuity detection and yields strong results. Together, these findings indicate that while estimator switching improves robustness in controlled studies, careful variance control often dominates in practical deployments.

</details>


### [354] [Scalable Neighborhood-Based Multi-Agent Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18190)
*Tim Goppelsroeder,Rasmus Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MADDPG-K算法，通过将每个智能体的中心化评论家限制为仅关注k个最近邻居，解决了标准MADDPG在大规模多智能体场景下的计算瓶颈问题，实现了常数级评论家输入规模和更优的运行时扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的中心化评论家方法（如MADDPG）在多智能体强化学习中表现出色，但其评论家网络的输入规模随智能体数量线性增长，导致在大规模场景下训练成本过高，存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出MADDPG-K方法，将每个智能体的评论家网络输入限制为基于欧氏距离度量下的k个最近邻居智能体的观测和动作，而非所有智能体，从而保证评论家输入规模恒定，并将二次复杂度开销转移到廉价的标量距离计算上，而非昂贵的神经网络矩阵运算。

Result: 在Multi-Particle Environment套件的协作和对抗环境中验证了方法的有效性：相比标准MADDPG取得了相当或更优的性能，在协作场景中收敛更快，且随着智能体数量增长展现出更好的运行时扩展性。

Conclusion: MADDPG-K成功解决了中心化评论家方法的可扩展性问题，在保持性能竞争力的同时显著提升了大规模多智能体场景下的训练效率，为可扩展的多智能体强化学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose MADDPG-K, a scalable extension to Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that addresses the computational limitations of centralized critic approaches. Centralized critics, which condition on the observations and actions of all agents, have demonstrated significant performance gains in cooperative and competitive multi-agent settings. However, their critic networks grow linearly in input size with the number of agents, making them increasingly expensive to train at scale. MADDPG-K mitigates this by restricting each agent's critic to the $k$ closest agents under a chosen metric which in our case is Euclidean distance. This ensures a constant-size critic input regardless of the total agent count. We analyze the complexity of this approach, showing that the quadratic cost it retains arises from cheap scalar distance computations rather than the expensive neural network matrix multiplications that bottleneck standard MADDPG. We validate our method empirically across cooperative and adversarial environments from the Multi-Particle Environment suite, demonstrating competitive or superior performance compared to MADDPG, faster convergence in cooperative settings, and better runtime scaling as the number of agents grows. Our code is available at https://github.com/TimGop/MADDPG-K .

</details>


### [355] [Attraction, Repulsion, and Friction: Introducing DMF, a Friction-Augmented Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18194)
*Arkadii Kazanskii,Tatiana Petrova,Konstantin Bagrianskii,Aleksandr Puzikov,Radu State*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Drifting Models进行了理论补完并提出改进模型DMF，证明了漂移场平衡的可识别性，并大幅降低了训练计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Drifting Models的分析存在两个未解决的理论问题：两粒子代理模型中漂移场局部排斥性缺乏收敛保证，且漂移场消失（$V_{p,q}\equiv 0$）并不能从理论上保证学习到的分布$q$与目标分布$p$匹配。本文旨在填补这些理论空白并改进模型性能。

Method: 1. 推导了两粒子代理模型的收缩阈值，证明了线性调度的摩擦系数可给出误差轨迹的有限视野边界；2. 在高斯核假设下证明了漂移场平衡的可识别性：若$V_{p,q}$在任意开集上消失，则必有$q=p$；3. 提出摩擦增强模型DMF（Drifting Model with Friction）。

Result: 理论贡献：证明了漂移场消失强制分布匹配（填补了Deng et al.命题3.1的逆命题）；实验贡献：DMF在FFHQ成人到儿童域转换任务上匹配或超越Optimal Flow Matching性能，同时训练计算量降低16倍。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上完善了Drifting Models的收敛性分析，证明了漂移场平衡的可识别性，并通过引入摩擦机制提出了更高效的DMF模型，实现了理论严谨性与实际效率的双重提升。

Abstract: Drifting Models [Deng et al., 2026] train a one-step generator by evolving samples under a kernel-based drift field, avoiding ODE integration at inference. The original analysis leaves two questions open. The drift-field iteration admits a locally repulsive regime in a two-particle surrogate, and vanishing of the drift ($V_{p,q}\equiv 0$) is not known to force the learned distribution $q$ to match the target $p$. We derive a contraction threshold for the surrogate and show that a linearly-scheduled friction coefficient gives a finite-horizon bound on the error trajectory. Under a Gaussian kernel we prove that the drift-field equilibrium is identifiable: vanishing of $V_{p,q}$ on any open set forces $q=p$, closing the converse of Proposition 3.1 of Deng et al. Our friction-augmented model, DMF (Drifting Model with Friction), matches or exceeds Optimal Flow Matching on FFHQ adult-to-child domain translation at 16x lower training compute.

</details>


### [356] [FSEVAL: Feature Selection Evaluation Toolbox and Dashboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18227)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FSEVAL，这是一个带有可视化仪表板的特征选择评估工具箱，旨在帮助研究人员全面、便捷地评估特征选择算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征选择是机器学习和数据挖掘中的基本任务，旨在区分冗余特征和有效特征以解决维度灾难并保持可解释性。然而，在监督和非监督设置中，缺乏标准化、统一的评估工具来全面评估不同的特征选择算法。

Method: 提出了FSEVAL工具箱，包含可视化仪表板，提供标准化、统一的评估和可视化功能，支持对特征选择算法进行全面评估。

Result: 开发了一个便捷的特征选择评估工具箱，能够帮助研究人员轻松进行广泛而全面的特征选择算法评估。

Conclusion: FSEVAL为特征选择算法的评估提供了标准化解决方案，降低了研究人员进行全面评估的难度，有助于推动特征选择领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Feature selection is a fundamental machine learning and data mining task, involved with discriminating redundant features from informative ones. It is an attempt to address the curse of dimensionality by removing the redundant features, while unlike dimensionality reduction methods, preserving explainability. Feature selection is conducted in both supervised and unsupervised settings, with different evaluation metrics employed to determine which feature selection algorithm is the best. In this paper, we propose FSEVAL, a feature selection evaluation toolbox accompanied with a visualization dashboard, with the goal to make it easy to comprehensively evaluate feature selection algorithms. FSEVAL aims to provide a standardized, unified, evaluation and visualization toolbox to help the researchers working in the field, conduct extensive and comprehensive evaluation of feature selection algorithms with ease.

</details>


### [357] [Semantic-based Distributed Learning for Diverse and Discriminative Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18237)
*Zhuojun Tian,Chaouki Ben Issaid,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的分布式学习框架，通过约束表示方差来解决传统方法中同类样本表示多样性坍塌的问题，在i.i.d.和非i.i.d.数据分布下均能学习到兼具判别性和多样性的全局结构表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模分布式场景中，传统任务特定方法往往产生非结构化嵌入，导致同类数据样本的变异性坍塌，无法充分利用数据的内在结构，难以满足复杂任务对智能协作的需求。

Method: 针对i.i.d.数据，引入表示方差约束重新表述并解耦全局优化函数，利用原始-对偶方法推导更新规则；针对非i.i.d.数据，采用聚类和虚拟节点复制策略，在各簇内使用块坐标下降法更新模型；节点间共享表示的语义信息，降低对通用神经网络架构的依赖。

Result: 理论证明了所提方法在两种数据分布下均能保持判别性和多样性，并在i.i.d.条件下保证收敛；在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上的仿真实验验证了算法在捕获全局结构表示方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式学习框架成功解决了表示多样性坍塌问题，能够在不同数据分布下学习到优质的全局结构表示，并通过节点间的语义信息共享提升了模型灵活性，为分布式协作学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In large-scale distributed scenarios, increasingly complex tasks demand more intelligent collaboration across networks, requiring the joint extraction of structural representations from data samples. However, conventional task-specific approaches often result in nonstructural embeddings, leading to collapsed variability among data samples within the same class, particularly in classification tasks. To address this issue and fully leverage the intrinsic structure of data for downstream applications, we propose a novel distributed learning framework that ensures both diverse and discriminative representations. For independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data, we reformulate and decouple the global optimization function by introducing constraints on representation variance. The update rules are then derived and simplified using a primal-dual approach. For non-i.i.d. data distributions, we tackle the problem by clustering and virtually replicating nodes, allowing model updates within each cluster using block coordinate descent. In both cases, the resulting optimal solutions are theoretically proven to maintain discriminative and diverse properties, with a guaranteed convergence for i.i.d. conditions. Additionally, semantic information from representations is shared among nodes, reducing the need for common neural network architectures. Finally, extensive simulations on MNIST, CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 confirm the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms in capturing global structural representations.

</details>


### [358] [Towards Disentangled Preference Optimization Dynamics Beyond Likelihood Displacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18239)
*Wei Chen,Yubing Wu,Junmei Yang,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao,John Paisley,Min Chen,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了偏好优化中“似然位移”现象的统一机制，提出了可测试的“解耦带”（DB）条件，并设计了即插即用的奖励校准（RC）方法，在不改变原目标函数的情况下有效缓解似然位移并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于边界的偏好优化目标函数普遍存在“似然位移”问题，即在压制被拒绝回复的同时也会意外压制被选择的回复，而目前缺乏通用的机制来防止这一现象。

Method: 1) 提出偏好优化的统一激励-分数分解，揭示不同目标函数共享相同的局部更新方向，仅标量权重不同；2) 通过分析选中/拒绝似然的动力学，识别出“解耦带”（DB）条件，用于判断训练何时能实现“压制败者、保持胜者”的理想路径；3) 基于DB提出即插即用的奖励校准（RC），自适应地重新平衡选中与拒绝样本的更新，以满足DB条件。

Result: 实证研究表明，RC方法能够引导训练趋向更解耦的动力学过程，在多种偏好优化目标函数上普遍缓解了似然位移问题，并经常提升下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为偏好优化提供了统一的理论视角和实用的解决方案，所提出的解耦带条件和奖励校准方法具有广泛适用性，无需重新设计基础目标函数即可有效改善训练动态和模型表现。

Abstract: Preference optimization is widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences. However, many margin-based objectives suppress the chosen response along with the rejected one, a phenomenon known as likelihood displacement, and no general mechanism currently prevents this across objectives.
  We bridge this gap by presenting a unified \emph{incentive-score decomposition} of preference optimization, revealing that diverse objectives share identical local update directions and differ only in their scalar weighting coefficients.
  Building on this decomposition, by analyzing the dynamics of the chosen/rejected likelihoods, we identify the \emph{disentanglement band} (DB), a simple, testable condition that characterizes when training can avoid likelihood displacement by realizing the preferred pathway: suppressing the loser while maintaining the winner, possibly after an initial transient.
  Leveraging the DB, we propose a plug-and-play \emph{reward calibration} (RC) that adaptively rebalances chosen versus rejected updates to satisfy the DB and mitigate likelihood displacement, without redesigning the base objective.
  Empirical results show that RC steers training toward more disentangled dynamics and often improves downstream performance across a range of objectives. Our code is available at https://github.com/IceyWuu/DisentangledPreferenceOptimization.

</details>


### [359] [Correction and Corruption: A Two-Rate View of Error Flow in LLM Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18245)
*Fernando Reitich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出配对结果测量接口审计大语言模型协议步骤，通过校正率(c)和破坏率(γ)两个指标预测准确率变化，识别混合偏移、呈现污染和状态不足三种失效机制，使协议步骤成为可审计模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型协议（多调用结构化过程）仅通过端到端准确率评估，缺乏对其何时有益、何时有害、分布偏移下行为是否迁移的深入理解，需要细粒度审计方法。

Method: 提出配对结果测量接口，记录基准正确位E₀和步骤后正确位E₁，分离校正（E₀=0→E₁=1）与破坏（E₀=1→E₁=0），定义校正率c和破坏率γ；开发混合偏移校正（难度代理条件化）、呈现污染检测和马尔可夫分解测试三种诊断方法。

Result: 在合成数学任务和GSM8K上验证：校准接口正确预测协议步骤激活/抑制时机；条件化难度代理恢复混合偏移下的稳定性；马尔可夫分解测试识别多步骤管道的可组合性边界。

Conclusion: 通过诊断测试的协议步骤可成为可审计模块：以估计增益为门控、以难度代理校正混合偏移、可预测地组合到多步骤管道中，为LLM协议提供系统化审计框架。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as protocols: structured multi-call procedures that spend additional computation to transform a baseline answer into a final one. These protocols are evaluated only by end-to-end accuracy, giving limited insight into when they help, when they hurt, and whether their behavior transfers under distribution shift or composition. We propose a paired-outcome measurement interface for auditing a single protocol step on exact-match tasks. For each instance, the interface records a baseline correctness bit $E_0\in\{0,1\}$ and a post-step correctness bit $E_1\in\{0,1\}$, separating correction ($E_0=0\to E_1=1$) from corruption ($E_0=1\to E_1=0$) through two rates: $c=\Pr(E_1=1\mid E_0=0)$ and $γ=\Pr(E_1=0\mid E_0=1)$. These rates predict accuracy changes and define a reusable empirical interface testable across seeds, mixtures, and pipelines. We identify three failure mechanisms. Under mixture shift, pooled estimates of $(c,γ)$ become biased when calibration and deployment mixtures differ; conditioning on a difficulty proxy restores stability without additional model calls. Under presentation contamination, selection protocols alter the interface through stable presentation artifacts when candidate content is fixed. Under state insufficiency, the correctness bit may not carry enough history for multi-step pipelines to compose predictably; a Markov factorization test identifies when composition is valid and where additional state is needed. When a protocol step passes these diagnostics, it becomes an auditable module: gated by estimated gain, conditioned on a difficulty proxy to correct mixture bias, and composed into multi-step pipelines with predictable accuracy. We demonstrate these ideas on synthetic mathematical tasks and on GSM8K, where the calibrated interface correctly predicts when protocol steps should be activated or suppressed.

</details>


### [360] [Universally Empowering Zeroth-Order Optimization via Adaptive Layer-wise Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18264)
*Fei Wang,Li Shen,Liang Ding,Chao Xue,Ye Liu,Changxing Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaLeZO框架，通过自适应分层扰动策略解决零阶优化在大语言模型微调中的收敛慢、方差高问题，实现1.7x-3.0x加速且不增加显存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化虽能节省显存，但存在收敛慢和方差高的问题。作者分析发现扰动生成和参数更新占训练延迟的40%以上，且标准均匀探索策略忽视了深度网络各层敏感性的异质性，导致计算资源浪费。

Method: 将层级选择建模为非平稳多臂老虎机问题，动态地将有限的扰动预算分配给最敏感的参数；引入基于有放回抽样的逆概率加权机制，保证梯度估计无偏性的同时起到时序去噪作用以降低方差。

Result: 在LLaMA和OPT模型（6.7B至30B参数）上的实验表明，相比最先进方法实现了1.7x至3.0x的时钟加速；AdaLeZO可作为通用即插即用模块无缝增强现有ZO优化器的效率，且不引入额外显存开销。

Conclusion: AdaLeZO成功解决了零阶优化在LLM微调中的效率瓶颈，证明了自适应分层策略的有效性，为零阶优化在实际应用中提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order optimization presents a promising memory-efficient paradigm for fine-tuning Large Language Models by relying solely on forward passes. However, its practical adoption is severely constrained by slow wall-clock convergence and high estimation variance. In this work, we dissect the runtime characteristics of ZO algorithms and identify a critical system bottleneck where the generation of perturbations and parameter updates accounts for over 40% of the training latency. We argue that the standard uniform exploration strategy is fundamentally flawed as it fails to account for the heterogeneous sensitivity of layers in deep networks, resulting in computationally wasteful blind searches. To address this structural mismatch, we propose AdaLeZO, an Adaptive Layer-wise ZO optimization framework. By formulating the layer selection process as a non-stationary Multi-Armed Bandit problem, AdaLeZO dynamically allocates the limited perturbation budget to the most sensitive parameters. We further introduce an Inverse Probability Weighting mechanism based on sampling with replacement, which guarantees unbiased gradient estimation while effectively acting as a temporal denoiser to reduce variance. Extensive experiments on LLaMA and OPT models ranging from 6.7B to 30B parameters demonstrate that AdaLeZO achieves 1.7x to 3.0x wall-clock acceleration compared to state-of-the-art methods. Crucially, AdaLeZO functions as a universal plug-and-play module that seamlessly enhances the efficiency of existing ZO optimizers without incurring additional memory overhead.

</details>


### [361] [Dissipative Latent Residual Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Modeling and Identification of Electromechanical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18277)
*Youyuan Long,Gokhan Solak,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiLaR-PINN方法，通过引入偏斜耗散形式的潜在残差网络并配合课程学习策略，在保证能量非增的物理约束下学习未建模的耗散效应，在直升机系统上实现了更准确的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机电系统的第一性原理模型常无法准确捕捉关节摩擦、杂散损耗和结构阻尼等复杂耗散效应；现有基于残差学习的物理信息神经网络（PINN）通常使用无约束的多层感知机（MLP）作为残差项，可能会向系统注入虚假能量，违反物理一致性。

Method: 提出DiLaR-PINN方法：1) 残差网络仅在不可观测的潜在状态分量上运作；2) 采用偏斜耗散形式参数化，保证任意网络参数下能量非增；3) 针对状态部分可观测问题，开发循环展开方案与基于课程的序列长度扩展策略以实现稳定且数据高效的训练。

Result: 在真实直升机系统上验证，与纯物理模型、非结构化残差MLP、带软耗散约束的DiLaR变体及黑盒LSTM四类基线相比，DiLaR-PINN能更准确地捕捉耗散效应，并在长期外推任务中取得更优性能。

Conclusion: DiLaR-PINN通过物理一致的网络结构设计，有效解决了残差学习中能量注入问题，为复杂机电系统的高精度动力学建模提供了一种可靠方法。

Abstract: Accurate dynamical modeling is essential for simulation and control of embodied systems, yet first-principles models of electromechanical systems often fail to capture complex dissipative effects such as joint friction, stray losses, and structural damping. While residual-learning physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) can effectively augment imperfect first-principles models with data-driven components, the residual terms are typically implemented as unconstrained multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), which may inadvertently inject artificial energy into the system.
  To more faithfully model the dissipative dynamics, we propose DiLaR-PINN, a dissipative latent residual PINN designed to learn unmodeled dissipative effects in a physically consistent manner. Structurally, the residual network operates only on unmeasurable (latent) state components and is parameterized in a skew-dissipative form that guarantees non-increasing energy for any choice of network parameters. To enable stable and data-efficient training under partial measurability of the state, we further develop a recurrent rollout scheme with a curriculum-based sequence length extension strategy.
